
--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/ArchiveAcceptance.cs ---
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Formats.Tar;
using System.IO;
using System.IO.Compression;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

internal static class ArchiveAcceptance
{
    public static async Task RunAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        int before = run.Failures;
        var engine = new ArchiveEngine(Path.Combine(run.Root, "tools", "7zip", "bin", "7z.dll"), WorkerPath(run.Root));
        var root = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "归档素材 中文 空格");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(root);
        var folder = Path.Combine(root, "资料");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(folder, "子目录"));
        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(folder, "空目录"));
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(folder, "说明.txt"), string.Concat(Enumerable.Repeat("猫咪文件助手 · archive content\r\n", 500)), Encoding.UTF8);
        File.WriteAllBytes(Path.Combine(folder, "子目录", "随机.bin"), RandomNumberGenerator.GetBytes(2 * 1024 * 1024 + 160 * 1024));
        var loose = Path.Combine(root, "文件.txt");
        File.WriteAllText(loose, "原文件保留 / same bytes after extraction\n", Encoding.UTF8);
        foreach (string path in Directory.GetFiles(root, "*", SearchOption.AllDirectories)) run.TrackSource(path);
        var expected = Manifest(root);
        var outputs = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "archive-outputs");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(outputs);
        string? standardZip = null;

        await run.CaseAsync("archive-engine-ready", () =>
        {
            Program.Assert(engine.IsReady, "Bundled archive worker/DLL is incomplete.");
            run.Evidence["archive-engine"] = new { dll = Path.Combine(run.Root, "tools", "7zip", "bin", "7z.dll"), worker = WorkerPath(run.Root) };
            return Task.CompletedTask;
        });
        foreach (var format in Enum.GetValues<ArchiveFormat>())
        foreach (var level in ArchiveCapabilities.Levels(format))
        {
            string name = $"archive-roundtrip-{format}-{level}";
            await run.CaseAsync(name, async () =>
            {
                var created = await Create(engine, [folder, loose, Path.Combine(folder, "说明.txt"), folder], outputs, name, new(format, level));
                var extracted = await Extract(engine, created.OutputPath!, outputs);
                AssertManifest(expected, extracted.OutputPath!);
                Program.Assert(Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(extracted.OutputPath!, "资料", "空目录")), "Empty directory was lost.");
                Program.Assert(created.Statistics is { SourceBytes: > 0, OutputBytes: > 0 }, "Archive statistics missing.");
                if (format == ArchiveFormat.Zip)
                {
                    using var zip = ZipFile.OpenRead(created.OutputPath!);
                    Program.Assert(zip.Entries.Any(e => e.FullName.Replace('\\', '/') == "资料/说明.txt"), "Independent ZIP reader cannot find the mapped path.");
                }
                if (format == ArchiveFormat.Tar)
                {
                    using var reader = new TarReader(File.OpenRead(created.OutputPath!));
                    int count = 0; while (reader.GetNextEntry() is not null) count++;
                    Program.Assert(count >= 5, "Independent TAR reader found an incomplete archive.");
                }
                if (format == ArchiveFormat.Zip && level == ArchiveLevel.Standard) standardZip = created.OutputPath;
                run.Evidence[name] = new { created.OutputPath, created.Statistics, extracted = extracted.OutputPath };
            });
        }

        await run.CaseAsync("archive-root-collisions-and-existing-results", async () =>
        {
            var second = Path.Combine(root, "第二来源", "资料"); Directory.CreateDirectory(second);
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(second, "说明.txt"), "different root");
            var first = await Create(engine, [folder, second], outputs, "同名包", new());
            var next = await Create(engine, [folder, second], outputs, "同名包", new());
            Program.Assert(first.OutputPath != next.OutputPath && File.Exists(first.OutputPath), "Creation overwrote an existing archive.");
            var extracted = await Extract(engine, first.OutputPath!, outputs);
            Program.Assert(File.Exists(Path.Combine(extracted.OutputPath!, "资料", "说明.txt")) && File.Exists(Path.Combine(extracted.OutputPath!, "资料 (2)", "说明.txt")), "Same-name roots were lost.");
            var again = await Extract(engine, first.OutputPath!, outputs);
            Program.Assert(again.OutputPath != extracted.OutputPath && Directory.Exists(extracted.OutputPath), "Extraction overwrote an existing directory.");
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("archive-output-inside-source-snapshot", async () =>
        {
            var inside = Path.Combine(root, "内部输出"); Directory.CreateDirectory(inside);
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(inside, "source.txt"), "freeze before staging");
            var created = await Create(engine, [inside], inside, "内部包", new());
            var extracted = await Extract(engine, created.OutputPath!, outputs);
            Program.Assert(Directory.GetFiles(extracted.OutputPath!, "*", SearchOption.AllDirectories).Length == 1, "Temporary/self output entered the archive.");
            Program.Assert(File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(extracted.OutputPath!, "内部输出", "source.txt")) == "freeze before staging", "Inside-source output damaged input.");
        });

        foreach (var format in new[] { ArchiveFormat.Zip, ArchiveFormat.SevenZip })
        await run.CaseAsync("archive-encryption-" + format, async () =>
        {
            string password = format == ArchiveFormat.Zip ? "Cat \" space ' 2026!" : "猫咪 🐈 \" 空格 ' 2026";
            var created = await Create(engine, [folder, loose], outputs, "加密-" + format, new(format, ArchiveLevel.Standard, password));
            var inspection = await engine.InspectAsync(created.OutputPath!);
            Program.Assert(inspection.Accepted && inspection.RequiresPassword, "Encryption was not detected.");
            var wrong = await engine.ExecuteAsync(new ExtractArchiveRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), created.OutputPath!, outputs, new("wrong password")));
            Program.Assert(wrong.Status == JobStatus.Failed && wrong.OutputPath is null, "Wrong password produced a committed output.");
            AssertManifest(expected, (await Extract(engine, created.OutputPath!, outputs, password)).OutputPath!);
            if (format == ArchiveFormat.Zip)
            {
                AssertZipAes256(created.OutputPath!);
            }
            if (format == ArchiveFormat.SevenZip)
                Program.Assert(inspection.EntryCount == 0 || inspection.RequiresPassword, "7Z encrypted header disclosed a plain inspection.");
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("archive-sevenzip-split-recombine-and-missing-volume", async () =>
        {
            var created = await Create(engine, [folder, loose], outputs, "分卷测试", new(ArchiveFormat.SevenZip, ArchiveLevel.Store, SplitBytes: 1024 * 1024));
            Program.Assert(created.OutputKind == ArchiveOutputKind.Directory, "Split output must be a committed group directory.");
            var volumes = Directory.GetFiles(created.OutputPath!, "*.7z.*").Order(StringComparer.Ordinal).ToArray();
            Program.Assert(volumes.Length >= 3 && volumes.All(v => new FileInfo(v).Length <= 1024 * 1024), "Split size contract failed.");
            Program.Assert(ArchiveImportService.NormalizeExtractionSources(volumes).Count == 1, "Multiple volumes were not grouped.");
            AssertManifest(expected, (await Extract(engine, volumes[0], outputs)).OutputPath!);
            string missing = volumes[1] + ".missing"; File.Move(volumes[1], missing);
            try
            {
                var result = await engine.ExecuteAsync(new ExtractArchiveRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), volumes[0], outputs, new()));
                Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Failed && result.OutputPath is null, "Missing volume was silently accepted.");
            }
            finally { File.Move(missing, volumes[1]); }
        });

        foreach (string fixture in new[] { "Rar.rar", "Rar5.rar" })
        await run.CaseAsync("archive-extract-" + fixture, async () =>
        {
            string path = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "fixtures", "archives", fixture);
            var extracted = await Extract(engine, path, outputs);
            var hashes = Manifest(extracted.OutputPath!);
            var known = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            {
                ["тест.txt"] = "4D581D93D369F6E1C9B295FF38D82DABD577F927DFAF0C35818C015C85E322D9",
                ["exe/test.exe"] = "8557928804F57ECC340B3BB38B095A3607474EC8DEB0076F316FCFE02B562106",
                ["jpg/test.jpg"] = "B251C7501FB0F55DD4A92FEABE0A6F5733BC40A02679498155FAE9B30138FC53"
            };
            Program.Assert(hashes.Count == known.Count && known.All(p => hashes.TryGetValue(p.Key, out var hash) && hash == p.Value), "RAR content differs from independently verified hashes.");
            Program.Assert(Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(extracted.OutputPath!, "Empty")), "RAR empty directory was lost.");
        });

        foreach (string type in new[] { "gzip", "bzip2", "xz" })
        foreach (bool tar in new[] { false, true })
        await run.CaseAsync($"archive-read-{(tar ? "tar-" : "")}{type}", async () =>
        {
            string source = loose;
            string suffix = type switch { "gzip" => "gz", "bzip2" => "bz2", _ => "xz" };
            if (tar) source = (await Create(engine, [folder, loose], outputs, "外层原始-" + type, new(ArchiveFormat.Tar, ArchiveLevel.Store))).OutputPath!;
            string path = Path.Combine(outputs, tar ? "包装-" + type + ".tar." + suffix : "单文件-" + type + "." + suffix);
            await Cli(run.Root, ["a", "-t" + type, "-y", "-sccUTF-8", "-bso0", path, source]);
            var extracted = await Extract(engine, path, outputs);
            if (tar) AssertManifest(expected, extracted.OutputPath!);
            else
            {
                var files = Directory.GetFiles(extracted.OutputPath!, "*", SearchOption.AllDirectories);
                Program.Assert(files.Length == 1 && Program.Hash(files[0]) == Program.Hash(loose), "Single compressed stream changed bytes.");
            }
        });

        var malicious = Path.Combine(root, "恶意包"); Directory.CreateDirectory(malicious);
        string sentinel = Path.Combine(outputs, "sentinel.txt"); File.WriteAllText(sentinel, "keep me");
        foreach (var (name, paths) in new (string, string[])[]
        {
            ("parent", ["../sentinel.txt"]), ("absolute", ["C:/outside.txt"]), ("unc", ["//server/share/a"]),
            ("ads", ["a.txt:stream"]), ("device", ["CON.txt"]), ("tail-dot", ["bad./file"]),
            ("duplicate", ["a.txt", "A.txt"]), ("file-directory", ["a", "a/file"])
        })
        await run.CaseAsync("archive-reject-path-" + name, async () =>
        {
            string zip = Path.Combine(malicious, name + ".zip"); WriteZip(zip, paths);
            var result = await engine.ExecuteAsync(new ExtractArchiveRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), zip, outputs, new()));
            Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Failed && result.OutputPath is null && File.ReadAllText(sentinel) == "keep me", "Unsafe archive escaped its staging boundary.");
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("archive-reject-unix-zip-link", async () =>
        {
            string zip = Path.Combine(malicious, "link.zip"); WriteZip(zip, ["link"], unixLink: true);
            var result = await engine.ExecuteAsync(new ExtractArchiveRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), zip, outputs, new()));
            Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Failed && result.OutputPath is null, "ZIP link was accepted.");
        });
        foreach (var type in new[] { TarEntryType.SymbolicLink, TarEntryType.HardLink, TarEntryType.Fifo })
        await run.CaseAsync("archive-reject-tar-special-" + type, async () =>
        {
            string path = Path.Combine(malicious, type + ".tar");
            using (var writer = new TarWriter(File.Create(path)))
            {
                var entry = new PaxTarEntry(type, "link");
                if (type is TarEntryType.SymbolicLink or TarEntryType.HardLink) entry.LinkName = "../sentinel.txt";
                writer.WriteEntry(entry);
            }
            var result = await engine.ExecuteAsync(new ExtractArchiveRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), path, outputs, new()));
            Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Failed && result.OutputPath is null, "TAR special entry was accepted.");
        });

        foreach (var (name, limits) in new[]
        {
            ("entries", new ArchiveLimits(MaximumEntries: 1)),
            ("depth", new ArchiveLimits(MaximumDepth: 1)),
            ("bytes", new ArchiveLimits(MaximumBytes: 20))
        })
        await run.CaseAsync("archive-limit-" + name, async () =>
        {
            Program.Assert(standardZip is not null, "Standard ZIP fixture missing.");
            var result = await engine.ExecuteAsync(new ExtractArchiveRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), standardZip!, outputs, new(Limits: limits)));
            Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Failed && result.OutputPath is null, "Safety limit did not stop extraction.");
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("archive-gnu-tar-current-directory-prefix", async () =>
        {
            string tarPath = Path.Combine(malicious, "gnu-prefix.tar");
            using (var writer = new TarWriter(File.Create(tarPath)))
            {
                writer.WriteEntry(new PaxTarEntry(TarEntryType.Directory, "."));
                writer.WriteEntry(new PaxTarEntry(TarEntryType.RegularFile, "./folder/file.txt") { DataStream = new MemoryStream(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes("GNU TAR compatible")) });
            }
            var extracted = await Extract(engine, tarPath, outputs);
            Program.Assert(File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(extracted.OutputPath!, "folder", "file.txt")) == "GNU TAR compatible", "Harmless GNU TAR prefix was rejected or mapped incorrectly.");
            string traversal = Path.Combine(malicious, "gnu-traversal.tar");
            using (var writer = new TarWriter(File.Create(traversal)))
                writer.WriteEntry(new PaxTarEntry(TarEntryType.RegularFile, "./../sentinel.txt") { DataStream = new MemoryStream([1]) });
            var rejected = await engine.ExecuteAsync(new ExtractArchiveRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), traversal, outputs, new()));
            Program.Assert(rejected.Status == JobStatus.Failed && File.ReadAllText(sentinel) == "keep me", "Prefix normalization admitted traversal.");
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("archive-tar-wrapper-write-budget", async () =>
        {
            var created = await Create(engine, [loose], outputs, "包装额度", new(ArchiveFormat.TarGZip));
            var result = await engine.ExecuteAsync(new ExtractArchiveRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), created.OutputPath!, outputs, new(Limits: new(MaximumBytes: 100))));
            Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Failed && result.OutputPath is null, "Wrapper layer bypassed the write budget.");
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("archive-corruption-failure-then-next-and-observer", async () =>
        {
            Program.Assert(standardZip is not null, "ZIP fixture missing.");
            string broken = Path.Combine(malicious, "broken.zip"); File.WriteAllBytes(broken, [1, 2, 3, 4]);
            ArchiveRequest[] requests = [new ExtractArchiveRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), broken, outputs, new()), new ExtractArchiveRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), standardZip!, outputs, new())];
            var result = await new SerialArchiveQueue(engine).RunAsync(requests, onCompleted: (_, _) => throw new InvalidOperationException("observer"));
            Program.Assert(result.FailedCount == 1 && result.CompletedCount == 1 && Directory.Exists(result.Results[1].Result.OutputPath), "Failure/observer stopped the next task or deleted a success.");
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("archive-cancel-and-no-orphans", async () =>
        {
            string large = Path.Combine(root, "取消.bin"); File.WriteAllBytes(large, RandomNumberGenerator.GetBytes(16 * 1024 * 1024));
            using var cancel = new CancellationTokenSource();
            var progress = new InlineProgress(p => { if (p.ProcessedBytes > 0) cancel.Cancel(); });
            var result = await engine.ExecuteAsync(new CreateArchiveRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), [large], outputs, "取消包", new(ArchiveFormat.SevenZip, ArchiveLevel.Ultra)), progress, cancel.Token);
            Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Cancelled && result.OutputPath is null, "Running compression was not cancelled.");
            await Task.Delay(200);
            Program.Assert(Process.GetProcessesByName("CatFileAssistant.ArchiveWorker").Length == 0, "Archive worker remained after cancellation.");
            Program.Assert(!Directory.GetFileSystemEntries(outputs).Any(p => Path.GetFileName(p).StartsWith(".cat-", StringComparison.Ordinal)), "Task temporary output remained after cancellation/failure.");
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("archive-invalid-output-keeps-source", async () =>
        {
            var result = await engine.ExecuteAsync(new CreateArchiveRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), [loose], loose, "invalid", new()));
            Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Failed && File.ReadAllText(loose).Contains("原文件保留"), "Invalid output damaged the source.");
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("archive-actual-write-budget-with-false-size", async () =>
        {
            string gz = Path.Combine(malicious, "false-size.gz");
            using (var gzip = new GZipStream(File.Create(gz), System.IO.Compression.CompressionMode.Compress))
                gzip.Write(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(new string('x', 4096)));
            byte[] data = File.ReadAllBytes(gz); BitConverter.GetBytes(1u).CopyTo(data, data.Length - 4); File.WriteAllBytes(gz, data);
            var result = await engine.ExecuteAsync(new ExtractArchiveRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), gz, outputs, new(Limits: new(MaximumBytes: 100))));
            Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Failed && result.OutputPath is null && result.Error?.Code.Contains("size-limit") == true, "Actual write budget trusted a false GZip ISIZE.");
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("archive-settings-unknown-enums-preserve-old-fields", () =>
        {
            string settingsDirectory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "archive-settings-migration"); Directory.CreateDirectory(settingsDirectory);
            var store = new SettingsStore(settingsDirectory);
            foreach (string unknown in new[] { "\"future\"", "12345", "{\"future\":true}" })
            {
                var unknownValue = System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer.Deserialize<System.Text.Json.JsonElement>(unknown);
                File.WriteAllText(store.FilePath, System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer.Serialize(new
                {
                    OutputDirectory = @"C:\KeepMe", PresetIds = new Dictionary<string, string> { ["Video"] = "video-mp4-h264" }, GifQuality = "High",
                    ArchiveFormat = unknownValue, ArchiveLevel = unknownValue, ArchiveGrouping = unknownValue
                }));
                var settings = store.Load();
                Program.Assert(settings.OutputDirectory == @"C:\KeepMe" && settings.GifQuality == GifQualityLevel.High && settings.PresetIds[ConversionMode.Video] == "video-mp4-h264", "Unknown archive enum reset old settings.");
                Program.Assert(settings.ArchiveFormat == ArchiveFormat.Zip && settings.ArchiveLevel == ArchiveLevel.Standard && settings.ArchiveGrouping == ArchiveGrouping.Combined, "Unknown archive enum did not return to safe defaults.");
            }
            return Task.CompletedTask;
        });
        await ArchiveFailureAcceptance.RunAsync(run);
        if (!run.NoGui) await RunGui(run, engine, loose);
        run.ArchiveAcceptancePassed = run.Failures == before;
    }

    private static async Task RunGui(AcceptanceRun run, IArchiveEngine engine, string source)
    {
        await run.CaseAsync("archive-wpf-pages-settings-and-layout", async () =>
        {
            var settings = new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "archive-ui-settings"));
            var model = new MainViewModel(settings: settings, archiveEngine: engine) { OutputDirectory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "archive-ui-output") };
            var window = new MainWindow(model) { ShowActivated = false }; window.Show();
            run.StopActiveWindow = async () => { model.Cancel(); model.CancelImport(); await model.ActiveRun; await model.ActiveImport; window.Close(); };
            try
            {
                model.SetArchiveCreatePage();
                await model.AddFilesAsync([source]);
                Program.Assert(model.ArchiveInputs.Count == 1 && model.CanStart, "Create inputs/start state not connected.");
                foreach (var (width, height) in new[] { (1200, 820), (960, 540), (620, 480) })
                {
                    window.Width = width; window.Height = height;
                    await window.Dispatcher.InvokeAsync(() => { window.UpdateLayout(); }, DispatcherPriority.ContextIdle);
                    Render(window, Path.Combine(run.Directory, $"archive-create-{width}x{height}.png"));
                    foreach (string name in new[] { "NavArchiveCreate", "NavArchiveExtract", "ArchiveOptionsScroll", "ArchiveQueueGrid" })
                        Program.Assert(window.FindName(name) is FrameworkElement, "Archive UI element missing: " + name);
                    Program.Assert(window.FindName("ArchiveOptionsScroll") is ScrollViewer scroll && scroll.ActualHeight > 0, "Archive options are inaccessible.");
                    var startButton = (FrameworkElement)window.FindName("StartButton");
                    var startPosition = startButton.TranslatePoint(new Point(0, 0), window);
                    Program.Assert(startPosition.Y >= 0 && startPosition.Y + startButton.ActualHeight <= window.ActualHeight, "Start button is outside the compact layout.");
                }
                model.SelectedArchiveFormat = ArchiveFormat.SevenZip; model.ArchiveSplitEnabled = true;
                model.ArchiveSplitMiB = "0.5"; Program.Assert(!model.CanStart, "Sub-MiB split size was accepted by UI.");
                model.ArchiveSplitMiB = "102401"; Program.Assert(!model.CanStart, "Oversized split was accepted by UI.");
                model.ArchiveSplitEnabled = false;
                model.ArchiveName = "CON"; Program.Assert(!model.CanStart, "Reserved package name was accepted by UI.");
                model.ArchiveName = "文件压缩包";
                model.SelectedArchiveFormat = ArchiveFormat.Tar;
                model.SelectedArchiveFormat = ArchiveFormat.Zip;
                model.ArchivePasswordEnabled = true; model.ArchivePassword = "UI secret stays local";
                await model.RunQueueAsync();
                Program.Assert(model.VisibleArchiveQueue.Any(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Completed), "WPF creation did not complete.");
                Program.Assert(model.VisibleArchiveQueue.Any(j => j.ResultSizeText.Contains("节省") || j.ResultSizeText.Contains("增加")), "Archive result omitted the size comparison percentage.");
                Program.Assert(!File.ReadAllText(settings.FilePath).Contains("UI secret"), "Password persisted to settings.");
                Program.Assert(model.ArchivePassword.Length == 0, "Creation password was not released after batch.");
                string created = model.VisibleArchiveQueue.First(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Completed).OutputPath!;
                model.SetArchiveExtractPage();
                await model.AddFilesAsync([created]); model.ArchivePasswordEnabled = true; model.ArchivePassword = "UI secret stays local";
                await model.RunQueueAsync();
                Program.Assert(model.VisibleArchiveQueue.Any(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Completed), "WPF extraction did not complete.");
                Render(window, Path.Combine(run.Directory, "archive-extract-completed.png"));
                model.SetArchiveCreatePage(); await model.AddFilesAsync([source]);
                Program.Assert(model.CanStart, "Same input could not be processed in a second batch.");
                model.Clear();
                string another = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "另一个输入.txt"); File.WriteAllText(another, "separate output");
                model.SelectedArchiveGrouping = ArchiveGrouping.Separate;
                await model.AddFilesAsync([source, another]); await model.RunQueueAsync();
                Program.Assert(model.VisibleArchiveQueue.Count == 2 && model.VisibleArchiveQueue.All(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Completed)
                    && model.VisibleArchiveQueue.Select(j => j.OutputPath).Distinct().Count() == 2, "Separate mode did not create one archive per input.");
                model.Clear(); model.SetGifPage();
                Program.Assert(model.IsGifPage && !model.IsArchivePage, "Archive page isolation broke GIF mode.");
            }
            finally { window.Close(); run.StopActiveWindow = null; }
        });
    }

    private static void Render(FrameworkElement visual, string path)
    {
        visual.UpdateLayout();
        var image = new RenderTargetBitmap((int)Math.Ceiling(visual.ActualWidth), (int)Math.Ceiling(visual.ActualHeight), 96, 96, PixelFormats.Pbgra32);
        image.Render(visual); var png = new PngBitmapEncoder(); png.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(image)); using var output = File.Create(path); png.Save(output);
    }
    private static string WorkerPath(string root)
    {
        string published = Path.Combine(root, "worker", "CatFileAssistant.ArchiveWorker.exe");
        return File.Exists(published) ? published : Path.Combine(root, "src", "CatFileAssistant.ArchiveWorker", "bin", "Release", "net10.0-windows", "win-x64", "CatFileAssistant.ArchiveWorker.exe");
    }
    private static async Task<ArchiveResult> Create(IArchiveEngine engine, string[] inputs, string output, string name, ArchiveCreateOptions options)
    {
        var result = await engine.ExecuteAsync(new CreateArchiveRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), inputs, output, name, options));
        Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Completed, "Create failed: " + result.Error?.Code + " " + result.Error?.Message);
        return result;
    }
    private static async Task<ArchiveResult> Extract(IArchiveEngine engine, string source, string output, string? password = null)
    {
        var result = await engine.ExecuteAsync(new ExtractArchiveRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), source, output, new(password)));
        Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Completed, "Extract failed: " + result.Error?.Code + " " + result.Error?.Message);
        return result;
    }
    private static Dictionary<string, string> Manifest(string root) => Directory.GetFiles(root, "*", SearchOption.AllDirectories).ToDictionary(p => Path.GetRelativePath(root, p).Replace('\\', '/'), Program.Hash, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    private static void AssertManifest(Dictionary<string, string> expected, string output)
    {
        var actual = Manifest(output);
        Program.Assert(actual.Count == expected.Count && expected.All(p => actual.TryGetValue(p.Key, out string? value) && p.Value == value), "Extracted file set or SHA-256 differs from original inputs.");
    }
    private static void WriteZip(string path, string[] paths, bool unixLink = false)
    {
        using (var zip = ZipFile.Open(path, ZipArchiveMode.Create))
        foreach (string name in paths)
        {
            var entry = zip.CreateEntry(name); if (unixLink) entry.ExternalAttributes = unchecked((int)0xA1FF0000);
            using var writer = new StreamWriter(entry.Open()); writer.Write("../sentinel.txt");
        }
        if (unixLink)
        {
            byte[] data = File.ReadAllBytes(path);
            for (int i = 0; i < data.Length - 46; i++) if (BitConverter.ToUInt32(data, i) == 0x02014b50) data[i + 5] = 3;
            File.WriteAllBytes(path, data);
        }
    }
    private static void AssertZipAes256(string path)
    {
        byte[] bytes = File.ReadAllBytes(path);
        int end = bytes.Length - 22;
        Program.Assert(BitConverter.ToUInt32(bytes, end) == 0x06054b50, "ZIP end record missing.");
        int offset = checked((int)BitConverter.ToUInt32(bytes, end + 16));
        int encrypted = 0;
        while (offset + 46 <= bytes.Length && BitConverter.ToUInt32(bytes, offset) == 0x02014b50)
        {
            int nameBytes = BitConverter.ToUInt16(bytes, offset + 28), extraBytes = BitConverter.ToUInt16(bytes, offset + 30), commentBytes = BitConverter.ToUInt16(bytes, offset + 32);
            bool directory = nameBytes > 0 && bytes[offset + 46 + nameBytes - 1] == (byte)'/';
            if (!directory)
            {
                Program.Assert((BitConverter.ToUInt16(bytes, offset + 8) & 1) != 0 && BitConverter.ToUInt16(bytes, offset + 10) == 99, "ZIP file content is not AES-encrypted.");
                bool aes256 = false;
                for (int extra = offset + 46 + nameBytes, limit = extra + extraBytes; extra + 4 <= limit;)
                {
                    int id = BitConverter.ToUInt16(bytes, extra), length = BitConverter.ToUInt16(bytes, extra + 2);
                    Program.Assert(extra + 4 + length <= limit, "ZIP extra field is truncated.");
                    if (id == 0x9901 && length >= 7) aes256 = bytes[extra + 8] == 3;
                    extra += 4 + length;
                }
                Program.Assert(aes256, "ZIP encryption strength differs from AES-256."); encrypted++;
            }
            offset += 46 + nameBytes + extraBytes + commentBytes;
        }
        Program.Assert(encrypted > 0, "ZIP contains no verified encrypted file.");
    }
    private static async Task Cli(string root, string[] args)
    {
        var start = new ProcessStartInfo(Path.Combine(root, "tools", "7zip", "bin", "7z.exe")) { UseShellExecute = false, CreateNoWindow = true, RedirectStandardOutput = true, RedirectStandardError = true };
        foreach (var argument in args) start.ArgumentList.Add(argument);
        using var process = Process.Start(start) ?? throw new IOException("CLI did not start");
        var stdout = process.StandardOutput.ReadToEndAsync(); var stderr = process.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync();
        await process.WaitForExitAsync(); await stdout;
        Program.Assert(process.ExitCode == 0, "Independent fixture generator failed: " + await stderr);
    }
    private sealed class InlineProgress(Action<ArchiveProgress> callback) : IProgress<ArchiveProgress>
    { public void Report(ArchiveProgress value) => callback(value); }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/ArchiveFailureAcceptance.cs ---
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;
using CatFileAssistant.Ownership;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

/// <summary>Exercises parent lifecycle failures with a controlled self-hosted worker, without requiring a build at test time.</summary>
internal static class ArchiveFailureAcceptance
{
    private const string FixtureDirectoryName = "archive-failure-worker";
    private const string FixtureMarkerName = ".acceptance-fake-worker";
    private const string FixtureMarker = "CatFileAssistant.Acceptance archive failure fixture v1";

    /// <summary>Call before creating the WPF application. Normal acceptance arguments return null.</summary>
    public static int? RunFakeWorker(string[] args)
    {
        if (args.Length != 1 || !args[0].StartsWith("--dll=", StringComparison.Ordinal)) return null;
        var dllPath = args[0][6..];
        if (!Path.IsPathFullyQualified(dllPath)) return null;
        var name = Path.GetFileName(dllPath);
        if (name is not ("fake-forcekill.dll" or "fake-commit.dll")) return null;
        var fixtureDirectory = Path.GetDirectoryName(Path.GetFullPath(dllPath))!;
        if (Path.GetFileName(fixtureDirectory) != FixtureDirectoryName || !File.Exists(dllPath)) return null;
        var marker = Path.Combine(fixtureDirectory, FixtureMarkerName);
        if (!File.Exists(marker) || File.ReadAllText(marker) != FixtureMarker) return null;
        try
        {
            Console.InputEncoding = new UTF8Encoding(false);
            Console.OutputEncoding = new UTF8Encoding(false);
            using var document = JsonDocument.Parse(Console.ReadLine() ?? throw new IOException("Missing fake worker request."));
            var request = document.RootElement;
            if (request.GetProperty("action").GetString() != "create") return 21;
            var outputDirectory = Path.GetFullPath(request.GetProperty("outputDirectory").GetString()!);
            var workDirectory = Path.GetFullPath(request.GetProperty("workDirectory").GetString()!);
            if (!string.Equals(Path.GetDirectoryName(workDirectory), Path.TrimEndingDirectorySeparator(outputDirectory), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                || !Path.GetFileName(workDirectory).StartsWith(".cat-archive-", StringComparison.Ordinal)
                || Directory.Exists(workDirectory) || File.Exists(workDirectory)) return 22;
            using var journal = ArchiveTemporaryJournal.Open(request.GetProperty("journalPath").GetString()!, workDirectory);
            journal.CreateDirectory(workDirectory);
            using (var file = journal.CreateFile(Path.Combine(workDirectory, "own.tmp"), FileAccess.Write))
            using (var writer = new StreamWriter(file, new UTF8Encoding(false))) writer.Write("Unfinished task-owned output.");
            if (request.TryGetProperty("password", out var password) && password.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.String)
                Console.Error.WriteLine("simulated native diagnostic: " + password.GetString());
            if (name == "fake-commit.dll")
            {
                var final = Path.Combine(outputDirectory, "committed-before-cancel.7z");
                using (var file = new FileStream(final, FileMode.CreateNew, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.None))
                using (var writer = new StreamWriter(file, new UTF8Encoding(false))) writer.Write("Committed output must survive cancellation.");
                Console.WriteLine(JsonSerializer.Serialize(new
                {
                    type = "result", status = "completed", outputPath = final, outputKind = "file",
                    sourceBytes = 1, outputBytes = new FileInfo(final).Length, entryCount = 1, format = "7z"
                }));
            }
            // The caller cancels on this progress event, after the temporary directory (and optional commit) exist.
            Console.WriteLine(JsonSerializer.Serialize(new
            {
                type = "progress", processedBytes = 0, processedEntries = 0, stage = "fake-ignore-cancel"
            }));
            Console.Out.Flush();
            Console.Error.Flush();
            // Deliberately never read the subsequent cancel message; only the parent's deadline can stop this process.
            Thread.Sleep(Timeout.Infinite);
            return 23;
        }
        catch (Exception) { return 24; }
    }

    public static async Task RunAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        foreach (var commit in new[] { false, true })
        {
            var name = commit ? "archive-committed-result-survives-forcekill-and-cancel" : "archive-forcekill-cleans-only-owned-staging";
            await run.CaseAsync(name, () => RunCaseAsync(run, commit, name));
        }
    }

    private static async Task RunCaseAsync(AcceptanceRun run, bool commit, string caseName)
    {
        var selfExecutable = Path.ChangeExtension(typeof(ArchiveFailureAcceptance).Assembly.Location, ".exe");
        Program.Assert(File.Exists(selfExecutable), "Acceptance apphost is missing; the lifecycle fixture does not require an SDK.");
        var fixtureDirectory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, FixtureDirectoryName);
        Directory.CreateDirectory(fixtureDirectory);
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(fixtureDirectory, FixtureMarkerName), FixtureMarker);
        var dllPath = Path.Combine(fixtureDirectory, commit ? "fake-commit.dll" : "fake-forcekill.dll");
        File.Copy(Path.Combine(run.Root, "tools", "7zip", "bin", "7z.dll"), dllPath, true);
        var outputDirectory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, caseName);
        var unrelated = Path.Combine(outputDirectory, ".cat-archive-unrelated-sentinel");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(unrelated);
        var sentinel = Path.Combine(unrelated, "keep.txt");
        File.WriteAllText(sentinel, "An unrelated task owns this directory.");
        var sentinelHash = Program.Hash(sentinel);
        var source = Path.Combine(fixtureDirectory, commit ? "commit-source.txt" : "forcekill-source.txt");
        File.WriteAllText(source, "Input must remain unchanged.");
        run.TrackSource(source);
        var log = new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(outputDirectory, "logs"));
        var engine = new ArchiveEngine(dllPath, selfExecutable, log);
        var secret = "PasswordMustNeverReachDiagnostics_" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
        using var cancellation = new CancellationTokenSource();
        var progress = new CancelOnProgress(cancellation);
        var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        var result = await engine.ExecuteAsync(new CreateArchiveRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), [source], outputDirectory,
            "failure-fixture", new ArchiveCreateOptions(ArchiveFormat.SevenZip, Password: secret)), progress, cancellation.Token)
            .WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(15));
        Program.Assert(progress.CancellationTriggered, "The self-hosted fake worker did not enter the deliberate hang.");
        Program.Assert(clock.Elapsed >= TimeSpan.FromSeconds(4.5) && clock.Elapsed < TimeSpan.FromSeconds(12),
            "The worker was not stopped through the five-second forced-cancellation path.");
        Program.Assert(result.Status == (commit ? JobStatus.Completed : JobStatus.Cancelled),
            commit ? "A reported commit became Cancelled after worker termination." : "A killed uncommitted task did not return Cancelled.");
        var remaining = Directory.GetDirectories(outputDirectory, ".cat-archive-*", SearchOption.TopDirectoryOnly);
        Program.Assert(remaining.Length == 1 && string.Equals(remaining[0], unrelated, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase),
            "Forced termination left its temporary directory or deleted another task's directory.");
        Program.Assert(File.Exists(sentinel) && Program.Hash(sentinel) == sentinelHash, "The unrelated staging sentinel was changed.");
        Program.Assert(File.Exists(log.FilePath) && !File.ReadAllText(log.FilePath).Contains(secret, StringComparison.Ordinal),
            "A password echoed by the simulated native worker reached the parent's diagnostics.");
        if (commit)
        {
            Program.Assert(result.OutputKind == ArchiveOutputKind.File && result.OutputPath is not null && File.Exists(result.OutputPath)
                && File.ReadAllText(result.OutputPath) == "Committed output must survive cancellation.",
                "The committed file was removed or changed by cancellation cleanup.");
        }
        else Program.Assert(Directory.GetFiles(outputDirectory, "*.7z", SearchOption.TopDirectoryOnly).Length == 0,
            "The cancelled task acquired a final archive.");
        run.AssertSourcesUnchanged();
        run.Evidence[caseName] = new
        {
            forceKillDeadlineSeconds = 5, elapsedSeconds = clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds,
            progress.CancellationTriggered, result.Status, result.OutputPath,
            exactOwnedDirectoryRemoved = true, unrelatedDirectoryAndSentinelRetained = true,
            nativeDiagnosticPasswordAbsent = true, committedResultPreserved = commit
        };
    }

    private sealed class CancelOnProgress(CancellationTokenSource cancellation) : IProgress<ArchiveProgress>
    {
        public bool CancellationTriggered { get; private set; }
        public void Report(ArchiveProgress value)
        {
            if (value.Stage != "fake-ignore-cancel") return;
            CancellationTriggered = true;
            cancellation.Cancel();
        }
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/ArchiveJournalAcceptance.cs ---
using System.IO;
using System.Text;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;
using CatFileAssistant.Ownership;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

internal static class ArchiveJournalAcceptance
{
    public static async Task RunAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        await run.CaseAsync("v10-archive-journal-preserves-added-user-files-and-removes-exact-owned-files", () => AddedFilesAsync(run));
        await run.CaseAsync("v10-archive-journal-does-not-follow-committed-results-or-replaced-identities", () => MovesAsync(run));
        await run.CaseAsync("v10-archive-journal-crash-partial-line-and-linear-receipts", () => PartialAsync(run));
        await run.CaseAsync("v10-archive-journal-integrates-with-verified-owned-artifacts-cleanup", () => RegisteredAsync(run));
    }

    private static (string Work, string Journal, string Directory) New(AcceptanceRun run, string name)
    {
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, name); Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var work = Path.Combine(directory, ".cat-archive-" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
        var journal = Path.Combine(directory, ".cat-archive-receipts-" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N") + ".jsonl");
        ArchiveTemporaryJournal.Create(journal, work);
        return (work, journal, directory);
    }
    private static void Write(ArchiveTemporaryJournal journal, string path, string value)
    {
        using var file = journal.CreateFile(path, FileAccess.Write);
        using var writer = new StreamWriter(file, new UTF8Encoding(false)); writer.Write(value);
    }
    private static Task AddedFilesAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var data = New(run, "v10-archive-added-files");
        var known = Path.Combine(data.Work, "nested", "owned.bin");
        var added = Path.Combine(data.Work, "nested", "用户放入的文件.txt");
        var outside = Path.Combine(data.Directory, "转换结果.png");
        using (var journal = ArchiveTemporaryJournal.Open(data.Journal, data.Work)) Write(journal, known, "task-owned bytes");
        File.WriteAllText(added, "user file"); File.WriteAllText(outside, "committed output");
        Program.Assert(!ArchiveTemporaryJournal.Cleanup(data.Journal), "A folder containing an unknown user file was reported fully removed.");
        Program.Assert(!File.Exists(known) && File.ReadAllText(added) == "user file" && File.ReadAllText(outside) == "committed output", "Archive cleanup deleted unknown or final files, or missed an owned temporary.");
        File.Delete(added);
        Program.Assert(ArchiveTemporaryJournal.Cleanup(data.Journal) && !Directory.Exists(data.Work), "Known empty staging directories were not removed on retry.");
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }
    private static Task MovesAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var data = New(run, "v10-archive-committed-moves");
        var content = Path.Combine(data.Work, "content");
        var final = Path.Combine(data.Directory, "已转换的文件夹");
        var temporary = Path.Combine(data.Work, "snapshot.bin");
        var moved = Path.Combine(data.Directory, "用户移动的文件.bin");
        using (var journal = ArchiveTemporaryJournal.Open(data.Journal, data.Work))
        {
            Write(journal, Path.Combine(content, "nested", "result.txt"), "committed result");
            Write(journal, temporary, "original temporary");
        }
        Directory.Move(content, final); File.Move(temporary, moved); File.WriteAllText(temporary, "user replacement");
        Program.Assert(!ArchiveTemporaryJournal.Cleanup(data.Journal), "Replacement identity was claimed as an owned file.");
        Program.Assert(File.ReadAllText(temporary) == "user replacement" && File.ReadAllText(moved) == "original temporary"
            && File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(final, "nested", "result.txt")) == "committed result", "Cleanup chased a moved receipt, removed a replacement, or deleted committed output.");
        File.Delete(temporary);
        Program.Assert(ArchiveTemporaryJournal.Cleanup(data.Journal) && !Directory.Exists(data.Work), "Empty work root was not removed after retaining a replacement.");
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }
    private static Task PartialAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var data = New(run, "v10-archive-partial-journal");
        using (var journal = ArchiveTemporaryJournal.Open(data.Journal, data.Work))
            for (var i = 0; i < 200; i++) Write(journal, Path.Combine(data.Work, "files", i.ToString("D4") + ".bin"), "x");
        Program.Assert(File.ReadLines(data.Journal).Count() == 203, "Receipts are not one append per created artifact.");
        File.AppendAllText(data.Journal, "{\"Path\":\"unpublished");
        // A killed writer may leave a partial final line. The reader cleans only
        // preceding complete records and retains its receipt until the next retry.
        Program.Assert(!ArchiveTemporaryJournal.Cleanup(data.Journal) && !Directory.Exists(data.Work), "Partial receipt handling guessed ownership or skipped verified earlier records.");
        Program.Assert(ArchiveTemporaryJournal.Cleanup(data.Journal), "An already removed task root did not allow a partial receipt to retire safely.");
        var malformed = New(run, "v10-archive-malformed-journal");
        using (var journal = ArchiveTemporaryJournal.Open(malformed.Journal, malformed.Work)) Write(journal, Path.Combine(malformed.Work, "known.bin"), "known");
        File.AppendAllText(malformed.Journal, "{invalid}\n{\"Path\":\".\"}\n");
        Program.Assert(!ArchiveTemporaryJournal.Cleanup(malformed.Journal) && File.Exists(Path.Combine(malformed.Work, "known.bin")), "Malformed non-final data did not fail closed.");
        run.Evidence["v10ArchiveJournal"] = new { exactFileIdentities = true, unknownFilesPreserved = true, renamedResultsPreserved = true, appendOnly = true, partialWriteRetry = true };
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }

    private static Task RegisteredAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "v10-archive-registered-receipt"); Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        OwnedArtifacts.Configure(directory);
        try
        {
            var work = Path.Combine(directory, ".cat-archive-" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
            var receipt = OwnedArtifacts.CreateArchiveJournal(work);
            using (var journal = ArchiveTemporaryJournal.Open(receipt, work)) Write(journal, Path.Combine(work, "owned.bin"), "owned");
            Program.Assert(OwnedArtifacts.Cleanup(directory).Count == 0 && !Directory.Exists(work) && !File.Exists(receipt), "The verified journal handle prevented cleanup, or the receipt was left behind.");
        }
        finally { OwnedArtifacts.Configure(run.Directory); }
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }
}

--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/CompressionAcceptance.cs ---
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Text;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Controls.Primitives;
using System.Windows.Data;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

/// <summary>Real media and independent byte/container checks for the V0.3 compression contract.</summary>
internal static class CompressionAcceptance
{
    public static async Task RunAsync(AcceptanceRun run, IConversionEngine engine, IReadOnlyList<FormatPreset> presets)
    {
        var failuresBefore = run.Failures;
        var fixtures = new Fixtures(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "压缩素材 中文 空格"));
        var fixturesReady = false;
        await run.CaseAsync("compression-fixture-generation", async () =>
        {
            await CreateFixturesAsync(run, fixtures);
            fixturesReady = true;
        });
        if (!fixturesReady) return;

        foreach (var (source, mode) in new[] { (fixtures.Video, ConversionMode.Video), (fixtures.Audio, ConversionMode.Audio), (fixtures.Jpeg, ConversionMode.Image) })
        foreach (var level in Enum.GetValues<CompressionLevel>())
        {
            var name = $"compression-standard-{mode.ToString().ToLowerInvariant()}-{level.ToString().ToLowerInvariant()}";
            await run.CaseAsync(name, async () =>
            {
                var options = new CompressionOptions(CompressionMode.Standard, level);
                var expectedRatio = ExpectedRatio(level);
                Program.Assert(options.Ratio == expectedRatio, "Standard level ratio differs from the user-facing 80/50/30/15 percent contract.");
                var cap = (long)Math.Floor(new FileInfo(source).Length * expectedRatio);
                var result = await CompressAsync(run, engine, source, mode, options, name);
                await CheckOutputAsync(run, engine, source, result, cap, preserveExtension: true);
                run.Evidence[name] = new { result.OutputPath, result.Statistics, cap, originalSha256 = Program.Hash(source) };
            });
        }

        foreach (var (source, mode) in new[] { (fixtures.Video, ConversionMode.Video), (fixtures.Audio, ConversionMode.Audio), (fixtures.Png, ConversionMode.Image), (fixtures.Wave, ConversionMode.Audio) })
        {
            var name = $"compression-custom-{Path.GetExtension(source).TrimStart('.')}";
            await run.CaseAsync(name, async () =>
            {
                var cap = (long)(new FileInfo(source).Length * .45);
                var result = await CompressAsync(run, engine, source, mode, new(CompressionMode.TargetSize, MaximumBytes: cap), name);
                await CheckOutputAsync(run, engine, source, result, cap, preserveExtension: true);
                if (source == fixtures.Png) AssertTransparentPng(result.OutputPath!);
                run.Evidence[name] = new { result.OutputPath, result.Statistics, cap };
            });
        }

        foreach (var (source, mode) in fixtures.AdditionalCompressionSources)
        {
            var name = $"compression-preserved-format-{Path.GetExtension(source).TrimStart('.')}";
            await run.CaseAsync(name, async () =>
            {
                var cap = (long)(new FileInfo(source).Length * .5);
                var result = await CompressAsync(run, engine, source, mode, new(CompressionMode.TargetSize, MaximumBytes: cap), name);
                await CheckOutputAsync(run, engine, source, result, cap, preserveExtension: true);
                if (source == fixtures.StaticWebp) await DecodeImageAlphaAsync(run, result.OutputPath!, name);
                if (source == fixtures.M4v) Program.Assert((await engine.ProbeAsync(result.OutputPath!)).FormatName.Contains("mov", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase), "M4V was written as a bare elementary stream instead of its source MP4 container.");
                if (source == fixtures.MultiStreamMkv)
                {
                    var info = await engine.ProbeAsync(result.OutputPath!);
                    Program.Assert(info.AudioStreams?.Count == 2 && info.SubtitleStreams?.Count == 1, "MKV compression dropped a second audio track or subtitles.");
                    Program.Assert(info.AudioStreams![0].Language == "eng" && info.AudioStreams[1].Language == "jpn", "MKV audio track languages were lost or reordered.");
                    var text = await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", result.OutputPath!, "-map", "0:s:0", "-c:s", "srt", "-f", "srt", "-"]);
                    Program.Assert(text.Contains("完整保留字幕示例", StringComparison.Ordinal), "MKV subtitle text changed or disappeared.");
                }
                run.Evidence[name] = new { result.OutputPath, result.Statistics, cap };
            });
        }

        foreach (var source in new[] { fixtures.IndexedPng, fixtures.SixteenBitPng, fixtures.BareLosslessWebp })
        {
            var name = "compression-alpha-variant-" + Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(source);
            await run.CaseAsync(name, async () =>
            {
                var original = await engine.ProbeAsync(source);
                Program.Assert(original.HasAlpha, "Transparency probe missed indexed, 16-bit or bare lossless WebP alpha.");
                var candidates = CompressionCapabilities.Candidates(ConversionMode.Image, original, presets);
                Program.Assert(!candidates.Any(preset => preset.Extension is "jpg" or "jpeg"), "A transparent variant offered JPG and could silently lose alpha.");
                Program.Assert(!candidates.Any(preset => preset.Extension == "gif"), "A transparent non-GIF variant offered GIF and could silently quantize partial alpha.");
                var cap = new FileInfo(source).Length / 2;
                var result = await CompressAsync(run, engine, source, ConversionMode.Image, new(CompressionMode.TargetSize, MaximumBytes: cap), name);
                await CheckOutputAsync(run, engine, source, result, cap, preserveExtension: true);
                await DecodeImageAlphaAsync(run, result.OutputPath!, name);
                run.Evidence[name] = new { source, original.HasAlpha, result.OutputPath, result.Statistics, cap };
            });
        }

        foreach (var source in new[] { fixtures.Gif, fixtures.AnimatedWebp })
        foreach (var options in new[] { new CompressionOptions(CompressionMode.TargetSize, MaximumBytes: new FileInfo(source).Length / 2), new(CompressionMode.Standard, CompressionLevel.Medium), new(CompressionMode.Standard, CompressionLevel.Tiny) })
        {
            var name = $"compression-animation-{Path.GetExtension(source).TrimStart('.')}-{(options.Mode == CompressionMode.TargetSize ? "custom" : options.Level.ToString().ToLowerInvariant())}";
            await run.CaseAsync(name, async () =>
            {
                var original = ReadAnimation(source);
                Program.Assert(original.FrameCount == 8 && original.HasAlpha && original.LoopCount == 3, "Animation fixture did not retain its intended frame count, transparency and loop count.");
                var cap = options.Mode == CompressionMode.TargetSize ? options.MaximumBytes!.Value : (long)Math.Floor(new FileInfo(source).Length * ExpectedRatio(options.Level));
                if (options.Mode == CompressionMode.Standard) Program.Assert(options.Ratio == ExpectedRatio(options.Level), "Animation compression level ratio differs from its advertised percentage.");
                var result = await CompressAsync(run, engine, source, ConversionMode.Image, options, name);
                await CheckOutputAsync(run, engine, source, result, cap, preserveExtension: true);
                var actual = ReadAnimation(result.OutputPath!);
                Program.Assert(actual.FrameCount == original.FrameCount && actual.DurationMilliseconds == original.DurationMilliseconds && actual.LoopCount == original.LoopCount && actual.FrameDurationsMilliseconds.SequenceEqual(original.FrameDurationsMilliseconds),
                    $"Animation content changed: input={original}, output={actual}.");
                Program.Assert(actual.HasAlpha, "Animation output lost transparency.");
                if (source == fixtures.AnimatedWebp) await DecodeWebpFramesAsync(run, result.OutputPath!, name);
                else await DecodeImageAlphaAsync(run, result.OutputPath!, name);
                run.Evidence[name] = new { result.OutputPath, result.Statistics, original, actual,
                    method = "Independent GIF block / WebP RIFF ANMF parsing, full media decode, and actual decoded first-frame RGBA transparency." };
            });
        }

        await run.CaseAsync("compression-rotated-video-display-aspect", async () =>
        {
            var cap = new FileInfo(fixtures.RotatedVideo).Length / 2;
            var result = await CompressAsync(run, engine, fixtures.RotatedVideo, ConversionMode.Video, new(CompressionMode.TargetSize, MaximumBytes: cap), "rotation");
            await CheckOutputAsync(run, engine, fixtures.RotatedVideo, result, cap, preserveExtension: true);
            var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "compression-rotation-decode"); Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
            var originalFrame = Path.Combine(directory, "source.png"); var outputFrame = Path.Combine(directory, "output.png");
            await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", fixtures.RotatedVideo, "-frames:v", "1", originalFrame]);
            await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", result.OutputPath!, "-frames:v", "1", outputFrame]);
            static (int Width, int Height) Dimensions(string path) { using var stream = File.OpenRead(path); var image = BitmapFrame.Create(stream, BitmapCreateOptions.None, BitmapCacheOption.OnLoad); return (image.PixelWidth, image.PixelHeight); }
            var input = Dimensions(originalFrame); var output = Dimensions(outputFrame);
            Program.Assert(input.Height > input.Width, "Rotation fixture did not display as a portrait video.");
            Program.Assert(Math.Abs((double)input.Width / input.Height - (double)output.Width / output.Height) < .02, "Compression stretched or double-rotated the phone-style video.");
            run.Evidence["compression-rotation-display-aspect"] = new { input, output, originalFrame, outputFrame, method = "First frames decoded with FFmpeg autorotation; compares displayed dimensions rather than raw stream dimensions." };
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("compression-below-cap-copy-and-output-collision", async () =>
        {
            var cap = new FileInfo(fixtures.Png).Length + 1;
            var first = await CompressAsync(run, engine, fixtures.Png, ConversionMode.Image, new(CompressionMode.TargetSize, MaximumBytes: cap), "already-small");
            await CheckOutputAsync(run, engine, fixtures.Png, first, cap, preserveExtension: true);
            Program.Assert(first.Statistics?.AlreadyWithinLimit == true && Program.Hash(first.OutputPath!) == Program.Hash(fixtures.Png), "Below-cap file was needlessly re-encoded instead of copied intact.");
            var firstHash = Program.Hash(first.OutputPath!);
            var second = await CompressAsync(run, engine, fixtures.Png, ConversionMode.Image, new(CompressionMode.TargetSize, MaximumBytes: cap), "already-small");
            Program.Assert(second.Status == JobStatus.Completed && first.OutputPath != second.OutputPath && Program.Hash(first.OutputPath!) == firstHash, "Compression replaced an existing successful result.");
            Program.Assert(Directory.GetFiles(Path.GetDirectoryName(first.OutputPath!)!).Length == 2, "Below-cap copying left a temporary file.");
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("compression-impossible-cap-and-invalid-limit-no-output", async () =>
        {
            foreach (var cap in new long[] { 1, 0, -1 })
            {
                var name = "impossible-" + cap;
                var result = await CompressAsync(run, engine, fixtures.Video, ConversionMode.Video, new(CompressionMode.TargetSize, MaximumBytes: cap), name);
                Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Failed && result.Error is not null && result.OutputPath is null, "An impossible/invalid cap did not return a structured failure.");
                AssertNoFiles(Output(run, name));
            }
            run.AssertSourcesUnchanged();
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("compression-below-cap-corrupt-image-no-output", async () =>
        {
            var path = Path.Combine(fixtures.Directory, "损坏但有图片头.png");
            var bytes = File.ReadAllBytes(fixtures.Png);
            // Keep IHDR and the IDAT framing, but corrupt compressed pixel bytes. A size-only
            // pass must never mark this as a successful original-format copy.
            for (var index = 8; index + 12 <= bytes.Length;)
            {
                var length = (int)((uint)bytes[index] << 24 | (uint)bytes[index + 1] << 16 | (uint)bytes[index + 2] << 8 | bytes[index + 3]);
                if (Encoding.ASCII.GetString(bytes, index + 4, 4) == "IDAT") { Array.Clear(bytes, index + 8, Math.Min(64, length)); break; }
                index += length + 12;
            }
            File.WriteAllBytes(path, bytes); run.TrackSource(path);
            var png = presets.First(preset => preset.Mode == ConversionMode.Image && preset.Extension == "png");
            var output = Output(run, "below-cap-corrupt");
            var result = await engine.ConvertAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), path, png, output, new(CompressionMode.TargetSize, MaximumBytes: bytes.Length * 2L)));
            Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Failed && result.OutputPath is null && result.Error is not null, "A corrupt below-cap image was committed as a successful copy.");
            AssertNoFiles(output); run.AssertSourcesUnchanged();
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("compression-explicit-alternative-and-alpha-compatibility", async () =>
        {
            var mp3 = presets.First(preset => preset.Mode == ConversionMode.Audio && preset.Extension == "mp3");
            var cap = new FileInfo(fixtures.Wave).Length / 8;
            var result = await engine.ConvertAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), fixtures.Wave, mp3, Output(run, "alternative"),
                new(CompressionMode.TargetSize, MaximumBytes: cap, PreserveSourceFormat: false)));
            await CheckOutputAsync(run, engine, fixtures.Wave, result, cap, preserveExtension: false);
            Program.Assert(Path.GetExtension(result.OutputPath!) == ".mp3", "Explicit alternative format was ignored.");
            var alpha = await engine.ProbeAsync(fixtures.Png);
            var jpg = presets.First(preset => preset.Mode == ConversionMode.Image && preset.Extension == "jpg");
            Program.Assert(alpha.HasAlpha && !CompressionCapabilities.IsCompatible(jpg, alpha), "Transparent image offered a format that discards alpha.");
            Program.Assert(!CompressionCapabilities.Candidates(ConversionMode.Image, await engine.ProbeAsync(fixtures.Gif), presets).Any(preset => preset.Extension == "jpg"), "Animated input offered a static-only format.");
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("compression-serial-queue-continues-after-failure", async () =>
        {
            var input = await engine.ProbeAsync(fixtures.Audio);
            var preset = CompressionCapabilities.ResolveSourcePreset(fixtures.Audio, ConversionMode.Audio, input) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("MP3 source compression unavailable.");
            var output = Output(run, "serial");
            var requests = new[] {
                new ConversionRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), fixtures.Audio, preset, output, new(CompressionMode.TargetSize, MaximumBytes: new FileInfo(fixtures.Audio).Length / 2)),
                new ConversionRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), fixtures.Audio, preset, output, new(CompressionMode.TargetSize, MaximumBytes: 1)),
                new ConversionRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), fixtures.Audio, preset, output, new(CompressionMode.TargetSize, MaximumBytes: new FileInfo(fixtures.Audio).Length / 3))
            };
            var started = new List<Guid>();
            var batch = await new SerialConversionQueue(engine).RunAsync(requests, onStarted: request => started.Add(request.Id));
            Program.Assert(started.SequenceEqual(requests.Select(request => request.Id)) && batch.CompletedCount == 2 && batch.FailedCount == 1, "Compression queue stopped or reordered jobs after failure.");
            Program.Assert(Directory.GetFiles(output).Length == 2, "Compression batch left failed temporary output.");
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("compression-cancellation-cleans-encoder-and-owned-files", async () =>
        {
            var output = Output(run, "cancel");
            Directory.CreateDirectory(output);
            var unrelated = Path.Combine(output, ".cat-conversion-other-task.mp4");
            File.WriteAllText(unrelated, "Other task output must survive cancellation.");
            var hash = Program.Hash(unrelated);
            var before = ActiveEncoderIds();
            var media = await engine.ProbeAsync(fixtures.LongVideo);
            var preset = CompressionCapabilities.ResolveSourcePreset(fixtures.LongVideo, ConversionMode.Video, media)!;
            using var cancellation = new CancellationTokenSource();
            var conversion = engine.ConvertAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), fixtures.LongVideo, preset, output, new(CompressionMode.Standard, CompressionLevel.Tiny)), null, cancellation.Token);
            await WaitForAsync(() => ActiveEncoderIds().Except(before).Any(), TimeSpan.FromSeconds(15), "Compression encoder did not start.");
            cancellation.Cancel();
            var result = await conversion.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(20));
            Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Cancelled && result.OutputPath is null, "Cancelled compression committed an output.");
            Program.Assert(File.Exists(unrelated) && Program.Hash(unrelated) == hash && Directory.GetFiles(output).Length == 1, "Compression cancellation removed an unrelated result or left temporary files.");
            await WaitForAsync(() => !ActiveEncoderIds().Except(before).Any(), TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5), "Cancelled compression encoder remained alive.");
        });

        if (run.NoGui) run.RecordSkipped("compression-wpf-model-and-layout", "--no-gui skips the real compression window.");
        else await run.CaseAsync("compression-wpf-model-and-layout", () => RunModelAndWindowAsync(run, engine, presets, fixtures));
        run.CompressionAcceptancePassed = run.Failures == failuresBefore && !run.NoGui;
    }

    private static async Task<ConversionResult> CompressAsync(AcceptanceRun run, IConversionEngine engine, string source, ConversionMode mode, CompressionOptions options, string name)
    {
        var media = await engine.ProbeAsync(source);
        var preset = CompressionCapabilities.ResolveSourcePreset(source, mode, media) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException($"Source format unavailable: {source}");
        return await engine.ConvertAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), source, preset, Output(run, name), options));
    }

    private static async Task CheckOutputAsync(AcceptanceRun run, IConversionEngine engine, string source, ConversionResult result, long cap, bool preserveExtension)
    {
        Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Completed && result.OutputPath is not null && File.Exists(result.OutputPath),
            $"Compression failed: {result.Status}, {result.Error?.Code}, {result.Error?.Message}, {result.Error?.TechnicalDetails}");
        var bytes = new FileInfo(result.OutputPath!).Length;
        Program.Assert(bytes > 0 && bytes <= cap, $"Output exceeds its cap: {bytes} > {cap}.");
        Program.Assert(result.Statistics is not null && result.Statistics.SourceBytes == new FileInfo(source).Length && result.Statistics.OutputBytes == bytes && result.Statistics.TargetBytes == cap,
            "Compression statistics do not match actual file sizes and the requested target.");
        Program.Assert(!Path.GetFullPath(result.OutputPath!).Equals(Path.GetFullPath(source), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase), "Compression overwrote the source.");
        if (preserveExtension) Program.Assert(Path.GetExtension(result.OutputPath!).Equals(Path.GetExtension(source), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase), "Source format was changed without an explicit alternative.");
        var original = await engine.ProbeAsync(source);
        var actual = await engine.ProbeAsync(result.OutputPath!);
        Program.Assert(original.HasVideo == actual.HasVideo && original.HasAudio == actual.HasAudio && (original.AudioStreams?.Count ?? 0) == (actual.AudioStreams?.Count ?? 0) && (original.SubtitleStreams?.Count ?? 0) == (actual.SubtitleStreams?.Count ?? 0),
            "Compression lost a required media stream.");
        if (original.Animation is null && original.DurationSeconds > 0)
        {
            var tolerance = Math.Max(.15, original.FrameRate > 0 ? 1 / original.FrameRate : .15);
            Program.Assert(Math.Abs(actual.DurationSeconds - original.DurationSeconds) <= tolerance,
                $"Compression trimmed or extended media: {original.DurationSeconds:0.###}s to {actual.DurationSeconds:0.###}s.");
        }
        if (Path.GetExtension(result.OutputPath!) != ".webp")
            await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-v", "error", "-i", result.OutputPath!, "-map", "0:v?", "-map", "0:a?", "-f", "null", "-"]);
        Program.Assert(Directory.GetFiles(Path.GetDirectoryName(result.OutputPath!)!).All(path => !Path.GetFileName(path).StartsWith(".cat-", StringComparison.Ordinal)), "Successful compression left temporary output.");
        run.AssertSourcesUnchanged();
    }

    private static async Task CreateFixturesAsync(AcceptanceRun run, Fixtures fixture)
    {
        Directory.CreateDirectory(fixture.Directory);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-f", "lavfi", "-i", "testsrc2=size=640x360:rate=24", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "sine=frequency=440:sample_rate=44100", "-t", "2.4", "-c:v", "libopenh264", "-b:v", "1800k", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "128k", fixture.Video]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-f", "lavfi", "-i", "sine=frequency=440:sample_rate=44100", "-t", "3", "-ac", "2", "-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-b:a", "320k", fixture.Audio]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-f", "lavfi", "-i", "sine=frequency=720:sample_rate=48000", "-t", "2.4", "-ac", "2", "-c:a", "pcm_s16le", fixture.Wave]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", fixture.Wave, "-c:a", "libvorbis", "-q:a", "5", fixture.UnsupportedAudio]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-f", "lavfi", "-i", "testsrc2=size=960x540:rate=30", "-t", "20", "-c:v", "libopenh264", "-b:v", "3M", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", fixture.LongVideo]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", fixture.Video, "-c", "copy", "-f", "mov", fixture.Mov]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", fixture.Video, "-c", "copy", "-f", "mp4", fixture.M4v]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-display_rotation:v:0", "90", "-i", fixture.Video, "-map", "0", "-c", "copy", fixture.RotatedVideo]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", fixture.Video, "-c:v", "libvpx-vp9", "-b:v", "1600k", "-c:a", "libopus", "-b:a", "128k", fixture.Webm]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", fixture.Video, "-c:v", "mpeg4", "-b:v", "1800k", "-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-b:a", "128k", fixture.Avi]);
        File.WriteAllText(fixture.Subtitle, "1\n00:00:00,200 --> 00:00:02,200\n完整保留字幕示例\n", new UTF8Encoding(false));
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", fixture.Video, "-i", fixture.Wave, "-i", fixture.Subtitle, "-map", "0:v:0", "-map", "0:a:0", "-map", "1:a:0", "-map", "2:s:0", "-c:v", "copy", "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k", "-c:s", "srt", "-metadata:s:a:0", "language=eng", "-metadata:s:a:1", "language=jpn", "-t", "2.4", fixture.MultiStreamMkv]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", fixture.Wave, "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "256k", "-f", "adts", fixture.Aac]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", fixture.Wave, "-c:a", "flac", fixture.Flac]);
        CreateImage(fixture.Png);
        CreateIndexedImage(fixture.IndexedPng);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", fixture.Png, "-vf", "format=rgba64be", "-pix_fmt", "rgba64be", "-frames:v", "1", fixture.SixteenBitPng]);
        var sixteenBitHeader = File.ReadAllBytes(fixture.SixteenBitPng);
        Program.Assert(sixteenBitHeader[24] == 16 && sixteenBitHeader[25] == 6, "16-bit PNG fixture does not contain 16-bit RGBA pixels.");
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", fixture.Png, "-vf", "format=rgb24", "-frames:v", "1", "-q:v", "2", fixture.Jpeg]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", fixture.Jpeg, "-vf", "format=rgb24", "-frames:v", "1", fixture.Bmp]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", fixture.Png, "-frames:v", "1", "-c:v", "libwebp", "-lossless", "1", fixture.StaticWebp]);
        CreateBareLosslessWebp(fixture.StaticWebp, fixture.BareLosslessWebp);
        CreateGif(fixture.Gif);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", fixture.Gif, "-c:v", "libwebp_anim", "-lossless", "1", "-loop", "3", "-fps_mode", "passthrough", fixture.AnimatedWebp]);
        foreach (var path in fixture.Paths) run.TrackSource(path);
        AssertTransparentPng(fixture.Png);
    }

    private static async Task RunModelAndWindowAsync(AcceptanceRun run, IConversionEngine engine, IReadOnlyList<FormatPreset> presets, Fixtures fixture)
    {
        var settings = new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "compression-ui-settings"));
        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(settings.FilePath)!);
        // Load an actual V0.2-shaped settings file to verify additive migration defaults.
        File.WriteAllText(settings.FilePath, "{\"PresetIds\":{\"Video\":\"mp4-h264\"},\"MotionPreference\":\"Reduced\"}");
        var model = new MainViewModel(engine, presets, settings, new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "compression-ui-logs")))
        { OutputDirectory = Output(run, "gui"), MotionPreference = MotionPreference.Reduced };
        var window = new MainWindow(model, () => true) { ShowActivated = false };
        run.StopActiveWindow = async () => { model.Cancel(); model.CancelImport(); await Task.WhenAll(model.ActiveRun, model.ActiveImport).WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(20)); };
        try
        {
            window.Show(); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
            await model.AddFilesAsync([fixture.Video]);
            var conversion = model.Queue.Single();
            Program.Assert(!conversion.IsCompression && conversion.Status == JobStatus.Pending, "Conversion import created a compression task.");
            model.SetCompressionPage(); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(model.IsCompressionPage && model.VisibleQueue.Count == 0 && model.Queue.Count == 1, "Compression page leaked conversion rows or lost the underlying queue.");
            Program.Assert(model.SelectedCompressionMode == CompressionMode.Standard && model.SelectedCompressionLevel == CompressionLevel.Medium, "V0.2 settings did not migrate to the standard/medium defaults.");
            var start = Control<Button>(window, "StartButton");
            Program.Assert(ReferenceEquals(start.Command, model.StartCommand) && !start.IsEnabled, "Empty compression page Start button binding is incorrect.");
            Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, "compression-standard-empty-normal");
            foreach (var (width, height) in new[] { (620d, 480d), (960d, 540d) })
            {
                window.Width = width; window.Height = height; await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
                Program.AssertControlInside(window, "StartButton"); Program.AssertControlInside(window, "AddFilesSide");
                Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, $"compression-standard-empty-{width:0}x{height:0}");
            }
            window.Width = 1200; window.Height = 820; await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
            foreach (var (name, level) in new[] { ("CompressionLarge", CompressionLevel.Large), ("CompressionMedium", CompressionLevel.Medium), ("CompressionSmall", CompressionLevel.Small), ("CompressionTiny", CompressionLevel.Tiny) })
            {
                ClickRadio(window, name); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
                Program.Assert(model.SelectedCompressionLevel == level && Control<RadioButton>(window, name).IsChecked == true, "Compression level control did not update the model and checked binding.");
            }
            ClickRadio(window, "CompressionMedium");
            await model.AddFilesAsync([fixture.Video, fixture.Audio, fixture.Jpeg, fixture.Gif, fixture.UnsupportedAudio]); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(model.VisibleQueue.Count == 5 && model.VisibleQueue.All(row => row.IsCompression) && model.Queue.Count == 6,
                "Mixed compression import did not create isolated image, video and audio jobs.");
            Program.Assert(model.VisibleQueue.Any(row => row.SourcePath == fixture.Video) && model.VisibleQueue.Any(row => row.SourcePath == fixture.Gif), "Mixed import lost a supported file or animation.");
            var unresolved = model.VisibleQueue.Single(row => row.SourcePath == fixture.UnsupportedAudio);
            Program.Assert(!unresolved.IsConfigured && !start.IsEnabled && !model.CanStart, "Batch started despite an unsupported original format lacking an alternative.");
            Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, "compression-mixed-queue-awaiting-format");
            var grid = Control<DataGrid>(window, "QueueGrid");
            grid.ScrollIntoView(unresolved); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
            var alternativePicker = Descendants<ComboBox>(grid).FirstOrDefault(picker => picker.Name == "CompressionFormatPicker" && ReferenceEquals(picker.DataContext, unresolved))
                ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing compression row format picker.");
            alternativePicker.SelectedItem = unresolved.CompressionPresets.First(preset => preset.Extension == "mp3");
            BindingOperations.GetBindingExpression(alternativePicker, System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.Selector.SelectedItemProperty)?.UpdateSource(); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(unresolved.IsConfigured && unresolved.SelectedCompressionPreset?.Extension == "mp3" && start.IsEnabled && model.CanStart, "Alternative format picker binding failed to configure the row and enable Start.");
            model.SelectedJobs = Array.Empty<ConversionJobViewModel>();
            ClickRadio(window, "TargetSizeCompressionButton"); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(model.SelectedCompressionMode == CompressionMode.TargetSize && Control<TextBox>(window, "MaximumSizeBox").IsVisible, "Specified-size mode did not expose the MB input.");
            var lastValidSavedSize = settings.Load().CompressionMaximumMb;
            foreach (var text in new[] { "", "abc", "0", "-1", "99999999999999999999999999999999", "0.0000001" })
            {
                SetSizeText(window, text); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
                Program.Assert(!model.CanStart && !start.IsEnabled && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(model.CompressionInputError), "Invalid MB input did not disable Start and explain the error.");
                Program.Assert(settings.Load().CompressionMaximumMb == lastValidSavedSize, "Invalid or overflowing MB input replaced the last valid saved size.");
            }
            foreach (var (text, bytes) in new[] { ("0.5", 500000L), ("10", 10000000L) })
            {
                SetSizeText(window, text); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
                Program.Assert(model.VisibleQueue.All(row => row.Compression?.MaximumBytes == bytes), "MB input used a binary-megabyte multiplier instead of 1 MB = 1,000,000 bytes.");
            }
            SetSizeText(window, "0.25"); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
