            Program.Assert(model.CanStart && start.IsEnabled && model.VisibleQueue.All(row => row.Compression?.MaximumBytes == 250000), "Decimal MB did not produce the decimal-byte target in all editable rows.");
            Program.Assert(unresolved.SelectedCompressionPreset?.Extension == "mp3", "Changing the target reset a user-selected alternative format.");
            Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, "compression-target-size-mixed-queue-normal");
            foreach (var (width, height) in new[] { (620d, 480d), (960d, 540d) })
            {
                window.Width = width; window.Height = height; await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
                Program.AssertControlInside(window, "StartButton"); Program.AssertControlInside(window, "MaximumSizeBox");
                Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, $"compression-target-size-{width:0}x{height:0}");
            }
            window.Width = 1200; window.Height = 820; await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
            var reopened = new MainViewModel(engine, presets, settings, new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "compression-ui-reopened-logs")));
            reopened.SetCompressionPage();
            Program.Assert(reopened.SelectedCompressionMode == CompressionMode.TargetSize && reopened.SelectedCompressionLevel == CompressionLevel.Medium && reopened.MaximumSizeMb == "0.25", "Compression mode, level or decimal MB setting did not persist.");
            var processing = model.RunQueueAsync();
            Program.Assert(model.IsProcessing && !model.CanEdit, "Compression did not enter its busy state.");
            var compressionRows = model.VisibleQueue.ToArray();
            var targets = compressionRows.Select(row => row.Compression).ToArray();
            var outputDirectory = model.OutputDirectory;
            model.SetMode(ConversionMode.Audio);
            model.OutputDirectory = outputDirectory + "-blocked";
            model.SelectedCompressionLevel = CompressionLevel.Tiny;
            Program.Assert(model.CanNavigate && model.IsConversionPage && model.Mode == ConversionMode.Audio &&
                !model.CanEdit && model.OutputDirectory == outputDirectory && model.SelectedCompressionLevel == CompressionLevel.Medium &&
                compressionRows.Select(row => row.Compression).SequenceEqual(targets) &&
                model.CurrentProcessingTask?.Action == FileProcessingAction.CompressMedia,
                "Busy browsing changed the active compression request or unlocked processing parameters.");
            model.SetCompressionPage();
            Program.Assert(model.VisibleQueue.SequenceEqual(compressionRows), "Returning while busy lost the compression source queue.");
            await processing.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(120)); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(window.IsVisible && model.VisibleQueue.All(row => row.Status == JobStatus.Completed && row.OutputBytes > 0 && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(row.ResultSizeText)), "Compression completion lost results, size feedback or the window.");
            Program.Assert(conversion.Status == JobStatus.Pending, "Compression executed a hidden conversion job.");
            Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, "compression-completed-size-statistics");
            run.Evidence["compression-gui-mixed-results"] = model.VisibleQueue.Select(row => new { row.SourcePath, row.OutputPath, row.OutputBytes, row.ResultSizeText, row.TargetSummary }).ToArray();
            model.SetMode(ConversionMode.Video); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(model.VisibleQueue.Count == 1 && ReferenceEquals(model.VisibleQueue[0], conversion), "Returning to conversion did not restore its pending queue.");
            await model.RunQueueAsync().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(60));
            Program.Assert(conversion.Status == JobStatus.Completed && model.Queue.Count(row => row.IsCompression && row.Status == JobStatus.Completed) == 5, "Conversion run changed compression results.");
            model.SetCompressionPage();
            var retrySource = Path.Combine(fixture.Directory, "重试 视频.mp4"); File.Copy(fixture.Video, retrySource); run.TrackSource(retrySource);
            await model.AddFilesAsync([retrySource]);
            var retry = model.VisibleQueue.Single(row => row.SourcePath == retrySource);
            model.SelectedJobs = new[] { retry }; SetSizeText(window, "0.001"); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(start.IsEnabled && retry.Compression?.MaximumBytes == 1000, "A valid tiny MB target was rejected or not applied to the selected pending job.");
            await model.RunQueueAsync().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(120));
            Program.Assert(retry.Status == JobStatus.Failed && retry.OutputPath is null, "Unreachable selected target did not fail safely.");
            SetSizeText(window, "0.25"); model.RetryFailed(); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(retry.Status == JobStatus.Pending && retry.Error is null && retry.Progress == 0 && retry.Compression?.MaximumBytes == 250000 && (retry.OutputBytes ?? 0) == 0, "Retry failed to reset result state while retaining updated compression settings.");
            await model.RunQueueAsync().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(120));
            Program.Assert(retry.Status == JobStatus.Completed && new FileInfo(retry.OutputPath!).Length <= 250000, "Retry did not apply the revised target.");
            run.Evidence["compression-wpf-binding-and-isolation"] = new { decimalMb = "0.25", maximumBytes = 250000, migratedV02Settings = true, allFourLevelBindings = true, invalidInputDisablesStart = true, explicitAlternativePicker = true, conversionAndCompressionQueueIsolation = true, failureRetryWithUpdatedSettings = true };
        }
        finally
        {
            model.Cancel(); model.CancelImport();
            try { await Task.WhenAll(model.ActiveRun, model.ActiveImport).WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(20)); } finally { window.Close(); run.StopActiveWindow = null; MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System); }
        }
    }

    private static T Control<T>(MainWindow window, string name) where T : FrameworkElement
        => window.FindName(name) as T ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing compression UI control: " + name);
    private static void ClickRadio(MainWindow window, string name)
    {
        var radio = Control<RadioButton>(window, name); radio.RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(ButtonBase.ClickEvent, radio));
    }
    private static void SetSizeText(MainWindow window, string text)
    {
        var box = Control<TextBox>(window, "MaximumSizeBox"); box.Text = text; BindingOperations.GetBindingExpression(box, TextBox.TextProperty)?.UpdateSource();
    }
    private static async Task FlushLayoutAsync(MainWindow window)
    {
        await window.Dispatcher.InvokeAsync(() => { window.UpdateLayout(); }, DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle); await Task.Delay(40); window.UpdateLayout();
    }
    private static IEnumerable<T> Descendants<T>(DependencyObject parent) where T : DependencyObject
    {
        for (var index = 0; index < VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(parent); index++)
        {
            var child = VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(parent, index); if (child is T match) yield return match;
            foreach (var descendant in Descendants<T>(child)) yield return descendant;
        }
    }

    private static void CreateImage(string path)
    {
        const int width = 384, height = 256;
        var pixels = new byte[width * height * 4];
        var random = new Random(31415);
        for (var y = 0; y < height; y++) for (var x = 0; x < width; x++)
        {
            var index = (y * width + x) * 4;
            pixels[index] = (byte)random.Next(256); pixels[index + 1] = (byte)random.Next(256); pixels[index + 2] = (byte)random.Next(256);
            pixels[index + 3] = (byte)(x < 20 || y < 20 || x >= width - 20 || y >= height - 20 ? 0 : 255);
        }
        var bitmap = BitmapSource.Create(width, height, 96, 96, PixelFormats.Bgra32, null, pixels, width * 4);
        var encoder = new PngBitmapEncoder(); encoder.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(bitmap));
        using var stream = File.Create(path); encoder.Save(stream);
    }

    private static void CreateIndexedImage(string path)
    {
        const int width = 384, height = 256;
        var pixels = new byte[width * height]; var random = new Random(1729);
        for (var y = 0; y < height; y++) for (var x = 0; x < width; x++) pixels[y * width + x] = (byte)(x < 20 || y < 20 || x >= width - 20 || y >= height - 20 ? 0 : random.Next(1, 16));
        var colors = Enumerable.Range(0, 16).Select(index => index == 0 ? Color.FromArgb(0, 0, 0, 0) : Color.FromArgb(255, (byte)(index * 17), (byte)((index * 53) % 256), (byte)((index * 97) % 256))).ToArray();
        var bitmap = BitmapSource.Create(width, height, 96, 96, PixelFormats.Indexed8, new BitmapPalette(colors), pixels, width);
        var encoder = new PngBitmapEncoder(); encoder.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(bitmap));
        using (var stream = File.Create(path)) encoder.Save(stream);
        AssertTransparentPng(path);
        var data = File.ReadAllBytes(path);
        Program.Assert(data[25] == 3 && Encoding.ASCII.GetString(data).Contains("tRNS", StringComparison.Ordinal), "Indexed PNG fixture lacks an indexed palette and transparency chunk.");
    }

    private static void CreateBareLosslessWebp(string source, string output)
    {
        var data = File.ReadAllBytes(source);
        for (var position = 12; position + 8 <= data.Length;)
        {
            var type = Encoding.ASCII.GetString(data, position, 4); var length = checked((int)BitConverter.ToUInt32(data, position + 4));
            Program.Assert(position + 8L + length <= data.Length, "Truncated lossless WebP fixture.");
            if (type == "VP8L")
            {
                Program.Assert(length >= 5 && data[position + 8] == 0x2F && (data[position + 12] & 0x10) != 0, "Lossless WebP fixture has no actual alpha bit.");
                var chunkLength = 8 + length + (length & 1);
                using var stream = File.Create(output); using var writer = new BinaryWriter(stream);
                writer.Write(Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes("RIFF")); writer.Write((uint)(4 + chunkLength)); writer.Write(Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes("WEBP")); writer.Write(data.AsSpan(position, chunkLength));
                return;
            }
            position += 8 + length + (length & 1);
        }
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Lossless WebP fixture omitted VP8L.");
    }

    private static void AssertTransparentPng(string path)
    {
        using var stream = File.OpenRead(path);
        var bitmap = new FormatConvertedBitmap(BitmapFrame.Create(stream, BitmapCreateOptions.PreservePixelFormat, BitmapCacheOption.OnLoad), PixelFormats.Bgra32, null, 0);
        var pixels = new byte[bitmap.PixelWidth * bitmap.PixelHeight * 4]; bitmap.CopyPixels(pixels, bitmap.PixelWidth * 4, 0);
        var alphas = Enumerable.Range(0, pixels.Length / 4).Select(index => pixels[index * 4 + 3]).ToArray();
        Program.Assert(alphas.Contains((byte)0) && alphas.Contains((byte)255), "PNG does not retain both fully transparent and visible pixels.");
    }

    // A valid deliberately unoptimized GIF makes compression measurable and avoids test dependence
    // on a third-party encoder's animation metadata defaults. Clear codes keep LZW at five bits.
    private static void CreateGif(string path)
    {
        const int width = 240, height = 160;
        using var stream = File.Create(path); using var writer = new BinaryWriter(stream);
        writer.Write(Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes("GIF89a")); writer.Write((ushort)width); writer.Write((ushort)height); writer.Write((byte)0xF3); writer.Write((byte)0); writer.Write((byte)0);
        for (var index = 0; index < 16; index++) { writer.Write((byte)(index * 17)); writer.Write((byte)((index * 53) % 256)); writer.Write((byte)((index * 97) % 256)); }
        writer.Write(new byte[] { 0x21, 0xFF, 0x0B }); writer.Write(Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes("NETSCAPE2.0")); writer.Write(new byte[] { 3, 1, 3, 0, 0 });
        var delays = new ushort[] { 8, 13, 20, 7, 11, 16, 9, 18 };
        var random = new Random(2718);
        foreach (var delay in delays)
        {
            writer.Write(new byte[] { 0x21, 0xF9, 4, 5 }); writer.Write(delay); writer.Write((byte)0); writer.Write((byte)0);
            writer.Write((byte)0x2C); writer.Write((ushort)0); writer.Write((ushort)0); writer.Write((ushort)width); writer.Write((ushort)height); writer.Write((byte)0); writer.Write((byte)4);
            var data = new List<byte>(); uint buffer = 0; var bits = 0;
            void Code(int code) { buffer |= (uint)code << bits; bits += 5; while (bits >= 8) { data.Add((byte)buffer); buffer >>= 8; bits -= 8; } }
            for (var y = 0; y < height; y++) for (var x = 0; x < width; x++) { Code(16); Code(x < 10 || y < 10 || x >= width - 10 || y >= height - 10 ? 0 : random.Next(1, 16)); }
            Code(17); if (bits > 0) data.Add((byte)buffer);
            for (var offset = 0; offset < data.Count; offset += 255) { var length = Math.Min(255, data.Count - offset); writer.Write((byte)length); writer.Write(data.GetRange(offset, length).ToArray()); }
            writer.Write((byte)0);
        }
        writer.Write((byte)0x3B);
    }

    private static AnimationEvidence ReadAnimation(string path)
        => Path.GetExtension(path).Equals(".gif", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? ReadGif(File.ReadAllBytes(path)) : ReadWebp(File.ReadAllBytes(path));

    private static AnimationEvidence ReadGif(byte[] data)
    {
        Program.Assert(data.Length > 13 && Encoding.ASCII.GetString(data, 0, 6).StartsWith("GIF8"), "Invalid GIF signature.");
        var position = 13 + ((data[10] & 0x80) != 0 ? 3 * (1 << ((data[10] & 7) + 1)) : 0);
        var frames = 0; long duration = 0; var loops = 0; var alpha = false; var delays = new List<int>();
        byte[] Blocks()
        {
            var result = new List<byte>();
            while (position < data.Length) { var size = data[position++]; if (size == 0) return result.ToArray(); Program.Assert(position + size <= data.Length, "Truncated GIF block."); result.AddRange(data.AsSpan(position, size).ToArray()); position += size; }
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Unterminated GIF block.");
        }
        while (position < data.Length)
        {
            var marker = data[position++];
            if (marker == 0x3B) return new(frames, duration, loops, alpha, delays.ToArray());
            if (marker == 0x21)
            {
                var label = data[position++]; var extension = Blocks();
                if (label == 0xF9) { Program.Assert(extension.Length == 4, "Invalid GIF control block."); var delay = BitConverter.ToUInt16(extension, 1) * 10; duration += delay; delays.Add(delay); alpha |= (extension[0] & 1) != 0; }
                else if (label == 0xFF && extension.Length >= 14 && Encoding.ASCII.GetString(extension, 0, 11) == "NETSCAPE2.0") loops = BitConverter.ToUInt16(extension, 12);
            }
            else if (marker == 0x2C)
            {
                Program.Assert(position + 9 <= data.Length, "Truncated GIF frame."); var packed = data[position + 8]; position += 9;
                if ((packed & 0x80) != 0) position += 3 * (1 << ((packed & 7) + 1));
                position++; Blocks(); frames++;
            }
            else throw new InvalidOperationException("Unknown GIF block marker.");
        }
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing GIF trailer.");
    }

    private static AnimationEvidence ReadWebp(byte[] data)
    {
        Program.Assert(data.Length > 20 && Encoding.ASCII.GetString(data, 0, 4) == "RIFF" && Encoding.ASCII.GetString(data, 8, 4) == "WEBP", "Invalid WebP RIFF.");
        Program.Assert(BitConverter.ToUInt32(data, 4) + 8L == data.Length, "WebP RIFF size differs from actual bytes.");
        var frames = 0; long duration = 0; var loops = 0; var alpha = false; var delays = new List<int>();
        for (var position = 12; position + 8 <= data.Length;)
        {
            var type = Encoding.ASCII.GetString(data, position, 4); var size = checked((int)BitConverter.ToUInt32(data, position + 4)); var start = position + 8;
            Program.Assert(start + (long)size <= data.Length, "Truncated WebP chunk.");
            if (type == "VP8X") alpha |= (data[start] & 0x10) != 0;
            if (type == "ANIM") { Program.Assert(size >= 6, "Invalid WebP ANIM."); loops = BitConverter.ToUInt16(data, start + 4); }
            if (type == "ANMF") { Program.Assert(size >= 16, "Invalid WebP ANMF."); frames++; var delay = data[start + 12] | data[start + 13] << 8 | data[start + 14] << 16; duration += delay; delays.Add(delay); }
            position = start + size + (size & 1);
        }
        Program.Assert(frames > 1, "WebP output is not animated.");
        return new(frames, duration, loops, alpha, delays.ToArray());
    }

    private static async Task DecodeImageAlphaAsync(AcceptanceRun run, string source, string name)
    {
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "compression-decoded-alpha", name); Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var decoded = Path.Combine(directory, "first-frame.png");
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", source, "-frames:v", "1", "-vf", "format=rgba", decoded]);
        AssertTransparentPng(decoded);
        run.Evidence[name + "-decoded-alpha"] = new { decoded, fullyTransparentAndOpaquePixelsVerified = true };
    }

    private static async Task DecodeWebpFramesAsync(AcceptanceRun run, string source, string name)
    {
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "compression-decoded-alpha", name); Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var data = File.ReadAllBytes(source); var frameIndex = 0;
        for (var position = 12; position + 8 <= data.Length;)
        {
            var type = Encoding.ASCII.GetString(data, position, 4); var size = checked((int)BitConverter.ToUInt32(data, position + 4)); var start = position + 8;
            Program.Assert(start + (long)size <= data.Length, "Truncated animation frame while independently decoding.");
            if (type == "ANMF")
            {
                var path = Path.Combine(directory, $"frame-{frameIndex:000}.webp");
                using (var stream = File.Create(path)) using (var writer = new BinaryWriter(stream))
                {
                    // Give FFmpeg each standalone pixel payload; no product parsing or decoder is used.
                    writer.Write(Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes("RIFF")); writer.Write((uint)(4 + 18 + size - 16)); writer.Write(Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes("WEBP"));
                    writer.Write(Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes("VP8X")); writer.Write((uint)10); writer.Write(new byte[] { 0x10, 0, 0, 0 });
                    writer.Write(data.AsSpan(start + 6, 6)); writer.Write(data.AsSpan(start + 16, size - 16));
                }
                if (frameIndex == 0) await DecodeImageAlphaAsync(run, path, name + "-first-frame");
                else await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", path, "-f", "null", "-"]);
                frameIndex++;
            }
            position = start + size + (size & 1);
        }
        Program.Assert(frameIndex == ReadWebp(data).FrameCount, "Independent WebP decoder did not process all frames.");
        run.Evidence[name + "-decoded-frames"] = new { frameCount = frameIndex, method = "ANMF image subchunks reconstructed as standalone RIFF WebP and independently decoded by FFmpeg." };
    }

    private static async Task<string> RunFfmpegAsync(AcceptanceRun run, string[] arguments)
    {
        var start = new ProcessStartInfo(Path.Combine(run.EngineDirectory, "ffmpeg.exe")) { UseShellExecute = false, CreateNoWindow = true, RedirectStandardError = true, RedirectStandardOutput = true };
        foreach (var argument in new[] { "-hide_banner", "-nostdin", "-y", "-loglevel", "error" }.Concat(arguments)) start.ArgumentList.Add(argument);
        using var process = Process.Start(start) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Compression fixture process did not start.");
        var stderr = process.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync(); var stdout = process.StandardOutput.ReadToEndAsync();
        try { await process.WaitForExitAsync().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(90)); }
        catch { if (!process.HasExited) process.Kill(true); await process.WaitForExitAsync(); throw; }
        var output = await stdout; Program.Assert(process.ExitCode == 0, "Media fixture/decode failed: " + await stderr); return output;
    }

    private static string Output(AcceptanceRun run, string name) => Path.Combine(run.Directory, "compression-outputs", name);
    private static double ExpectedRatio(CompressionLevel level) => level switch
    { CompressionLevel.Large => .8, CompressionLevel.Medium => .5, CompressionLevel.Small => .3, CompressionLevel.Tiny => .15, _ => throw new InvalidOperationException("Unexpected compression level.") };
    private static void AssertNoFiles(string path) => Program.Assert(!Directory.Exists(path) || Directory.GetFiles(path, "*", SearchOption.AllDirectories).Length == 0, "Failed compression left final or temporary output.");
    private static HashSet<int> ActiveEncoderIds()
    {
        var ids = new HashSet<int>(); foreach (var process in Process.GetProcessesByName("ffmpeg")) { using (process) ids.Add(process.Id); } return ids;
    }
    private static async Task WaitForAsync(Func<bool> condition, TimeSpan timeout, string message)
    {
        var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew(); while (!condition()) { if (clock.Elapsed > timeout) throw new InvalidOperationException(message); await Task.Delay(30); }
    }

    private sealed record AnimationEvidence(int FrameCount, long DurationMilliseconds, int LoopCount, bool HasAlpha, IReadOnlyList<int> FrameDurationsMilliseconds);
    private sealed record Fixtures(string Directory)
    {
        public string Video => Path.Combine(Directory, "复杂 视频.mp4");
        public string LongVideo => Path.Combine(Directory, "取消 长视频.mp4");
        public string Audio => Path.Combine(Directory, "高码率 音频.mp3");
        public string Wave => Path.Combine(Directory, "无损 音频.wav");
        public string UnsupportedAudio => Path.Combine(Directory, "待选格式 音频.ogg");
        public string Mov => Path.Combine(Directory, "原格式 视频.mov");
        public string M4v => Path.Combine(Directory, "原格式 视频.m4v");
        public string RotatedVideo => Path.Combine(Directory, "手机 旋转视频.mp4");
        public string Webm => Path.Combine(Directory, "原格式 视频.webm");
        public string Avi => Path.Combine(Directory, "原格式 视频.avi");
        public string MultiStreamMkv => Path.Combine(Directory, "双音轨 字幕视频.mkv");
        public string Subtitle => Path.Combine(Directory, "字幕 样本.srt");
        public string Aac => Path.Combine(Directory, "原格式 音频.aac");
        public string Flac => Path.Combine(Directory, "原格式 音频.flac");
        public string Png => Path.Combine(Directory, "透明 图片.png");
        public string IndexedPng => Path.Combine(Directory, "索引调色板透明.png");
        public string SixteenBitPng => Path.Combine(Directory, "16位透明.png");
        public string BareLosslessWebp => Path.Combine(Directory, "无VP8X透明.webp");
        public string Jpeg => Path.Combine(Directory, "复杂 图片.jpg");
        public string Gif => Path.Combine(Directory, "透明 变帧延时.gif");
        public string AnimatedWebp => Path.Combine(Directory, "透明 动态.webp");
        public string Bmp => Path.Combine(Directory, "原格式 图片.bmp");
        public string StaticWebp => Path.Combine(Directory, "透明 静态.webp");
        public IEnumerable<(string, ConversionMode)> AdditionalCompressionSources => [(Mov, ConversionMode.Video), (M4v, ConversionMode.Video), (Webm, ConversionMode.Video), (Avi, ConversionMode.Video), (MultiStreamMkv, ConversionMode.Video), (Aac, ConversionMode.Audio), (Flac, ConversionMode.Audio), (Bmp, ConversionMode.Image), (StaticWebp, ConversionMode.Image)];
        public IEnumerable<string> Paths => [Video, LongVideo, Audio, Wave, UnsupportedAudio, Png, IndexedPng, SixteenBitPng, BareLosslessWebp, Jpeg, Gif, AnimatedWebp, Mov, M4v, RotatedVideo, Webm, Avi, MultiStreamMkv, Subtitle, Aac, Flac, Bmp, StaticWebp];
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/DesktopPetMenuAcceptance.cs ---
using System.IO;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Controls.Primitives;
using System.Windows.Input;
using System.Windows.Interop;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Themes;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

/// <summary>Real popup HWNDs and physical pointer paths, including a slow leftward submenu crossing.</summary>
internal static class DesktopPetMenuAcceptance
{
    public static async Task RunAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        ThemeManager.EnsureInitialized();
        var preference = ThemeManager.Preference;
        NativeMethods.GetCursorPos(out var originalPointer);
        var monitor = DesktopEnvironment.GetMonitors().First(item => item.Primary);
        var blank = new Border { Background = Brushes.White };
        var owner = new Window { Title = "桌宠菜单交互验收", Content = blank, Width = 320, Height = 160,
            Left = (monitor.WorkArea.Left + 60) / monitor.DpiScaleX,
            Top = (monitor.WorkArea.Top + 40) / monitor.DpiScaleY, ShowInTaskbar = false, Topmost = true };
        using var pet = new DesktopPetWindow(() => true);
        pet.Configure(false);
        pet.SetPosition(monitor.WorkArea.Left + 32, monitor.WorkArea.Bottom - 180);
        var menu = pet.InteractionMenu;
        var commands = new List<string>();
        pet.InteractionRequested += commands.Add;
        var observations = new List<object>();
        var inputDiagnostics = new List<object>();
        var routedMoveCount = 0;
        string? lastRoutedMoveSource = null;
        MouseEventHandler recordMove = (_, args) => { routedMoveCount++; lastRoutedMoveSource = Describe(args.OriginalSource as IInputElement); };
        menu.AddHandler(Mouse.PreviewMouseMoveEvent, recordMove, true);
        var expectedCommands = new Dictionary<string, string>
        {
            ["选择文件…"] = "choose-files", ["选择文件夹…"] = "choose-folder", ["摸摸头"] = "pet-head",
            ["一起玩耍"] = "play", ["喂食"] = "feed", ["开始工作"] = "work", ["停止工作"] = "stop-work", ["喵喵商店"] = "shop"
        };
        var dwell = Math.Max(500, SystemParameters.MenuShowDelay + 120);
        run.Evidence["desktopPetSubmenuPointerPaths"] = new { dwellMilliseconds = dwell, observations,
            actualNativeCursorInput = true, leftAndRightEdges = true };
        run.Evidence["desktopPetMenuInputDiagnostics"] = inputDiagnostics;
        run.StopActiveWindow = () => { pet.Dispose(); if (owner.IsVisible) owner.Close(); return Task.CompletedTask; };
        try
        {
            owner.Show();
            pet.SetEnvironmentVisible(true);
            await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle);
            var headers = menu.Items.OfType<PetSubmenuItem>().ToArray();
            Program.Assert(headers.Length == 2, "The production pet menu lost its two submenu headers.");
            foreach (var left in new[] { true, false })
            {
                ThemeManager.SetPreference(left ? "Dark" : "Light");
                foreach (var header in headers)
                    foreach (var item in header.Items.OfType<MenuItem>())
                    {
                        var popup = await OpenAsync(left, header, item.Header?.ToString() ?? "command");
                        var headerBounds = Bounds(header);
                        var submenu = (FrameworkElement)popup.Child;
                        var submenuBounds = Bounds(submenu);
                        var actuallyLeft = submenuBounds.Left < headerBounds.Left;
                        Program.Assert(actuallyLeft == left, "The real submenu opened on the wrong side for the screen-edge case.");
                        Program.Assert(left ? submenuBounds.Right >= headerBounds.Left - 1 : submenuBounds.Left <= headerBounds.Right + 1,
                            "The popup leaves a physical gap between the parent header and its submenu.");
                        var y = headerBounds.Top + headerBounds.Height / 2;
                        var inside = new Point(left ? headerBounds.Left + 2 : headerBounds.Right - 2, y);
                        var crossing = new Point(left ? (headerBounds.Left + submenuBounds.Right) / 2
                            : (headerBounds.Right + submenuBounds.Left) / 2, y);
                        var entered = new Point(left ? submenuBounds.Right - 9 : submenuBounds.Left + 9, y);
                        var pointerPath = new[] { inside, crossing, entered, Center(item) };
                        foreach (var point in pointerPath)
                        {
                            await MoveAsync(point);
                            RecordInput("slow-crossing", left, header, item.Header?.ToString() ?? "command", 0);
                            await KeepOpenAsync(left, header, dwell, "Slow pointer crossing at " + point);
                        }
                        Program.Assert(item.IsHighlighted, "The pointer could not enter and highlight the production submenu command.");
                        if (header == headers[1] && item == header.Items[0]) CaptureMenu(run, menu, submenu, left ? "pet-menu-left-dark" : "pet-menu-right-light");
                        var before = commands.Count;
                        Click();
                        await Task.Delay(150);
                        var label = item.Header?.ToString() ?? "";
                        Program.Assert(commands.Count == before + 1 && commands[^1] == expectedCommands[label] && !menu.IsOpen,
                            "Clicking the submenu did not invoke exactly its existing command and close the menu: " + label);
                        Program.Assert(Mouse.Captured is null, "Invoking a pet menu command left mouse capture active.");
                        observations.Add(new { side = left ? "Left" : "Right", label, headerBounds = headerBounds.ToString(),
                            submenuBounds = submenuBounds.ToString(), pointerPath = pointerPath.Select(point => point.ToString()).ToArray(),
                            command = commands[^1], closedAfterCommand = !menu.IsOpen });
                    }
            }

            await OpenAsync(true, headers[1], "escape-dismissal");
            Escape(); await Task.Delay(150);
            Escape(); await Task.Delay(150);
            Program.Assert(!menu.IsOpen && Mouse.Captured is null, "Escape did not close the submenu and root menu or release capture.");

            await OpenAsync(false, headers[1], "outside-click-dismissal");
            await MoveAsync(Center(blank)); Click(); await Task.Delay(150);
            Program.Assert(!menu.IsOpen && Mouse.Captured is null, "Clicking the owned blank window did not close the pet menu and release capture.");

            var closingPopup = await OpenAsync(true, headers[1], "pet-disposal");
            pet.Dispose();
            await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle);
            Program.Assert(!menu.IsOpen && !closingPopup.IsOpen && headers.All(item => !item.IsSubmenuOpen) && Mouse.Captured is null,
                "Closing the pet left a submenu popup or mouse capture behind.");
            run.Evidence["desktopPetSubmenuLifecycle"] = new { commandsInvokedExactlyOnce = commands.Count == expectedCommands.Count * 2,
                escapeClosesBothLevels = true, outsideClickCloses = true, petDisposalClosesPopups = true, captureReleased = true };

            async Task<Popup> OpenAsync(bool left, PetSubmenuItem header, string stage)
            {
                menu.IsOpen = false;
                await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle);
                // Every popup sequence starts with an actual click in this test's own window.
                // Native mouse packets, rather than a cursor warp, also update WPF's captured mouse device.
                var ownerHandle = new WindowInteropHelper(owner).Handle;
                owner.Activate();
                var activationRequested = SetForegroundWindow(ownerHandle);
                await Task.Delay(80);
                var ownedBlank = Center(blank);
                await MoveAsync(ownedBlank);
                await Task.Delay(80);
                var blankHit = WindowFromPoint(new NativeMethods.NativePoint((int)Math.Round(ownedBlank.X), (int)Math.Round(ownedBlank.Y)));
                NativeMethods.GetWindowThreadProcessId(blankHit, out var blankProcess);
                inputDiagnostics.Add(new { stage, side = left ? "Left" : "Right", step = "owned-window-input-initialization",
                    ownerHandle = ownerHandle.ToInt64(), activationRequested, blankHit = blankHit.ToInt64(), blankProcess,
                    foreground = NativeMethods.GetForegroundWindow().ToInt64() });
                Program.Assert(blankProcess == (uint)Environment.ProcessId, "The menu test's blank input point was covered by another process; refusing to click it.");
                Click();
                await Task.Delay(100);
                Program.Assert(NativeMethods.GetForegroundWindow() == ownerHandle,
                    "The menu test's owned window did not acquire foreground after its real blank click.");
                await MoveAsync(new Point(left ? monitor.WorkArea.Right - 8 : monitor.WorkArea.Left + 8, monitor.WorkArea.Top + 230));
                await Task.Delay(80);
                menu.PlacementTarget = pet;
                menu.Placement = PlacementMode.MousePoint;
                menu.IsOpen = true;
                await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle);
                header.ApplyTemplate();
                menu.UpdateLayout();
                // Popup HWND placement and theme measurement can settle after its first layout.
                // Aim at the realized header, then recalculate once if native placement moved it.
                await Task.Delay(150);
                var headerTarget = Center(header);
                inputDiagnostics.Add(new { stage, side = left ? "Left" : "Right", step = "realized-header-target",
                    target = headerTarget.ToString(), headerBounds = Bounds(header).ToString() });
                await MoveAsync(headerTarget);
                NativeMethods.GetCursorPos(out var hoveredPointer);
                if (!Bounds(header).Contains(new Point(hoveredPointer.X, hoveredPointer.Y)))
                    await MoveAsync(Center(header));
                var watch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
                do
                {
                    await Task.Delay(100);
                    RecordInput("hover", left, header, stage, watch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds);
                }
                while (menu.IsOpen && !header.IsSubmenuOpen && watch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds < Math.Max(2000, dwell * 3));
                Program.Assert(menu.IsOpen && header.IsSubmenuOpen, "Hovering the production menu header did not open its submenu: " +
                    (left ? "Left/" : "Right/") + header.Header + "/" + stage + ". See desktopPetMenuInputDiagnostics.");
                var popup = header.Template.FindName("PART_Popup", header) as Popup;
                Program.Assert(popup is { IsOpen: true, Child: FrameworkElement { IsVisible: true } }, "The production submenu popup is not visible.");
                return popup!;
            }
            void RecordInput(string step, bool left, MenuItem header, string stage, double elapsed)
            {
                NativeMethods.GetCursorPos(out var cursor);
                var foreground = NativeMethods.GetForegroundWindow();
                NativeMethods.GetWindowThreadProcessId(foreground, out var foregroundProcess);
                var popup = header.Template.FindName("PART_Popup", header) as Popup;
                var position = new Point(cursor.X, cursor.Y);
                var nativeHit = WindowFromPoint(cursor);
                NativeMethods.GetWindowThreadProcessId(nativeHit, out var nativeHitProcess);
                var nativeHitClass = new StringBuilder(256);
                NativeMethods.GetClassName(nativeHit, nativeHitClass, nativeHitClass.Capacity);
                inputDiagnostics.Add(new { stage, side = left ? "Left" : "Right", step, elapsedMilliseconds = elapsed,
                    header = header.Header?.ToString(), pointer = position.ToString(), menu.IsOpen, menu.IsMouseOver,
                    headerMouseOver = header.IsMouseOver, header.IsKeyboardFocusWithin, header.IsHighlighted, header.IsSubmenuOpen,
                    headerBounds = TryBounds(header), menuBounds = TryBounds(menu),
                    popupOpen = popup?.IsOpen, popupBounds = popup?.Child is FrameworkElement child ? TryBounds(child) : null,
                    nativeHitWindow = nativeHit.ToInt64(), nativeHitProcess, nativeHitClass = nativeHitClass.ToString(),
                    menuNativeHandle = (PresentationSource.FromVisual(menu) as HwndSource)?.Handle.ToInt64(),
                    headerNativeHandle = (PresentationSource.FromVisual(header) as HwndSource)?.Handle.ToInt64(),
                    popupNativeHandle = popup?.Child is Visual visual ? (PresentationSource.FromVisual(visual) as HwndSource)?.Handle.ToInt64() : null,
                    menuInputHit = menu.IsVisible ? Describe(menu.InputHitTest(menu.PointFromScreen(position))) : null,
                    directlyOver = Describe(Mouse.DirectlyOver), captured = Describe(Mouse.Captured),
                    routedMoveCount, lastRoutedMoveSource, wpfMenuPointer = Mouse.GetPosition(menu).ToString(),
                    foreground = foreground.ToInt64(), foregroundProcess });
            }
            async Task KeepOpenAsync(bool left, MenuItem header, int milliseconds, string stage)
            {
                for (var elapsed = 0; elapsed < milliseconds; elapsed += 50)
                {
                    await Task.Delay(50);
                    if (!menu.IsOpen || !header.IsSubmenuOpen)
                        RecordInput("slow-crossing-closed", left, header, stage, elapsed + 50);
                    Program.Assert(menu.IsOpen && header.IsSubmenuOpen, stage + " closed the submenu while moving toward its child items.");
                }
            }
        }
        finally
        {
            menu.IsOpen = false;
            menu.RemoveHandler(Mouse.PreviewMouseMoveEvent, recordMove);
            pet.Dispose();
            if (owner.IsVisible) owner.Close();
            SetCursorPos(originalPointer.X, originalPointer.Y);
            ThemeManager.SetPreference(preference);
            run.StopActiveWindow = null;
        }
    }

    private static Rect Bounds(FrameworkElement element)
        => new(element.PointToScreen(new Point()), element.PointToScreen(new Point(element.ActualWidth, element.ActualHeight)));
    private static Point Center(FrameworkElement element)
        => element.PointToScreen(new Point(element.ActualWidth / 2, element.ActualHeight / 2));
    private static string? TryBounds(FrameworkElement element)
        => element.IsVisible && PresentationSource.FromVisual(element) is not null ? Bounds(element).ToString() : null;
    private static string? Describe(IInputElement? element) => element switch
    {
        MenuItem item => "MenuItem:" + item.Header,
        TextBlock text => "TextBlock:" + text.Text,
        FrameworkElement framework => framework.GetType().Name + ":" + framework.Name,
        _ => element?.GetType().Name
    };
    private static async Task MoveAsync(Point target)
    {
        NativeMethods.GetCursorPos(out var current);
        var virtualLeft = GetSystemMetrics(76); var virtualTop = GetSystemMetrics(77);
        var virtualWidth = Math.Max(2, GetSystemMetrics(78)); var virtualHeight = Math.Max(2, GetSystemMetrics(79));
        const int steps = 6;
        for (var step = 1; step <= steps; step++)
        {
            var x = current.X + (target.X - current.X) * step / steps;
            var y = current.Y + (target.Y - current.Y) * step / steps;
            var packet = new NativeInput { Type = 0, Mouse = new NativeMouseInput {
                X = (int)Math.Round((x - virtualLeft) * 65535 / (virtualWidth - 1)),
                Y = (int)Math.Round((y - virtualTop) * 65535 / (virtualHeight - 1)),
                Flags = 0x0001 | 0x2000 | 0x4000 | 0x8000 } };
            Program.Assert(SendInput(1, [packet], Marshal.SizeOf<NativeInput>()) == 1,
                "Windows rejected the owned menu test's native mouse movement: " + Marshal.GetLastWin32Error());
            await Task.Delay(16);
        }
        await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Input);
    }
    private static void Click()
    {
        NativeInput[] packets = [new() { Mouse = new NativeMouseInput { Flags = 0x0002 } }, new() { Mouse = new NativeMouseInput { Flags = 0x0004 } }];
        Program.Assert(SendInput(2, packets, Marshal.SizeOf<NativeInput>()) == 2,
            "Windows rejected the owned menu test's native click: " + Marshal.GetLastWin32Error());
    }
    private static void Escape() { keybd_event(0x1B, 0, 0, UIntPtr.Zero); keybd_event(0x1B, 0, 0x0002, UIntPtr.Zero); }

    private static void CaptureMenu(AcceptanceRun run, ContextMenu menu, FrameworkElement submenu, string name)
    {
        var parentBounds = Bounds(menu); var childBounds = Bounds(submenu); var combined = Rect.Union(parentBounds, childBounds);
        var drawing = new DrawingVisual();
        using (var context = drawing.RenderOpen())
        {
            context.DrawRectangle(new VisualBrush(menu), null, new Rect(parentBounds.TopLeft - (Vector)combined.TopLeft, parentBounds.Size));
            context.DrawRectangle(new VisualBrush(submenu), null, new Rect(childBounds.TopLeft - (Vector)combined.TopLeft, childBounds.Size));
        }
        var bitmap = new RenderTargetBitmap((int)Math.Ceiling(combined.Width), (int)Math.Ceiling(combined.Height), 96, 96, PixelFormats.Pbgra32);
        bitmap.Render(drawing);
        var encoder = new PngBitmapEncoder(); encoder.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(bitmap));
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "screenshots"); Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var path = Path.Combine(directory, name + ".png");
        using (var file = File.Create(path)) encoder.Save(file);
        run.Evidence[name] = new { path, note = "Production WPF popup visuals composed at their observed physical screen coordinates; no other applications captured." };
    }

    [DllImport("user32.dll")] [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] private static extern bool SetCursorPos(int x, int y);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] private static extern void keybd_event(byte key, byte scan, uint flags, UIntPtr extraInfo);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] private static extern IntPtr WindowFromPoint(NativeMethods.NativePoint point);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] private static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr window);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] private static extern int GetSystemMetrics(int index);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", SetLastError = true)] private static extern uint SendInput(uint count, [In] NativeInput[] inputs, int size);
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] private struct NativeInput { public uint Type; public NativeMouseInput Mouse; }
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] private struct NativeMouseInput
    { public int X, Y; public uint Data, Flags, Time; public UIntPtr ExtraInfo; }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/DesktopPetPlatformAcceptance.cs ---
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Interop;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

internal static class DesktopPetPlatformAcceptance
{
    private const uint ProbeActivateMessage = 0x8051;
    // A short-lived owned child supplies real foreground HWNDs without depending on an installed game.
    public static int? RunProbe(string[] args)
    {
        if (args.Length < 3 || args[0] != "--pet-game-probe") return null;
        var fullscreen = args[1] == "fullscreen";
        var application = new Application { ShutdownMode = ShutdownMode.OnExplicitShutdown };
        application.Startup += (_, _) =>
        {
            var monitor = DesktopEnvironment.GetMonitors().First(item => item.Primary);
            var window = new Window
            {
                Title = "V0.8 桌宠游戏隐藏验收", Width = 440, Height = 280, ShowInTaskbar = false,
                WindowStyle = fullscreen ? WindowStyle.None : WindowStyle.SingleBorderWindow,
                ResizeMode = ResizeMode.NoResize, Topmost = true, ShowActivated = true,
                Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(223, 226, 246)),
                Content = new TextBlock { Text = "桌宠隐藏验收\n窗口将在检查完成后关闭", FontSize = 22,
                    TextAlignment = TextAlignment.Center, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center }
            };
            window.Closing += (_, _) =>
            {
                if (args.Length < 5 || !uint.TryParse(args[3], out var restoreProcessId) ||
                    !long.TryParse(args[4], out var restoreHandleValue)) return;
                var restoreHandle = new IntPtr(restoreHandleValue);
                NativeMethods.GetWindowThreadProcessId(restoreHandle, out var actualRestoreProcessId);
                // The foreground child explicitly gives its own parent permission before its HWND disappears.
                // Never request activation for an unrelated application.
                var owned = actualRestoreProcessId == restoreProcessId;
                var allowed = owned && AllowSetForegroundWindow(restoreProcessId);
                var requested = owned && SetForegroundWindow(restoreHandle);
                File.WriteAllText(args[2] + ".restore.json", JsonSerializer.Serialize(new { restoreProcessId,
                    actualRestoreProcessId, restoreHandle = restoreHandleValue, owned, allowed, requested,
                    foregroundWindow = NativeMethods.GetForegroundWindow().ToInt64() }));
            };
            window.Closed += (_, _) => application.Shutdown();
            window.Show();
            var handle = new WindowInteropHelper(window).Handle;
            HwndSource.FromHwnd(handle)?.AddHook((IntPtr _, int message, IntPtr _, IntPtr _, ref bool handled) =>
            {
                if ((uint)message == ProbeActivateMessage)
                {
                    handled = true;
                    window.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Input, new Action(() => { window.Activate(); SetForegroundWindow(handle); }));
                }
                return IntPtr.Zero;
            });
            if (fullscreen) NativeMethods.SetWindowPos(handle, new IntPtr(-1), (int)monitor.Bounds.Left, (int)monitor.Bounds.Top,
                (int)monitor.Bounds.Width, (int)monitor.Bounds.Height, 0x0040);
            else NativeMethods.SetWindowPos(handle, new IntPtr(-1), (int)monitor.WorkArea.Left + 140, (int)monitor.WorkArea.Top + 100,
                (int)(440 * monitor.DpiScaleX), (int)(280 * monitor.DpiScaleY), 0x0040);
            var handshakeTemporary = args[2] + ".tmp";
            File.WriteAllText(handshakeTemporary, JsonSerializer.Serialize(new { handle = handle.ToInt64(), mode = args[1], processId = Environment.ProcessId }));
            File.Move(handshakeTemporary, args[2], true);
            var timeout = new DispatcherTimer { Interval = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(20) };
            timeout.Tick += (_, _) => { timeout.Stop(); window.Close(); }; timeout.Start();
        };
        return application.Run();
    }

    public static async Task RunForegroundProbeAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System);
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "desktop-pet-real-foreground"); Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var manifest = WriteAtlasFixture(directory);
        var originalForeground = NativeMethods.GetForegroundWindow();
        var restoreWindow = new Window { Title = "V0.8 桌宠前台验收", Width = 320, Height = 160, ShowInTaskbar = false,
            ShowActivated = true, WindowStyle = WindowStyle.SingleBorderWindow, ResizeMode = ResizeMode.NoResize, Background = Brushes.AliceBlue,
            Content = new TextBlock { Text = "正在检查桌宠的自动隐藏与恢复", Margin = new Thickness(20), TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap } };
        using var pet = new DesktopPetWindow(); pet.Configure(false); pet.LoadSprites(manifest);
        var monitor = DesktopEnvironment.GetMonitors().First(item => item.Primary);
        pet.SetSize(120); pet.SetPosition(monitor.WorkArea.Left + 40, monitor.WorkArea.Bottom - 160); pet.SetEnvironmentVisible(true);
        IReadOnlyList<string> games = Array.Empty<string>();
        using var observer = new DesktopEnvironment(hidden => pet.SetEnvironmentVisible(!hidden), () => games);
        var checks = new List<object>();
        var activationAttempts = new List<object>();
        var restorationAttempts = new List<object>();
        run.Evidence["desktopPetForegroundActivation"] = new { originalForeground = originalForeground.ToInt64(), attempts = activationAttempts };
        run.Evidence["desktopPetForegroundRestoration"] = new { timeoutSeconds = 3, attempts = restorationAttempts };
        run.Evidence["desktopPetRealForegroundGames"] = new { checks, actualSeparateProcessWindows = true, restoredAfterEachProbe = false };
        try
        {
            restoreWindow.Show();
            await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle);
            var restoreHandle = new WindowInteropHelper(restoreWindow).Handle;
            restoreWindow.Activate();
            Program.Assert(await ActivateProbeAsync(restoreHandle, (uint)Environment.ProcessId, activationAttempts),
                "The owned ordinary foreground window could not become active before the game probes.");
            foreach (var mode in new[] { "fullscreen", "windowed" })
            {
                var handshake = Path.Combine(directory, mode + ".json");
                var start = new ProcessStartInfo(Environment.ProcessPath!) { UseShellExecute = false, CreateNoWindow = true,
                    WindowStyle = ProcessWindowStyle.Hidden, WorkingDirectory = run.Root };
                if (string.Equals(Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(Environment.ProcessPath), "dotnet", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    start.ArgumentList.Add(typeof(Program).Assembly.Location);
                start.ArgumentList.Add("--pet-game-probe"); start.ArgumentList.Add(mode); start.ArgumentList.Add(handshake);
                start.ArgumentList.Add(Environment.ProcessId.ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
                start.ArgumentList.Add(restoreHandle.ToInt64().ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
                using var child = Process.Start(start) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Could not start the desktop foreground test helper.");
                IntPtr probeHandle = IntPtr.Zero;
                try
                {
                    var deadline = DateTime.UtcNow.AddSeconds(8);
                    while (!File.Exists(handshake) && !child.HasExited && DateTime.UtcNow < deadline) await Task.Delay(75);
                    Program.Assert(File.Exists(handshake), "The desktop foreground test helper did not create its window.");
                    using (var data = JsonDocument.Parse(File.ReadAllText(handshake))) probeHandle = new IntPtr(data.RootElement.GetProperty("handle").GetInt64());
                    games = mode == "windowed" ? new[] { Path.GetFileName(Environment.ProcessPath!) } : Array.Empty<string>();
                    var allowed = AllowSetForegroundWindow((uint)child.Id);
                    activationAttempts.Add(new { step = "grant-owned-child-foreground", processId = child.Id, allowed });
                    PostMessage(probeHandle, ProbeActivateMessage, IntPtr.Zero, IntPtr.Zero);
                    Program.Assert(await ActivateProbeAsync(probeHandle, (uint)child.Id, activationAttempts),
                        "Windows did not allow the owned game probe to become foreground after a bounded dispatcher wait. See desktopPetForegroundActivation evidence; a normal interactive desktop token may be required.");
                    await Task.Delay(150); observer.Refresh(); await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle);
                    Program.Assert(observer.IsBlocked && !pet.IsVisible && pet.SpriteView.ActiveFrameTimerCount == 0 && pet.ActiveMovementTimerCount == 0,
                        "The real fullscreen/windowed foreground child did not hide the pet and stop its rendering.");
                    Program.Assert(mode != "windowed" || observer.BlockReason == "自定义游戏名单", "The real windowed game was not detected by executable name.");
                    checks.Add(new { mode, childProcessId = child.Id, foregroundWindow = probeHandle.ToInt64(), observer.BlockReason, hiddenRenderingStopped = true });
                }
                finally
                {
                    if (!child.HasExited)
                    {
                        if (probeHandle != IntPtr.Zero) PostMessage(probeHandle, 0x0010, IntPtr.Zero, IntPtr.Zero);
                        try { await child.WaitForExitAsync().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5)); }
                        catch (TimeoutException) { child.Kill(); await child.WaitForExitAsync(); }
                    }
                }
                games = Array.Empty<string>();
                if (File.Exists(handshake + ".restore.json"))
                {
                    using var handoff = JsonDocument.Parse(File.ReadAllText(handshake + ".restore.json"));
                    restorationAttempts.Add(new { mode, step = "child-close-handoff", handoff = handoff.RootElement.Clone() });
                }
                var restoreWatch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
                restoreWindow.Activate();
                Program.Assert(await ActivateProbeAsync(restoreHandle, (uint)Environment.ProcessId, activationAttempts),
                    "The owned restoration window could not become foreground after a bounded dispatcher wait.");
                var restored = false;
                do
                {
                    observer.Refresh(); await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle);
                    var foreground = NativeMethods.GetForegroundWindow();
                    NativeMethods.GetWindowThreadProcessId(foreground, out var foregroundProcessId);
                    var notificationResult = NativeMethods.SHQueryUserNotificationState(out var notificationState);
                    var hasBounds = DesktopEnvironment.TryWindowRect(foreground, out var bounds);
                    restored = foreground == restoreHandle && !observer.IsBlocked && pet.IsVisible;
                    restorationAttempts.Add(new { mode, step = "observe-restoration", elapsedMilliseconds = restoreWatch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds,
                        foregroundWindow = foreground.ToInt64(), foregroundProcessId, expectedForegroundWindow = restoreHandle.ToInt64(),
                        notificationResult, notificationState, hasBounds, bounds = bounds.ToString(),
                        observer.IsBlocked, observer.BlockReason, petVisible = pet.IsVisible, restored });
                    if (restored) break;
                    await Task.Delay(75);
                } while (restoreWatch.Elapsed < TimeSpan.FromSeconds(3));
                Program.Assert(restored, "The pet did not restore after the real foreground game closed. See desktopPetForegroundRestoration native state evidence.");
            }
            run.Evidence["desktopPetRealForegroundGames"] = new { checks, actualSeparateProcessWindows = true, restoredAfterEachProbe = true };
        }
        finally
        {
            pet.SetEnvironmentVisible(false); restoreWindow.Close();
        }
    }

    private static async Task<bool> ActivateProbeAsync(IntPtr handle, uint expectedProcessId, List<object> evidence)
    {
        var target = NativeMethods.GetWindowThreadProcessId(handle, out var processId);
        Program.Assert(processId == expectedProcessId, "Foreground activation was refused because the window is not an owned acceptance probe.");
        var current = GetCurrentThreadId();
        for (var attempt = 0; attempt < 20; attempt++)
        {
            var requested = SetForegroundWindow(handle);
            await Task.Delay(75); // Cross-thread activation is queued; let both owned dispatchers process it.
            var foreground = NativeMethods.GetForegroundWindow();
            evidence.Add(new { processId, targetWindow = handle.ToInt64(), attempt, requested, foregroundWindow = foreground.ToInt64() });
            if (foreground == handle) return true;
            if (attempt == 4 && target != 0 && target != current)
            {
                // Only the two owned acceptance threads may be attached. Never attach to another application's input.
                var attached = AttachThreadInput(current, target, true);
                try { if (attached) { BringWindowToTop(handle); SetForegroundWindow(handle); } }
                finally { if (attached) AttachThreadInput(current, target, false); }
                await Task.Delay(75);
                if (NativeMethods.GetForegroundWindow() == handle) return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    public static void RunGeometry(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var workArea = new Rect(-1920, 0, 1920, 1040);
        var topLeft = DesktopEnvironment.ClampPosition(new Point(-3000, -400), new Size(160, 160), workArea);
        var bottomRight = DesktopEnvironment.ClampPosition(new Point(1000, 1800), new Size(160, 160), workArea);
        Program.Assert(topLeft == new Point(-1920, 0) && bottomRight == new Point(-160, 880), "The desktop pet escaped a negative-coordinate monitor work area.");
        Program.Assert(DesktopEnvironment.ClampPosition(new Point(0, 0), new Size(3000, 2000), workArea) == workArea.TopLeft,
            "Oversized desktop content did not clamp safely.");
        var monitor = new Rect(0, 0, 1920, 1080);
        Program.Assert(DesktopEnvironment.CoversMonitor(new Rect(-1, -1, 1922, 1082), monitor), "Fullscreen detection lost its pixel tolerance.");
        Program.Assert(!DesktopEnvironment.CoversMonitor(new Rect(0, 0, 1920, 1040), monitor), "An ordinary maximized work-area window was classified as fullscreen.");
        Program.Assert(DesktopEnvironment.MatchesExecutable(@"C:\Games\CatGame.EXE", new[] { " catgame.exe " }) &&
            !DesktopEnvironment.MatchesExecutable(@"C:\Games\CatGameHelper.exe", new[] { "catgame.exe" }), "The game list is not an exact executable-name match.");
        Program.Assert(DesktopPetWindow.IsShortClick(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(299)) &&
            !DesktopPetWindow.IsShortClick(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(300)), "The desktop click/long-press boundary is incorrect.");
        run.Evidence["desktopPetGeometry"] = new { negativeMonitor = true, workAreaClamp = true, fullscreenTolerance = 2, exactExecutableNames = true, longPressMilliseconds = 300 };
    }

    public static async Task RunWindowAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System);
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "desktop-pet-platform");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var manifest = WriteAtlasFixture(directory);
        var monitor = DesktopEnvironment.GetMonitors().FirstOrDefault(item => item.Primary) ?? DesktopEnvironment.GetMonitors().First();
        using var window = new DesktopPetWindow();
        window.Configure(false);
        window.LoadSprites(manifest);
        window.SetSize(120);
        window.SetPosition(monitor.WorkArea.Left + 60, monitor.WorkArea.Top + 100);
        var foreground = NativeMethods.GetForegroundWindow();
        window.SetEnvironmentVisible(true);
        await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle);
        await Task.Delay(70);
        Program.Assert(NativeMethods.GetForegroundWindow() == foreground, "Showing the desktop pet took foreground focus.");
        var style = NativeMethods.GetWindowLongPtr(new WindowInteropHelper(window).Handle, -20).ToInt64();
        Program.Assert((style & 0x08000000) != 0 && (style & 0x00000080) != 0 && !window.ShowInTaskbar, "Desktop pet native nonactivation/toolwindow styles are missing.");
        Program.Assert(window.SpriteView.ContainsVisiblePixel(new Point(60, 60)) && !window.SpriteView.ContainsVisiblePixel(new Point(1, 1)),
            "The production alpha hit mask did not distinguish the cat and transparent padding.");
        Program.Assert(window.SpriteView.InputHitTest(new Point(60, 60)) is not null,
            "The visible sprite has no routed WPF input target for clicking or dropping files.");
        var rendered = new RenderTargetBitmap(120, 120, 96, 96, PixelFormats.Pbgra32);
        rendered.Render(window.SpriteView);
        var paintedPixel = new byte[4]; rendered.CopyPixels(new Int32Rect(60, 60, 1, 1), paintedPixel, 4, 0);
        Program.Assert(paintedPixel[3] == 255 && paintedPixel[2] == 190,
            "The shared atlas drawing frame did not render its cropped pixels in the actual view.");
        var handle = new WindowInteropHelper(window).Handle;
        var solid = window.SpriteView.PointToScreen(new Point(60, 60));
        var clear = window.SpriteView.PointToScreen(new Point(1, 1));
        Program.Assert(SendMessage(handle, 0x0084, IntPtr.Zero, PackPoint(clear)).ToInt64() == -1 &&
            SendMessage(handle, 0x0084, IntPtr.Zero, PackPoint(solid)).ToInt64() != -1,
            "The native transparent hit route did not pass padding through to the desktop.");
        Program.Assert(window.SpriteView.FrameIntervalMilliseconds >= 33 && PetSpriteView.CachedAtlasBytes <= 24 * 1024 * 1024,
            "The sprite player exceeded its frame-rate or atlas cache limit.");
        Program.Assert(window.SpriteView.ActiveFrameTimerCount == 1, "The actual visible sprite frame timer did not run.");
        var observedFrames = new HashSet<int> { window.SpriteView.RenderedFrameIndex };
        for (var sample = 0; sample < 7; sample++) { await Task.Delay(40); observedFrames.Add(window.SpriteView.RenderedFrameIndex); }
        Program.Assert(observedFrames.Count > 1, "The actual sprite frame did not advance.");
        window.SetAnimation("work");
        Program.Assert(!window.TryWalk(40), "The desktop pet roamed while working.");
        window.SetAnimation("idle");
        Program.Assert(window.TryWalk(40), "An idle desktop pet did not begin walking.");
        await Task.Delay(100);
        window.SetEnvironmentVisible(false);
        var hiddenFrame = window.SpriteView.RenderedFrameIndex;
        await Task.Delay(200);
        Program.Assert(!window.IsVisible && !window.IsWalking && window.SpriteView.ActiveFrameTimerCount == 0 &&
            window.ActiveMovementTimerCount == 0 && window.ActiveHoldTimerCount == 0 &&
            window.SpriteView.ActiveTransitionClockCount == 0 && window.SpriteView.RenderedFrameIndex == hiddenFrame,
            "Hidden desktop pet retained rendering, transition or movement work.");
        window.SetEnvironmentVisible(true);
        window.SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode.Reduced);
        await Task.Delay(50);
        Program.Assert(!window.TryWalk(40) && window.SpriteView.ActiveFrameTimerCount == 0 && window.SpriteView.ActiveTransitionClockCount == 0,
            "Reduced motion retained a desktop sprite or roaming animation.");
        var foregroundBeforeBubble = NativeMethods.GetForegroundWindow();
        window.ShowMessage("文件处理完成啦。", () => { }, "查看结果");
        var foregroundAfterBubble = NativeMethods.GetForegroundWindow();
        var bubble = Application.Current!.Windows.OfType<Window>().Single(item => item.Title == "猫咪的提醒" && item.IsVisible);
        var bubbleStyle = NativeMethods.GetWindowLongPtr(new WindowInteropHelper(bubble).Handle, -20).ToInt64();
        Program.Assert(foregroundAfterBubble == foregroundBeforeBubble, "A completion bubble took foreground focus.");
        Program.Assert((bubbleStyle & 0x08000000) != 0 && (bubbleStyle & 0x00000080) != 0 && !bubble.ShowActivated && !bubble.ShowInTaskbar,
            "The actual completion bubble lacks native nonactivation/toolwindow styles.");
        window.SetEnvironmentVisible(false);
        Program.Assert(Application.Current?.Windows.OfType<Window>().All(item => item.Title != "猫咪的提醒" || !item.IsVisible) != false,
            "Hiding the desktop pet left its completion bubble visible.");
        window.SetPosition(monitor.WorkArea.Right + 500, monitor.WorkArea.Bottom + 500);
        var position = window.Position;
        var clampMonitor = DesktopEnvironment.GetWorkAreaForPosition(monitor.WorkArea.Right + 560, monitor.WorkArea.Bottom + 560);
        var expected = DesktopEnvironment.ClampPosition(new Point(monitor.WorkArea.Right + 500, monitor.WorkArea.Bottom + 500),
            new Size(120 * clampMonitor.DpiScaleX, 120 * clampMonitor.DpiScaleY), clampMonitor.WorkArea);
        Program.Assert(Math.Abs(position.X - expected.X) <= 2 && Math.Abs(position.Y - expected.Y) <= 2, "The actual native desktop window was not clamped to its monitor.");
        window.SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode.Gentle);
        window.SetEnvironmentVisible(true);
        var detectFullscreen = true;
        IReadOnlyList<string> games = Array.Empty<string>();
        var observation = new DesktopEnvironmentObservation(5, false, "ordinary.exe", false, true, false, true,
            new Rect(monitor.Bounds.Left, monitor.Bounds.Top, 500, 400), monitor.Bounds);
        var visibilityChanges = 0;
        using (var observer = new DesktopEnvironment(hidden => { visibilityChanges++; window.SetEnvironmentVisible(!hidden); },
                   () => games, () => detectFullscreen, () => observation))
        {
            observation = observation with { Captioned = false, WindowBounds = monitor.Bounds };
            observer.Refresh();
            Program.Assert(observer.IsBlocked && !window.IsVisible && window.SpriteView.ActiveFrameTimerCount == 0,
                "A borderless foreground observation did not hide the real window and stop its rendering.");
            observer.Refresh();
            Program.Assert(visibilityChanges == 1, "An unchanged fullscreen observation repeatedly changed visibility.");
            observation = observation with { Captioned = true, WindowBounds = new Rect(monitor.Bounds.Left, monitor.Bounds.Top, 500, 400), Executable = "game.exe" };
            games = new[] { "game.exe" }; observer.Refresh();
            Program.Assert(observer.BlockReason == "自定义游戏名单" && observer.IsBlocked,
                "A listed windowed game did not keep the desktop pet hidden.");
            detectFullscreen = false; observer.Refresh();
            Program.Assert(!observer.IsBlocked && window.IsVisible, "Disabling game/fullscreen detection did not restore the desktop pet.");
            observation = observation with { NotificationState = 1 }; observer.Refresh();
            Program.Assert(observer.IsBlocked && !window.IsVisible, "An inactive/locked session stayed visible when fullscreen detection was disabled.");
            observation = observation with { NotificationState = 5 }; observer.Refresh();
            Program.Assert(!observer.IsBlocked && window.IsVisible, "The desktop pet did not restore after session availability returned.");
            observer.Dispose();
            Program.Assert(observer.ActiveTimerCount == 0 && !observer.HasNativeForegroundHook,
                "Disposing desktop observation left a timer or native hook active.");
        }
        window.SetEnvironmentVisible(false);
        run.Evidence["desktopPetWindow"] = new
        {
            actualNativeStylesChecked = true, didNotTakeForegroundFocus = true, actualAlphaHitMaskChecked = true,
            nativePaddingPassThroughChecked = true, routedWpfInputTargetChecked = true, spritePresentationFps = 30, movementMaximumFps = 60,
            actualAtlasDrawingRenderedPixelChecked = true,
            variableHeightAtlasRowsRenderedAndHitTested = true,
            boundedSharedAtlasCacheBytes = PetSpriteView.CachedAtlasBytes,
            injectedBorderlessAndWindowedGameObservationsHideRealWindow = true, lockHidesWithFullscreenDetectionDisabled = true,
            visibilityChangesDeduplicated = true, detectorDisposalStopsTimersAndHooks = true,
            visibleFrameIndices = observedFrames, hiddenTimersAndClocksStopped = true, reducedMotionRespected = true,
            workPausesRoaming = true, completionBubbleHidesWithPet = true, completionBubbleDoesNotTakeForeground = true, physicalBoundsClamped = true,
            foregroundBeforeBubble = foregroundBeforeBubble.ToInt64(), foregroundAfterBubble = foregroundAfterBubble.ToInt64(), bubbleExtendedStyle = bubbleStyle,
            fixture = "Small deterministic transparent atlas, exercising the production sprite player and native window; production artwork is checked separately."
        };
        MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System);
    }

    private static string WriteAtlasFixture(string directory)
    {
        const int cell = 32;
        const int columns = 6;
        const int rows = 2;
        var pixels = new byte[cell * columns * cell * rows * 4];
        var width = cell * columns;
        for (var row = 0; row < rows; row++)
            for (var column = 0; column < columns; column++)
                for (var y = 7; y < cell - 3; y++)
                    for (var x = 7; x < cell - 7; x++)
                    {
                        var index = (((row * cell + y) * width) + column * cell + x) * 4;
                        pixels[index] = (byte)(120 + column * 12);
                        pixels[index + 1] = 150;
                        pixels[index + 2] = 190;
                        pixels[index + 3] = 255;
                    }
        var bitmap = BitmapSource.Create(width, cell * rows, 96, 96, PixelFormats.Bgra32, null, pixels, width * 4);
        var encoder = new PngBitmapEncoder();
        encoder.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(bitmap));
        using (var file = File.Create(Path.Combine(directory, "test-atlas.png"))) encoder.Save(file);
        var path = Path.Combine(directory, "test-sprites.json");
        File.WriteAllText(path, JsonSerializer.Serialize(new
        {
            atlases = new[] { new { file = "test-atlas.png", columns, rows, rowBounds = new[] { new[] { 0d, .5625d }, new[] { .5d, .5d } } } },
            animations = new Dictionary<string, object>
            {
                ["idle"] = new { atlas = 0, row = 0, frames = 6, frameMs = 50, frameDurationsMs = new[] { 84, 84, 84, 84, 84, 84 } },
                ["walk"] = new { atlas = 0, row = 0, frames = 6, frameMs = 50 },
                ["work"] = new { atlas = 0, row = 1, frames = 6, frameMs = 50 }
            }
        }));
        return path;
    }

    private static IntPtr PackPoint(Point point) => new(((int)Math.Round(point.Y) & 0xFFFF) << 16 | ((int)Math.Round(point.X) & 0xFFFF));
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] private static extern IntPtr SendMessage(IntPtr window, uint message, IntPtr wParam, IntPtr lParam);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] private static extern bool PostMessage(IntPtr window, uint message, IntPtr wParam, IntPtr lParam);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] private static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr window);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] private static extern bool BringWindowToTop(IntPtr window);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] private static extern bool AttachThreadInput(uint first, uint second, bool attach);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] private static extern bool AllowSetForegroundWindow(uint processId);
    [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] private static extern uint GetCurrentThreadId();
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/FilePetBridgeAcceptance.cs ---
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Text.Json;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

/// <summary>Production view-model/import/queue integration with deterministic engines.
/// Real codec and archive validation remains covered by the existing engine acceptance cases.</summary>
internal static class FilePetBridgeAcceptance
{
    public static async Task RunAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        await run.CaseAsync("pet-file-picker-eleven-routes-mixed-input-and-volumes", () => RoutesAsync(run));
        await run.CaseAsync("pet-file-import-current-receipts-and-moved-input", () => ImportReceiptsAsync(run));
        await run.CaseAsync("pet-unified-four-queues-and-merged-archive-rewards", () => QueueFeedsAsync(run));
        await run.CaseAsync("pet-progress-throttle-commit-boundary-and-observer-isolation", () => ProgressAsync(run));
        await run.CaseAsync("pet-busy-import-cancel-partial-and-password-exhaustion", () => OutcomesAsync(run));
        await run.CaseAsync("pet-recovery-retry-actual-request-and-safe-snapshot", () => RecoveryRetryAsync(run));
    }

    private static async Task RoutesAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var directory = FixtureDirectory(run, "routes");
        var paths = new[] { Touch(directory, "video.mp4"), Touch(directory, "audio.mp3"), Touch(directory, "image.png"),
            Touch(directory, "encrypted.zip"), Path.Combine(directory, "folder"), Touch(directory, "split.7z.001"), Touch(directory, "split.7z.002") };
        Directory.CreateDirectory(paths[4]);
        var missing = Path.Combine(directory, "moved.mp4");
        var mixed = paths.Concat([missing, paths[0]]).ToArray();
        var counts = new Dictionary<FileProcessingAction, int>
        {
            [FileProcessingAction.Video] = 1, [FileProcessingAction.Audio] = 1, [FileProcessingAction.Image] = 1,
            [FileProcessingAction.ExtractAudio] = 1, [FileProcessingAction.CompressMedia] = 3, [FileProcessingAction.Gif] = 1,
            [FileProcessingAction.CreateArchive] = 7, [FileProcessingAction.ExtractArchive] = 3,
            [FileProcessingAction.RecoverPassword] = 3, [FileProcessingAction.ObfuscateImage] = 1,
            [FileProcessingAction.DeobfuscateImage] = 1
        };
        var model = CreateModel(run, new BridgeEngine(), "route-counts");
        var choices = model.GetFileActionChoices(mixed);
        Program.Assert(choices.Count == 11 && choices.All(choice => choice.TotalCount == 8)
            && choices.All(choice => choice.CompatibleCount == counts[choice.Action]),
            "The file action picker failed to count all 11 routes, directories, volumes, missing files or duplicate inputs.");
        foreach (var action in Enum.GetValues<FileProcessingAction>())
        {
            var engine = new BridgeEngine();
            model = CreateModel(run, engine, "import-" + action);
            var imported = await model.ImportForActionAsync(action, mixed);
            Program.Assert(!imported.Busy && imported.AttemptedPaths.Count == 8
                && imported.AcceptedPaths.Count == counts[action]
                && imported.RejectedPaths.Count == 8 - counts[action]
                && imported.RejectedPaths.All(rejection => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rejection.Reason)),
                $"{action} did not retain incompatible paths with a reason or deduplicate input paths.");
            Program.Assert(engine.ExecutionCount == 0 && !model.IsProcessing,
                "Selecting an action started file processing before the user confirmed parameters.");
            if (action is FileProcessingAction.ExtractArchive or FileProcessingAction.RecoverPassword)
            {
                var sources = action == FileProcessingAction.ExtractArchive
                    ? model.ArchiveQueue.Select(row => row.SourcePath).ToArray()
                    : model.RecoveryQueue.Select(row => row.SourcePath).ToArray();
                Program.Assert(sources.Length == 2 && sources.Contains(paths[5]) && !sources.Contains(paths[6]),
                    "The desktop import did not reuse canonical first-volume deduplication.");
            }
            else if (action == FileProcessingAction.CreateArchive)
                Program.Assert(model.ArchiveQueue.Any(row => row.SourcePath == paths[4] && row.IsDirectory),
                    "A folder was not routed to archive creation as an ordinary directory.");
        }
        run.Evidence["petFileRoutes"] = new { actionCount = 11, mixedInput = true, missingAndIncompatibleRetainedWithReasons = true,
            canonicalFirstVolume = true, folderArchive = true, noAutomaticExecution = true };
    }

    private static async Task ImportReceiptsAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var directory = FixtureDirectory(run, "receipts");
        var video = Touch(directory, "receipt.mp4");
        var engine = new BridgeEngine();
        var model = CreateModel(run, engine, "receipts-model");
        await model.ImportForActionAsync(FileProcessingAction.Video, [video]);
        await model.RunQueueAsync();
        engine.FailProbe = true;
        var damaged = await model.ImportForActionAsync(FileProcessingAction.Video, [video]);
        Program.Assert(damaged.AcceptedPaths.Count == 0 && damaged.RejectedPaths.Single().Reason.Contains("检查失败")
            && model.Queue.Count == 1, "A historical completed row concealed a failed reinspection.");
        engine.FailProbe = false;
        var fresh = await model.ImportForActionAsync(FileProcessingAction.Video, [video]);
        Program.Assert(fresh.AcceptedPaths.Count == 1 && model.Queue.Count == 2 && model.PendingCount == 1,
            "Dropping a completed file again did not create a new user-confirmed processing request.");
        var duplicate = await model.ImportForActionAsync(FileProcessingAction.Video, [video, video]);
        Program.Assert(duplicate.AcceptedPaths.Count == 1 && model.Queue.Count == 2,
            "Dropping a file already pending created a duplicate job or falsely rejected it.");
