        var plain = Touch(directory, "plain.zip");
        model = CreateModel(run, new BridgeEngine(), "plain-recovery");
        var unnecessary = await model.ImportForActionAsync(FileProcessingAction.RecoverPassword, [plain]);
        Program.Assert(unnecessary.AcceptedPaths.Count == 0 && unnecessary.RejectedPaths.Single().Reason.Contains("无需密码"),
            "A recovery rejection did not return its actual per-file reason.");
        var marked = Touch(directory, "marked.png");
        var imageResult = await model.ImportForActionAsync(FileProcessingAction.ObfuscateImage, [marked]);
        Program.Assert(imageResult.AcceptedPaths.Count == 0 && imageResult.RejectedPaths.Single().Reason.Contains("混淆标记"),
            "An already marked image did not retain its specific rejection reason.");

        var first = Touch(directory, "broken.7z.001");
        var later = Touch(directory, "broken.7z.002");
        File.Move(first, first + ".moved");
        var missingFirst = await model.ImportForActionAsync(FileProcessingAction.ExtractArchive, [later]);
        Program.Assert(missingFirst.AcceptedPaths.Count == 0 && missingFirst.RejectedPaths.Single().SourcePath == later
            && missingFirst.RejectedPaths.Single().Reason.Contains("首卷"), "A missing first volume was accepted or its original later-volume path was lost.");
        File.Move(video, video + ".moved");
        var moved = await model.ImportForActionAsync(FileProcessingAction.Video, [video]);
        Program.Assert(moved.AcceptedPaths.Count == 0 && moved.RejectedPaths.Single().SourcePath == video,
            "A file moved after desktop staging was not rejected with its original path.");
        run.Evidence["petImportReceipts"] = new { actualCurrentInspection = true, completedFileNewRequest = true,
            pendingDuplicateAvoided = true, recoveryAndImageReasons = true, movedFileAndMissingFirstVolume = true };
    }

    private static async Task QueueFeedsAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var directory = FixtureDirectory(run, "feeds");
        var video = Touch(directory, "feed.mp4");
        var audio = Touch(directory, "feed.mp3");
        var image = Touch(directory, "feed.png");
        var archive = Touch(directory, "feed.zip");
        var wallet = new PetStateStore(Path.Combine(directory, "wallet"));
        var operationIds = new HashSet<Guid>();
        foreach (var action in Enum.GetValues<FileProcessingAction>())
        {
            var engine = new BridgeEngine();
            var model = CreateModel(run, engine, "feed-" + action);
            var source = action switch
            {
                FileProcessingAction.Audio => audio,
                FileProcessingAction.Image or FileProcessingAction.ObfuscateImage or FileProcessingAction.DeobfuscateImage => image,
                FileProcessingAction.ExtractArchive or FileProcessingAction.RecoverPassword => archive,
                _ => video
            };
            await model.ImportForActionAsync(action, [source]);
            if (action == FileProcessingAction.ExtractArchive) model.ArchivePassword = BridgeEngine.Secret;
            if (action == FileProcessingAction.CompressMedia)
                foreach (var row in model.Queue.Where(row => row.NeedsFormatSelection)) row.SelectedCompressionPreset = row.CompressionPresets.First();
            var snapshots = new List<ProcessingTaskSnapshot>();
            var summaries = new List<ProcessingBatchSummary>();
            model.ProcessingTaskChanged += (_, snapshot) => snapshots.Add(snapshot);
            model.ProcessingBatchFinished += (_, summary) => summaries.Add(summary);
            await model.RunQueueAsync();
            Program.Assert(engine.ExecutionCount == 1 && summaries.Count == 1, $"{action} did not expose exactly one batch through the unified task feed.");
            var summary = summaries.Single();
            Program.Assert(summary.Action == action && summary.CompletedCount == 1 && summary.SuccessfulCount == 1
                && summary.FailedCount == 0 && summary.PendingCount == 0 && summary.Tasks.Single().Action == action
                && summary.Tasks.Single().Index == 1 && summary.Tasks.Single().Count == 1
                && snapshots.First().Status == JobStatus.Processing && snapshots.Last().Status == JobStatus.Completed
                && operationIds.Add(summary.SuccessfulRequestIds.Single()), $"{action} emitted incorrect unified results or request identities.");
            Program.Assert(wallet.SettleBatch(summary.BatchId, summary.SuccessfulRequestIds).CoinsChanged == 5
                && wallet.SettleBatch(summary.BatchId, summary.SuccessfulRequestIds).CoinsChanged == 0,
                $"{action} was not rewarded exactly once for a real successful request.");
            if (action == FileProcessingAction.ExtractArchive)
                Program.Assert(summary.Tasks.Single().OutputIsDirectory && Directory.Exists(summary.Tasks.Single().OutputPath),
                    "Extraction completion lost its output-directory link.");
            if (action == FileProcessingAction.RecoverPassword)
                Program.Assert(!JsonSerializer.Serialize(summary).Contains(BridgeEngine.Secret, StringComparison.Ordinal),
                    "A successful recovery password was exposed in a pet receipt.");
        }
        Program.Assert(wallet.Snapshot.Coins == 85, "All eleven real route requests did not credit exactly 55 coins.");

        var combinedEngine = new BridgeEngine();
        var combined = CreateModel(run, combinedEngine, "merged-archive");
        await combined.ImportForActionAsync(FileProcessingAction.CreateArchive, [video, audio]);
        combined.SelectedArchiveGrouping = ArchiveGrouping.Combined;
        combined.ArchiveName = "merged";
        ProcessingBatchSummary? merged = null;
        combined.ProcessingBatchFinished += (_, summary) => merged = summary;
        await combined.RunQueueAsync();
        Program.Assert(merged is { SuccessfulCount: 1, CompletedCount: 1 } && merged.Tasks.Single().Count == 1
            && combined.ArchiveQueue.Count(row => row.Status == JobStatus.Completed) == 2 && combinedEngine.ExecutionCount == 1
            && wallet.SettleBatch(merged.BatchId, merged.SuccessfulRequestIds).CoinsChanged == 5,
            "Combined archive rows paid separately instead of representing one actual archive request.");
        run.Evidence["petTaskFeeds"] = new { routesExecuted = 11, fourSerialQueuesIntegrated = true,
            committedOutputLinks = true, successfulRequestCoins = 5, mergedArchiveRewardOnce = true,
            repeatedCompletionReconciliationRewardOnce = true, recoveryPasswordExcluded = true };
    }

    private static async Task ProgressAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var directory = FixtureDirectory(run, "progress");
        var video = Touch(directory, "progress.mp4");
        var engine = new BridgeEngine { ExerciseProgress = true };
        var model = CreateModel(run, engine, "progress-model");
        await model.ImportForActionAsync(FileProcessingAction.Video, [video]);
        var snapshots = new List<(ProcessingTaskSnapshot Snapshot, long Timestamp)>();
        var finished = new List<ProcessingBatchSummary>();
        model.ProcessingTaskChanged += (_, _) => throw new InvalidOperationException("Controlled presentation observer failure.");
        model.ProcessingTaskChanged += (_, snapshot) => snapshots.Add((snapshot, Stopwatch.GetTimestamp()));
        model.ProcessingBatchFinished += (_, _) => throw new InvalidOperationException("Controlled presentation observer failure.");
        model.ProcessingBatchFinished += (_, summary) => finished.Add(summary);
        await model.RunQueueAsync();
        var processing = snapshots.Where(item => item.Snapshot.Status == JobStatus.Processing).ToArray();
        var known = processing.Where(item => item.Snapshot.Percent.HasValue).ToArray();
        Program.Assert(processing.Any(item => item.Snapshot.Percent is null)
            && known.Length >= 2 && known.All(item => item.Snapshot.Percent < 100)
            && known.Any(item => item.Snapshot.Percent == 99.5),
            "Unknown progress, monotonic clamping or the output-commit boundary was not preserved.");
        Program.Assert(known.Zip(known.Skip(1)).All(pair => pair.Second.Snapshot.Percent >= pair.First.Snapshot.Percent)
            && known.Zip(known.Skip(1)).All(pair => Stopwatch.GetElapsedTime(pair.First.Timestamp, pair.Second.Timestamp).TotalMilliseconds >= 240),
            "Repeated calibration passes regressed progress or pet progress notifications exceeded four per second.");
        Program.Assert(known.Length <= 5 && snapshots.Last().Snapshot.Percent == 100
            && finished.Single().SuccessfulCount == 1 && model.Queue.Single().Status == JobStatus.Completed,
            "Presentation observer failures changed a committed file result, or progress bursts bypassed throttling.");
        var receipt = finished.Single();
        var wallet = new PetStateStore(Path.Combine(directory, "wallet"));
        Program.Assert(wallet.SettleBatch(receipt.BatchId, receipt.SuccessfulRequestIds).CoinsChanged == 5,
            "Size-calibration passes paid separately instead of one completed request.");
        run.Evidence["petProgressFeed"] = new { unknownPercentSupported = true, maximumProcessingPercent = 99.5,
            progressRefreshLimitPerSecond = 4, calibrationMonotonic = true, committedOnlyHundred = true,
            observerFailureCannotChangeResult = true };
    }

    private static async Task OutcomesAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var directory = FixtureDirectory(run, "outcomes");
        var first = Touch(directory, "first.mp4");
        var second = Touch(directory, "second.mp4");
        var staged = Touch(directory, "staged.mp4");
        var engine = new BridgeEngine { Hold = true };
        var model = CreateModel(run, engine, "busy-cancel");
        await model.ImportForActionAsync(FileProcessingAction.Video, [first, second]);
        ProcessingBatchSummary? cancelled = null;
        model.ProcessingBatchFinished += (_, summary) => cancelled = summary;
        var active = model.RunQueueAsync();
        await WaitAsync(() => engine.ExecutionCount == 1, "The controlled batch did not start.");
        var busy = await model.ImportForActionAsync(FileProcessingAction.Audio, [staged]);
        Program.Assert(busy.Busy && busy.AttemptedPaths.Single() == staged && busy.AcceptedPaths.Count == 0
            && model.Mode == ConversionMode.Video && model.Queue.Count == 2,
            "A busy desktop import changed current parameters, queue or lost its staged path.");
        model.Cancel();
        await active;
        Program.Assert(cancelled is { Cancelled: true, CancelledCount: 1, PendingCount: 1, SuccessfulCount: 0 }
            && model.Queue[1].Status == JobStatus.Pending,
            "A stopped batch did not preserve the unstarted job or reported a reward for cancellation.");
        engine.Hold = false;
        var afterBusy = await model.ImportForActionAsync(FileProcessingAction.Video, busy.AttemptedPaths);
        Program.Assert(afterBusy.AcceptedPaths.Single() == staged && !model.IsProcessing && engine.ExecutionCount == 1,
            "Continuing the staged import auto-started a task or failed to use the original paths.");

        model = CreateModel(run, new BridgeEngine(), "partial");
        var failed = Touch(directory, "failed.mp4");
        await model.ImportForActionAsync(FileProcessingAction.Video, [first, failed, second]);
        ProcessingBatchSummary? partial = null;
        model.ProcessingBatchFinished += (_, summary) => partial = summary;
        await model.RunQueueAsync();
        var wallet = new PetStateStore(Path.Combine(directory, "wallet"));
        Program.Assert(partial is { CompletedCount: 2, FailedCount: 1, SuccessfulCount: 2, PendingCount: 0 }
            && wallet.SettleBatch(partial.BatchId, partial.SuccessfulRequestIds).CoinsChanged == 10,
            "A partial-success batch did not distinguish failed tasks and pay only successful requests.");
        run.Evidence["petBusyAndOutcomeFeed"] = new { busyImportDoesNotChangeParameters = true,
            stagedPathsRemainUserConfirmed = true, cancelledAndPendingCounts = true, partialReward = 10 };
    }

    private static async Task RecoveryRetryAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var directory = FixtureDirectory(run, "recovery");
        var source = Touch(directory, "recover.zip");
        var engine = new BridgeEngine { ExhaustRecovery = true };
        var model = CreateModel(run, engine, "recovery-model");
        await model.ImportForActionAsync(FileProcessingAction.RecoverPassword, [source]);
        var summaries = new List<ProcessingBatchSummary>();
        var snapshots = new List<ProcessingTaskSnapshot>();
        model.ProcessingBatchFinished += (_, summary) => summaries.Add(summary);
        model.ProcessingTaskChanged += (_, snapshot) => snapshots.Add(snapshot);
        var wallet = new PetStateStore(Path.Combine(directory, "wallet"));
        await model.RunQueueAsync();
        var exhausted = summaries.Single();
        Program.Assert(exhausted.ExhaustedCount == 1 && exhausted.SuccessfulCount == 0 && exhausted.FailedCount == 0
            && wallet.SettleBatch(exhausted.BatchId, exhausted.SuccessfulRequestIds).CoinsChanged == 0,
            "Exhausting password candidates was treated as successful recovery or paid coins.");
        engine.ExhaustRecovery = false;
        model.TryRecoveryWithCpu(model.RecoveryQueue.Single());
        await model.RunQueueAsync();
        var found = summaries.Last();
        Program.Assert(found.SuccessfulCount == 1 && found.ExhaustedCount == 0
            && found.Tasks.Single().OperationId != exhausted.Tasks.Single().OperationId
            && wallet.SettleBatch(found.BatchId, found.SuccessfulRequestIds).CoinsChanged == 5,
            "Retrying an exhausted recovery did not produce a distinct actual request and one success reward.");
        // A user may explicitly retry the same completed row; it is a new engine execution.
        model.RecoveryQueue.Single().Reset();
        await model.RunQueueAsync();
        var repeatedRequest = summaries.Last();
        Program.Assert(repeatedRequest.SuccessfulRequestIds.Single() != found.SuccessfulRequestIds.Single()
            && wallet.SettleBatch(repeatedRequest.BatchId, repeatedRequest.SuccessfulRequestIds).CoinsChanged == 5,
            "Distinct actual recovery executions reused a globally rewarded row identifier.");
        Program.Assert(!JsonSerializer.Serialize(summaries).Contains(BridgeEngine.Secret, StringComparison.Ordinal)
            && !JsonSerializer.Serialize(snapshots).Contains(BridgeEngine.Secret, StringComparison.Ordinal),
            "Recovery progress or completion exposed a password/candidate in pet presentation receipts.");
        run.Evidence["petRecoveryOutcomeFeed"] = new { exhaustionReward = 0, retryFoundReward = 5,
            requestIdentityPerActualExecution = true, passwordsAndCandidatesExcluded = true };
    }

    private static MainViewModel CreateModel(AcceptanceRun run, BridgeEngine engine, string name)
    {
        var directory = FixtureDirectory(run, name);
        return new MainViewModel(engine, PresetCatalog.Load(Path.Combine(run.Root, "shared", "presets.json")),
            new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(directory, "settings")), new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(directory, "logs")),
            engine, engine, engine) { OutputDirectory = Path.Combine(directory, "outputs") };
    }

    private static string FixtureDirectory(AcceptanceRun run, string name)
    {
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-file-bridge", name);
        Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        return directory;
    }

    private static string Touch(string directory, string name)
    {
        var path = Path.Combine(directory, name);
        File.WriteAllText(path, "Deterministic bridge fixture; no actual codec decoding required.");
        return path;
    }

    private static async Task WaitAsync(Func<bool> predicate, string message)
    {
        var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        while (!predicate()) { Program.Assert(clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds < 5, message); await Task.Delay(10); }
    }

    private sealed class BridgeEngine : IConversionEngine, IArchiveEngine, IPasswordRecoveryEngine, IImageObfuscationEngine
    {
        public const string Secret = "432143214321";
        public bool FailProbe { get; set; }
        public bool Hold { get; set; }
        public bool ExerciseProgress { get; set; }
        public bool ExhaustRecovery { get; set; }
        public int ExecutionCount { get; private set; }

        public Task<MediaInfo> ProbeAsync(string path, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            if (FailProbe) throw new ConversionEngineException(new("fixture.probe", "检查失败：文件目前无法解码。", "请重新选择文件。"));
            var mode = ImportService.Classify(path);
            return Task.FromResult(new MediaInfo(mode != ConversionMode.Audio, mode != ConversionMode.Image, 1,
                mode switch { ConversionMode.Audio => "mp3", ConversionMode.Image => "png", _ => "mp4" },
                mode == ConversionMode.Image ? "png" : "h264", mode == ConversionMode.Audio ? "mp3" : "aac",
                1024, 100, 100, 25));
        }

        public async Task<ConversionResult> ConvertAsync(ConversionRequest request, IProgress<ConversionProgress>? progress = null,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            ExecutionCount++;
            if (Hold) await Task.Delay(Timeout.Infinite, cancellationToken);
            if (Path.GetFileName(request.SourcePath).StartsWith("failed", StringComparison.Ordinal))
                return new(JobStatus.Failed, null, new("fixture.failure", "这个文件遇到了问题。", "请重试。"));
            if (ExerciseProgress)
            {
                progress?.Report(new(null, TimeSpan.Zero));
                await Task.Delay(280, cancellationToken);
                for (var index = 0; index < 100; index++) progress?.Report(new(100, TimeSpan.Zero, 1));
                await Task.Delay(280, cancellationToken);
                progress?.Report(new(12, TimeSpan.Zero, 2, 3));
                await Task.Delay(280, cancellationToken);
                progress?.Report(new(double.NaN, TimeSpan.Zero, 3, 3));
                await Task.Delay(280, cancellationToken);
            }
            return new(JobStatus.Completed, CommitOutput(request.OutputDirectory, request.Id, false), null);
        }

        Task<ArchiveInspection> IArchiveEngine.InspectAsync(string sourcePath, string? password, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
            => Task.FromResult(new ArchiveInspection(sourcePath, "zip", 1024, 1,
                !Path.GetFileName(sourcePath).StartsWith("plain", StringComparison.Ordinal)));

        public Task<ArchiveResult> ExecuteAsync(ArchiveRequest request, IProgress<ArchiveProgress>? progress = null,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            ExecutionCount++;
            var isDirectory = request is ExtractArchiveRequest;
            return Task.FromResult(new ArchiveResult(JobStatus.Completed, CommitOutput(request.OutputDirectory, request.Id, isDirectory),
                isDirectory ? ArchiveOutputKind.Directory : ArchiveOutputKind.File));
        }

        public async Task<RecoveryResult> RecoverAsync(RecoveryRequest request, IProgress<RecoveryProgress>? progress = null,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            ExecutionCount++;
            progress?.Report(new(0, 0, Secret));
            await Task.Delay(20, cancellationToken);
            return ExhaustRecovery ? new(RecoveryStatus.Exhausted, CompletedCandidates: 10, TotalCandidates: 10)
                : new(RecoveryStatus.Found, Secret, 1, 10);
        }

        Task<ImageObfuscationInfo> IImageObfuscationEngine.InspectAsync(string path, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
            => Task.FromResult(new ImageObfuscationInfo(100, 100, 8, false, Path.GetFileName(path).StartsWith("marked", StringComparison.Ordinal)));

        public Task<ImageObfuscationResult> ProcessAsync(ImageObfuscationRequest request, IProgress<ImageObfuscationProgress>? progress = null,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            ExecutionCount++;
            return Task.FromResult(new ImageObfuscationResult(JobStatus.Completed, CommitOutput(request.OutputDirectory, request.Id, false), null,
                new(1024, 10, 100, 100, request.Options.Mode, request.Options.Iterations, true)));
        }

        private static string CommitOutput(string directory, Guid id, bool isDirectory)
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
            var path = Path.Combine(directory, id.ToString("N") + (isDirectory ? "" : ".fixture"));
            if (isDirectory) Directory.CreateDirectory(path);
            else File.WriteAllText(path, "Committed deterministic fixture.");
            return path;
        }
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/GifAcceptance.cs ---
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Globalization;
using System.IO;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Controls.Primitives;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

/// <summary>Real video encoding, independent GIF block checks and a real STA GIF page.</summary>
internal static class GifAcceptance
{
    private static readonly (GifQualityLevel Quality, int LongestEdge, int Fps, int Colors)[] Tiers =
    [
        (GifQualityLevel.Original, 1440, 60, 256), (GifQualityLevel.High, 1280, 30, 256),
        (GifQualityLevel.Medium, 800, 20, 128), (GifQualityLevel.Low, 480, 12, 64), (GifQualityLevel.VeryLow, 240, 6, 32)
    ];

    public static async Task RunAsync(AcceptanceRun run, IConversionEngine engine, IReadOnlyList<FormatPreset> presets)
    {
        var failuresBefore = run.Failures;
        var fixture = new Fixtures(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "GIF 素材 中文 空格"));
        var ready = false;
        await run.CaseAsync("gif-fixture-generation", async () => { await CreateFixturesAsync(run, fixture); ready = true; });
        if (!ready) return;
        var preset = presets.Single(value => value.Id == "video-gif");
        Program.Assert(preset.Mode == ConversionMode.Gif && preset.Extension == "gif", "The video-gif preset is not dedicated to the GIF operation.");

        foreach (var tier in Tiers)
        {
            var name = "gif-tier-" + tier.Quality.ToString().ToLowerInvariant();
            await run.CaseAsync(name, async () =>
            {
                var result = await ConvertAsync(run, engine, fixture.Video, preset, tier.Quality, name);
                var actual = await CheckOutputAsync(run, engine, fixture.Video, result);
                Program.Assert(Math.Max(actual.Width, actual.Height) == tier.LongestEdge, "Tier did not enforce its longest-edge limit.");
                Program.Assert(Math.Abs((double)actual.Width / actual.Height - 16d / 9) < .005, "Tier changed the video aspect ratio.");
                Program.Assert(actual.MaximumPaletteColors <= tier.Colors + 1, $"Tier palette exceeded {tier.Colors} colors plus a possible transparent optimization color.");
                var original = await ProbeVideoAsync(run, fixture.Video);
                Program.Assert(tier.Quality == GifQualityLevel.Original ? actual.FrameCount == original.FrameCount
                    : actual.FrameCount > 0 && actual.FrameCount < original.FrameCount && actual.FrameCount <= Math.Ceiling(original.DurationSeconds * tier.Fps) + 1,
                    "Tier dropped original frames or failed to limit its output frame rate.");
                run.Evidence[name] = new { result.OutputPath, result.GifStatistics, original, actual, tier.LongestEdge, tier.Fps, tier.Colors,
                    method = "Independent GIF image/GCE/palette/NETSCAPE block parsing, selected source video stream duration and full FFmpeg decode." };
            });
        }

        foreach (var source in new[] { fixture.Small, fixture.SingleFrame, fixture.VariableFrameRate, fixture.LongerAudio, fixture.Rotated, fixture.CoverVideo, fixture.NonIntegralFrameRate, fixture.NonSquarePixels })
        {
            var name = "gif-media-" + Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(source);
            await run.CaseAsync(name, async () =>
            {
                var quality = source == fixture.VariableFrameRate ? GifQualityLevel.Original : GifQualityLevel.Medium;
                var result = await ConvertAsync(run, engine, source, preset, quality, name);
                var actual = await CheckOutputAsync(run, engine, source, result);
                var original = await ProbeVideoAsync(run, source);
                if (source == fixture.NonSquarePixels)
                    Program.Assert(Math.Max(actual.Width, actual.Height) <= 800 && Math.Abs((double)actual.Width / actual.Height - 32d / 9) < .02,
                        "GIF did not convert non-square sample pixels to the source display aspect ratio.");
                else if (source == fixture.Rotated)
                    Program.Assert(actual.Width == 180 && actual.Height == 320, "GIF did not preserve the phone-style display rotation.");
                else Program.Assert(actual.Width == original.Width && actual.Height == original.Height, "A small input was enlarged.");
                if (source == fixture.Small || source == fixture.SingleFrame || source == fixture.VariableFrameRate)
                    Program.Assert(actual.FrameCount == original.FrameCount, "Low-FPS, single-frame or original VFR input was duplicated or dropped.");
                if (source == fixture.VariableFrameRate)
                    Program.Assert(actual.FrameDelaysMilliseconds.Distinct().Count() > 1, "Original VFR timing was flattened to a constant frame rate.");
                if (source == fixture.LongerAudio)
                    Program.Assert(actual.DurationMilliseconds < 1200, "A longer audio track extended the GIF beyond its actual video stream.");
                if (source == fixture.NonIntegralFrameRate)
                    Program.Assert(actual.FrameCount is >= 19 and <= 21, "24-FPS medium input was over-thinned to 12 FPS instead of using timestamp sampling near the 20-FPS limit.");
                run.Evidence[name] = new { result.OutputPath, result.GifStatistics, original, actual };
            });
        }

        await run.CaseAsync("gif-null-options-default-medium", async () =>
        {
            var result = await engine.ConvertAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), fixture.Video, preset, Output(run, "default")));
            var actual = await CheckOutputAsync(run, engine, fixture.Video, result);
            Program.Assert(Math.Max(actual.Width, actual.Height) == 800 && actual.FrameCount <= 13 && actual.MaximumPaletteColors <= 129,
                "A caller omitting GifOptions did not receive the documented medium default.");
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("gif-import-actual-video-and-silent-support", async () =>
        {
            var importer = new ImportService(engine);
            Program.Assert((await importer.InspectAsync(fixture.Small, ConversionMode.Gif)).Accepted, "Silent video was rejected in GIF mode.");
            var cover = await importer.InspectAsync(fixture.CoverOnly, ConversionMode.Gif);
            Program.Assert(!cover.Accepted && cover.Error?.Code == "VIDEO_MISSING", "An attached cover image was accepted as an actual video.");
            var audio = await importer.InspectAsync(fixture.Audio, ConversionMode.Gif);
            Program.Assert(!audio.Accepted && audio.Error?.Code == "INPUT_TYPE", "GIF import did not keep the video extension whitelist.");
            var damaged = await importer.InspectAsync(fixture.Damaged, ConversionMode.Gif);
            Program.Assert(!damaged.Accepted && damaged.Error is not null, "Damaged video import succeeded.");
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("gif-unrepresentable-original-fails-and-high-retry-succeeds", async () =>
        {
            var output = Output(run, "timing-retry");
            var failed = await engine.ConvertAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), fixture.UltraHighFrameRate, preset, output, Gif: new(GifQualityLevel.Original)));
            Program.Assert(failed.Status == JobStatus.Failed && failed.OutputPath is null && failed.Error is not null,
                "120-FPS original silently dropped frames or changed playback instead of failing safely.");
            Program.Assert(failed.Error!.Suggestion.Contains("高", StringComparison.Ordinal), "Original timing failure did not suggest the high tier.");
            AssertNoFiles(output);
            var retry = await engine.ConvertAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), fixture.UltraHighFrameRate, preset, output, Gif: new(GifQualityLevel.High)));
            await CheckOutputAsync(run, engine, fixture.UltraHighFrameRate, retry);
            run.Evidence["gif-timing-failure-and-retry"] = new { failed.Error, retry.OutputPath, retry.GifStatistics };
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("gif-serial-queue-continues-and-output-collisions", async () =>
        {
            var output = Output(run, "serial");
            var requests = new[] { fixture.Small, fixture.Damaged, fixture.Small }
                .Select(source => new ConversionRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), source, preset, output, Gif: new(GifQualityLevel.Low))).ToArray();
            var started = new List<Guid>();
            var batch = await new SerialConversionQueue(engine).RunAsync(requests, onStarted: request => started.Add(request.Id));
            Program.Assert(started.SequenceEqual(requests.Select(value => value.Id)) && batch.CompletedCount == 2 && batch.FailedCount == 1,
                "GIF queue skipped, reordered or stopped after a damaged file.");
            var successful = batch.Results.Where(value => value.Result.Status == JobStatus.Completed).Select(value => value.Result).ToArray();
            Program.Assert(successful[0].OutputPath != successful[1].OutputPath && Directory.GetFiles(output).Length == 2, "GIF overwrote an existing result or left failed temporary files.");
            var firstHash = Program.Hash(successful[0].OutputPath!);
            foreach (var result in successful) await CheckOutputAsync(run, engine, fixture.Small, result);
            Program.Assert(Program.Hash(successful[0].OutputPath!) == firstHash, "Checking a second GIF changed its prior result.");
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("gif-cancellation-cleans-owned-files-and-encoder", async () =>
        {
            var output = Output(run, "cancel"); Directory.CreateDirectory(output);
            var unrelated = Path.Combine(output, ".cat-conversion-other-task.gif"); File.WriteAllText(unrelated, "Keep this other task file.");
            var hash = Program.Hash(unrelated); var before = ActiveEncoderIds();
            using var cancellation = new CancellationTokenSource();
            var task = engine.ConvertAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), fixture.LongVideo, preset, output, Gif: new(GifQualityLevel.Original)), null, cancellation.Token);
            await WaitForAsync(() => ActiveEncoderIds().Except(before).Any(), "GIF encoder never started.");
            cancellation.Cancel();
            var result = await task.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(20));
            Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Cancelled && result.OutputPath is null, "Cancellation committed an unfinished GIF.");
            Program.Assert(File.Exists(unrelated) && Program.Hash(unrelated) == hash && Directory.GetFiles(output, "*", SearchOption.AllDirectories).Length == 1,
                "GIF cancellation removed another task's file or left temporary output.");
            await WaitForAsync(() => !ActiveEncoderIds().Except(before).Any(), "Cancelled GIF encoder remained alive.");
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("gif-settings-v03-migration-and-invalid-quality", () => CheckSettingsAsync(run));
        if (run.NoGui) run.RecordSkipped("gif-wpf-page-queue-and-layout", "--no-gui skips the real GIF window.");
        else await run.CaseAsync("gif-wpf-page-queue-and-layout", () => RunWindowAsync(run, engine, presets, fixture));
        run.GifAcceptancePassed = run.Failures == failuresBefore && !run.NoGui;
    }

    private static async Task CreateFixturesAsync(AcceptanceRun run, Fixtures fixture)
    {
        Directory.CreateDirectory(fixture.Directory);
        async Task Video(string path, string size, string rate, string duration) => await RunFfmpegAsync(run,
            ["-f", "lavfi", "-i", $"testsrc2=size={size}:rate={rate}", "-t", duration, "-c:v", "libopenh264", "-b:v", "2M", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", path]);
        await Video(fixture.Video, "1440x810", "60", "0.6");
        await Video(fixture.Small, "160x90", "3", "1");
        await Video(fixture.NonIntegralFrameRate, "160x90", "24", "1");
        await Video(fixture.SingleFrame, "160x90", "1", "1");
        await Video(fixture.UltraHighFrameRate, "160x90", "120", "0.5");
        await Video(fixture.LongVideo, "960x540", "30", "20");
        var rotationBase = Path.Combine(fixture.Directory, "rotation-base.mp4");
        await Video(rotationBase, "320x180", "8", "1");
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-display_rotation:v:0", "90", "-i", rotationBase, "-map", "0:v:0", "-c", "copy", fixture.Rotated]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", rotationBase, "-vf", "setsar=2/1", "-c:v", "libopenh264", "-b:v", "1M", fixture.NonSquarePixels]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-f", "lavfi", "-i", "testsrc2=size=160x90:rate=10", "-t", "1.2", "-vf", "select='not(eq(mod(n,3),1))'", "-fps_mode", "vfr", "-c:v", "libopenh264", "-b:v", "1M", fixture.VariableFrameRate]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-f", "lavfi", "-i", "sine=frequency=440:sample_rate=44100", "-t", "3", "-c:a", "aac", fixture.Audio]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", fixture.Small, "-i", fixture.Audio, "-map", "0:v:0", "-map", "1:a:0", "-c", "copy", fixture.LongerAudio]);
        var cover = Path.Combine(fixture.Directory, "cover.jpg");
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-f", "lavfi", "-i", "color=c=red:size=96x64", "-frames:v", "1", cover]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", fixture.Small, "-i", cover, "-map", "0:v:0", "-map", "1:v:0", "-c:v:0", "copy", "-c:v:1", "mjpeg", "-disposition:v:1", "attached_pic", fixture.CoverVideo]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", fixture.Audio, "-i", cover, "-map", "0:a:0", "-map", "1:v:0", "-c", "copy", "-disposition:v:0", "attached_pic", fixture.CoverOnly]);
        File.WriteAllText(fixture.Damaged, "Not a media container.");
        foreach (var path in fixture.Paths) run.TrackSource(path);
    }

    private static Task<ConversionResult> ConvertAsync(AcceptanceRun run, IConversionEngine engine, string source, FormatPreset preset, GifQualityLevel quality, string name)
        => engine.ConvertAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), source, preset, Output(run, name), Gif: new(quality)));

    private static async Task<GifEvidence> CheckOutputAsync(AcceptanceRun run, IConversionEngine engine, string source, ConversionResult result)
    {
        Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Completed && result.OutputPath is not null && File.Exists(result.OutputPath),
            $"GIF conversion failed: {result.Error?.Code}: {result.Error?.Message} {result.Error?.TechnicalDetails}");
        Program.Assert(Path.GetExtension(result.OutputPath!) == ".gif", "GIF output extension is wrong.");
        var actual = ReadGif(File.ReadAllBytes(result.OutputPath!));
        Program.Assert(actual.InfiniteLoop && actual.FrameCount > 0 && actual.FrameDelaysMilliseconds.All(delay => delay > 0),
            "GIF omitted infinite looping, frames or positive raw frame delays.");
        var original = await ProbeVideoAsync(run, source);
        Program.Assert(Math.Abs(actual.DurationMilliseconds - original.DurationSeconds * 1000) <= 20.001,
            $"GIF changed one-loop duration: source video={original.DurationSeconds:0.######}s, GIF raw delays={actual.DurationMilliseconds}ms.");
        var statistics = result.GifStatistics;
        Program.Assert(statistics is not null && statistics.SourceBytes == new FileInfo(source).Length && statistics.OutputBytes == new FileInfo(result.OutputPath!).Length,
            "GIF size statistics do not match actual source and output bytes.");
        var probe = await engine.ProbeAsync(result.OutputPath!);
        Program.Assert(probe.VideoCodec == "gif" && probe.FormatName.Split(',').Contains("gif") && !probe.HasAudio, "Output is not a real silent GIF container.");
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-min_delay", "0", "-ignore_loop", "1", "-i", result.OutputPath!, "-map", "0:v:0", "-f", "null", "-"]);
        Program.Assert(Directory.GetFiles(Path.GetDirectoryName(result.OutputPath!)!, "*", SearchOption.AllDirectories).All(path => !Path.GetFileName(path).StartsWith(".cat-", StringComparison.Ordinal)),
            "Completed GIF left temporary output.");
        run.AssertSourcesUnchanged();
        return actual;
    }

    private static async Task<VideoEvidence> ProbeVideoAsync(AcceptanceRun run, string source)
    {
        var json = await RunProcessAsync(Path.Combine(run.EngineDirectory, "ffprobe.exe"),
            ["-v", "error", "-count_frames", "-show_entries", "stream=codec_type,width,height,duration,nb_read_frames:stream_disposition=attached_pic", "-of", "json", source]);
        using var document = JsonDocument.Parse(json);
        var stream = document.RootElement.GetProperty("streams").EnumerateArray().First(value => value.GetProperty("codec_type").GetString() == "video"
            && (!value.TryGetProperty("disposition", out var disposition) || !disposition.TryGetProperty("attached_pic", out var attached) || attached.GetInt32() == 0));
        return new(stream.GetProperty("width").GetInt32(), stream.GetProperty("height").GetInt32(),
            int.Parse(stream.GetProperty("nb_read_frames").GetString()!, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
            double.Parse(stream.GetProperty("duration").GetString()!, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
    }

    private static Task CheckSettingsAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var store = new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "gif-settings-migration"));
        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(store.FilePath)!);
        foreach (var quality in new[] { "", ",\"GifQuality\":\"future-quality\"", ",\"GifQuality\":999" })
        {
            File.WriteAllText(store.FilePath, "{\"OutputDirectory\":\"existing-output\",\"PresetIds\":{\"Video\":\"mp4-h264\"},\"CompressionMode\":\"TargetSize\",\"CompressionMaximumMb\":0.25,\"MotionPreference\":\"Reduced\"" + quality + "}");
            var settings = store.Load();
            Program.Assert(settings.GifQuality == GifQualityLevel.Medium && settings.OutputDirectory == "existing-output"
                && settings.PresetIds[ConversionMode.Video] == "mp4-h264" && settings.CompressionMode == CompressionMode.TargetSize
                && settings.CompressionMaximumMb == .25m && settings.MotionPreference == MotionPreference.Reduced,
                "V0.3 missing/invalid GIF setting lost old settings or failed to default to medium.");
        }
        run.Evidence["gif-settings-migration"] = new { missingSetting = true, unknownString = true, unknownNumber = true, oldSettingsRetained = true };
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }

    private static async Task RunWindowAsync(AcceptanceRun run, IConversionEngine engine, IReadOnlyList<FormatPreset> presets, Fixtures fixture)
    {
        var settings = new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "gif-ui-settings"));
        var model = new MainViewModel(engine, presets, settings, new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "gif-ui-logs")))
        { OutputDirectory = Output(run, "ui"), MotionPreference = MotionPreference.Reduced };
        var window = new MainWindow(model, () => true) { ShowActivated = false };
        run.StopActiveWindow = async () => { model.Cancel(); model.CancelImport(); await Task.WhenAll(model.ActiveRun, model.ActiveImport).WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(20)); };
        try
        {
            window.Show(); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
            await model.AddFilesAsync([fixture.Small]); var conversion = model.VisibleQueue.Single();
            model.SetCompressionPage(); await model.AddFilesAsync([fixture.Small]); var compression = model.VisibleQueue.Single();
            model.SetMode(ConversionMode.Gif); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(model.IsGifPage && model.VisibleQueue.Count == 0 && model.Queue.Count == 2 && model.SelectedGifQuality == GifQualityLevel.Medium,
                "GIF page leaked another operation's rows or failed its initial medium default.");
            var start = Control<Button>(window, "StartButton");
            Program.Assert(!start.IsEnabled && model.StartButtonText == "开始生成 GIF" && model.AvailablePresets.Count == 1 && model.SelectedPreset?.Id == "video-gif", "GIF page is not fixed to GIF or its Start binding is incorrect.");
            foreach (var (name, quality) in new[] { ("GifOriginal", GifQualityLevel.Original), ("GifHigh", GifQualityLevel.High), ("GifMedium", GifQualityLevel.Medium), ("GifLow", GifQualityLevel.Low), ("GifVeryLow", GifQualityLevel.VeryLow) })
            {
                var radio = Control<RadioButton>(window, name); radio.RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(ButtonBase.ClickEvent, radio)); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
                Program.Assert(model.SelectedGifQuality == quality && radio.IsChecked == true, "A GIF quality radio did not update its model and checked binding.");
            }
            model.SelectedGifQuality = GifQualityLevel.Medium;
            await model.AddFilesAsync([fixture.Small, fixture.VariableFrameRate, fixture.Small]);
            Program.Assert(model.VisibleQueue.Count == 2 && model.Queue.Count == 4 && model.VisibleQueue.All(row => row.IsGif && !row.IsCompression), "Same video in three operations was conflated, or duplicate GIF import created extra rows.");
            var small = model.VisibleQueue.Single(row => row.SourcePath == fixture.Small);
            var variable = model.VisibleQueue.Single(row => row.SourcePath == fixture.VariableFrameRate);
            model.SelectedJobs = new[] { small }; model.SelectedGifQuality = GifQualityLevel.Low;
            Program.Assert(small.Gif?.Quality == GifQualityLevel.Low && variable.Gif?.Quality == GifQualityLevel.Medium && conversion.Status == JobStatus.Pending && compression.Status == JobStatus.Pending,
                "Selected GIF tier change touched another row or operation.");
            await AssertRowSummaryVisibleAsync(window, small);
            await AssertRowSummaryVisibleAsync(window, variable);
            model.SelectedJobs = Array.Empty<ConversionJobViewModel>(); model.SelectedGifQuality = GifQualityLevel.High;
            Program.Assert(model.VisibleQueue.All(row => row.Gif?.Quality == GifQualityLevel.High), "Unselected tier change did not apply to all editable GIF rows.");
            model.SelectedJobs = new[] { small }; model.SelectedGifQuality = GifQualityLevel.Low;
            model.SelectedJobs = new[] { variable }; model.SelectedGifQuality = GifQualityLevel.High;
            model.SelectedJobs = new[] { small };
            var high = Control<RadioButton>(window, "GifHigh"); high.RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(ButtonBase.ClickEvent, high));
            Program.Assert(small.Gif?.Quality == GifQualityLevel.High, "Re-clicking the current default tier did not apply it to a selected row with another tier.");
            await AssertRowSummaryVisibleAsync(window, small);
            model.SelectedJobs = Array.Empty<ConversionJobViewModel>();
            foreach (var (width, height) in new[] { (1200d, 820d), (620d, 480d), (960d, 540d) })
            {
                window.Width = width; window.Height = height; await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
                foreach (var name in new[] { "StartButton", "AddFilesSide", "GifOptionsScroll", "NavGif" }) Program.AssertControlInside(window, name);
                Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, $"gif-queued-{width:0}x{height:0}");
            }
            window.Width = 1200; window.Height = 820; await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
            var reopen = new MainViewModel(engine, presets, settings, new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "gif-reopen-logs")));
            reopen.SetMode(ConversionMode.Gif);
            Program.Assert(reopen.SelectedGifQuality == GifQualityLevel.High && reopen.OutputDirectory == model.OutputDirectory, "GIF quality or output directory was not remembered.");
            var active = model.RunQueueAsync(); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(model.IsProcessing && !model.CanEdit && !Control<RadioButton>(window, "GifLow").IsEnabled && !Control<Button>(window, "AddFilesSide").IsEnabled, "GIF processing did not disable imports and quality controls.");
            var gifRows = model.VisibleQueue.ToArray();
            model.SetMode(ConversionMode.Audio);
            Program.Assert(model.CanNavigate && model.IsConversionPage && model.Mode == ConversionMode.Audio, "Busy GIF processing did not allow read-only browsing.");
            model.SetCompressionPage(); model.SelectedGifQuality = GifQualityLevel.Original;
            await model.AddFilesAsync([fixture.SingleFrame]);
            Program.Assert(model.IsCompressionPage && !model.CanEdit && model.SelectedGifQuality == GifQualityLevel.High &&
                gifRows.All(row => row.Gif?.Quality == GifQualityLevel.High) && model.Queue.Count == 4 &&
                model.CurrentProcessingTask?.Action == FileProcessingAction.Gif,
                "Busy browsing changed the active GIF request, quality or queues.");
            model.SetGifPage();
            Program.Assert(model.VisibleQueue.SequenceEqual(gifRows), "Returning while busy lost the GIF source queue.");
            await active.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(120)); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(window.IsVisible && model.VisibleQueue.All(row => row.Status == JobStatus.Completed && row.OutputBytes > 0 && row.ResultSizeText.Contains("→", StringComparison.Ordinal)), "GIF completion lost output sizes, rows or its window.");
            Program.Assert(conversion.Status == JobStatus.Pending && compression.Status == JobStatus.Pending, "GIF batch executed a hidden conversion or compression row.");
            var completedTiers = model.VisibleQueue.Select(row => row.Gif?.Quality).ToArray();
            model.SelectedGifQuality = GifQualityLevel.Original;
            Program.Assert(model.VisibleQueue.Select(row => row.Gif?.Quality).SequenceEqual(completedTiers), "Changing the default tier rewrote completed history.");
            await model.AddFilesAsync([fixture.UltraHighFrameRate]); var retry = model.VisibleQueue.Single(row => row.SourcePath == fixture.UltraHighFrameRate);
            Program.Assert(retry.Gif?.Quality == GifQualityLevel.Original, "Future imports did not use the new default tier.");
            await model.RunQueueAsync().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(60));
            Program.Assert(retry.Status == JobStatus.Failed && retry.Error is not null && retry.OutputPath is null, "Original timing failure was not exposed in its GIF row.");
            model.SelectedJobs = new[] { retry }; model.SelectedGifQuality = GifQualityLevel.High; model.RetryFailed();
            Program.Assert(retry.Status == JobStatus.Pending && retry.Error is null && retry.Progress == 0 && retry.GifStatistics is null && retry.Gif?.Quality == GifQualityLevel.High,
                "Retry did not clear result state while retaining the updated tier.");
            await model.RunQueueAsync().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(60)); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(retry.Status == JobStatus.Completed && retry.OutputBytes > 0, "GIF retry did not use the corrected tier.");
            await AssertRowSummaryVisibleAsync(window, retry);
            Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, "gif-completed-size-statistics");
            run.Evidence["gif-wpf-page-and-isolation"] = new { allFiveRadioBindings = true, selectedAndAllEditableScope = true,
                threeIndependentQueues = true, busyMutationBlocked = true, completedHistoryRetained = true, qualityRemembered = true,
                failedOriginalRetriedAsHigh = true, actualRowQualityTextVisibleAndUpdated = true,
                rows = model.VisibleQueue.Select(row => new { row.SourcePath, row.OutputPath, row.TargetSummary, row.ResultSizeText, row.OutputBytes }).ToArray() };
            model.RemoveJobs([conversion, compression]);
            Program.Assert(model.Queue.Contains(conversion) && model.Queue.Contains(compression), "GIF removal operated on hidden other-operation rows.");
            model.Clear(); Program.Assert(model.Queue.Count == 2 && model.VisibleQueue.Count == 0, "Clearing GIF rows cleared another operation.");
            model.SetMode(ConversionMode.Video); Program.Assert(model.VisibleQueue.Count == 1 && ReferenceEquals(model.VisibleQueue[0], conversion), "Conversion queue did not survive GIF operations.");
            model.SetCompressionPage(); Program.Assert(model.VisibleQueue.Count == 1 && ReferenceEquals(model.VisibleQueue[0], compression), "Compression queue did not survive GIF operations.");
        }
        finally
        {
            model.Cancel(); model.CancelImport();
            try { await Task.WhenAll(model.ActiveRun, model.ActiveImport).WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(20)); }
            finally { window.Close(); run.StopActiveWindow = null; MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System); }
        }
    }

    private static GifEvidence ReadGif(byte[] data)
    {
        Program.Assert(data.Length > 13 && Encoding.ASCII.GetString(data, 0, 6) is "GIF87a" or "GIF89a", "Invalid GIF signature.");
        var width = BitConverter.ToUInt16(data, 6); var height = BitConverter.ToUInt16(data, 8); var position = 13;
        var globalColors = 0; var maximumColors = 0; var frames = 0; var loop = false; var pendingDelay = -1; var delays = new List<int>();
        int Palette(int packed)
        {
            var entries = 1 << ((packed & 7) + 1); var colors = new HashSet<int>();
            Program.Assert(position + entries * 3 <= data.Length, "Truncated GIF palette.");
            for (var index = 0; index < entries; index++) { colors.Add(data[position] << 16 | data[position + 1] << 8 | data[position + 2]); position += 3; }
            return colors.Count;
        }
        if ((data[10] & 128) != 0) globalColors = Palette(data[10]);
        byte[] Blocks()
        {
            var bytes = new List<byte>();
            while (position < data.Length)
            {
                var size = data[position++]; if (size == 0) return bytes.ToArray();
                Program.Assert(position + size <= data.Length, "Truncated GIF subblock.");
                bytes.AddRange(data.AsSpan(position, size).ToArray()); position += size;
            }
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Unterminated GIF subblock.");
        }
        while (position < data.Length)
        {
            var marker = data[position++];
            if (marker == 0x3B)
            {
                Program.Assert(position == data.Length && pendingDelay == -1, "GIF has trailing data or an orphan frame delay.");
                return new(width, height, frames, delays.Sum(value => (long)value), loop, maximumColors, delays.ToArray());
            }
            if (marker == 0x21)
            {
                Program.Assert(position < data.Length, "Truncated GIF extension."); var label = data[position++]; var extension = Blocks();
                if (label == 0xF9) { Program.Assert(extension.Length == 4 && pendingDelay == -1, "Invalid/duplicated GIF frame control."); pendingDelay = BitConverter.ToUInt16(extension, 1) * 10; }
                if (label == 0xFF && extension.Length >= 14 && Encoding.ASCII.GetString(extension, 0, 11) == "NETSCAPE2.0")
                    loop = extension[11] == 1 && BitConverter.ToUInt16(extension, 12) == 0;
            }
            else if (marker == 0x2C)
            {
                Program.Assert(position + 9 <= data.Length && pendingDelay >= 0, "Truncated GIF image or missing frame control.");
                var packed = data[position + 8]; position += 9;
                var colors = (packed & 128) != 0 ? Palette(packed) : globalColors;
                maximumColors = Math.Max(maximumColors, colors);
                Program.Assert(position < data.Length && colors > 0, "GIF image has no palette or LZW code size."); position++; Blocks();
                frames++; delays.Add(pendingDelay); pendingDelay = -1;
            }
            else throw new InvalidOperationException("Unexpected GIF block marker.");
        }
        throw new InvalidOperationException("GIF trailer is missing.");
    }

    private static T Control<T>(MainWindow window, string name) where T : FrameworkElement
        => window.FindName(name) as T ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing GIF UI control: " + name);
    private static async Task AssertRowSummaryVisibleAsync(MainWindow window, ConversionJobViewModel row)
    {
        var grid = Control<DataGrid>(window, "QueueGrid");
        grid.ScrollIntoView(row); await FlushLayoutAsync(window);
        var summary = Descendants<TextBlock>(grid).SingleOrDefault(text => ReferenceEquals(text.DataContext, row) && text.Text == row.TargetSummary);
        Program.Assert(summary is not null && summary.IsVisible && summary.ActualWidth > 0 && summary.ActualHeight > 0,
            "The actual GIF row does not visibly show its current quality: " + row.TargetSummary);
    }
    private static IEnumerable<T> Descendants<T>(DependencyObject parent) where T : DependencyObject
    {
        for (var index = 0; index < VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(parent); index++)
        {
            var child = VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(parent, index);
            if (child is T match) yield return match;
            foreach (var descendant in Descendants<T>(child)) yield return descendant;
        }
    }
    private static async Task FlushLayoutAsync(MainWindow window)
    { await window.Dispatcher.InvokeAsync(window.UpdateLayout, DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle); await Task.Delay(40); window.UpdateLayout(); }
    private static string Output(AcceptanceRun run, string name) => Path.Combine(run.Directory, "gif-outputs", name);
    private static void AssertNoFiles(string directory) => Program.Assert(!Directory.Exists(directory) || Directory.GetFiles(directory, "*", SearchOption.AllDirectories).Length == 0, "Failed GIF left final or temporary output.");
    private static HashSet<int> ActiveEncoderIds()
    { var result = new HashSet<int>(); foreach (var process in Process.GetProcessesByName("ffmpeg")) { using (process) result.Add(process.Id); } return result; }
    private static async Task WaitForAsync(Func<bool> condition, string message)
    { var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew(); while (!condition()) { if (clock.Elapsed > TimeSpan.FromSeconds(15)) throw new InvalidOperationException(message); await Task.Delay(30); } }
    private static Task<string> RunFfmpegAsync(AcceptanceRun run, string[] arguments) => RunProcessAsync(Path.Combine(run.EngineDirectory, "ffmpeg.exe"),
        ["-hide_banner", "-nostdin", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", .. arguments]);
    private static async Task<string> RunProcessAsync(string executable, string[] arguments)
    {
        var start = new ProcessStartInfo(executable) { UseShellExecute = false, CreateNoWindow = true, RedirectStandardError = true, RedirectStandardOutput = true };
        foreach (var argument in arguments) start.ArgumentList.Add(argument);
        using var process = Process.Start(start) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("GIF fixture/probe process did not start.");
        var errors = process.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync(); var output = process.StandardOutput.ReadToEndAsync();
        try { await process.WaitForExitAsync().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(90)); }
        catch { if (!process.HasExited) process.Kill(true); await process.WaitForExitAsync(); throw; }
        Program.Assert(process.ExitCode == 0, "GIF fixture/probe/decode failed: " + await errors); return await output;
    }

    private sealed record VideoEvidence(int Width, int Height, int FrameCount, double DurationSeconds);
    private sealed record GifEvidence(int Width, int Height, int FrameCount, long DurationMilliseconds, bool InfiniteLoop,
        int MaximumPaletteColors, IReadOnlyList<int> FrameDelaysMilliseconds);
    private sealed record Fixtures(string Directory)
    {
        public string Video => Path.Combine(Directory, "五档 横屏60帧.mp4");
        public string Small => Path.Combine(Directory, "小尺寸 低帧率.mp4");
        public string NonIntegralFrameRate => Path.Combine(Directory, "24帧 中档时间抽样.mp4");
        public string NonSquarePixels => Path.Combine(Directory, "非方形像素 视频.mp4");
        public string SingleFrame => Path.Combine(Directory, "单帧 视频.mp4");
        public string VariableFrameRate => Path.Combine(Directory, "可变帧率 视频.mp4");
        public string UltraHighFrameRate => Path.Combine(Directory, "120帧 安全失败.mp4");
        public string LongVideo => Path.Combine(Directory, "长视频 取消.mp4");
        public string Rotated => Path.Combine(Directory, "竖屏 旋转视频.mp4");
        public string LongerAudio => Path.Combine(Directory, "长音轨 短视频.mp4");
        public string CoverVideo => Path.Combine(Directory, "真视频 加封面.mp4");
        public string CoverOnly => Path.Combine(Directory, "仅音频和封面.mp4");
        public string Audio => Path.Combine(Directory, "长音轨.m4a");
        public string Damaged => Path.Combine(Directory, "损坏 视频.mp4");
        public IEnumerable<string> Paths => [Video, Small, NonIntegralFrameRate, NonSquarePixels, SingleFrame, VariableFrameRate, UltraHighFrameRate, LongVideo, Rotated, LongerAudio, CoverVideo, CoverOnly, Audio, Damaged];
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/HomeCompanionAcceptance.cs ---
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Automation;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Controls.Primitives;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Themes;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

/// <summary>Exercises the production home scene against the same controller, account and sprite player as the desktop pet.</summary>
internal static class HomeCompanionAcceptance
{
    public static async Task RunAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        await run.CaseAsync("home-companion-local-time-greeting-pools-and-random-selection", () => GreetingsAsync(run));
        if (run.NoGui)
        {
            run.RecordSkipped("home-companion-production-wpf", "--no-gui skips the actual home companion, shared-controller and window lifecycle checks.");
            return;
        }
        var theme = ThemeManager.Preference;
        try
        {
            ThemeManager.EnsureInitialized();
            await run.CaseAsync("home-companion-single-controller-desktop-off-greeting-once-and-shared-states", () => SharedStatesAsync(run));
            await RunPettingAsync(run);
            await run.CaseAsync("home-companion-shared-wallet-accessories-and-single-work-clock", () => EconomyAsync(run));
            await run.CaseAsync("home-companion-processing-state-and-startup-does-not-interrupt-work", () => ProcessingAsync(run));
            await run.CaseAsync("home-companion-light-dark-layout-navigation-hide-minimize-and-disposal", () => LifecycleAsync(run));
        }
        finally
        {
            ThemeManager.SetPreference(theme);
            MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System);
        }
    }

    public static Task RunPettingAsync(AcceptanceRun run) => run.CaseAsync("pet-repeated-head-pats-replay-random-dialogue-and-shared-clock", async () =>
    {
        var language = Locale.Language;
        await using var scene = await Scene.OpenAsync(run, "repeated-petting");
        var controller = scene.Controller;
        var home = RequireHome(scene.Window, controller);
        controller.ChangeSettings(settings => { settings.Enabled = true; settings.MotionMode = PetMotionMode.Gentle; });
        await FlushAsync(scene.Window);
        var desktop = controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView;
        var completedDesktop = 0;
        var completedHome = 0;
        desktop.AnimationCompleted += () => completedDesktop++;
        home.SpriteView.AnimationCompleted += () => completedHome++;
        var messages = new HashSet<string>();
        string? previous = null;

        async Task WaitFor(Func<bool> condition, string failure)
        {
            var elapsed = Stopwatch.StartNew();
            while (!condition() && elapsed.Elapsed < TimeSpan.FromSeconds(4)) await Task.Delay(20);
            Program.Assert(condition(), failure);
        }
        void PetAgain()
        {
            controller.Interact("pet-head");
            Program.Assert(controller.CurrentAnimation == "happy" && home.SpriteView.Animation == "happy"
                && desktop.RenderedFrameIndex == 0 && home.SpriteView.RenderedFrameIndex == 0,
                "A new head pat did not replay from the first frame in both views.");
            Program.Assert(controller.LatestMessage != previous, "Consecutive head pats repeated the same reply.");
            previous = controller.LatestMessage;
            messages.Add(previous);
        }
        try
        {
            // Reproduce the original bug: the same non-looping pose has finished,
            // but the controller still requests happy during its transient window.
            for (var repeat = 0; repeat < 3; repeat++)
            {
                PetAgain();
                var expected = repeat + 1;
                await WaitFor(() => completedDesktop == expected && completedHome == expected,
                    "Repeated head petting never completed another real animation in both views.");
                var lastFrame = desktop.RenderedFrameIndex;
                RefreshController(controller);
                Program.Assert(desktop.RenderedFrameIndex == lastFrame && desktop.ActiveFrameTimerCount == 0,
                    "Routine state synchronization restarted a completed gesture.");
            }
            PetAgain();
            await WaitFor(() => desktop.RenderedFrameIndex > 0 && home.SpriteView.RenderedFrameIndex > 0,
                "Replayed petting stayed on its first frame.");
            PetAgain(); // An explicit click during playback also starts a fresh gesture.
            await WaitFor(() => desktop.RenderedFrameIndex > 0 && home.SpriteView.RenderedFrameIndex > 0,
                "A mid-gesture head pat froze playback.");
            var frame = desktop.RenderedFrameIndex;
            RefreshController(controller);
            Program.Assert(desktop.RenderedFrameIndex == frame, "Heartbeat reset an in-progress head pat.");

            controller.Account.StartWork();
            var before = controller.Account.Snapshot.WorkElapsed;
            PetAgain();
            await Task.Delay(250);
            controller.Account.Tick();
            Program.Assert(controller.Account.Snapshot.WorkRunning && controller.Account.Snapshot.WorkElapsed > before
                && controller.CurrentAnimation == "happy", "Petting interrupted the shared work account.");

            foreach (var selectedLanguage in new[] { "zh-CN", "en-US" })
            {
                Locale.Language = selectedLanguage;
                for (var sample = 0; sample < 12; sample++)
                {
                    PetAgain();
                    await FlushAsync(scene.Window);
                    var translated = Locale.T(controller.LatestMessage);
                    Program.Assert(home.DisplayedMessage == translated
                        && Descendants<TextBlock>(DesktopMessageWindow(controller)).Any(block => block.Text == translated),
                        "A head-pat reply was not localized in both visible presentations.");
                    Program.Assert(Locale.Catalog.ContainsKey(controller.LatestMessage), "A head-pat reply lacks an English translation.");
                }
            }
            controller.ChangeSettings(settings => settings.MotionMode = PetMotionMode.Reduced);
            PetAgain();
            Program.Assert(!desktop.HasActiveAnimation && !home.SpriteView.HasActiveAnimation,
                "Head-pat replay overrode reduced motion.");
            controller.HideTemporarily();
            controller.ChangeSettings(settings => settings.MotionMode = PetMotionMode.Gentle);
            PetAgain();
            Program.Assert(!desktop.HasActiveAnimation && !desktop.HasTextureReference && home.SpriteView.HasActiveAnimation,
                "Petting restarted clocks or textures on the hidden desktop view.");
            run.Evidence["repeatedPetting"] = new { completedReplays = 3, midGestureReplay = true,
                synchronizedHomeAndDesktop = true, workContinues = true, messages,
                languages = new[] { "zh-CN", "en-US" }, noAdjacentDuplicateReplies = true,
                normalSyncDoesNotRestart = true, reducedMotionAndHiddenViewRespected = true };
        }
        finally { Locale.Language = language; }
    });

    private static Task GreetingsAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var observations = new List<object>();
        for (var hour = 0; hour < 24; hour++)
        {
            var choices = HomePetGreeting.ChoicesForHour(hour);
            Program.Assert(choices.Count >= 2 && choices.All(value => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value)) &&
                choices.Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Count() == choices.Count,
                "Each local-time greeting pool needs several distinct, readable phrases at hour " + hour + ".");
            var date = new DateTime(2026, 10, 15, hour, 30, 0, DateTimeKind.Local);
            var random = new Random(1900 + hour);
            var selected = Enumerable.Range(0, 32).Select(_ => HomePetGreeting.Choose(date, random)).ToArray();
            Program.Assert(selected.All(choices.Contains) && selected.Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Count() >= 2,
                "Startup greeting does not draw randomly from the supplied local-time pool at hour " + hour + ".");
            Program.Assert(HomePetGreeting.Choose(date, new Random(851)) == HomePetGreeting.Choose(date, new Random(851)),
                "Injected randomness is not deterministic for the greeting fixture.");
            observations.Add(new { localHour = hour, choices, selectedDistinct = selected.Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).ToArray() });
        }
        Program.Assert(!HomePetGreeting.ChoicesForHour(8).SequenceEqual(HomePetGreeting.ChoicesForHour(20)),
            "Morning and evening use the same greeting pool.");
        Program.Assert(HomePetGreeting.ChoicesForHour(8).Any(value => value.Contains("早", StringComparison.Ordinal)) &&
            HomePetGreeting.ChoicesForHour(20).Any(value => value.Contains("晚上", StringComparison.Ordinal)),
            "Morning/evening pools do not contain their corresponding local-time welcome.");
        run.Evidence["homeCompanionGreetingPools"] = new { observations,
            inputClock = "Explicit DateTimeKind.Local values; no UTC hour conversion and no system clock changes.",
            randomSamplesPerHour = 32 };
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }

    private static async Task SharedStatesAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        await using var scene = await Scene.OpenAsync(run, "shared-states");
        var (window, controller) = (scene.Window, scene.Controller);
        var home = RequireHome(window, controller);
        Program.Assert(!controller.Model.PetSettings.Enabled && !controller.DesktopWindow.IsVisible &&
            controller.ActivePreviewCount == 1 && controller.ActiveHeartbeatCount == 1,
            "The home scene requires enabling the desktop window, or created another preview/heartbeat.");
        Program.Assert(home.SpriteView.HasSpriteAtlas && home.SpriteView.AssetError is null && home.SpriteView.IsVisible &&
            home.SpriteView.HasActiveAnimation, "The home companion is a static image instead of the production animated pet.");
        Program.Assert(window.HomeGreetingConsidered && window.HomeGreetingCount == 1 &&
            !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(window.HomeGreetingText) && home.DisplayedMessage == window.HomeGreetingText,
            "The normal first-show-then-controller-attach startup did not greet exactly once.");
        AssertSharedState(controller, home, "happy", "Startup greeting did not select the shared greeting gesture.");
        var greeting = window.HomeGreetingText;
        var initialSequence = controller.Account.Snapshot.Sequence;

        var pettingButton = Descendants<Button>(home).Single(button => AutomationProperties.GetAutomationId(button) == "HomePetInteract");
        pettingButton.RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(Button.ClickEvent));
        await FlushAsync(window);
        AssertSharedState(controller, home, "happy", "Head-petting did not reach both views.");
        Program.Assert(home.DisplayedMessage == controller.LatestMessage, "Home did not display the shared interaction response.");
        Descendants<Button>(home).Single(button => Equals(button.Content, "一起玩耍")).RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(Button.ClickEvent));
        await FlushAsync(window);
        AssertSharedState(controller, home, "play", "Playing did not reach both views.");
        var interactionMessage = controller.LatestMessage;
        Program.Assert(!controller.TryStartupGreeting("不应覆盖现有互动的问候") && controller.LatestMessage == interactionMessage,
            "A second startup greeting displaced an existing shared interaction.");
        EndTransient(controller);
        RefreshController(controller);
        await FlushAsync(window);
        AssertSharedState(controller, home, "idle", "A finished shared interaction did not return home and desktop to idle.");

        // Move only the controller's existing inactivity timestamps. No duplicate pet state or fake sprite is introduced.
        SetControllerField(controller, "lastInteraction", DateTime.UtcNow.AddMinutes(-3));
        SetControllerField(controller, "nextWake", DateTime.UtcNow.AddMinutes(5));
        SetControllerField(controller, "nextIdle", DateTime.UtcNow.AddMinutes(5));
        RefreshController(controller);
        await FlushAsync(window);
        AssertSharedState(controller, home, "sleep", "The same inactivity state did not put both views to sleep.");
        controller.Interact("pet-head");
        await FlushAsync(window);
        AssertSharedState(controller, home, "happy", "An interaction failed to wake both presentations.");

        for (var cycle = 0; cycle < 6; cycle++)
        {
            var old = RequireHome(window, controller);
            window.ShowTaskCenterPage();
            await FlushAsync(window);
            AssertInactive(old, "Leaving home retained its production sprite clocks.");
            Program.Assert(controller.ActivePreviewCount == 0 && controller.ActiveHeartbeatCount == 0,
                "A hidden desktop pet plus an absent home scene retained a controller heartbeat.");
            window.ShowHomePage();
            await FlushAsync(window);
            home = RequireHome(window, controller);
            Program.Assert(controller.ActivePreviewCount == 1 && controller.ActiveHeartbeatCount == 1 &&
                window.HomeGreetingCount == 1 && window.HomeGreetingText == greeting,
                "Returning to home duplicated a preview, heartbeat or startup greeting.");
        }
        Program.Assert(controller.Account.Snapshot.Sequence == initialSequence,
            "Displaying, interacting with or revisiting the home scene unexpectedly wrote or replaced the wallet.");
        var uniqueFrames = await CountUniqueFramesAsync(home.SpriteView);
        Program.Assert(uniqueFrames >= 3, "The home scene never visibly changed across real rendered animation samples.");
        Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, "home-companion-startup-and-shared-interaction");
        run.Evidence["homeCompanionSharedStates"] = new { sharedControllerReference = true, sharedAccountReference = true,
            desktopEnabled = controller.Model.PetSettings.Enabled, firstStartupGreeting = greeting,
            window.HomeGreetingCount, controller.ActivePreviewCount, controller.ActiveHeartbeatCount,
            interactionSleepWakeSynchronized = true, navigationCycles = 6, uniqueRenderedFrames = uniqueFrames,
            simulatedInactivityOnly = true, systemAnimationOverrideUsed = false };
    }

    private static async Task EconomyAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        await using var scene = await Scene.OpenAsync(run, "shared-economy");
        var (window, controller) = (scene.Window, scene.Controller);
        var home = RequireHome(window, controller);
        controller.ChangeSettings(settings => { settings.Enabled = true; settings.MotionMode = PetMotionMode.Reduced; });
        EndTransient(controller);
        RefreshController(controller);
        await FlushAsync(window);
        Program.Assert(controller.DesktopWindow.IsVisible && controller.ActiveHeartbeatCount == 1 &&
            ReferenceEquals(home.Controller.Account, controller.Account),
            "The home and desktop presentations do not share one account and one controller timer.");

        // Fund through the existing deduplicated task settlement API, then exercise real purchase/equip paths.
        var funded = controller.Account.SettleBatch(Guid.NewGuid(), Enumerable.Range(0, 40).Select(_ => Guid.NewGuid()));
        Program.Assert(funded.Succeeded && funded.CoinsChanged == 200, "Could not fund the deterministic accessory fixture.");
        var beforeHome = FixedPoseHash(home.SpriteView);
        var beforeDesktop = FixedPoseHash(controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView);
        foreach (var accessory in new[] { PetShopItemId.Collar, PetShopItemId.StarClip })
        {
            Program.Assert(controller.Account.Buy(accessory).Succeeded && controller.Account.Equip(accessory).Succeeded,
                "The shared account failed the accessory transaction for " + accessory + ".");
        }
        await FlushAsync(window);
        var equipped = controller.Account.Snapshot;
        var afterHome = FixedPoseHash(home.SpriteView);
        var afterDesktop = FixedPoseHash(controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView);
        Program.Assert(equipped.Coins == PetStateStore.InitialCoins + 200 - 60 - 80 &&
            equipped.IsEquipped(PetShopItemId.Collar) && equipped.IsEquipped(PetShopItemId.StarClip),
            "The scene duplicated spending or lost one of the shared accessory slots.");
        Program.Assert(beforeHome != afterHome && beforeDesktop != afterDesktop,
            "Account accessory changes did not change the actual rendered cat in both presentations.");
        foreach (var slot in new[] { PetAccessorySlot.Neck, PetAccessorySlot.Head })
            Program.Assert(controller.Account.Unequip(slot).Succeeded, "A shared accessory could not be removed.");
        await FlushAsync(window);
        Program.Assert(FixedPoseHash(home.SpriteView) == beforeHome && FixedPoseHash(controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView) == beforeDesktop,
            "Removing the same shared accessories did not restore both original rendered poses.");

        Program.Assert(controller.Account.Buy(PetShopItemId.Fish).Succeeded, "The shared account could not buy food for the feeding route.");
        window.ShowPetPage("inventory");
        await FlushAsync(window);
        AssertInactive(home, "Opening the real inventory left a second home cat playing behind the page.");
        var feed = Descendants<Button>(window).FirstOrDefault(button => button.IsVisible && button.IsEnabled && Equals(button.Content, "喂食"));
        Program.Assert(feed is not null, "The real inventory has no enabled feeding action for the shared purchased fish.");
        feed!.RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(Button.ClickEvent));
        window.ShowHomePage();
        await FlushAsync(window);
        home = RequireHome(window, controller);
        AssertSharedState(controller, home, "feed", "Feeding in the real inventory did not reach the home and desktop cat.");
        Program.Assert(controller.Account.Snapshot.Quantity(PetShopItemId.Fish) == 0 && home.DisplayedMessage == controller.LatestMessage &&
            window.HomeGreetingCount == 1, "Returning from inventory duplicated food, lost the response or replayed the startup greeting.");

        controller.ChangeSettings(settings => settings.MotionMode = PetMotionMode.Gentle);
        EndTransient(controller);
        RefreshController(controller);
        var monitor = DesktopEnvironment.GetMonitors().First(item => item.Primary);
        controller.DesktopWindow.SetPosition(monitor.WorkArea.Left + 160, monitor.WorkArea.Top + 100);
        Program.Assert(controller.DesktopWindow.TryWalk(-40), "The real desktop walking state could not be exercised.");
        var walkReady = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        while (controller.CurrentAnimation != "walk" && walkReady.Elapsed < TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5)) await FlushAsync(window);
        await FlushAsync(window);
        AssertSharedState(controller, home, "walk", "The real desktop walking pose did not reach its home presentation.");
        Program.Assert(controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView.RenderTransform.Value.M11 < 0 && home.SpriteView.RenderTransform.Value.M11 < 0,
            "The home cat did not inherit the actual desktop cat's facing direction.");
        var sharedWalkReady = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        while ((!controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView.IsWalkLoopReady || !home.SpriteView.IsWalkLoopReady || controller.DesktopWindow.WalkDistanceDips < 2)
            && sharedWalkReady.Elapsed < TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5)) await Task.Delay(10);
        Program.Assert(controller.DesktopWindow.IsWalking && controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView.IsWalkLoopReady && home.SpriteView.IsWalkLoopReady,
            "The shared desktop/home presentation did not reach distance-bound walking.");
        for (var sample = 0; sample < 6; sample++)
        {
            Program.Assert(controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView.RenderedFrameIndex == home.SpriteView.RenderedFrameIndex,
                "Home walking drifted away from the desktop's actual travelled-distance phase.");
            await Task.Delay(40);
        }
        controller.DesktopWindow.StopWalk();
        var walkStopped = Stopwatch.StartNew();
