        while (controller.DesktopWindow.IsWalking && walkStopped.Elapsed < TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5)) await FlushAsync(window);
        await FlushAsync(window);
        AssertSharedState(controller, home, "idle", "Stopping desktop walking left a different pose in the home scene.");
        Program.Assert(home.SpriteView.RenderTransform.Value.M11 < 0 && controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView.RenderTransform.Value.M11 < 0,
            "Landing instantly flipped the cat instead of retaining its facing direction.");

        controller.ChangeSettings(settings => { settings.Enabled = false; settings.MotionMode = PetMotionMode.Gentle; });
        EndTransient(controller);
        RefreshController(controller);
        Program.Assert(controller.Account.StartWork().Succeeded, "The home-only companion could not start shared leisure work.");
        RefreshController(controller);
        await FlushAsync(window);
        AssertSharedState(controller, home, "work", "Leisure work did not reach both presentations.");
        Program.Assert(!controller.TryStartupGreeting("不应打断休闲工作的问候"), "A startup greeting interrupted shared leisure work.");
        Program.Assert(controller.Account.Snapshot.WorkRunning && controller.ActiveHeartbeatCount == 1,
            "Leisure work did not run while the home companion was the sole visible presentation.");
        var beforeWork = controller.Account.Snapshot.WorkElapsed;
        var watch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        await Task.Delay(2250);
        controller.Account.Tick();
        var elapsed = watch.Elapsed;
        var used = controller.Account.Snapshot.WorkElapsed - beforeWork;
        Program.Assert(used.TotalMilliseconds >= 1200 && used.TotalMilliseconds <= elapsed.TotalMilliseconds + 350,
            $"The shared work clock stalled or credited time twice: elapsed={elapsed.TotalMilliseconds:0}ms, used={used.TotalMilliseconds:0}ms.");
        var old = home;
        window.ShowSettingsPage();
        await FlushAsync(window);
        AssertInactive(old, "Leaving the home-only working cat retained animation clocks.");
        Program.Assert(controller.Account.Snapshot.WorkRunning && controller.ActiveHeartbeatCount == 1,
            "Hiding both presentations stopped the single runtime work heartbeat.");
        var hiddenElapsed = controller.Account.Snapshot.WorkElapsed;
        await Task.Delay(1150);
        controller.Account.Tick();
        Program.Assert((controller.Account.Snapshot.WorkElapsed - hiddenElapsed).TotalMilliseconds >= 800,
            "Hidden runtime work did not continue accumulating.");
        window.ShowHomePage();
        await FlushAsync(window);
        home = RequireHome(window, controller);
        Program.Assert(controller.Account.Snapshot.WorkRunning && controller.ActiveHeartbeatCount == 1 && window.HomeGreetingCount == 1,
            "Returning to the same home work state failed to resume, or replayed the greeting.");
        AssertSharedState(controller, home, "work", "Returning home created a separate idle pet instead of the existing worker.");
        Program.Assert(controller.Account.CancelWork().Succeeded, "Could not stop the shared leisure-work fixture.");
        run.Evidence["homeCompanionEconomy"] = new { oneAccount = controller.Account.FilePath,
            fundedCoins = funded.CoinsChanged, coinsAfterSharedAccessoryPurchases = equipped.Coins,
            homeAndDesktopAccessoryPixelsChangedAndRestored = true,
            actualInventoryFeedingSynchronized = true,
            actualDesktopWalkingPoseAndFacingSynchronized = true,
            elapsedMilliseconds = elapsed.TotalMilliseconds, creditedMilliseconds = used.TotalMilliseconds,
            invisibleWorkContinues = true, workContinuedOnReturn = true, controller.ActiveHeartbeatCount };
    }

    private static async Task ProcessingAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        await using var scene = await Scene.OpenAsync(run, "processing", beginProcessingBeforeAttach: true);
        var (window, controller) = (scene.Window, scene.Controller);
        var home = RequireHome(window, controller);
        Program.Assert(window.HomeGreetingConsidered && window.HomeGreetingCount == 0,
            "A delayed first home view played its startup greeting over an active file-processing task.");
        AssertSharedState(controller, home, "work", "The home scene did not inherit an existing processing state.");
        controller.Interact("pet-head");
        await FlushAsync(window);
        AssertSharedState(controller, home, "work", "Home interaction interrupted the file-processing animation.");
        Program.Assert(!window.Model.CanEdit && controller.ActiveHeartbeatCount == 1,
            "Displaying the home pet changed processing availability or duplicated heartbeat ownership.");
        window.ShowTaskCenterPage();
        await FlushAsync(window);
        AssertInactive(home, "Navigating away during processing left the home sprite running.");
        window.ShowHomePage();
        await FlushAsync(window);
        home = RequireHome(window, controller);
        AssertSharedState(controller, home, "work", "Returning home lost the ongoing batch's shared work state.");
        var balance = controller.Account.Snapshot.Coins;
        scene.Engine.Release.TrySetResult();
        await window.Model.ActiveRun.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5));
        await FlushAsync(window);
        Program.Assert(window.Model.Queue.Single().Status == JobStatus.Completed &&
            controller.Account.Snapshot.Coins == balance + PetStateStore.FileRewardCoins,
            "The real model batch did not complete or home participation duplicated its shared reward.");
        AssertSharedState(controller, home, "success", "The shared result did not reach home and desktop together.");
        Program.Assert(window.HomeGreetingCount == 0 && window.HomeGreetingConsidered,
            "A skipped startup greeting was replayed later over the completed result.");
        var originalOutcome = new { requestCount = scene.Engine.RequestCount, window.HomeGreetingCount, controller.Account.Snapshot.Coins };
        controller.ChangeSettings(settings => settings.CompletionNotifications = false);
        controller.ToggleVisibility();
        controller.HideTemporarily();
        controller.ToggleVisibility();
        AssertNoCompletionNotification(controller);
        Program.Assert(home.DisplayedMessage == controller.LatestMessage && home.DisplayedMessage.Contains("完成 1 项", StringComparison.Ordinal),
            "Turning completion bubbles off replaced the shared result with an old deferred summary.");
        await scene.DisposeAsync();

        await using var silentScene = await Scene.OpenAsync(run, "processing-no-completion-bubbles", beginProcessingBeforeAttach: true);
        var silentController = silentScene.Controller;
        var silentHome = RequireHome(silentScene.Window, silentController);
        silentController.ChangeSettings(settings =>
        {
            settings.CompletionNotifications = false;
            settings.Enabled = true;
            settings.HideForFullscreen = false;
        });
        Program.Assert(silentController.IsPetVisible && silentController.DesktopWindow.IsVisible,
            "The disabled-completion-bubble fixture requires a genuinely visible desktop companion.");
        // The held request began before controller attachment. Report a later live update
        // after the model's numeric throttle so this check begins with real processing text.
        await Task.Delay(300);
        silentScene.Engine.ReportProgress(99);
        await FlushAsync(silentScene.Window);
        Program.Assert(silentHome.DisplayedMessage.Contains("99%", StringComparison.Ordinal) && silentScene.Window.Model.IsProcessing,
            "The disabled-bubble fixture did not first display a live processing message.");
        var progressBubble = DesktopMessageWindow(silentController);
        Program.Assert(progressBubble.IsVisible && Descendants<TextBlock>(progressBubble).Any(text => text.Text.Contains("99%", StringComparison.Ordinal)),
            "The visible desktop companion did not actually show its live 99% progress bubble.");
        var silentBalance = silentController.Account.Snapshot.Coins;
        silentScene.Engine.Release.TrySetResult();
        await silentScene.Window.Model.ActiveRun.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5));
        Program.Assert(silentController.DesktopWindow.IsVisible && !progressBubble.IsVisible,
            "Silent completion retained the desktop's old progress bubble until its timeout or a later visibility change.");
        await FlushAsync(silentScene.Window);
        Program.Assert(silentScene.Window.Model.Queue.Single().Status == JobStatus.Completed &&
            silentController.Account.Snapshot.Coins == silentBalance + PetStateStore.FileRewardCoins,
            "Disabling completion bubbles lost the result or changed the single shared reward.");
        AssertSharedState(silentController, silentHome, "success", "Disabling desktop bubbles suppressed the shared completion animation.");
        Program.Assert(silentHome.DisplayedMessage == silentController.LatestMessage &&
            silentHome.DisplayedMessage.Contains("完成 1 项", StringComparison.Ordinal) &&
            !silentHome.DisplayedMessage.Contains("99%", StringComparison.Ordinal),
            "Disabling desktop bubbles left the home companion showing stale processing text.");
        AssertNoCompletionNotification(silentController);
        silentController.ToggleVisibility();
        silentController.HideTemporarily();
        silentController.ToggleVisibility();
        AssertNoCompletionNotification(silentController);
        Program.Assert(silentScene.Window.HomeGreetingCount == 0 && silentScene.Engine.RequestCount == 1 &&
            silentController.Account.Snapshot.Coins == silentBalance + PetStateStore.FileRewardCoins,
            "Returning the muted desktop companion replayed a greeting, request or reward.");
        run.Evidence["homeCompanionProcessing"] = new { originalOutcome.requestCount,
            startupSkippedDuringExistingBatch = true, sharedWorkAndSuccess = true, duplicateRewards = false,
            originalOutcome.HomeGreetingCount, originalOutcome.Coins,
            notificationsDisabled = new { requestCount = silentScene.Engine.RequestCount, sharedResultStillUpdates = true,
                completionAnimation = silentController.CurrentAnimation, message = silentHome.DisplayedMessage,
                rewardCoins = silentController.Account.Snapshot.Coins - silentBalance, desktopBubbleHidden = true,
                visibleDesktopProgressBubbleObserved = true, oldProgressBubbleClearedBeforeVisibilityChanges = true,
                noDeferredSummary = true, previouslyQueuedSummaryDiscardedWhenMuted = true },
            fixture = "Held IConversionEngine request through the production MainViewModel. No engine UI or state is reconstructed." };
    }

    private static async Task LifecycleAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        await using var scene = await Scene.OpenAsync(run, "layout-lifecycle");
        var (window, controller) = (scene.Window, scene.Controller);
        var discarded = new List<HomeCompanionView>();
        var captures = 0;
        foreach (var theme in new[] { "Light", "Dark" })
        {
            window.Model.ThemePreference = theme;
            foreach (var (width, height) in new[] { (1200d, 820d), (960d, 540d), (620d, 480d) })
            {
                if (window.HomeCompanion is { } old) discarded.Add(old);
                window.ShowHomePage();
                window.Width = width; window.Height = height;
                await FlushAsync(window);
                var home = RequireHome(window, controller);
                home.BringIntoView();
                await FlushAsync(window);
                AssertInsideVisibleViewport(home.SpriteView, window);
                var message = Descendants<TextBlock>(home).FirstOrDefault(item => item.Text == home.DisplayedMessage);
                Program.Assert(message is not null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(home.DisplayedMessage),
                    "The home companion has no actual readable greeting/status message.");
                AssertInsideVisibleViewport(message!, window);
                foreach (var previous in discarded.Where(item => !ReferenceEquals(item, home)))
                    AssertInactive(previous, "Rebuilding a responsive/themed home retained a discarded sprite player.");
                Program.Assert(controller.ActivePreviewCount == 1 && controller.ActiveHeartbeatCount == 1 && window.HomeGreetingCount == 1,
                    "A themed or responsive home rebuild duplicated the preview, clock or greeting.");
                Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, $"home-companion-{theme.ToLowerInvariant()}-{width:0}x{height:0}");
                captures++;
            }
        }
        var current = RequireHome(window, controller);
        window.Hide();
        await FlushAsync(window);
        AssertInactive(current, "Hiding the main window retained home animations.");
        Program.Assert(controller.ActivePreviewCount == 0 && controller.ActiveHeartbeatCount == 0,
            "Hiding the main window retained home companion registration or heartbeat.");
        window.Show();
        await FlushAsync(window);
        current = RequireHome(window, controller);
        Program.Assert(controller.ActivePreviewCount == 1 && controller.ActiveHeartbeatCount == 1 && current.SpriteView.HasActiveAnimation,
            "Showing the same home window did not restore exactly one animated companion.");
        window.WindowState = WindowState.Minimized;
        await FlushAsync(window);
        AssertInactive(current, "Minimizing the main window retained home animations.");
        Program.Assert(controller.ActivePreviewCount == 0 && controller.ActiveHeartbeatCount == 0,
            "Minimizing the home window retained its registration or heartbeat.");
        window.WindowState = WindowState.Normal;
        await FlushAsync(window);
        current = RequireHome(window, controller);
        Program.Assert(controller.ActivePreviewCount == 1 && controller.ActiveHeartbeatCount == 1 && window.HomeGreetingCount == 1,
            "Restoring the home window duplicated registration or replayed its greeting.");
        foreach (var show in new Action[] { window.ShowTaskCenterPage, window.ShowSettingsPage, window.ShowAboutPage,
            () => window.ShowPetPage(), window.ShowFilePage })
        {
            current = RequireHome(window, controller);
            show();
            await FlushAsync(window);
            AssertInactive(current, "A non-home page retained the home sprite's registration or clocks.");
            Program.Assert(!Descendants<HomeCompanionView>(window).Any(item => item.IsVisible) && controller.ActivePreviewCount == 0,
                "The in-app companion appears outside the home page.");
            window.ShowHomePage();
            await FlushAsync(window);
        }
        current = RequireHome(window, controller);
        window.ApproveExit(); window.Close();
        await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle);
        AssertInactive(current, "Closing the main window left its home pet alive.");
        Program.Assert(controller.ActivePreviewCount == 0 && controller.ActiveHeartbeatCount == 0,
            "Closing the main window retained a home registration or heartbeat.");
        controller.Animate("happy", "这个已关闭的首页不应再接收事件。");
        AssertInactive(current, "A later controller event restarted the closed home scene.");
        run.Evidence["homeCompanionLifecycle"] = new { productionWpfCaptures = captures,
            themes = new[] { "Light", "Dark" }, sizes = new[] { "1200x820", "960x540", "620x480" },
            uniqueHomeViewInstances = discarded.Append(current).Distinct().Count(), hiddenMinimizedAndClosedStopAllHomeClocks = true,
            nonHomePagesChecked = 5, startupGreetingStillSingle = window.HomeGreetingCount == 1,
            dpi = VisualTreeHelper.GetDpi(window).PixelsPerInchX,
            limitations = "Logical WPF viewports at observed host DPI; physical input, real display scaling and other Windows versions require separate checks." };
    }

    private static HomeCompanionView RequireHome(MainWindow window, PetController controller)
    {
        var home = window.HomeCompanion ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("HomeCompanion was not created on the production home page.");
        Program.Assert(window.CurrentShellPage == "home" && home.IsVisible && home.IsRegistered &&
            ReferenceEquals(home.Controller, controller) && ReferenceEquals(home.Controller.Model, window.Model),
            "The visible home scene is disconnected from the app's existing pet controller/model.");
        return home;
    }

    private static void AssertSharedState(PetController controller, HomeCompanionView home, string expected, string message)
        => Program.Assert(controller.CurrentAnimation == expected && controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView.Animation == expected &&
            home.SpriteView.Animation == expected && ReferenceEquals(home.Controller, controller), message +
            $" controller={controller.CurrentAnimation}, desktop={controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView.Animation}, home={home.SpriteView.Animation}.");

    private static void AssertInactive(HomeCompanionView home, string message)
        => Program.Assert(!home.IsRegistered && !home.SpriteView.HasActiveAnimation && home.SpriteView.ActiveFrameTimerCount == 0 &&
            home.SpriteView.ActiveAmbientClockCount == 0 && home.SpriteView.ActiveTransitionClockCount == 0, message);

    private static void AssertNoCompletionNotification(PetController controller)
    {
        Program.Assert(!DesktopMessageWindow(controller).IsVisible, "Completion notifications were disabled but the desktop bubble is visible.");
        foreach (var field in new[] { "deferredCompleted", "deferredFailed", "deferredStopped", "deferredNotFound" })
            Program.Assert((int)(typeof(PetController).GetField(field, BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic)?.GetValue(controller)
                ?? throw new MissingFieldException(typeof(PetController).FullName, field)) == 0,
                "Completion notifications were disabled but a deferred summary remains queued: " + field + ".");
    }

    private static Window DesktopMessageWindow(PetController controller)
        => typeof(DesktopPetWindow).GetField("messageWindow", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic)?.GetValue(controller.DesktopWindow)
            as Window ?? throw new MissingFieldException(typeof(DesktopPetWindow).FullName, "messageWindow");

    private static async Task FlushAsync(Window window)
    {
        await window.Dispatcher.InvokeAsync(window.UpdateLayout, DispatcherPriority.ContextIdle);
        await Task.Delay(50);
    }

    private static void EndTransient(PetController controller)
    {
        SetControllerField(controller, "transientUntil", DateTime.UtcNow.AddSeconds(-1));
        SetControllerField(controller, "nextIdle", DateTime.UtcNow.AddMinutes(2));
    }

    private static void SetControllerField(PetController controller, string field, DateTime value)
        => (typeof(PetController).GetField(field, BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic)
            ?? throw new MissingFieldException(typeof(PetController).FullName, field)).SetValue(controller, value);

    private static void RefreshController(PetController controller)
        => (typeof(PetController).GetMethod("Tick", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic)
            ?? throw new MissingMethodException(typeof(PetController).FullName, "Tick")).Invoke(controller, new object?[] { null, EventArgs.Empty });

    private static string FixedPoseHash(PetSpriteView view)
    {
        view.SetFrameForTesting("idle", 0);
        view.UpdateLayout();
        return RenderHash(view);
    }

    private static string RenderHash(PetSpriteView view)
    {
        var width = Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Ceiling(view.ActualWidth));
        var height = Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Ceiling(view.ActualHeight));
        var bitmap = new RenderTargetBitmap(width, height, 96, 96, PixelFormats.Pbgra32);
        bitmap.Render(view);
        var pixels = new byte[width * height * 4]; bitmap.CopyPixels(pixels, width * 4, 0);
        Program.Assert(Enumerable.Range(0, width * height).Count(index => pixels[index * 4 + 3] != 0) > 100,
            "The production pet rendering sample is empty.");
        return Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(pixels));
    }

    private static async Task<int> CountUniqueFramesAsync(PetSpriteView view)
    {
        var hashes = new HashSet<string>();
        for (var sample = 0; sample < 12; sample++)
        {
            await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Render);
            hashes.Add(RenderHash(view));
            await Task.Delay(100);
        }
        return hashes.Count;
    }

    private static IEnumerable<T> Descendants<T>(DependencyObject parent) where T : DependencyObject
    {
        for (var index = 0; index < VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(parent); index++)
        {
            var child = VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(parent, index);
            if (child is T match) yield return match;
            foreach (var nested in Descendants<T>(child)) yield return nested;
        }
    }

    private static void AssertInsideVisibleViewport(FrameworkElement element, MainWindow window)
    {
        var root = (FrameworkElement)window.Content;
        Program.Assert(element.IsVisible && element.ActualWidth > 0 && element.ActualHeight > 0,
            "A home companion element has no visible measured bounds.");
        var viewport = new Rect(0, 0, root.ActualWidth, root.ActualHeight);
        for (DependencyObject? parent = element; parent is not null && !ReferenceEquals(parent, root); parent = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(parent))
            if (parent is ScrollContentPresenter presenter)
                viewport.Intersect(presenter.TransformToAncestor(root).TransformBounds(new Rect(0, 0, presenter.ActualWidth, presenter.ActualHeight)));
        var bounds = element.TransformToAncestor(root).TransformBounds(new Rect(0, 0, element.ActualWidth, element.ActualHeight));
        Program.Assert(!viewport.IsEmpty && bounds.Left >= viewport.Left - 2 && bounds.Top >= viewport.Top - 2 &&
            bounds.Right <= viewport.Right + 2 && bounds.Bottom <= viewport.Bottom + 2,
            $"Home pet/message is clipped at {window.ActualWidth}x{window.ActualHeight}: content={bounds}, actualViewport={viewport}.");
    }

    private sealed class Scene : IAsyncDisposable
    {
        private readonly AcceptanceRun run;
        private bool disposed;
        public MainWindow Window { get; }
        public PetController Controller { get; }
        public HeldEngine Engine { get; }
        private Scene(AcceptanceRun run, MainWindow window, PetController controller, HeldEngine engine)
        { this.run = run; Window = window; Controller = controller; Engine = engine; }

        public static async Task<Scene> OpenAsync(AcceptanceRun run, string name, bool beginProcessingBeforeAttach = false)
        {
            var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "home-companion-" + name);
            Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
            var store = new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(directory, "settings"));
            store.Save(new AppSettings { OutputDirectory = Path.Combine(directory, "outputs"), ThemePreference = "Light",
                MotionPreference = MotionPreference.Reduced,
                Pet = new PetSettings { Enabled = false, HideForFullscreen = false, Roaming = false, Topmost = false,
                    MotionMode = PetMotionMode.Gentle, CompletionNotifications = true, Size = 160 } });
            var engine = new HeldEngine();
            var model = new MainViewModel(engine, PresetCatalog.Load(Path.Combine(run.Root, "shared", "presets.json")), store,
                new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(directory, "logs")));
            if (beginProcessingBeforeAttach)
            {
                var input = Path.Combine(directory, "首页共享工作状态.mp4");
                File.WriteAllText(input, "Deterministic UI lifecycle fixture; no real media engine invoked.");
                run.TrackSource(input);
                await model.ImportForActionAsync(FileProcessingAction.Video, new[] { input });
                _ = model.RunQueueAsync();
                await engine.Started.Task.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5));
            }
            var window = new MainWindow(model) { ShowActivated = false, Width = 1200, Height = 820 };
            window.ShowHomePage(); window.Show();
            var controller = new PetController(model) { MainWindowProvider = () => window };
            var scene = new Scene(run, window, controller, engine);
            run.StopActiveWindow = async () => await scene.DisposeAsync();
            try
            {
                window.AttachPetController(controller);
                await FlushAsync(window);
                return scene;
            }
            catch
            {
                await scene.DisposeAsync();
                throw;
            }
        }

        public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()
        {
            if (disposed) return;
            disposed = true;
            Window.Model.Cancel(); Engine.Release.TrySetResult();
            try { await Window.Model.ActiveRun.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5)); }
            finally
            {
                Window.ApproveExit(); Window.Close(); Controller.Dispose(); run.StopActiveWindow = null;
            }
        }
    }

    private sealed class HeldEngine : IConversionEngine
    {
        private IProgress<ConversionProgress>? progressObserver;
        public int RequestCount { get; private set; }
        public TaskCompletionSource Started { get; } = new(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
        public TaskCompletionSource Release { get; } = new(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
        public Task<MediaInfo> ProbeAsync(string path, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
            => Task.FromResult(new MediaInfo(true, true, 1, "mp4", "h264", "aac", 1024, 64, 64, 25));
        public void ReportProgress(double? percent) => progressObserver?.Report(new(percent, TimeSpan.Zero));
        public async Task<ConversionResult> ConvertAsync(ConversionRequest request, IProgress<ConversionProgress>? progress = null,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            RequestCount++; progressObserver = progress; ReportProgress(25); Started.TrySetResult();
            await Release.Task.WaitAsync(cancellationToken);
            Directory.CreateDirectory(request.OutputDirectory);
            var output = Path.Combine(request.OutputDirectory, "home-companion-result.mp4");
            File.WriteAllText(output, "Committed shared home/desktop result.");
            return new(JobStatus.Completed, output, null);
        }
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/HomeCompanionPerformanceAcceptance.cs ---
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Interop;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;
using Microsoft.Win32;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

/// <summary>Measures the production home and desktop views together; never substitutes a fake sprite or work clock.</summary>
internal static class HomeCompanionPerformanceAcceptance
{
    public static async Task RunAsync(AcceptanceRun run, IConversionEngine engine, IReadOnlyList<FormatPreset> presets)
    {
        const int sampleSeconds = 60;
        var output = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "home-companion-performance-output");
        var settings = new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "home-companion-performance-settings"));
        settings.Save(new AppSettings
        {
            OutputDirectory = output,
            MotionPreference = MotionPreference.System,
            Pet = new PetSettings { Enabled = false, Roaming = false, Topmost = true, HideForFullscreen = false, MotionMode = PetMotionMode.Gentle, Size = 160 }
        });
        var model = new MainViewModel(engine, presets, settings, new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "home-companion-performance-logs")));
        model.SelectedPreset = presets.First(preset => preset.Mode == ConversionMode.Video && preset.ExpectedCodec == "hevc");
        var window = new MainWindow(model, () => true) { Width = 1200, Height = 820, Topmost = true, ShowActivated = true, WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.Manual };
        var monitor = DesktopEnvironment.GetMonitors().FirstOrDefault(item => item.Primary) ?? DesktopEnvironment.GetMonitors().First();
        window.Left = monitor.WorkArea.Left / monitor.DpiScaleX + 16;
        window.Top = monitor.WorkArea.Top / monitor.DpiScaleY + 16;
        window.ShowHomePage();
        window.Show();
        window.Activate();
        PetController? controller = null;
        var idleSamples = new List<IdleSample>();
        var conversions = new List<ConversionSample>();
        run.StopActiveWindow = async () => { model.Cancel(); await model.ActiveRun; };
        using var process = Process.GetCurrentProcess();
        try
        {
            // Realize the original file page before the no-controller baseline so its later
            // DataGrid/template allocation is not attributed to the new pet presentation.
            window.ShowFilePage();
            await SettleAsync(window);
            window.ShowHomePage();
            await SettleAsync(window);
            // This is the same home window before AttachPetController, matching the previous home UI.
            idleSamples.Add(await MeasureIdleAsync(process, window, null, Scene.Baseline, sampleSeconds));
            SaveProgress(run, idleSamples, conversions);

            controller = new PetController(model) { MainWindowProvider = () => window };
            window.AttachPetController(controller);
            controller.ChangeSettings(value => value.Enabled = true);
            PositionDesktopPet(window, controller);
            await SettleAsync(window, 100);
            window.HomeCompanion?.BringIntoView();
            await SettleAsync(window, 3500); // Finish the once-per-session welcome before idle sampling.
            idleSamples.Add(await MeasureIdleAsync(process, window, controller, Scene.Both, sampleSeconds));
            SaveProgress(run, idleSamples, conversions);

            controller.ChangeSettings(value => value.Enabled = false);
            await SettleAsync(window);
            idleSamples.Add(await MeasureIdleAsync(process, window, controller, Scene.HomeOnly, sampleSeconds));
            SaveProgress(run, idleSamples, conversions);

            window.ShowFilePage();
            await SettleAsync(window);
            idleSamples.Add(await MeasureIdleAsync(process, window, controller, Scene.AllOff, sampleSeconds));
            SaveProgress(run, idleSamples, conversions);

            // Keep the real HEVC workload, import path and stopwatch boundaries used by PetPerformanceAcceptance.
            foreach (var enabled in new[] { false, true })
            {
                await SetConversionSceneAsync(window, controller, enabled);
                conversions.Add(await ConvertAsync(model, run.LongVideo, enabled, warmup: true, pair: 0));
                SaveProgress(run, idleSamples, conversions);
            }
            for (var pair = 0; pair < 3; pair++)
            {
                foreach (var enabled in pair % 2 == 0 ? new[] { false, true } : new[] { true, false })
                {
                    await SetConversionSceneAsync(window, controller, enabled);
                    conversions.Add(await ConvertAsync(model, run.LongVideo, enabled, warmup: false, pair: pair + 1));
                    SaveProgress(run, idleSamples, conversions);
                }
            }
            // RenderTargetBitmap/PNG capture can retain native allocations. Capture only after
            // every CPU/memory sample and timed conversion, never between measured scenes.
            await SetConversionSceneAsync(window, controller, bothEnabled: true);
            Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, "home-companion-performance-both-visible");

            var baseline = idleSamples.Single(item => item.Scene == Scene.Baseline.ToString());
            var comparisons = idleSamples.Where(item => item.Scene != baseline.Scene).Select(item => new
            {
                item.Scene,
                cpuIncreasePercent = Math.Max(0, item.CpuPercent - baseline.CpuPercent),
                memoryIncreaseBytes = Math.Max(0, item.AfterGcPrivateBytes - baseline.AfterGcPrivateBytes),
                cpuPassed = item.CpuPercent - baseline.CpuPercent <= 1,
                memoryPassed = item.AfterGcPrivateBytes - baseline.AfterGcPrivateBytes <= 50L * 1024 * 1024
            }).ToArray();
            var on = conversions.Where(item => !item.Warmup && item.BothEnabled).Select(item => item.Seconds).ToArray();
            var off = conversions.Where(item => !item.Warmup && !item.BothEnabled).Select(item => item.Seconds).ToArray();
            var onMedian = Median(on); var offMedian = Median(off);
            var increase = Math.Max(0, (onMedian - offMedian) / offMedian * 100);
            var allScenesValid = idleSamples.All(item => item.ValidThroughout);
            var cpuPassed = comparisons.All(item => item.cpuPassed);
            var memoryPassed = comparisons.All(item => item.memoryPassed);
            var conversionsSucceeded = conversions.All(item => item.Completed);
            var conversionPassed = increase <= 5;
            run.Evidence["homeCompanionPerformance"] = new
            {
                machine = new { os = Environment.OSVersion.VersionString, cpu = CpuName(), logicalProcessors = Environment.ProcessorCount,
                    renderTier = RenderCapability.Tier >> 16, dpi = VisualTreeHelper.GetDpi(window).PixelsPerInchX },
                sampleSeconds,
                requestedQuick = run.Quick,
                quickDoesNotShortenThisMeasurement = true,
                windowsClientAreaAnimation = SystemParameters.ClientAreaAnimation,
                systemMotionAllowedForTesting = false,
                petMotionMode = PetMotionMode.Gentle.ToString(),
                roaming = false,
                measurementWindowsTopmost = true,
                foregroundActivationRequested = true,
                visibilityControl = "The main window and desktop pet are topmost and placed in disjoint physical rectangles so another ordinary app cannot occlude the sampled animation; this is a measurement condition, not a changed product default.",
                roamingReason = "Fixed position keeps the actual desktop sprite outside the main window for the entire measurement; the existing desktop-only performance case remains separate.",
                processCpuPercentMethod = "Process.TotalProcessorTime / Stopwatch elapsed / logical processors * 100; per-second state observations are diagnostics, not FPS measurements.",
                memoryMethod = "PrivateMemorySize64 after full GC and pending finalizers, compared with the same home window before controller attachment; no GC runs inside the timed CPU interval.",
                baselineDefinition = "Production MainWindow on home, before AttachPetController, with no home companion or desktop window. The existing file page and its layout/templates are realized once before returning to home and measuring this baseline.",
                screenshotTiming = "Captured after all four idle scenes and all real conversions; screenshot allocations are excluded from measured private-memory snapshots.",
                samples = idleSamples,
                comparisons,
                conversions,
                bothEnabledConversionSeconds = on,
                allOffConversionSeconds = off,
                bothEnabledMedianSeconds = onMedian,
                allOffMedianSeconds = offMedian,
                conversionIncreasePercent = increase,
                source = run.LongVideo,
                preset = model.SelectedPreset.Id,
                budgets = new { idleCpuIncreasePercent = 1, privateMemoryIncreaseMiB = 50, conversionMedianIncreasePercent = 5 },
                allScenesValid, cpuPassed, memoryPassed, conversionsSucceeded, conversionPassed,
                limitation = "This process and host only. Actual Gentle mode, the current Windows animation preference, one controller and one account are used. The two views retain their production rendering clocks. GPU-presented FPS, other hardware, DPI, Windows versions and games are not inferred from these measurements."
            };
            run.SaveReport();
            // Preserve every sample and workload result before evaluating budgets; failures never relax a threshold.
            Program.Assert(allScenesValid, "Home/desktop visibility, non-overlap, registration or clock state changed during a 60-second performance sample; inspect the recorded observations.");
            Program.Assert(conversionsSucceeded, "A real HEVC warmup or measured conversion failed; inspect homeCompanionPerformance.conversions.");
            Program.Assert(cpuPassed, "Home/desktop idle CPU increase exceeded 1%; inspect every scene comparison.");
            Program.Assert(memoryPassed, "Home/desktop private-memory increase exceeded 50 MiB; inspect every scene comparison.");
            Program.Assert(conversionPassed, $"Home plus desktop HEVC conversion median increase {increase:F2}% exceeded 5%.");
        }
        finally
        {
            if (model.IsProcessing) { model.Cancel(); await model.ActiveRun; }
            controller?.Dispose();
            window.ApproveExit(); window.Close();
            run.StopActiveWindow = null;
        }
    }

    private static void SaveProgress(AcceptanceRun run, List<IdleSample> samples, List<ConversionSample> conversions)
    {
        run.Evidence["homeCompanionPerformanceProgress"] = new { requiredSecondsPerScene = 60, samples, conversions };
        run.SaveReport();
    }
    private static async Task SettleAsync(MainWindow window, int milliseconds = 1500)
    {
        await window.Dispatcher.InvokeAsync(window.UpdateLayout, DispatcherPriority.ContextIdle);
        await Task.Delay(milliseconds);
    }
    private static async Task SetConversionSceneAsync(MainWindow window, PetController controller, bool bothEnabled)
    {
        controller.ChangeSettings(value => value.Enabled = bothEnabled);
        if (bothEnabled) { window.ShowHomePage(); PositionDesktopPet(window, controller); }
        else window.ShowFilePage();
        await SettleAsync(window, 300);
        if (bothEnabled) { window.HomeCompanion?.BringIntoView(); await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ContextIdle); }
    }
    private static async Task<ConversionSample> ConvertAsync(MainViewModel model, string source, bool bothEnabled, bool warmup, int pair)
    {
        model.Clear();
        await model.AddFilesAsync([source]);
        await Task.Delay(250);
        var watch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        await model.RunQueueAsync().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5));
        watch.Stop();
        var row = model.VisibleQueue.Single();
        Console.WriteLine($"HOME PET CONVERSION {(bothEnabled ? "both-on" : "all-off")} {(warmup ? "warmup" : "pair " + pair)}: {watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds:F3}s ({row.Status})");
        return new(bothEnabled, warmup, pair, watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, row.Status == JobStatus.Completed, row.Status.ToString());
    }
    private static async Task<IdleSample> MeasureIdleAsync(Process process, MainWindow window, PetController? controller, Scene scene, int seconds)
    {
        var beforeMemory = AfterGcPrivateBytes(process);
        var observations = new List<SceneObservation> { Observe(window, controller, scene) };
        process.Refresh();
        var cpu = process.TotalProcessorTime;
        var watch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        for (var sample = 0; sample < seconds; sample++)
        {
            await Task.Delay(1000);
            observations.Add(Observe(window, controller, scene));
        }
        watch.Stop();
        process.Refresh();
        var cpuSeconds = (process.TotalProcessorTime - cpu).TotalSeconds;
        var beforeGcMemory = process.PrivateMemorySize64;
        var afterMemory = AfterGcPrivateBytes(process);
        var cpuPercent = cpuSeconds / watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds / Environment.ProcessorCount * 100;
        Console.WriteLine($"HOME PET CPU {scene}: {cpuPercent:F4}% over {watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds:F2}s; private {afterMemory / 1048576d:F2} MiB");
        return new(scene.ToString(), seconds, watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, cpuSeconds, cpuPercent, beforeMemory, beforeGcMemory, afterMemory,
            observations.All(item => item.Valid), PetSpriteView.CachedAtlasBytes, observations);
    }
    private static long AfterGcPrivateBytes(Process process)
    {
        GC.Collect(); GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers(); GC.Collect();
        process.Refresh();
        return process.PrivateMemorySize64;
    }
    private static SceneObservation Observe(MainWindow window, PetController? controller, Scene expected)
    {
        var home = window.HomeCompanion;
        var desktop = controller?.DesktopWindow;
        var homeClocks = Clocks(home?.SpriteView);
        var desktopClocks = Clocks(desktop?.SpriteView);
        var mainBounds = Bounds(window);
        var petBounds = desktop is null ? Rect.Empty : Bounds(desktop);
        var notCoveredByMain = desktop is not null && !mainBounds.IsEmpty && !petBounds.IsEmpty && !mainBounds.IntersectsWith(petBounds);
        var previews = controller?.ActivePreviewCount ?? 0;
        var heartbeat = controller?.ActiveHeartbeatCount ?? 0;
        var visibleMain = window.IsVisible && window.WindowState != WindowState.Minimized;
        var homeVisible = home is { IsVisible: true, IsRegistered: true };
        var desktopVisible = desktop is { IsVisible: true, IsEnvironmentVisible: true };
        var homeAnimating = home?.SpriteView.HasActiveAnimation == true;
        var desktopAnimating = desktop?.SpriteView.HasActiveAnimation == true;
        var homeInsideViewport = home is not null && InsideViewport(home.SpriteView, window);
        var atlasLoaded = home?.SpriteView.HasSpriteAtlas == true && desktop?.SpriteView.HasSpriteAtlas == true;
        var valid = visibleMain && (expected switch
        {
            Scene.Baseline => window.CurrentShellPage == "home" && controller is null && home is null && previews == 0 && heartbeat == 0,
            Scene.Both => window.CurrentShellPage == "home" && homeVisible && homeInsideViewport && desktopVisible && notCoveredByMain && previews == 1 && heartbeat == 1 && homeAnimating && desktopAnimating && atlasLoaded,
            Scene.HomeOnly => window.CurrentShellPage == "home" && homeVisible && homeInsideViewport && !desktopVisible && previews == 1 && heartbeat == 1 && homeAnimating && atlasLoaded && desktopClocks.Total == 0,
            Scene.AllOff => window.CurrentShellPage != "home" && !homeVisible && !desktopVisible && previews == 0 && heartbeat == 0 && homeClocks.Total == 0 && desktopClocks.Total == 0,
            _ => false
        });
        return new(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow, valid, window.CurrentShellPage, visibleMain, homeVisible, homeInsideViewport, desktopVisible, notCoveredByMain,
            previews, heartbeat, controller?.CurrentAnimation, home?.SpriteView.Animation, desktopClocks, homeClocks,
            Rectangle(mainBounds), Rectangle(petBounds));
    }
    private static ClockObservation Clocks(PetSpriteView? view) => view is null ? new(0, 0, 0) :
        new(view.ActiveFrameTimerCount, view.ActiveTransitionClockCount, view.ActiveAmbientClockCount);
    private static bool InsideViewport(FrameworkElement element, MainWindow window)
    {
        if (!element.IsVisible || element.ActualWidth <= 0 || element.ActualHeight <= 0 || window.Content is not FrameworkElement root) return false;
        var viewport = new Rect(0, 0, root.ActualWidth, root.ActualHeight);
        for (DependencyObject? parent = element; parent is not null && !ReferenceEquals(parent, root); parent = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(parent))
            if (parent is ScrollContentPresenter presenter)
                viewport.Intersect(presenter.TransformToAncestor(root).TransformBounds(new Rect(0, 0, presenter.ActualWidth, presenter.ActualHeight)));
        var bounds = element.TransformToAncestor(root).TransformBounds(new Rect(0, 0, element.ActualWidth, element.ActualHeight));
        return !viewport.IsEmpty && bounds.Left >= viewport.Left - 2 && bounds.Top >= viewport.Top - 2 && bounds.Right <= viewport.Right + 2 && bounds.Bottom <= viewport.Bottom + 2;
    }
    private static Rect Bounds(Window window) => DesktopEnvironment.TryWindowRect(new WindowInteropHelper(window).Handle, out var bounds) ? bounds : Rect.Empty;
    private static RectangleObservation? Rectangle(Rect bounds) => bounds.IsEmpty ? null : new(bounds.Left, bounds.Top, bounds.Width, bounds.Height);
    private static void PositionDesktopPet(MainWindow window, PetController controller)
    {
        var main = Bounds(window);
        foreach (var monitor in DesktopEnvironment.GetMonitors().OrderByDescending(item => item.Primary))
        {
            var width = controller.Model.PetSettings.Size * monitor.DpiScaleX;
            var height = controller.Model.PetSettings.Size * monitor.DpiScaleY;
            var area = monitor.WorkArea;
            foreach (var point in new[] { new Point(area.Right - width - 24, area.Bottom - height - 24), new Point(area.Right - width - 24, area.Top + 24),
                new Point(area.Left + 24, area.Bottom - height - 24), new Point(area.Left + 24, area.Top + 24) })
            {
                var candidate = new Rect(point, new Size(width, height));
                if (!area.Contains(candidate) || main.IntersectsWith(candidate)) continue;
                controller.DesktopWindow.SetPosition(point.X, point.Y);
                return;
            }
        }
        // Keep the observation invalid rather than silently measuring an occluded pet on a display too small for this fixture.
        Console.WriteLine("HOME PET PERFORMANCE: no non-overlapping position available for the 1200x820 main window and desktop pet.");
    }
    private static double Median(IEnumerable<double> values)
    {
        var sorted = values.Order().ToArray();
        return sorted[sorted.Length / 2];
    }
    private static string CpuName()
    {
        try { using var key = Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey(@"HARDWARE\DESCRIPTION\System\CentralProcessor\0"); return key?.GetValue("ProcessorNameString")?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? "unavailable"; }
        catch (System.Security.SecurityException) { return "unavailable"; }
        catch (UnauthorizedAccessException) { return "unavailable"; }
    }
    private enum Scene { Baseline, Both, HomeOnly, AllOff }
    private sealed record ClockObservation(int Frames, int Transitions, int Ambient) { public int Total => Frames + Transitions + Ambient; }
    private sealed record RectangleObservation(double Left, double Top, double Width, double Height);
    private sealed record SceneObservation(DateTimeOffset ObservedUtc, bool Valid, string Page, bool MainVisible, bool HomeVisible, bool HomeSpriteInsideViewport, bool DesktopVisible,
        bool DesktopNotCoveredByMain, int PreviewCount, int HeartbeatCount, string? SharedAnimation, string? HomeAnimation,
        ClockObservation DesktopClocks, ClockObservation HomeClocks, RectangleObservation? MainBounds, RectangleObservation? DesktopBounds);
    private sealed record IdleSample(string Scene, int RequestedSeconds, double ElapsedSeconds, double ProcessCpuSeconds, double CpuPercent,
        long BeforeSampleAfterGcPrivateBytes, long BeforeFinalGcPrivateBytes, long AfterGcPrivateBytes, bool ValidThroughout, long SharedAtlasCacheBytes,
        IReadOnlyList<SceneObservation> Observations);
    private sealed record ConversionSample(bool BothEnabled, bool Warmup, int Pair, double Seconds, bool Completed, string Status);
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/ImageObfuscationAcceptance.cs ---
using System.Buffers.Binary;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.IO.Compression;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

/// <summary>Real PNG/RGBA checks against independently generated Python reference vectors.</summary>
internal static class ImageObfuscationAcceptance
{
    public static async Task RunAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var before = run.Failures;
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "图片混淆 中文 空格");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var engine = new ImageObfuscationEngine(run.EngineDirectory, new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(directory, "logs")));
        var vectorPath = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "fixtures", "image-obfuscation", "vectors.json");
        using var vectors = JsonDocument.Parse(File.ReadAllText(vectorPath));
        string? marked = null;
        byte[]? markedOriginal = null;
        foreach (var vector in vectors.RootElement.GetProperty("vectors").EnumerateArray())
        {
            var width = vector.GetProperty("width").GetInt32(); var height = vector.GetProperty("height").GetInt32();
            var bits = vector.GetProperty("bitDepth").GetInt32(); var mode = Enum.Parse<ImageObfuscationMode>(vector.GetProperty("mode").GetString()!);
            var iterations = vector.GetProperty("iterations").GetInt32(); var expected = vector.GetProperty("sha256").GetString();
            var name = $"image-interoperation-{mode}-{width}x{height}-{bits}bit-{iterations}round";
            await run.CaseAsync(name, async () =>
            {
                var original = Pixels(width, height, bits);
                var source = Path.Combine(directory, $"source-{width}x{height}-{bits}.png");
                if (!File.Exists(source)) { WritePng(source, width, height, bits, original); run.TrackSource(source); }
                var mixed = await engine.ProcessAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), source, Output(run, name), ImageObfuscationOperation.Obfuscate, new(mode, iterations)));
                AssertCompleted(mixed);
                run.TrackSource(mixed.OutputPath!);
                var mixedPixels = await DecodeAsync(run, mixed.OutputPath!, bits);
                Program.Assert(Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(mixedPixels)) == expected, "Output disagrees with independent reference vector.");
                var info = await engine.InspectAsync(mixed.OutputPath!);
                Program.Assert(info.IsMarked && info.StoredMode == mode && info.StoredIterations == iterations && info.Width == width && info.Height == height && info.BitDepth == bits,
                    "PNG does not carry the correct restoration information.");
                // Deliberately select different manual parameters: the marked image must use its stored values.
                var restored = await engine.ProcessAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), mixed.OutputPath!, Output(run, name + "-restore"), ImageObfuscationOperation.Deobfuscate,
                    new(mode == ImageObfuscationMode.Pixel ? ImageObfuscationMode.Block : ImageObfuscationMode.Pixel, 10)));
                AssertCompleted(restored);
                Program.Assert(restored.Statistics?.Verified == true && await MatchesAsync(original, run, restored.OutputPath!, bits), "Marked PNG was not restored pixel for pixel.");
                Program.Assert(!(await engine.InspectAsync(restored.OutputPath!)).IsMarked, "Restored PNG retains a stale mixed-image marker.");
                if (mode == ImageObfuscationMode.Pixel && bits == 8 && width == 65) { marked = mixed.OutputPath; markedOriginal = original; }
                run.Evidence[name] = new { mixed.OutputPath, restoredPath = restored.OutputPath, expectedPixelSha256 = expected, mixed.Statistics, exactRoundTrip = true,
                    reference = "Independent Python hashlib/Fisher-Yates and original BSD Gilbert traversal" };
            });
        }
        var formatSource = Path.Combine(directory, "source-65x47-8.png");
        foreach (var extension in new[] { "jpg", "bmp", "webp" })
        {
            await run.CaseAsync("image-static-input-" + extension, async () =>
            {
                var source = Path.Combine(directory, "常见输入." + extension);
                await FfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", formatSource, "-frames:v", "1", source]); run.TrackSource(source);
                var normalized = await DecodeAsync(run, source, 8);
                foreach (var mode in Enum.GetValues<ImageObfuscationMode>())
                {
                    var mixed = await engine.ProcessAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), source, Output(run, "format-" + extension), ImageObfuscationOperation.Obfuscate, new(mode)));
                    AssertCompleted(mixed);
                    var result = await engine.ProcessAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), mixed.OutputPath!, Output(run, "format-" + extension), ImageObfuscationOperation.Deobfuscate, new(mode)));
                    AssertCompleted(result);
                    Program.Assert(await MatchesAsync(normalized, run, result.OutputPath!, 8), "Static input did not preserve its decoded RGBA pixels.");
                }
            });
        }
        if (marked is not null && markedOriginal is not null)
        {
            var markedFile = marked; var original = markedOriginal;
            await run.CaseAsync("image-marked-repeated-mixing-rejected", async () =>
            {
                var result = await engine.ProcessAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), markedFile, Output(run, "reject-repeat"), ImageObfuscationOperation.Obfuscate, new()));
                Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Failed && result.OutputPath is null, "Marked input was mixed again with a lost prior layer.");
            });
            await run.CaseAsync("image-unmarked-manual-restoration-is-explicitly-unverified", async () =>
            {
                var stripped = Path.Combine(directory, "无标记.png");
                WritePng(stripped, 65, 47, 8, await DecodeAsync(run, markedFile, 8));
                run.TrackSource(stripped);
                var restored = await engine.ProcessAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), stripped, Output(run, "manual"), ImageObfuscationOperation.Deobfuscate, new(ImageObfuscationMode.Pixel, 3)));
                AssertCompleted(restored);
                Program.Assert(restored.Statistics?.Verified == false && await MatchesAsync(original, run, restored.OutputPath!, 8), "Manual restoration is wrong or falsely authenticated.");
            });
            await run.CaseAsync("image-tampered-pixels-with-valid-marker-rejected", async () =>
            {
                var data = await DecodeAsync(run, markedFile, 8); data[24] ^= 127;
                var damaged = Path.Combine(directory, "像素篡改.png"); WritePng(damaged, 65, 47, 8, data);
                CopyAncillaryChunks(markedFile, damaged);
                run.TrackSource(damaged);
                var output = Output(run, "tamper");
                var result = await engine.ProcessAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), damaged, output, ImageObfuscationOperation.Deobfuscate, new()));
                Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Failed && result.OutputPath is null, "Altered image passed the original pixel digest.");
                AssertNoTemporaryFiles(output);
            });
            await run.CaseAsync("image-determinism-path-independent-and-output-no-overwrite", async () =>
            {
                var copied = Path.Combine(directory, "相同像素另一文件名.png"); File.Copy(formatSource, copied);
                var output = Output(run, "collision");
                var first = await engine.ProcessAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), copied, output, ImageObfuscationOperation.Obfuscate, new(ImageObfuscationMode.Pixel, 3)));
                AssertCompleted(first); var hash = Program.Hash(first.OutputPath!);
                Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(output, "相同像素另一文件名_混淆 (2).png"));
                var second = await new ImageObfuscationEngine(run.EngineDirectory).ProcessAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), copied, output, ImageObfuscationOperation.Obfuscate, new(ImageObfuscationMode.Pixel, 3)));
                AssertCompleted(second);
                var deterministicPixels = await DecodeAsync(run, first.OutputPath!, 8);
                Program.Assert(first.OutputPath != second.OutputPath && Program.Hash(first.OutputPath!) == hash &&
                    await MatchesAsync(deterministicPixels, run, markedFile, 8), "Output overwrote a file or depends on source path/process random state.");
                Program.Assert(await MatchesAsync(deterministicPixels, run, second.OutputPath!, 8), "A second engine instance generated different pixels.");
            });
            await run.CaseAsync("image-invalid-duplicate-oversized-and-future-markers-rejected", async () =>
            {
                foreach (var variant in new[] { "future", "duplicate", "oversized", "invalid" })
                {
                    var altered = Path.Combine(directory, "无效标记-" + variant + ".png"); RewriteMarker(markedFile, altered, variant); run.TrackSource(altered);
                    var result = await engine.ProcessAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), altered, Output(run, "reject-markers"), ImageObfuscationOperation.Deobfuscate, new()));
                    Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Failed && result.OutputPath is null, "Invalid/future marker was ignored or trusted.");
                }
                AssertNoTemporaryFiles(Output(run, "reject-markers"));
            });
        }
        await run.CaseAsync("image-exif-orientation-normalized-before-permutation", async () =>
        {
            var jpeg = Path.Combine(directory, "常见输入.jpg"); var jpegPixels = await DecodeAsync(run, jpeg, 8);
            var original = File.ReadAllBytes(jpeg); var oriented = Path.Combine(directory, "旋转照片.jpg");
            byte[] exif = [69,120,105,102,0,0,73,73,42,0,8,0,0,0,1,0,18,1,3,0,1,0,0,0,6,0,0,0,0,0,0,0];
            using (var output = File.Create(oriented)) { output.Write(original, 0, 2); output.Write(new byte[] { 255,225,0,34 }); output.Write(exif); output.Write(original, 2, original.Length - 2); }
            run.TrackSource(oriented);
            var expected = new byte[jpegPixels.Length];
            for (var y = 0; y < 47; y++) for (var x = 0; x < 65; x++) jpegPixels.AsSpan((y * 65 + x) * 4, 4).CopyTo(expected.AsSpan((x * 47 + 46 - y) * 4, 4));
            var mixed = await engine.ProcessAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), oriented, Output(run, "orientation"), ImageObfuscationOperation.Obfuscate, new(ImageObfuscationMode.Gilbert)));
            AssertCompleted(mixed); var info = await engine.InspectAsync(mixed.OutputPath!);
            Program.Assert(info.Width == 47 && info.Height == 65, "EXIF rotation did not normalize its actual display dimensions.");
            var restored = await engine.ProcessAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), mixed.OutputPath!, Output(run, "orientation"), ImageObfuscationOperation.Deobfuscate, new()));
            AssertCompleted(restored); Program.Assert(await MatchesAsync(expected, run, restored.OutputPath!, 8), "Restored EXIF photo differs from the independently rotated decoded pixels.");
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("image-invalid-animation-and-options-rejected", async () =>
        {
            var animation = Path.Combine(directory, "动态图.webp");
            await FfmpegAsync(run, ["-f", "lavfi", "-i", "testsrc2=size=32x32:rate=2", "-t", "1", "-loop", "0", animation]);
            var corrupt = Path.Combine(directory, "损坏.png"); File.WriteAllText(corrupt, "not a PNG");
            foreach (var source in new[] { animation, corrupt })
            {
                var result = await engine.ProcessAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), source, Output(run, "reject-input"), ImageObfuscationOperation.Obfuscate, new()));
                Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Failed && result.OutputPath is null, "Animated or damaged input was silently processed.");
            }
            foreach (var options in new[] { new ImageObfuscationOptions((ImageObfuscationMode)777), new ImageObfuscationOptions(Iterations: 0), new ImageObfuscationOptions(Iterations: 11) })
                Program.Assert((await engine.ProcessAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), formatSource, Output(run, "reject-input"), ImageObfuscationOperation.Obfuscate, options))).Status == JobStatus.Failed, "Invalid options were accepted.");
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("image-apng-and-oversized-dimensions-rejected-without-scaling", async () =>
        {
            var apng = Path.Combine(directory, "动态PNG.png");
            await FfmpegAsync(run, ["-f", "lavfi", "-i", "testsrc2=size=32x32:rate=2", "-t", "1", "-plays", "0", "-f", "apng", apng]);
            var tooWide = Path.Combine(directory, "超宽.png"); WritePng(tooWide, 8193, 1, 8, new byte[8193 * 4]);
            var tooMany = Path.Combine(directory, "超像素.png"); WritePng(tooMany, 4096, 4096, 8, new byte[4096 * 4096 * 4]);
            foreach (var source in new[] { apng, tooWide, tooMany })
            {
                var result = await engine.ProcessAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), source, Output(run, "reject-limits"), ImageObfuscationOperation.Obfuscate, new()));
                Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Failed && result.OutputPath is null, "APNG or excessive image was silently reduced/processed.");
            }
            AssertNoTemporaryFiles(Output(run, "reject-limits"));
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("image-cancellation-cleans-only-owned-work", async () =>
        {
            var output = Output(run, "cancel"); Directory.CreateDirectory(output);
            var unrelated = Path.Combine(output, ".cat-image-other-task.png"); File.WriteAllText(unrelated, "another task"); var hash = Program.Hash(unrelated);
            using var cancellation = new CancellationTokenSource();
            var progress = new CallbackProgress<ImageObfuscationProgress>(_ => cancellation.Cancel());
            var result = await engine.ProcessAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), formatSource, output, ImageObfuscationOperation.Obfuscate, new()), progress, cancellation.Token);
            Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Cancelled && result.OutputPath is null && Program.Hash(unrelated) == hash, "Cancel committed output or deleted another task's work.");
            Program.Assert(Directory.GetFiles(output, "*", SearchOption.AllDirectories).Length == 1, "Cancellation left task-owned scratch files.");
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("image-serial-failure-continues-and-observer-isolation", async () =>
        {
            var requests = new[] { formatSource, Path.Combine(directory, "损坏.png"), formatSource }
                .Select(path => new ImageObfuscationRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), path, Output(run, "serial"), ImageObfuscationOperation.Obfuscate, new(ImageObfuscationMode.Gilbert))).ToArray();
            var batch = await new SerialImageObfuscationQueue(engine).RunAsync(requests, onCompleted: (_, _) => throw new Exception("observer"));
            Program.Assert(batch.CompletedCount == 2 && batch.FailedCount == 1 && batch.Results.Where(value => value.Result.Status == JobStatus.Completed).All(value => File.Exists(value.Result.OutputPath)), "Queue failed to isolate a file or observer failure.");
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("image-cancel-active-encoder-and-serial-pending-retained", async () =>
        {
            var source = Path.Combine(directory, "处理期间取消.png"); WritePng(source, 2048, 1024, 8, Pixels(2048, 1024, 8)); run.TrackSource(source);
            var output = Output(run, "cancel-active"); using var cancellation = new CancellationTokenSource();
            var encodingStarted = new TaskCompletionSource<int[]>(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
            var started = new List<Guid>();
            var requests = new[] { source, formatSource }.Select(path => new ImageObfuscationRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), path, output, ImageObfuscationOperation.Obfuscate, new(ImageObfuscationMode.Pixel))).ToArray();
            var active = new SerialImageObfuscationQueue(engine).RunAsync(requests, onStarted: request => started.Add(request.Id),
                progressFactory: _ => new CallbackProgress<ImageObfuscationProgress>(value => { if (value.Stage == "保存无损 PNG") encodingStarted.TrySetResult(EncoderIds()); }), cancellationToken: cancellation.Token);
            var beforeIds = await encodingStarted.Task.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30));
            var deadline = Stopwatch.StartNew();
            while (!EncoderIds().Except(beforeIds).Any() && !active.IsCompleted && deadline.Elapsed < TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10)) await Task.Delay(10);
            Program.Assert(EncoderIds().Except(beforeIds).Any(), "Active encoding process was not exercised.");
            cancellation.Cancel(); var batch = await active.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(20));
            Program.Assert(batch.Cancelled && batch.Results.Count == 1 && started.SequenceEqual(new[] { requests[0].Id }) && batch.Results[0].Result.Status == JobStatus.Cancelled, "Cancellation started or changed the pending second task.");
            Program.Assert(!EncoderIds().Except(beforeIds).Any(), "Cancelled image child process remained alive."); AssertNoTemporaryFiles(output);
            foreach (var request in requests) Program.Assert(!Directory.GetFiles(Path.GetTempPath(), $"*{request.Id:N}*").Any(), "Cancelled image operation left a source snapshot in the system temporary directory.");
        });
        if (run.NoGui) run.RecordSkipped("image-wpf-page-isolation-and-layout", "--no-gui skips the image tool window.");
        else
        {
            await run.CaseAsync("image-wpf-page-isolation-and-layout", () => ImageObfuscationWindowAcceptance.RunAsync(run, formatSource, engine));
            await run.CaseAsync("image-wpf-active-close-waits-for-cancellation", () => ImageObfuscationWindowAcceptance.RunCloseAsync(run, formatSource));
        }
        run.AssertSourcesUnchanged();
        run.ImageObfuscationAcceptancePassed = run.Failures == before && !run.NoGui;
    }
    internal static byte[] Pixels(int width, int height, int bits)
    {
        var stride = bits == 16 ? 8 : 4; var data = new byte[width * height * stride];
        for (var i = 0; i < width * height; i++) for (var c = 0; c < 4; c++)
        {
            if (bits == 8) data[i * stride + c] = c == 3 ? new byte[] { 0, 1, 127, 255 }[i % 4] : (byte)((i * 17 + c * 43) & 255);
            else BinaryPrimitives.WriteUInt16BigEndian(data.AsSpan(i * stride + c * 2), c == 3 ? new ushort[] { 0, 1, 32767, 65535 }[i % 4] : (ushort)((i * 997 + c * 12289) & 65535));
        }
        return data;
    }
    internal static void WritePng(string path, int width, int height, int bits, byte[] data)
    {
        using var file = File.Create(path); file.Write(new byte[] { 137, 80, 78, 71, 13, 10, 26, 10 });
        var header = new byte[13]; BinaryPrimitives.WriteInt32BigEndian(header, width); BinaryPrimitives.WriteInt32BigEndian(header.AsSpan(4), height); header[8] = (byte)bits; header[9] = 6;
        WriteChunk(file, "IHDR", header);
        using var packed = new MemoryStream();
        using (var zlib = new ZLibStream(packed, System.IO.Compression.CompressionLevel.Optimal, true))
            for (var row = 0; row < height; row++) { zlib.WriteByte(0); zlib.Write(data, row * width * (bits == 16 ? 8 : 4), width * (bits == 16 ? 8 : 4)); }
        WriteChunk(file, "IDAT", packed.ToArray()); WriteChunk(file, "IEND", []);
    }
    private static void WriteChunk(Stream file, string tag, byte[] payload)
    {
        Span<byte> word = stackalloc byte[4]; BinaryPrimitives.WriteInt32BigEndian(word, payload.Length); file.Write(word);
        var body = Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes(tag).Concat(payload).ToArray(); file.Write(body);
        uint crc = 0xffffffff; foreach (var value in body) { crc ^= value; for (var i = 0; i < 8; i++) crc = (crc & 1) == 0 ? crc >> 1 : (crc >> 1) ^ 0xedb88320; }
        BinaryPrimitives.WriteUInt32BigEndian(word, ~crc); file.Write(word);
    }
    private static void CopyAncillaryChunks(string source, string destination)
    {
        var input = File.ReadAllBytes(source); var output = File.ReadAllBytes(destination); using var stream = new MemoryStream();
        stream.Write(output, 0, output.Length - 12);
        for (var at = 8; at + 12 <= input.Length;)
        {
            var length = BinaryPrimitives.ReadInt32BigEndian(input.AsSpan(at)); var tag = Encoding.ASCII.GetString(input, at + 4, 4);
            if (tag is not ("IHDR" or "IDAT" or "IEND")) stream.Write(input, at, length + 12);
            at += length + 12;
        }
        stream.Write(output, output.Length - 12, 12); File.WriteAllBytes(destination, stream.ToArray());
    }
    private static void RewriteMarker(string source, string target, string variant)
    {
        var bytes = File.ReadAllBytes(source); using var output = File.Create(target); output.Write(bytes, 0, 8); var found = false;
        for (var at = 8; at + 12 <= bytes.Length;)
        {
            var length = BinaryPrimitives.ReadInt32BigEndian(bytes.AsSpan(at)); var tag = Encoding.ASCII.GetString(bytes, at + 4, 4); var payload = bytes.AsSpan(at + 8, length).ToArray();
            if (tag == "tEXt" && Encoding.UTF8.GetString(payload).StartsWith("CatFileAssistant.Obfuscation\0", StringComparison.Ordinal))
            {
                found = true;
                payload = variant switch
                {
                    "future" => Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(Encoding.UTF8.GetString(payload).Replace("\"version\":1", "\"version\":999", StringComparison.Ordinal)),
                    "oversized" => payload.Concat(new byte[9000]).ToArray(),
                    "invalid" => Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes("CatFileAssistant.Obfuscation\0{"), _ => payload
                };
                WriteChunk(output, tag, payload); if (variant == "duplicate") WriteChunk(output, tag, payload);
            }
            else output.Write(bytes, at, length + 12);
            at += length + 12;
        }
        Program.Assert(found, "Marker mutation did not exercise a real marker.");
    }
    internal static async Task<byte[]> DecodeAsync(AcceptanceRun run, string path, int bits)
        => await FfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", path, "-frames:v", "1", "-pix_fmt", bits == 16 ? "rgba64be" : "rgba", "-f", "rawvideo", "pipe:1"]);
    private static async Task<bool> MatchesAsync(byte[] expected, AcceptanceRun run, string path, int bits)
    { var actual = await DecodeAsync(run, path, bits); return expected.SequenceEqual(actual); }
    private static async Task<byte[]> FfmpegAsync(AcceptanceRun run, string[] arguments)
    {
        var start = new ProcessStartInfo(Path.Combine(run.EngineDirectory, "ffmpeg.exe")) { UseShellExecute = false, CreateNoWindow = true, RedirectStandardError = true, RedirectStandardOutput = true };
        foreach (var value in new[] { "-hide_banner", "-nostdin", "-y", "-loglevel", "error" }.Concat(arguments)) start.ArgumentList.Add(value);
        using var process = Process.Start(start) ?? throw new IOException("Reference FFmpeg did not start.");
        using var bytes = new MemoryStream(); var read = process.StandardOutput.BaseStream.CopyToAsync(bytes); var error = process.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync();
        try { await process.WaitForExitAsync().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(60)); await read; }
        catch { if (!process.HasExited) process.Kill(true); await process.WaitForExitAsync(); throw; }
        Program.Assert(process.ExitCode == 0, "Reference FFmpeg failed: " + await error); return bytes.ToArray();
    }
    private static string Output(AcceptanceRun run, string name) => Path.Combine(run.Directory, "image-outputs", name);
    private static int[] EncoderIds() => Process.GetProcessesByName("ffmpeg").Select(process => { using (process) return process.Id; }).ToArray();
    private static void AssertCompleted(ImageObfuscationResult result) => Program.Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Completed && File.Exists(result.OutputPath), $"Image processing failed: {result.Error?.Code} {result.Error?.Message} {result.Error?.TechnicalDetails}");
    private static void AssertNoTemporaryFiles(string output) => Program.Assert(!Directory.Exists(output) || Directory.GetFiles(output, "*", SearchOption.AllDirectories).Length == 0, "Failed image operation left output files.");
    private sealed class CallbackProgress<T>(Action<T> action) : IProgress<T> { public void Report(T value) => action(value); }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/ImageObfuscationWindowAcceptance.cs ---
using System.IO;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Controls.Primitives;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

internal static class ImageObfuscationWindowAcceptance
{
    public static async Task RunAsync(AcceptanceRun run, string source, IImageObfuscationEngine imageEngine)
    {
        var presets = PresetCatalog.Load(Path.Combine(run.Root, "shared", "presets.json"));
        var settings = new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "image-ui-settings"));
        var model = new MainViewModel(new FfmpegConversionEngine(run.EngineDirectory), presets, settings,
            new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "image-ui-logs")), imageObfuscationEngine: imageEngine)
        { OutputDirectory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "image-outputs", "ui"), MotionPreference = MotionPreference.Reduced };
        var window = new MainWindow(model, () => true) { ShowActivated = false };
        run.StopActiveWindow = async () => { model.Cancel(); model.CancelImport(); await Task.WhenAll(model.ActiveRun, model.ActiveImport).WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(20)); };
        try
        {
            window.Show(); await FlushAsync(window);
            model.SetMode(ConversionMode.Image); await model.AddFilesAsync([source]); var conversion = model.VisibleQueue.Single();
            Control<RadioButton>(window, "NavImageObfuscate").RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(ButtonBase.ClickEvent)); await FlushAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(model.IsImageObfuscatePage && model.VisibleQueue.Count == 0 && !model.IsConversionPage && !model.IsMediaPage, "Image page leaked media conversion controls or rows.");
            foreach (var (name, mode) in new[] { ("ImageGilbertMode", ImageObfuscationMode.Gilbert), ("ImageBlockMode", ImageObfuscationMode.Block), ("ImagePixelMode", ImageObfuscationMode.Pixel) })
            {
                var radio = Control<RadioButton>(window, name); radio.RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(ButtonBase.ClickEvent, radio)); await FlushAsync(window);
                Program.Assert(model.SelectedImageObfuscationMode == mode && radio.IsChecked == true, "Image mode radio binding did not update.");
            }
            model.ImageObfuscationIterations = 3;
            await model.AddFilesAsync([source, source]);
            Program.Assert(model.VisibleImageObfuscationQueue.Count == 1 && model.ImageObfuscationQueue.Count == 1, "Duplicate import was not scoped to the image operation.");
            var row = model.VisibleImageObfuscationQueue.Single();
            foreach (var (width, height) in new[] { (1200d, 820d), (620d, 480d), (960d, 540d) })
            {
                window.Width = width; window.Height = height; await FlushAsync(window);
                foreach (var name in new[] { "StartButton", "AddFilesSide", "ImageObfuscationOptionsScroll", "ImageObfuscationQueueGrid" }) Program.AssertControlInside(window, name);
                Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, $"image-obfuscation-{width:0}x{height:0}");
            }
            window.Width = 1200; window.Height = 820; await FlushAsync(window);
            var active = model.RunQueueAsync();
            Program.Assert(model.IsProcessing && !model.CanEdit, "Image batch does not participate in global busy state.");
            model.SetMode(ConversionMode.Audio); model.SelectedImageObfuscationMode = ImageObfuscationMode.Block;
            Program.Assert(model.IsConversionPage && model.Mode == ConversionMode.Audio && row.Operation == ImageObfuscationOperation.Obfuscate &&
                row.Options.Mode == ImageObfuscationMode.Pixel && row.Options.Iterations == 3 && model.SelectedImageObfuscationMode == ImageObfuscationMode.Pixel &&
                !Control<RadioButton>(window, "ImageBlockMode").IsEnabled, "Busy browsing changed the active image operation or unlocked its parameters.");
            await active.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(60)); await FlushAsync(window);
            model.SetImageObfuscatePage(); await FlushAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(row.Status == JobStatus.Completed && row.OutputPath is not null && row.Statistics?.OutputBytes > 0 && conversion.Status == JobStatus.Pending, "Image processing ran another queue or lost its result.");
            Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, "image-obfuscation-completed");
            var marked = row.OutputPath!;
            model.SetImageDeobfuscatePage(); await model.AddFilesAsync([marked]);
            var restore = model.VisibleImageObfuscationQueue.Single();
            Program.Assert(restore.UsesStoredOptions && restore.Options.Mode == ImageObfuscationMode.Pixel && restore.Options.Iterations == 3 && model.ImageObfuscationQueue.Count == 2, "Restoration did not detect stored options or mixed queues.");
            model.SelectedImageObfuscationMode = ImageObfuscationMode.Gilbert; model.ImageObfuscationIterations = 10;
            Program.Assert(restore.Options.Mode == ImageObfuscationMode.Pixel && restore.Options.Iterations == 3, "Manual options overwrote automatic restoration information.");
            await model.RunQueueAsync().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(60)); await FlushAsync(window);
            var originalPixels = await ImageObfuscationAcceptance.DecodeAsync(run, source, 8);
            var restoredPixels = await ImageObfuscationAcceptance.DecodeAsync(run, restore.OutputPath!, 8);
            Program.Assert(restore.Status == JobStatus.Completed && restore.Statistics?.Verified == true &&
                originalPixels.SequenceEqual(restoredPixels), "UI restoration failed its exact pixel check.");
            Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, "image-deobfuscation-verified");
