            model.Clear(); Program.Assert(model.VisibleImageObfuscationQueue.Count == 0 && model.ImageObfuscationQueue.Contains(row), "Clearing restore rows cleared the mixing queue.");
            model.SetImageObfuscatePage(); Program.Assert(model.VisibleImageObfuscationQueue.Count == 1 && row.Options.Mode == ImageObfuscationMode.Pixel && row.Options.Iterations == 3, "Changing defaults rewrote completed mixing history.");
            model.SetMode(ConversionMode.Image); Program.Assert(model.VisibleQueue.Count == 1 && ReferenceEquals(model.VisibleQueue.Single(), conversion), "Media conversion queue was not preserved.");
            var reopened = new MainViewModel(new FfmpegConversionEngine(run.EngineDirectory), presets, settings, imageObfuscationEngine: imageEngine);
            Program.Assert(reopened.SelectedImageObfuscationMode == ImageObfuscationMode.Gilbert && reopened.ImageObfuscationIterations == 10, "Image preferences were not remembered.");
            run.Evidence["image-wpf-page-isolation-and-layout"] = new { separateQueues = true, busyMutationBlocked = true, autoParametersProtected = true,
                completedHistoryPreserved = true, preferencesRemembered = true, minimumWindow = "620x480", exactRestoration = true };
        }
        finally
        {
            model.Cancel(); model.CancelImport();
            try { await Task.WhenAll(model.ActiveRun, model.ActiveImport).WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(20)); }
            finally { window.Close(); run.StopActiveWindow = null; MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System); }
        }
    }
    private static T Control<T>(MainWindow window, string name) where T : FrameworkElement => window.FindName(name) as T ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing image control: " + name);
    private static async Task FlushAsync(MainWindow window) { await window.Dispatcher.InvokeAsync(() => { window.UpdateLayout(); }, DispatcherPriority.ContextIdle); await Task.Delay(30); }

    public static async Task RunCloseAsync(AcceptanceRun run, string source)
    {
        var imageEngine = new BlockingImageEngine();
        var model = new MainViewModel(presets: PresetCatalog.Load(Path.Combine(run.Root, "shared", "presets.json")),
            settings: new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "image-close-settings")), imageObfuscationEngine: imageEngine);
        var window = new MainWindow(model, () => true) { ShowActivated = false };
        try
        {
            window.Show(); model.SetImageObfuscatePage(); await model.AddFilesAsync([source]);
            var active = model.RunQueueAsync(); await imageEngine.Started.Task.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10));
            window.Close(); await active.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10)); await FlushAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(imageEngine.CancellationObserved && !window.IsVisible && !model.IsProcessing && model.VisibleImageObfuscationQueue.Single().Status == JobStatus.Cancelled,
                "Closing an active image window did not wait for and record cancellation.");
        }
        finally { model.Cancel(); model.CancelImport(); await Task.WhenAll(model.ActiveRun, model.ActiveImport).WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10)); if (window.IsVisible) window.Close(); }
    }
    private sealed class BlockingImageEngine : IImageObfuscationEngine
    {
        public TaskCompletionSource Started { get; } = new(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
        public bool CancellationObserved { get; private set; }
        public Task<ImageObfuscationInfo> InspectAsync(string path, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default) => Task.FromResult(new ImageObfuscationInfo(65, 47, 8, true, false));
        public async Task<ImageObfuscationResult> ProcessAsync(ImageObfuscationRequest request, IProgress<ImageObfuscationProgress>? progress = null, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            Started.TrySetResult();
            try { await Task.Delay(Timeout.Infinite, cancellationToken); }
            catch (OperationCanceledException) { CancellationObserved = true; throw; }
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Blocked operation unexpectedly resumed.");
        }
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/MotionAcceptance.cs ---
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Animation;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Controls;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

internal static class MotionAcceptance
{
    public static void AssertVisibleCatsAnimating(DependencyObject root)
    {
        var cats = FindCats(root).ToArray();
        Program.Assert(cats.Length >= 2, "The main window does not contain both full and queue cat controls.");
        foreach (var cat in cats.Where(cat => !cat.IsVisible)) AssertMotionStopped(cat, "invisible main-window cat");
        Program.Assert(cats.Count(cat => cat.HasActiveMotion) <= 1, "Both main-window cats are running decorative motion together.");
    }

    private static IEnumerable<CatView> FindCats(DependencyObject root)
    {
        if (root is CatView cat) yield return cat;
        for (var index = 0; index < VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(root); index++)
            foreach (var child in FindCats(VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(root, index))) yield return child;
    }

    public static async Task RunModelAsync(AcceptanceRun run, IReadOnlyList<FormatPreset> presets)
    {
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "motion-model");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var store = new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(directory, "settings"));
        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(store.FilePath)!);
        // A settings file written before motion preferences existed must remain readable.
        File.WriteAllText(store.FilePath, "{\"OutputDirectory\":\"motion-output\",\"PresetIds\":{}}");
        var engine = new ControlledEngine();
        var model = new MainViewModel(engine, presets, store, new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(directory, "logs")))
        { OutputDirectory = Path.Combine(directory, "outputs") };
        try
        {
            Program.Assert(model.MotionPreference == MotionPreference.System, "Old settings did not default to system motion preferences.");
            model.MotionPreference = MotionPreference.Reduced;
            Program.Assert(MotionPreferences.ReduceMotion, "The reduced preference did not reach the shared motion controller.");
            var reopened = new MainViewModel(engine, presets, store, new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(directory, "logs")));
            Program.Assert(reopened.MotionPreference == MotionPreference.Reduced, "Motion preferences did not survive reopening.");
            Program.Assert(reopened.OutputDirectory == model.OutputDirectory, "Saving motion preferences lost the output directory.");
            model.MotionPreference = MotionPreference.System;

            var statusSource = Path.Combine(directory, "progress-status.mp4");
            File.WriteAllText(statusSource, "Progress status presentation fixture.");
            var progressRow = new ConversionJobViewModel(statusSource, new(true, true, 1, "mp4", "h264", "aac"), presets.First(preset => preset.Mode == ConversionMode.Video));
            progressRow.Status = JobStatus.Processing;
            progressRow.Progress = 99.5;
            Program.Assert(!progressRow.StatusText.Contains("100", StringComparison.Ordinal) && progressRow.StatusText.Contains("99", StringComparison.Ordinal),
                "A still-processing file rounded 99.5% to 100% before output commitment.");
            progressRow.Progress = 100;
            Program.Assert(!progressRow.StatusText.Contains("100", StringComparison.Ordinal), "Processing status claimed 100% before completion.");
            progressRow.Status = JobStatus.Completed;
            Program.Assert(progressRow.StatusText == "已完成", "A committed completed file still displayed processing progress.");

            string Source(string name)
            {
                var path = Path.Combine(directory, name + ".mp4");
                File.WriteAllText(path, "Deterministic model-only test fixture; not a real media conversion.");
                return path;
            }

            Program.Assert(model.CompletionCue == 0, "A new model already contained a completion cue.");
            await model.AddFilesAsync([Source("first"), Source("second")]);
            await model.RunQueueAsync();
            Program.Assert(model.CompletedCount == 2 && model.CompletionCue == 1, "An all-success batch did not emit exactly one cue.");
            model.SetDragging(true); model.SetDragging(false);
            Program.Assert(model.AssistantState == AssistantState.SUCCESS && model.CompletionCue == 1,
                "Restoring success after drag produced a duplicate cue.");
            await model.RunQueueAsync();
            Program.Assert(model.CompletionCue == 1, "Running a queue with no pending jobs emitted a cue.");

            model.Clear();
            await model.AddFilesAsync([Source("third")]);
            await model.RunQueueAsync();
            Program.Assert(model.CompletionCue == 2, "A new successful batch did not advance the cue.");

            model.Clear();
            await model.AddFilesAsync([Source("mixed-success"), Source("mixed-failed")]);
            await model.RunQueueAsync();
            Program.Assert(model.CompletedCount == 1 && model.FailedCount == 1 && model.AssistantState == AssistantState.ERROR,
                "The controlled mixed batch did not exercise partial failure.");
            Program.Assert(model.CompletionCue == 2, "Partial failure emitted a success cue.");

            model.Clear();
            engine.HoldConversion = true;
            engine.Started = false;
            await model.AddFilesAsync([Source("cancelled"), Source("waiting")]);
            var active = model.RunQueueAsync();
            await WaitAsync(() => engine.Started, "Controlled cancellation never started.");
            model.Cancel();
            await active.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5));
            Program.Assert(model.Queue[0].Status == JobStatus.Cancelled && model.Queue[1].Status == JobStatus.Pending,
                "Controlled cancellation did not leave the pending file available.");
            Program.Assert(model.CompletionCue == 2 && model.AssistantState == AssistantState.IDLE, "Cancellation emitted a success cue.");
            run.Evidence["motionModel"] = new
            {
                oldSettingsDefault = "System", persistedPreference = "Reduced", successfulBatches = 2,
                finalCompletionCue = model.CompletionCue, mixedFailureAndCancellationDoNotCelebrate = true,
                method = "Deterministic IConversionEngine double; full runner separately checks cues during real FFmpeg conversions."
            };
        }
        finally { model.Cancel(); model.CancelImport(); MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System); }
    }

    public static async Task RunControlAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var originalSystemAnimation = SystemParameters.ClientAreaAnimation;
        using var controlledMotion = MotionPreferences.OverrideSystemMotionForTesting(true);
        MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System);
        var cat = new CatView { Width = 144, Height = 144, IsEmpty = true };
        var host = new Grid();
        host.Children.Add(cat);
        // Keep the actual visual exposed without taking keyboard focus. An occluded
        // WPF window can throttle animation ticks past the entire 160 ms blink.
        var window = new Window { Title = "猫咪动效验收", Width = 360, Height = 280, Content = host, ShowActivated = false, Topmost = true };
        using var activeFrames = new RenderSampler();
        activeFrames.Start();
        window.Show();
        try
        {
            await SettleAsync();
            Program.Assert(!cat.IsSleeping, "A new cat started asleep.");
            var breathing = await ObserveTransformMotionAsync(cat, "BreathOffset", 500, .02, "idle breathing");
            await CheckBlinkLifecycleAsync(run, cat, host, window);
            cat.State = AssistantState.SUCCESS;
            await SettleAsync();
            Program.Assert(cat.CelebrationCount == 0, "Setting success without a batch cue celebrated.");
            cat.CompletionCue = 1;
            var celebration = await ObserveTransformMotionAsync(cat, "BodyOffset", 620, 1, "unique completion celebration");
            Program.Assert(cat.CelebrationCount == 1, "The visible first completion cue was not consumed exactly once.");
            run.Evidence["actualCatTransformMotion"] = new
            {
                breathing, celebration,
                method = "Actual production TranslateTransform.Y sampled every 16 ms; a clock flag or celebration count alone does not prove movement.",
                windowTopmost = true, showActivated = false,
                visibilityPolicy = "The test window stays exposed above the foreground app to avoid WPF occlusion throttling; it is closed in finally."
            };
            cat.State = AssistantState.DRAGGING;
            cat.State = AssistantState.SUCCESS;
            cat.CompletionCue = 1;
            await SettleAsync();
            Program.Assert(cat.CelebrationCount == 1, "Success restored after dragging replayed celebration.");

            cat.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
            await SettleAsync();
            AssertMotionStopped(cat, "collapsed");
            cat.CompletionCue = 2;
            await SettleAsync();
            cat.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
            await SettleAsync();
            Program.Assert(cat.CelebrationCount == 1, "A cue received while hidden replayed on visibility restoration.");

            window.WindowState = WindowState.Minimized;
            await SettleAsync();
            AssertMotionStopped(cat, "minimized");
            window.WindowState = WindowState.Normal;
            await SettleAsync();
            Program.Assert(cat.CelebrationCount == 1, "Restoring a minimized window replayed success.");

            cat.State = AssistantState.PROCESSING;
            await SettleAsync();
            if (!MotionPreferences.ReduceMotion) Program.Assert(cat.HasActiveMotion, "Visible processing has no active decorative motion.");
            MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.Reduced);
            await SettleAsync();
            AssertMotionStopped(cat, "reduced preference");
            cat.State = AssistantState.SUCCESS;
            cat.CompletionCue = 3;
            await SettleAsync();
            Program.Assert(cat.CelebrationCount == 2, "Reduced motion did not consume the visible completion cue.");
            AssertMotionStopped(cat, "reduced success");
            MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System);
            await SettleAsync();
            Program.Assert(cat.CelebrationCount == 2, "Turning motion back on replayed a consumed cue.");

            cat.State = AssistantState.IDLE;
            cat.IsEmpty = true;
            cat.SetIdleElapsedForTesting(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(119));
            await SettleAsync();
            Program.Assert(!cat.IsSleeping, "The cat slept before the 120-second idle threshold.");
            cat.SetIdleElapsedForTesting(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(121));
            await Task.Delay(900);
            Program.Assert(cat.IsSleeping, "An empty inactive cat did not enter the sleeping pose.");
            cat.Wake();
            await SettleAsync();
            Program.Assert(!cat.IsSleeping, "Explicit interaction did not wake the sleeping cat.");
            cat.IsEmpty = false;
            cat.SetIdleElapsedForTesting(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(121));
            await SettleAsync();
            Program.Assert(!cat.IsSleeping, "A cat with a nonempty queue went to sleep.");

            host.Children.Remove(cat);
            await SettleAsync();
            AssertMotionStopped(cat, "unloaded");
            host.Children.Add(cat);
            await SettleAsync();
            Program.Assert(cat.CelebrationCount == 2, "Reloading the same control replayed a consumed cue.");
            await CheckProgressTruthAsync(run, host);
            await CaptureCatPosesAsync(run, cat);
            run.Evidence["motionControlLifecycle"] = new
            {
                cat.CelebrationCount, duplicateCueSuppressed = true, hiddenCueSuppressed = true,
                minimizedAndUnloadedClocksStopped = true, reducedMotionChangedLive = true,
                sleepThresholdSeconds = 120, sleepClock = "internal elapsed-time injection; production timer remains unchanged",
                transformClocksChecked = true, dpi = VisualTreeHelper.GetDpi(cat).PixelsPerInchX,
                systemClientAreaAnimation = originalSystemAnimation, controlledAnimationOverride = true,
                policy = "Test-only scope exercises normal and reduced paths without changing Windows settings.",
                activeRendering = activeFrames.Metrics,
                samplingMethod = "CompositionTarget.Rendering observer stays subscribed for the control lifecycle, using the same real render scheduling sampler as performance acceptance.",
                samplingBoundary = "Topmost non-activating test window and active rendering observation prevent occlusion throttling from skipping short animations; this does not measure unattended occluded-window animation cadence."
            };
        }
        finally
        {
            window.Close();
            await SettleAsync();
            AssertMotionStopped(cat, "closed window");
            activeFrames.Stop();
            MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System);
        }
    }

    public static async Task RunPerformanceAsync(AcceptanceRun run, IConversionEngine engine, IReadOnlyList<FormatPreset> presets)
    {
        var originalSystemAnimation = SystemParameters.ClientAreaAnimation;
        using var controlledMotion = MotionPreferences.OverrideSystemMotionForTesting(true);
        var model = new MainViewModel(engine, presets, new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "motion-perf-settings")),
            new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "motion-perf-logs")))
        { OutputDirectory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "outputs", "motion-performance") };
        model.SelectedPreset = presets.First(preset => preset.Mode == ConversionMode.Video && preset.ExpectedCodec == "hevc");
        var window = new MainWindow(model, () => true) { ShowActivated = false, Topmost = true };
        run.StopActiveWindow = async () => { model.Cancel(); await model.ActiveRun.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(15)); };
        window.Show();
        try
        {
            model.MotionPreference = MotionPreference.System;
            await Task.Delay(800); // Exclude load, JIT and the entrance transition from frame sampling.
            var systemEnabled = !MotionPreferences.ReduceMotion;
            using var idle = new RenderSampler();
            idle.Start();
            await Task.Delay(5000);
            idle.Stop();

            var emptyCat = window.FindName("EmptyCat") as CatView ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Empty cat not found for response sampling.");
            var responseMilliseconds = new List<double>();
            for (var index = 0; index < 24; index++)
            {
                using var nextFrame = new RenderSampler();
                nextFrame.Start();
                var watch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
                emptyCat.IsHovered = !emptyCat.IsHovered;
                await nextFrame.NextFrame.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(2));
                responseMilliseconds.Add(watch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds);
                await Task.Delay(40);
            }
            emptyCat.IsHovered = false;

            // Warm each preference once; paired alternating order reduces JIT and thermal/order bias.
            foreach (var preference in new[] { MotionPreference.System, MotionPreference.Reduced })
            {
                model.MotionPreference = preference;
                model.Clear();
                await model.AddFilesAsync([run.LongVideo]);
                await model.RunQueueAsync().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5));
                RequireSingleCompleted(model);
            }
            var durationsOn = new List<double>();
            var durationsReduced = new List<double>();
            var framesOn = new List<RenderMetrics>();
            var framesReduced = new List<RenderMetrics>();
            for (var pair = 0; pair < 3; pair++)
            {
                var order = pair % 2 == 0 ? new[] { MotionPreference.System, MotionPreference.Reduced } : new[] { MotionPreference.Reduced, MotionPreference.System };
                foreach (var preference in order)
                {
                    model.MotionPreference = preference;
                    model.Clear();
                    await model.AddFilesAsync([run.LongVideo]);
                    await Task.Delay(250);
                    using var sampler = new RenderSampler();
                    sampler.Start();
                    var watch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
                    await model.RunQueueAsync().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5));
                    watch.Stop(); sampler.Stop();
                    RequireSingleCompleted(model);
                    var metrics = sampler.Metrics;
                    if (preference == MotionPreference.System) { durationsOn.Add(watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds); framesOn.Add(metrics); }
                    else { durationsReduced.Add(watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds); framesReduced.Add(metrics); }
                    Console.WriteLine($"MOTION {preference} pair {pair + 1}: {watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds:F3}s; render scheduling p95 {metrics.P95Milliseconds:F2}ms");
                }
            }

            var onMedian = Percentile(durationsOn, .5);
            var reducedMedian = Percentile(durationsReduced, .5);
            var differencePercent = Math.Abs(onMedian - reducedMedian) / reducedMedian * 100;
            var idleMetrics = idle.Metrics;
            var renderingBudgetPassed = idleMetrics.Samples >= 30 && idleMetrics.P95Milliseconds <= 20 && framesOn.All(value => value.Samples >= 30 && value.P95Milliseconds <= 20);
            var responseBudgetPassed = responseMilliseconds.Max() <= 100;
            var conversionBudgetPassed = differencePercent <= 3;
            run.Evidence["motionPerformance"] = new
            {
                measurement = "CompositionTarget.Rendering callback scheduling in a topmost non-activating test window to avoid occlusion throttling, measured with Stopwatch; not GPU present/frame-duration measurement",
                motionEnabledDuringMeasurement = systemEnabled, systemClientAreaAnimation = originalSystemAnimation,
                controlledAnimationOverride = true,
                overrideMethod = "Test-only application policy scope; Windows settings are not changed. Reduced preference remains effective.",
                monitorDpi = VisualTreeHelper.GetDpi((Visual)window.Content).PixelsPerInchX, renderTier = RenderCapability.Tier >> 16,
                idle = idleMetrics, whileConvertingSystem = framesOn, whileConvertingReduced = framesReduced,
                hoverToNextRenderMilliseconds = responseMilliseconds, responseP95Milliseconds = Percentile(responseMilliseconds, .95),
                responseMaxMilliseconds = responseMilliseconds.Max(), conversionSystemSeconds = durationsOn,
                conversionReducedSeconds = durationsReduced, systemMedianSeconds = onMedian, reducedMedianSeconds = reducedMedian,
                medianDifferencePercent = differencePercent, source = run.LongVideo, preset = model.SelectedPreset!.Id,
                budgets = new { renderSchedulingP95Milliseconds = 20, responseMaxMilliseconds = 100, conversionMedianDifferencePercent = 3 },
                renderingBudgetPassed, responseBudgetPassed, conversionBudgetPassed,
                limitations = new[] { "Desktop refresh/compositor and host load affect callback timings.", "Enabled-motion path was explicitly enabled by an internal test scope; recorded Windows defaults remain unchanged.", "No claim about physical input latency, GPU-presented 60 FPS, untested Windows versions, or other DPI configurations." }
            };
            run.SaveReport();
            Program.Assert(systemEnabled, "System animation preference is disabled; enabled-motion performance budget cannot be validated in this session.");
            Program.Assert(renderingBudgetPassed, "Render scheduling p95 exceeded 20 ms or too few callbacks were observed. See recorded samples.");
            Program.Assert(responseBudgetPassed, "Hover-to-next-render response exceeded 100 ms. See recorded samples.");
            Program.Assert(conversionBudgetPassed, $"Enabled/reduced conversion median difference was {differencePercent:F2}% (budget 3%).");
        }
        finally
        {
            model.Cancel(); model.CancelImport();
            await Task.WhenAll(model.ActiveRun, model.ActiveImport).WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(15));
            window.Close(); run.StopActiveWindow = null;
            MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System);
        }
    }

    private static void RequireSingleCompleted(MainViewModel model)
        => Program.Assert(model.Queue.Count == 1 && model.Queue[0].Status == JobStatus.Completed && File.Exists(model.Queue[0].OutputPath), "Performance workload failed to commit a real output.");

    private static async Task CheckProgressTruthAsync(AcceptanceRun run, Grid host)
    {
        var bar = new ProgressBar { Minimum = 0, Maximum = 100, Width = 220, Height = 6, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Bottom, Margin = new Thickness(12) };
        host.Children.Add(bar);
        try
        {
            MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System);
            await SettleAsync();
            var settleWatch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
            UiMotion.SetSmoothProgress(bar, 65);
            Program.Assert(bar.HasAnimatedProperties,
                $"Enabled progress did not register an animation clock: value={bar.Value:0.###}, hasClock={bar.HasAnimatedProperties}.");
            var observed = new List<double>();
            for (var sample = 0; sample < 12; sample++)
            {
                observed.Add(bar.Value);
                Program.Assert(bar.Value >= 0 && bar.Value <= 65, "Interpolated progress exceeded its confirmed target.");
                await Task.Delay(16);
            }
            // WPF starts a new animation on a subsequent render tick. This is a
            // bounded clock-cleanup check; performance budgets stay in their own run.
            while (Math.Abs(bar.Value - 65) >= .001 || bar.HasAnimatedProperties)
            {
                Program.Assert(settleWatch.Elapsed < TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1),
                    $"Progress did not settle and release its clock within one second: value={bar.Value:0.###}, hasClock={bar.HasAnimatedProperties}, elapsed={settleWatch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds:0}ms, samples=[{string.Join(", ", observed.Select(value => value.ToString("0.###")))}].");
                observed.Add(bar.Value);
                Program.Assert(bar.Value >= 0 && bar.Value <= 65, "Interpolated progress exceeded its confirmed target while settling.");
                await Task.Delay(16);
            }
            settleWatch.Stop();
            Program.Assert(observed.Any(value => value < 65 - .001),
                $"Enabled progress had no observed value below its confirmed target: samples=[{string.Join(", ", observed.Select(value => value.ToString("0.###")))}], value={bar.Value:0.###}, hasClock={bar.HasAnimatedProperties}.");
            UiMotion.SetSmoothProgress(bar, 99.5);
            await Task.Delay(30);
            Program.Assert(bar.Value <= 99.5, "Progress approached 100% before its real target.");
            MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.Reduced);
            Program.Assert(Math.Abs(bar.Value - 99.5) < .001 && !bar.HasAnimatedProperties, "Reduced motion did not immediately synchronize progress to the confirmed target.");
            MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System);
            UiMotion.SetSmoothProgress(bar, 100);
            UiMotion.SetSmoothProgress(bar, 0);
            Program.Assert(bar.Value == 0 && !bar.HasAnimatedProperties, "Reset/retry progress animated down or retained an obsolete clock.");
            await Task.Delay(180);
            Program.Assert(bar.Value == 0, "An old completed animation resurrected progress after retry.");
            UiMotion.SetSmoothProgress(bar, 40);
            host.Children.Remove(bar);
            await SettleAsync();
            Program.Assert(Math.Abs(bar.Value - 40) < .001 && !bar.HasAnimatedProperties, "Unloading a progress bar left its interpolation clock active.");
            run.Evidence["progressPresentationTruth"] = new
            {
                samplesToTarget65 = observed, target65SettledAndClockReleasedMilliseconds = settleWatch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds,
                clockCleanupTimeoutMilliseconds = 1000, neverExceedsConfirmedTarget = true,
                reducedSynchronizesImmediately = true, retrySnapsDownImmediately = true, unloadReleasesClock = true,
                nearCompleteStatus = "Processing 99.5 or 100 remains 99% until JobStatus.Completed; completed text is 已完成."
            };
        }
        finally { UiMotion.Stop(bar); host.Children.Remove(bar); MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System); }
    }

    private static async Task CaptureCatPosesAsync(AcceptanceRun run, CatView cat)
    {
        // Freeze the rig's actual production poses for inspectable static images.
        MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.Reduced);
        cat.Presentation = CatPresentation.Full;
        cat.Width = cat.Height = 144;
        cat.IsEmpty = true;
        cat.IsHovered = false;
        cat.Wake();
        var captures = new List<object>();
        foreach (var state in new[] { AssistantState.IDLE, AssistantState.DRAGGING, AssistantState.PROCESSING, AssistantState.SUCCESS, AssistantState.ERROR })
        {
            cat.State = state;
            await SettleAsync();
            captures.Add(SaveCatRender(run, cat, "full-" + state.ToString().ToLowerInvariant(), 192));
        }
        cat.State = AssistantState.IDLE;
        cat.SetIdleElapsedForTesting(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(121));
        await SettleAsync();
        Program.Assert(cat.IsSleeping, "Sleeping-pose capture did not enter sleep.");
        captures.Add(SaveCatRender(run, cat, "full-sleep", 192));
        cat.Wake();
        cat.Presentation = CatPresentation.Compact;
        cat.Width = cat.Height = 56;
        await SettleAsync();
        captures.Add(SaveCatRender(run, cat, "compact-56", 192));
        cat.Width = 42; cat.Height = 38;
        await SettleAsync();
        captures.Add(SaveCatRender(run, cat, "queue-avatar-42x38", 192));
        cat.Presentation = CatPresentation.Full;
        cat.Width = cat.Height = 144;
        await SettleAsync();
        foreach (var dpi in new[] { 96d, 120d, 144d, 192d }) captures.Add(SaveCatRender(run, cat, "full-idle-export-" + dpi, dpi));
        run.Evidence["catPoseRenders"] = new
        {
            captures, method = "RenderTargetBitmap of the actual loaded WPF CatView at each static production pose; no manually drawn replacement image.",
            animationDuringCapture = "Reduced, to freeze the actual rig pose",
            limitation = "Requested bitmap DPI changes export resolution; it does not switch Windows scaling, execute OnDpiChanged, or prove cross-monitor DPI behavior."
        };
    }

    private static object SaveCatRender(AcceptanceRun run, CatView cat, string name, double exportDpi)
    {
        cat.UpdateLayout();
        Program.Assert(cat.ActualWidth > 0 && cat.ActualHeight > 0, "Cat render has no layout size.");
        var bitmap = new RenderTargetBitmap((int)Math.Ceiling(cat.ActualWidth * exportDpi / 96),
            (int)Math.Ceiling(cat.ActualHeight * exportDpi / 96), exportDpi, exportDpi, PixelFormats.Pbgra32);
        // Referencing the live production visual through a brush normalizes the host's
        // layout offset. Rendering the element directly also renders its parent offset.
        var drawingVisual = new DrawingVisual();
        using (var drawing = drawingVisual.RenderOpen())
            drawing.DrawRectangle(new VisualBrush(cat) { Stretch = Stretch.Fill }, null, new Rect(0, 0, cat.ActualWidth, cat.ActualHeight));
        bitmap.Render(drawingVisual);
        var alphaBounds = InspectVisiblePixelBounds(bitmap);
        var encoder = new PngBitmapEncoder();
        encoder.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(bitmap));
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "screenshots", "cat-poses");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var path = Path.Combine(directory, name + ".png");
        using (var output = File.Create(path)) encoder.Save(output);
        return new { path, cat.ActualWidth, cat.ActualHeight, bitmap.PixelWidth, bitmap.PixelHeight, exportDpi,
            actualHostDpi = VisualTreeHelper.GetDpi(cat).PixelsPerInchX, presentation = cat.Presentation.ToString(), state = cat.State.ToString(), cat.IsSleeping, alphaBounds };
    }

    private static object InspectVisiblePixelBounds(BitmapSource bitmap)
    {
        var stride = bitmap.PixelWidth * 4;
        var pixels = new byte[stride * bitmap.PixelHeight];
        bitmap.CopyPixels(pixels, stride, 0);
        var left = bitmap.PixelWidth;
        var top = bitmap.PixelHeight;
        var right = -1;
        var bottom = -1;
        var visible = 0;
        for (var y = 0; y < bitmap.PixelHeight; y++)
            for (var x = 0; x < bitmap.PixelWidth; x++)
            {
                if (pixels[y * stride + x * 4 + 3] <= 16) continue;
                visible++;
                left = Math.Min(left, x); top = Math.Min(top, y);
                right = Math.Max(right, x); bottom = Math.Max(bottom, y);
            }
        var width = Math.Max(0, right - left + 1);
        var height = Math.Max(0, bottom - top + 1);
        var coverage = (double)visible / (bitmap.PixelWidth * bitmap.PixelHeight);
        Program.Assert(coverage >= .12 && width >= bitmap.PixelWidth * .55 && height >= bitmap.PixelHeight * .4,
            $"Cat screenshot is blank or severely cropped: alpha coverage {coverage:P1}, visible bounds {width}x{height} in {bitmap.PixelWidth}x{bitmap.PixelHeight}.");
        return new { left, top, right, bottom, width, height, visiblePixelCount = visible, visiblePixelFraction = coverage,
            method = "Pbgra32 alpha > 16; broad shape and coverage sanity, not pixel-perfect visual approval" };
    }

    private static async Task SettleAsync()
    {
        await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle);
        await Task.Delay(180);
    }

    private static async Task<object> ObserveTransformMotionAsync(CatView cat, string transformName, int durationMilliseconds, double minimumRange, string reason)
    {
        var transform = cat.FindName(transformName) as TranslateTransform
            ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Production cat transform not found: " + transformName);
        var values = new List<double>();
        var watch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        do
        {
            Program.Assert(double.IsFinite(transform.Y), "Production transform has a non-finite Y during " + reason + ".");
            values.Add(transform.Y);
            await Task.Delay(16);
        } while (watch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds < durationMilliseconds);
        values.Add(transform.Y);
        var range = values.Max() - values.Min();
        Program.Assert(range >= minimumRange,
            $"The actual production transform did not move during {reason}: range={range:0.###}, samples=[{FormatSamples(values)}].");
        return new { transformName, durationMilliseconds, actualSampleMilliseconds = watch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds,
            minimumRange, observedRange = range, samples = values };
    }

    private static async Task CheckBlinkLifecycleAsync(AcceptanceRun run, CatView cat, Grid host, Window window)
    {
        var normalSamples = new List<double>();
        MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System);
        cat.State = AssistantState.IDLE;
        await SettleAsync();
        AssertEyeBaseline(cat, 0, "normal idle");
        await StartObservedBlinkAsync(cat, normalSamples);
        var completionWatch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        while (cat.IsBlinking || Math.Abs(cat.ClosedEyesOpacity) > .001)
        {
            Program.Assert(completionWatch.Elapsed < TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1),
                $"Normal blink did not return to the open-eye baseline: opacity={cat.ClosedEyesOpacity:0.###}, isBlinking={cat.IsBlinking}, samples=[{FormatSamples(normalSamples)}].");
            RecordEyeSample(cat, normalSamples);
            await Task.Delay(16);
        }
        RecordEyeSample(cat, normalSamples);
        Program.Assert(normalSamples.Any(value => value > .02), "The normal blink never changed the actual closed-eye opacity.");

        await StartObservedBlinkAsync(cat);
        cat.State = AssistantState.SUCCESS;
        AssertEyeBaseline(cat, 1, "switching from a blink to success");
        await Task.Delay(350);
        AssertEyeBaseline(cat, 1, "a cancelled idle blink completing after success");
        cat.BlinkForTesting();
        await SettleAsync();
        AssertEyeBaseline(cat, 1, "success cannot start an idle blink");

        cat.State = AssistantState.IDLE;
        await SettleAsync();
        await StartObservedBlinkAsync(cat);
        MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.Reduced);
        AssertEyeBaseline(cat, 0, "reduced motion during blink");
        await SettleAsync();
        AssertMotionStopped(cat, "reduced motion during blink");
        await Task.Delay(350);
        AssertEyeBaseline(cat, 0, "an obsolete blink callback after reducing motion");
        cat.State = AssistantState.SUCCESS;
        AssertEyeBaseline(cat, 1, "reduced success");
        await SettleAsync();
        AssertMotionStopped(cat, "reduced success eye pose");
        cat.State = AssistantState.IDLE;
        MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System);
        await SettleAsync();

        await StartObservedBlinkAsync(cat);
        cat.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
        AssertEyeBaseline(cat, 0, "hidden during blink");
        AssertMotionStopped(cat, "hidden during blink");
        cat.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
        await SettleAsync();
        AssertEyeBaseline(cat, 0, "restored after hidden blink");

        await StartObservedBlinkAsync(cat);
        window.WindowState = WindowState.Minimized;
        AssertEyeBaseline(cat, 0, "minimized during blink");
        AssertMotionStopped(cat, "minimized during blink");
        window.WindowState = WindowState.Normal;
        await SettleAsync();
        AssertEyeBaseline(cat, 0, "restored after minimized blink");

        await StartObservedBlinkAsync(cat);
        host.Children.Remove(cat);
        // Dispatch Unloaded without letting the 160 ms blink finish naturally.
        await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle);
        AssertEyeBaseline(cat, 0, "unloaded during blink");
        AssertMotionStopped(cat, "unloaded during blink");
        host.Children.Add(cat);
        await SettleAsync();
        AssertEyeBaseline(cat, 0, "reloaded after blink");

        foreach (var state in new[] { AssistantState.DRAGGING, AssistantState.PROCESSING, AssistantState.ERROR })
        {
            cat.State = AssistantState.IDLE;
            await SettleAsync();
            await StartObservedBlinkAsync(cat);
            cat.State = state;
            AssertEyeBaseline(cat, 0, "switching from blink to " + state);
            await Task.Delay(350);
            AssertEyeBaseline(cat, 0, "cancelled blink callback in " + state);
        }
        cat.State = AssistantState.IDLE;
        await SettleAsync();
        run.Evidence["blinkOpacityLifecycle"] = new
        {
            normalClosedEyeOpacitySamples = normalSamples, observedActualOpacityAnimation = true,
            idleBaseline = 0, successBaseline = 1,
            successDoesNotBlink = true, obsoleteIdleCompletionCannotOverwriteSuccess = true,
            reducedHiddenMinimizedUnloadedAndStateChangeCancelBlink = true,
            allUiElementAnimationClocksCheckedAfterStopping = true,
            method = "Production BlinkForTesting entry point and actual ClosedEyesOpacity; no replacement eye animation in tests."
        };
    }

    private static async Task StartObservedBlinkAsync(CatView cat, List<double>? samples = null)
    {
        var observations = samples ?? new List<double>();
        var beforeStart = DescribeBlinkState(cat);
        cat.BlinkForTesting();
        var afterStart = DescribeBlinkState(cat);
        var watch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        while (cat.ClosedEyesOpacity <= .02)
        {
            RecordEyeSample(cat, observations);
            Program.Assert(watch.Elapsed < TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(500),
                $"Blink did not animate closed-eye opacity: opacity={cat.ClosedEyesOpacity:0.###}, isBlinking={cat.IsBlinking}, samples=[{FormatSamples(observations)}]. Before: {beforeStart}. Immediately after start: {afterStart}. At timeout: {DescribeBlinkState(cat)}.");
            await Task.Delay(16);
        }
        RecordEyeSample(cat, observations);
        Program.Assert(cat.IsBlinking, "The observed closed-eye overlay changed without an active blink.");
    }

    private static string DescribeBlinkState(CatView cat)
    {
        const BindingFlags flags = BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance;
        var type = typeof(CatView);
        object? Property(string name) => type.GetProperty(name, flags)?.GetValue(cat);
        object? Field(string name) => type.GetField(name, flags)?.GetValue(cat);
        var window = Window.GetWindow(cat);
        var clock = Field("blinkClock") as AnimationClock;
        return $"State={cat.State}, IsEmpty={cat.IsEmpty}, IsSleeping={cat.IsSleeping}, HasActiveMotion={cat.HasActiveMotion}, IsLoaded={cat.IsLoaded}, IsVisible={cat.IsVisible}, ReduceMotion={MotionPreferences.ReduceMotion}, CanDisplay={Property("CanDisplay")}, CanMove={Property("CanMove")}, CanBlink={Property("CanBlink")}, attached={Field("attached")}, assetFailed={Field("assetFailed")}, IsBlinking={cat.IsBlinking}, clockState={clock?.CurrentState}, clockTime={clock?.CurrentTime}, ownerState={window?.WindowState}, ownerVisible={window?.IsVisible}, ownerActive={window?.IsActive}, LastPresentationError={cat.LastPresentationError ?? "none"}";
    }

    private static void RecordEyeSample(CatView cat, List<double> samples)
    {
        var opacity = cat.ClosedEyesOpacity;
        Program.Assert(double.IsFinite(opacity) && opacity >= 0 && opacity <= 1,
            $"Closed-eye opacity is outside [0,1]: {opacity}.");
        samples.Add(opacity);
    }

    private static void AssertEyeBaseline(CatView cat, double expected, string reason)
        => Program.Assert(!cat.IsBlinking && Math.Abs(cat.ClosedEyesOpacity - expected) < .001,
            $"Eye pose did not reset after {reason}: expected={expected}, opacity={cat.ClosedEyesOpacity:0.###}, isBlinking={cat.IsBlinking}.");

    private static string FormatSamples(IEnumerable<double> values)
        => string.Join(", ", values.Select(value => value.ToString("0.###")));

    private static void AssertMotionStopped(CatView cat, string reason)
    {
        Program.Assert(!cat.HasActiveMotion, "Decorative motion remained active after " + reason + ".");
        Program.Assert(CountTransformClocks(cat) == 0, "RenderTransform animation clocks remained after " + reason + ".");
        Program.Assert(CountUiElementClocks(cat) == 0, "UIElement animation clocks (including opacity) remained after " + reason + ".");
    }

    private static int CountUiElementClocks(DependencyObject parent)
    {
        var count = parent is UIElement { HasAnimatedProperties: true } ? 1 : 0;
        for (var index = 0; index < VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(parent); index++) count += CountUiElementClocks(VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(parent, index));
        return count;
    }

    private static int CountTransformClocks(DependencyObject parent)
    {
        var count = parent is UIElement element ? CountAnimatedTransform(element.RenderTransform) : 0;
        for (var index = 0; index < VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(parent); index++) count += CountTransformClocks(VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(parent, index));
        return count;
    }

    private static int CountAnimatedTransform(Transform transform)
        => (transform.HasAnimatedProperties ? 1 : 0) + (transform is TransformGroup group ? group.Children.Sum(CountAnimatedTransform) : 0);

    private static async Task WaitAsync(Func<bool> predicate, string message)
    {
        var watch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        while (!predicate()) { Program.Assert(watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds < 5, message); await Task.Delay(10); }
    }

    private static double Percentile(IEnumerable<double> values, double fraction)
    {
        var sorted = values.Order().ToArray();
        return sorted.Length == 0 ? 0 : sorted[Math.Clamp((int)Math.Ceiling(sorted.Length * fraction) - 1, 0, sorted.Length - 1)];
    }

    private sealed class ControlledEngine : IConversionEngine
    {
        public bool HoldConversion { get; set; }
        public bool Started { get; set; }
        public Task<MediaInfo> ProbeAsync(string path, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
            => Task.FromResult(new MediaInfo(true, true, 1, "mp4", "h264", "aac"));
        public async Task<ConversionResult> ConvertAsync(ConversionRequest request, IProgress<ConversionProgress>? progress = null, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            Started = true;
            try { await Task.Delay(HoldConversion ? Timeout.Infinite : 20, cancellationToken); }
            catch (OperationCanceledException) { return new(JobStatus.Cancelled, null, null); }
            if (request.SourcePath.Contains("failed", StringComparison.Ordinal)) return new(JobStatus.Failed, null, new("TEST_FAILURE", "Controlled failure", "Test fixture"));
            Directory.CreateDirectory(request.OutputDirectory);
            var output = Path.Combine(request.OutputDirectory, request.Id + "." + request.Preset.Extension);
            File.WriteAllText(output, "Model test output; real output validation occurs in FFmpeg acceptance.");
            return new(JobStatus.Completed, output, null);
        }
    }

    private sealed record RenderMetrics(int Samples, double MeanMilliseconds, double P50Milliseconds, double P95Milliseconds, double MaximumMilliseconds, double SchedulingCallbacksPerSecond);

    private sealed class RenderSampler : IDisposable
    {
        private readonly Stopwatch clock = new();
        private readonly List<double> intervals = [];
        private readonly TaskCompletionSource firstFrame = new(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
        private double? previous;
        private TimeSpan? renderingTime;
        private bool subscribed;
        public Task NextFrame => firstFrame.Task;
        public RenderMetrics Metrics => new(intervals.Count, intervals.Count == 0 ? 0 : intervals.Average(),
            Percentile(intervals, .5), Percentile(intervals, .95), intervals.Count == 0 ? 0 : intervals.Max(),
            intervals.Count == 0 ? 0 : 1000 / intervals.Average());
        public void Start() { if (subscribed) return; subscribed = true; clock.Start(); CompositionTarget.Rendering += OnRendering; }
        public void Stop() { if (!subscribed) return; subscribed = false; CompositionTarget.Rendering -= OnRendering; clock.Stop(); }
        private void OnRendering(object? sender, EventArgs args)
        {
            if (args is RenderingEventArgs rendering)
            {
                if (renderingTime == rendering.RenderingTime) return;
                renderingTime = rendering.RenderingTime;
            }
            var current = clock.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds;
            if (previous.HasValue) intervals.Add(current - previous.Value);
            previous = current;
            firstFrame.TrySetResult();
        }
        public void Dispose() => Stop();
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/PetAccessoryAcceptance.cs ---
using System.IO;
using System.Text.Json.Nodes;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

internal static class PetAccessoryAcceptance
{
    public static async Task RunAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        await run.CaseAsync("pet-v101-vector-accessories-follow-independent-head-neck-and-mirror", () => PoseAsync(run));
        await run.CaseAsync("pet-v101-foreground-paws-occlude-neck-accessories-without-hiding-star", () => OcclusionAsync(run));
        await run.CaseAsync("pet-legacy-v2-accessory-pose-and-occlusion-preserve-original-body", () => LegacyMetadataAsync(run));
        await run.CaseAsync("pet-v101-accessories-contact-real-cat-at-three-sizes-three-render-dpis", () => ProductionAsync(run));
    }

    private static Task LegacyMetadataAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var source = Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(typeof(PetSpriteView).Assembly.Location)!, "Assets", "Pet", "animations.json");
        var manifest = JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(source))!.AsObject();
        Program.Assert(manifest["version"]!.GetValue<int>() == 2, "The restored release must use the original v2 drawings.");
        using var original = new PetSpriteView(); original.LoadManifest(source);
        var body = original.GetFrameDiagnostics("idle")[0];
        foreach (var atlas in manifest["atlases"]!.AsArray())
            atlas!["file"] = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(source)!, atlas["file"]!.GetValue<string>()));
        var anchor = manifest["animations"]!["idle"]!["anchors"]![0]!;
        anchor["headAngle"] = 13; anchor["neckAngle"] = -7; anchor["accessoryScale"] = .9;
        anchor["accessoryOcclusion"] = JsonNode.Parse("[[[0.45,0.65],[0.6,0.65],[0.6,0.8],[0.45,0.8]]]");
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-accessories"); Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var fixture = Path.Combine(directory, "legacy-attachment-fixture.json"); File.WriteAllText(fixture, manifest.ToJsonString());
        using var view = new PetSpriteView(); view.LoadManifest(fixture); view.SetFrameForTesting("idle", 0);
        var pose = view.CurrentFrameDiagnostics!;
        Program.Assert(view.AssetError is null && view.UsesLegacyClips && pose.HeadAngle == 13 && pose.NeckAngle == -7 && pose.AccessoryScale == .9,
            "Original-format attachment metadata lost its rotation or size.");
        Program.Assert(view.CurrentAccessoryOcclusion is { IsFrozen: true } mask && mask.FillContains(new Point(80, 112)),
            "Original-format sleeping/head/paw occlusion did not reach the accessory view.");
        Program.Assert(pose.SourceRect == body.SourceRect && pose.Placement == body.Placement && pose.CanvasSize == body.CanvasSize,
            "Accessory metadata changed the original cat drawing or its placement.");
        run.Evidence["legacyAccessoryMetadata"] = new { originalFormat = 2, originalBodyPreserved = true, rotationAndScaleRetained = true, foregroundOcclusionRetained = true };
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }

    private static async Task OcclusionAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var view = new PetAccessoryView(); var host = new Grid(); host.Children.Add(view);
        view.SetEquipment(true, true, true); view.SetPose(new Point(107, 44), new Point(83, 106));
        var plain = Render(host, 160, 1); var foreground = new RectangleGeometry(new Rect(78, 102, 12, 18));
        view.SetNeckOcclusion(foreground); await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Render);
        var clipped = Render(host, 160, 1);
        static int OpaqueIn(BitmapSource source, Rect region)
        {
            var pixels = Pixels(source); var count = 0;
            for (var y = (int)region.Top; y < region.Bottom; y++) for (var x = (int)region.Left; x < region.Right; x++)
                if (pixels[(y * source.PixelWidth + x) * 4 + 3] > 16) count++;
            return count;
        }
        Program.Assert(OpaqueIn(plain, new Rect(78, 102, 12, 18)) >= 20 && OpaqueIn(clipped, new Rect(78, 102, 12, 18)) == 0,
            "The drawn foreground paw did not remove the collar/bell pixels behind it.");
        Program.Assert(OpaqueIn(clipped, new Rect(64, 102, 13, 12)) > 8 && DifferenceCount(plain, clipped) > 20,
            "The occlusion hid the entire necklace instead of only the foreground paw area.");
        var starRegion = view.PoseDiagnostics.StarBounds;
        Program.Assert(GoldCenter(plain, starRegion) == GoldCenter(clipped, starRegion), "Neck occlusion incorrectly hid or moved the star clip.");
        view.RenderTransform = new ScaleTransform(-1, 1, 80, 80); var mirrored = Render(host, 160, 1);
        Program.Assert(OpaqueIn(mirrored, new Rect(70, 102, 12, 18)) == 0, "The paw occlusion did not follow mirroring in the same artwork plane.");
        view.RenderTransform = Transform.Identity; view.SetNeckOcclusion(null); var restored = Render(host, 160, 1);
        Program.Assert(DifferenceCount(plain, restored) == 0 && view.CacheMode is null, "Clearing a paw mask changed the accessory geometry or retained a raster overlay.");
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-accessories"); Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        Save(clipped, Path.Combine(directory, "native-foreground-paw-occlusion.png"));
        run.Evidence["v101AccessoryOcclusion"] = new { nativeVectorSubtract = true, collarAndBellBehindPaw = true, starUnaffected = true, mirroredWithBody = true, clearingRestoresExactGeometry = true, bodyArtworkModified = false };
    }

    private static async Task PoseAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-accessories"); Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var view = new PetAccessoryView();
        var host = new Grid(); host.Children.Add(view);
        view.SetEquipment(true, true, true);
        view.SetPose(new Point(107, 44), new Point(83, 106));
        var still = Render(host, 160, 1);
        view.SetPose(new Point(103, 47), new Point(85, 104), 27, -13, 1.12);
        await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Render);
        var turned = Render(host, 160, 1);
        var diagnostics = view.PoseDiagnostics;
        var normalCenter = GoldCenter(turned, diagnostics.StarBounds);
        Program.Assert(DifferenceCount(still, turned) > 80, "Changing head/neck poses did not move the rendered accessories.");
        Program.Assert(diagnostics.StarBounds.Contains(diagnostics.StarAnchor), "The star geometry is detached from its attachment point.");
        Program.Assert(view.CacheMode is null && view.Effect is null, "Accessories use a raster cache or blur effect.");
        Save(turned, Path.Combine(directory, "native-head-neck-rotation.png"));
        view.RenderTransform = new ScaleTransform(-1, 1, 80, 80);
        await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Render);
        var mirrored = Render(host, 160, 1);
        var mirroredStarBounds = new Rect(160 - diagnostics.StarBounds.Right, diagnostics.StarBounds.Top, diagnostics.StarBounds.Width, diagnostics.StarBounds.Height);
        var mirrorCenter = GoldCenter(mirrored, mirroredStarBounds);
        Save(mirrored, Path.Combine(directory, "native-head-neck-rotation-mirrored.png"));
        Program.Assert(Math.Abs(mirrorCenter.X - (160 - normalCenter.X)) <= 1.2 && Math.Abs(mirrorCenter.Y - normalCenter.Y) <= .7,
            $"Mirroring the cat did not mirror the accessories around the same origin: {normalCenter} -> {mirrorCenter}.");
        view.SetEquipment(false, false, false);
        Program.Assert(Pixels(Render(host, 160, 1)).Where((_, index) => index % 4 == 3).All(alpha => alpha == 0), "Unequipping left accessory pixels behind.");
        run.Evidence["v101AccessoryPose"] = new { normalCenter, mirrorCenter, separateHeadAndNeckAngles = true, localScale = 1.12, nativeVector = true, rasterCache = false, blurEffect = false };
    }

    private static async Task ProductionAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-accessories"); Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        using var sprite = new PetSpriteView();
        sprite.LoadManifest(Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(typeof(PetSpriteView).Assembly.Location)!, "Assets", "Pet", "animations.json"));
        sprite.SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode.Reduced);
        var host = new Grid(); host.Children.Add(sprite);
        var window = new Window { Title = "猫咪配饰验收", Content = host, Width = 280, Height = 300, ShowActivated = false, Topmost = true };
        var observations = new List<object>();
        window.Show();
        try
        {
            foreach (var size in new[] { 120, 160, 200 })
            foreach (var state in new[] { "idle", "work", "walk" })
            {
                sprite.Width = sprite.Height = size;
                var frames = sprite.GetFrameDiagnostics(state);
                Program.Assert(frames.Count > 0, "Missing accessory test action: " + state);
                sprite.SetFrameForTesting(state, frames.Count / 2);
                window.UpdateLayout(); await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Render);
                var accessory = Descendants<PetAccessoryView>(sprite).Single();
                var pose = accessory.PoseDiagnostics;
                var toSprite = accessory.TransformToAncestor(sprite);
                var star = toSprite.Transform(pose.StarAnchor);
                var neck = toSprite.Transform(pose.NeckAnchor);
                Program.Assert(sprite.ContainsVisiblePixel(star), $"{state} star attachment floats outside the actual cat silhouette at {size} DIP.");
                Program.Assert(sprite.ContainsVisiblePixel(neck), $"{state} collar is detached from the actual neck/scarf at {size} DIP.");
                foreach (var dpiScale in new[] { 1d, 1.5d, 2d })
                {
                    sprite.SetAccessories(false, false); var plain = Render(sprite, size, dpiScale);
                    sprite.SetAccessories(true, true); var equipped = Render(sprite, size, dpiScale);
                    var changed = DifferenceCount(plain, equipped);
                    Program.Assert(changed >= 18 * dpiScale * dpiScale, $"{state} accessories vanished at {size} DIP / {96 * dpiScale} DPI.");
                    Program.Assert(accessory.CacheMode is null && accessory.Effect is null, "Production accessories became a blurred raster layer.");
                    var path = Path.Combine(directory, $"{state}-{size}dip-{96 * dpiScale:0}dpi.png"); Save(equipped, path);
                    observations.Add(new { state, sizeDip = size, renderDpi = 96 * dpiScale, star, neck, changedPixels = changed, file = path });
                }
            }
            run.Evidence["v101AccessoryProduction"] = new { observations,
                dpiMethod = "Actual production WPF control rendered at 96/144/192 DPI targets; this does not change Windows monitor DPI.",
                silhouetteContact = "Attachment points checked against the actual texture alpha before accessory drawing.", nativeVector = true };
        }
        finally { window.Close(); }
    }

    private static IEnumerable<T> Descendants<T>(DependencyObject parent) where T : DependencyObject
    {
        for (var index = 0; index < VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(parent); index++)
        {
            var child = VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(parent, index);
            if (child is T item) yield return item;
            foreach (var nested in Descendants<T>(child)) yield return nested;
        }
    }
    private static BitmapSource Render(FrameworkElement view, int size, double dpiScale)
    {
        view.Measure(new Size(size, size)); view.Arrange(new Rect(0, 0, size, size)); view.UpdateLayout();
        var bitmap = new RenderTargetBitmap((int)Math.Ceiling(size * dpiScale), (int)Math.Ceiling(size * dpiScale), 96 * dpiScale, 96 * dpiScale, PixelFormats.Pbgra32);
        bitmap.Render(view); bitmap.Freeze(); return bitmap;
    }
    private static byte[] Pixels(BitmapSource image)
    {
        var bytes = new byte[image.PixelWidth * image.PixelHeight * 4]; image.CopyPixels(bytes, image.PixelWidth * 4, 0); return bytes;
    }
    private static int DifferenceCount(BitmapSource before, BitmapSource after)
    {
        var a = Pixels(before); var b = Pixels(after); var count = 0;
        for (var index = 0; index < a.Length; index += 4)
            if (Math.Abs(a[index] - b[index]) + Math.Abs(a[index + 1] - b[index + 1]) + Math.Abs(a[index + 2] - b[index + 2]) + Math.Abs(a[index + 3] - b[index + 3]) > 24) count++;
        return count;
    }
    private static Point GoldCenter(BitmapSource image, Rect region)
    {
        var pixels = Pixels(image); var count = 0; var sumX = 0d; var sumY = 0d;
        for (var y = 0; y < image.PixelHeight; y++)
        for (var x = 0; x < image.PixelWidth; x++)
        {
            if (!region.Contains(new Point(x + .5, y + .5))) continue;
            var index = (y * image.PixelWidth + x) * 4;
            if (pixels[index + 3] < 180 || pixels[index + 2] < 170 || pixels[index + 1] < 110 || pixels[index + 2] - pixels[index] < 45) continue;
            count++; sumX += x + .5; sumY += y + .5;
        }
        Program.Assert(count >= 8, "The star clip has no solid visible fill.");
        return new Point(sumX / count, sumY / count);
    }
    private static void Save(BitmapSource image, string path)
    {
        var encoder = new PngBitmapEncoder(); encoder.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(image)); using var file = File.Create(path); encoder.Save(file);
    }
}

using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using System.Windows.Interop;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;

using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

internal static class PetAnimationAcceptance
{
    public static Task RunSettingsAsync(AcceptanceRun run) => run.CaseAsync("pet-v081-independent-motion-setting-and-legacy-migration", () =>
    {
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-motion-settings");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var store = new SettingsStore(directory);
        foreach (var global in new[] { MotionPreference.System, MotionPreference.Reduced })
        {
            File.WriteAllText(store.FilePath, JsonSerializer.Serialize(new { MotionPreference = global.ToString(), OutputDirectory = "kept-output", CompressionMaximumMb = 17, Pet = new { Roaming = false, Size = 200 } }));
            var migrated = store.Load();
            Program.Assert(migrated.Pet.MotionMode == (global == MotionPreference.Reduced ? PetMotionMode.Reduced : PetMotionMode.Gentle), "A legacy animation preference was not migrated correctly.");
            Program.Assert(migrated.MotionPreference == global && migrated.OutputDirectory == "kept-output" && migrated.CompressionMaximumMb == 17 && !migrated.Pet.Roaming && migrated.Pet.Size == 200, "Pet migration altered existing conversion or pet preferences.");
        }
        foreach (var mode in Enum.GetValues<PetMotionMode>())
        {
            var settings = new AppSettings { MotionPreference = MotionPreference.Reduced, OutputDirectory = "roundtrip-output", Pet = new PetSettings { MotionMode = mode, Roaming = false, Size = 120 } };
            store.Save(settings);
            var loaded = new SettingsStore(directory).Load();
            Program.Assert(loaded.Pet.MotionMode == mode && loaded.Pet.Clone().MotionMode == mode && loaded.MotionPreference == MotionPreference.Reduced && loaded.OutputDirectory == "roundtrip-output", "Independent pet motion mode was not preserved on restart.");
        }
        File.WriteAllText(store.FilePath, "{\"MotionPreference\":\"System\",\"Pet\":{\"MotionMode\":999}}");
        Program.Assert(store.Load().Pet.MotionMode == PetMotionMode.Gentle, "An invalid pet motion mode did not use the gentle default.");
        run.Evidence["petV081MotionSettings"] = new { legacySystemBecomesGentle = true, legacyReducedRemainsReduced = true, allThreeModesRoundTrip = true, conversionPreferencesPreserved = true, invalidValueUsesGentle = true };
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    });

    public static async Task RunAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        await PetAccessoryAcceptance.RunAsync(run);
        await run.CaseAsync("pet-v081-real-idle-sleep-continuous-motion-and-recordings", () => ContinuousAsync(run));

        await run.CaseAsync("pet-v09-default-gentle-controller-menu-hide-work-and-completion-motion", () => ControllerLifecycleAsync(run));
