    private static DesktopPetWindow OpenPet(AcceptanceRun run, int size = 160)
    {
        var window = new DesktopPetWindow();
        window.Configure(false);
        window.LoadSprites(Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(typeof(PetSpriteView).Assembly.Location)!, "Assets", "Pet", "animations.json"));
        Program.Assert(window.SpriteView.HasSpriteAtlas && window.SpriteView.AssetError is null, "The production pet atlas did not load for animation acceptance.");
        window.SetSize(size);
        var monitor = DesktopEnvironment.GetMonitors().First(item => item.Primary);
        window.SetPosition(monitor.WorkArea.Left + 30, monitor.WorkArea.Top + 70);
        window.SetEnvironmentVisible(true);
        window.UpdateLayout();
        return window;
    }

    private static async Task ContinuousAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        using var window = OpenPet(run);
        window.SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode.Gentle);
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-animation-recordings");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        using var manifest = JsonDocument.Parse(File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(typeof(PetSpriteView).Assembly.Location)!, "Assets", "Pet", "animations.json")));
        var manifestVersion = manifest.RootElement.TryGetProperty("version", out var version) ? version.GetInt32() : 2;
        var recordings = new List<object>();
        foreach (var state in new[] { "idle", "sleep", "work" })
        {
            window.SetAnimation(state);
            await Task.Delay(220);
            var hashes = new HashSet<string>();
            var transforms = new List<Matrix>();
            var frameIndices = new List<int>();
            // Sixteen seconds includes complete breathing and weight-shift cycles;
            // sleeping is recorded for one approximately 4.8-second breathing cycle.
            var count = state == "idle" ? 480 : state == "sleep" ? 144 : 96;
            var watch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
            for (var sample = 0; sample < count; sample++)
            {
                // Real controller refreshes must leave the breathing clock running.
                window.SetAnimation(state);
                await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Render);
                var bitmap = Render(window.SpriteView, 160);
                var bytes = Pixels(bitmap);
                if (sample < 30) hashes.Add(Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(bytes)));
                transforms.Add(window.SpriteView.AmbientTransform);
                frameIndices.Add(window.SpriteView.RenderedFrameIndex);
                SavePng(bitmap, Path.Combine(directory, $"{state}-{sample:D3}.png"));
                await Task.Delay(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(Math.Max(1, (sample + 1) * (1000d / 30) - watch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds)));
            }
            Program.Assert(hashes.Count >= 5, $"{state} remained visually still during its first seconds; {hashes.Count} unique rendered samples.");
            var heightRange = transforms.Max(item => item.M22) - transforms.Min(item => item.M22);
            var nativePoseLoop = manifestVersion >= 3 && state == "work";
            if (nativePoseLoop)
            {
                Program.Assert(window.SpriteView.ActiveAmbientClockCount == 0 && window.SpriteView.ActiveTransitionClockCount == 0 &&
                    window.SpriteView.ActiveFrameTimerCount == 1 && Math.Abs(heightRange) < .000001 && frameIndices.Distinct().Count() >= 5,
                    "Authored work poses are being replaced by breathing/opacity clocks, or the actual pose sequence did not advance.");
            }
            else
            {
                Program.Assert(window.SpriteView.ActiveAmbientClockCount == 1, $"{state} has no independent ambient animation clock.");
                Program.Assert(heightRange >= .004 && heightRange <= (state == "sleep" ? .013 : .016), $"{state} breathing is missing or exceeded the planned height amplitude ({heightRange:F5}).");
            }
            var gif = await EncodeGifAsync(run, directory, state);
            recordings.Add(new { state, renderedFrames = count, uniqueFirstSecondFrames = hashes.Count, heightScaleRange = heightRange, frameIndices, elapsedSeconds = watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, gif,
                windowsClientAreaAnimation = SystemParameters.ClientAreaAnimation, MotionPreferences.SystemAllowsMotion,
                systemAnimationOverrideUsed = false, nativePoseLoop, source = "RenderTargetBitmap of the actual visible production SpriteView; no reconstructed artwork or generated frames." });
        }
        run.Evidence["petV081ContinuousMotion"] = recordings;
        window.SetEnvironmentVisible(false);
    }

    private static async Task BoundsAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        using var window = OpenPet(run);
        window.SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode.Reduced);
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-sleep-boundaries");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var manifestPath = Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(typeof(PetSpriteView).Assembly.Location)!, "Assets", "Pet", "animations.json");
        using var manifest = JsonDocument.Parse(File.ReadAllText(manifestPath));
        var diagnostics = window.SpriteView.GetFrameDiagnostics("sleep");
        Program.Assert(diagnostics.Count > 0, "The sleep clip contains no frames.");
        var sleepDefinition = manifest.RootElement.GetProperty("animations").GetProperty("sleep");
        var legacySleep = !sleepDefinition.TryGetProperty("segments", out _);
        var sourceGeometry = new List<object>();
        if (legacySleep)
        {
            // Preserve the exact legacy connected-tail check for the original six-cell sheet.
            var atlasIndex = sleepDefinition.GetProperty("atlas").GetInt32();
            var atlasFile = manifest.RootElement.GetProperty("atlases")[atlasIndex].GetProperty("file").GetString()!;
            var atlas = new BitmapImage(new Uri(Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(manifestPath)!, atlasFile)));
            Program.Assert(diagnostics.Count == 6, "The legacy sleep clip no longer contains its six source cells.");
            var components = Components(atlas, diagnostics.Min(item => item.SourceRect.Y), diagnostics.Max(item => item.SourceRect.Y + item.SourceRect.Height));
            var bodyComponents = components.Where(item => item.Count >= 1000).OrderBy(item => item.Bounds.X).ToArray();
            Program.Assert(bodyComponents.Length == 6, $"Expected six independent legacy sleeping cats, found {bodyComponents.Length}.");
            for (var frame = 0; frame < diagnostics.Count; frame++)
            {
                var bounds = diagnostics[frame].SourceRect; var component = bodyComponents[frame].Bounds;
                Program.Assert(Contains(bounds, component), $"Sleep frame {frame} cuts its connected tail: source={bounds}, complete={component}.");
                Program.Assert(bodyComponents.Where((_, index) => index != frame).All(item => !Intersects(bounds, item.Bounds)), $"Sleep frame {frame} samples its neighboring cat.");
                Program.Assert(diagnostics[frame].Placement.Left > 0 && diagnostics[frame].Placement.Top > 0 && diagnostics[frame].Placement.Right < diagnostics[frame].CanvasSize.Width && diagnostics[frame].Placement.Bottom < diagnostics[frame].CanvasSize.Height, $"Legacy sleep frame {frame} lacks breathing safety padding.");
            }
        }
        foreach (var animation in manifest.RootElement.GetProperty("animations").EnumerateObject().Where(x => x.Value.TryGetProperty("segments", out _)))
            ValidatePagedSourceFrames(window.SpriteView, manifest.RootElement, manifestPath, animation.Name, sourceGeometry);
        var captures = new List<object>();
        var allActionFrames = 0;
        foreach (var size in new[] { 120, 160, 200 })
        {
            window.SetSize(size);
            window.UpdateLayout();
            for (var frame = 0; frame < diagnostics.Count; frame++)
            {
                window.SpriteView.SetFrameForTesting("sleep", frame);
                window.SpriteView.SetAccessories(frame % 2 == 0, frame % 2 == 0);
                foreach (var mirrored in new[] { false, true })
                {
                    window.SpriteView.RenderTransform = mirrored ? new ScaleTransform(-1, 1, size / 2d, size / 2d) : Transform.Identity;
                    await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Render);
                    var bitmap = Render(window.SpriteView, size);
                    var pixels = Pixels(bitmap);
                    Program.Assert(ClearEdges(pixels, size), $"Sleep frame {frame} touches a viewport edge at {size} DIP (mirror={mirrored}).");
                    var path = Path.Combine(directory, $"sleep-{size}-frame-{frame}-{(mirrored ? "left" : "right")}.png");
                    SavePng(bitmap, path);
                    captures.Add(new { sizeDip = size, frame, mirrored, file = path });
                }
            }
        }
        window.SpriteView.RenderTransform = Transform.Identity;
        window.SpriteView.SetAccessories(false, false);
        foreach (var size in new[] { 120, 160, 200 })
        {
            window.SetSize(size); window.UpdateLayout();
            foreach (var animation in manifest.RootElement.GetProperty("animations").EnumerateObject())
                for (var frame = 0; frame < window.SpriteView.GetFrameDiagnostics(animation.Name).Count; frame++)
                {
                    window.SpriteView.SetFrameForTesting(animation.Name, frame);
                    await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Render);
                    var bitmap = Render(window.SpriteView, size);
                    Program.Assert(ClearEdges(Pixels(bitmap), size), $"{animation.Name} frame {frame} touches a viewport edge at {size} DIP.");
                    allActionFrames++;
                }
        }
        window.SetSize(160);
        window.UpdateLayout();
        window.SpriteView.SetAccessories(false, false);
        window.SetAnimation("idle");
        await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Render);
        var stillPixels = Pixels(Render(window.SpriteView, 160));
        var headPoint = Enumerable.Range(8, 144).SelectMany(y => Enumerable.Range(8, 144).Select(x => new Point(x, y)))
            .First(point => Interior(stillPixels, 160, (int)point.X, (int)point.Y) && window.SpriteView.IsHeadHit(point));
        window.SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode.Gentle);
        window.SetAnimation("idle");
        await Task.Delay(200);
        var testedPixels = 0;
        var transforms = new List<Matrix>();
        for (var sample = 0; sample < 16; sample++)
        {
            await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Render);
            var bitmap = Render(window.SpriteView, 160);
            var pixels = Pixels(bitmap);
            transforms.Add(window.SpriteView.AmbientTransform);
            var movingHead = window.SpriteView.AmbientTransform.Transform(headPoint);
            Program.Assert(window.SpriteView.ContainsVisiblePixel(movingHead) && window.SpriteView.IsHeadHit(movingHead), "Breathing moved the rendered head away from its petting hit region.");
            foreach (var mirrored in new[] { false, true })
            {
                window.SpriteView.RenderTransform = mirrored ? new ScaleTransform(-1, 1, 80, 80) : Transform.Identity;
                var screenHead = window.SpriteView.PointToScreen(movingHead);
                var handle = new WindowInteropHelper(window).Handle;
                Program.Assert(SendMessage(handle, 0x0084, IntPtr.Zero, PackPoint(screenHead)).ToInt64() != -1, "Native hit testing missed a moving or mirrored head.");
                var screenPadding = window.SpriteView.PointToScreen(new Point(1, 1));
                Program.Assert(SendMessage(handle, 0x0084, IntPtr.Zero, PackPoint(screenPadding)).ToInt64() == -1, "Moving or mirrored transparent padding did not pass clicks through.");
            }
            window.SpriteView.RenderTransform = Transform.Identity;
            for (var y = 10; y < 150; y += 14)
                for (var x = 10; x < 150; x += 14)
                    if (Interior(pixels, 160, x, y))
                    {
                        Program.Assert(window.SpriteView.ContainsVisiblePixel(new Point(x, y)), $"The moving visible cat missed hit testing at ({x},{y}).");
                        testedPixels++;
                    }
            Program.Assert(!window.SpriteView.ContainsVisiblePixel(new Point(1, 1)), "Ambient motion blocked transparent desktop padding.");
            await Task.Delay(100);
        }
        Program.Assert(testedPixels > 100 && transforms.Distinct().Count() > 5, "Transformed hit coverage did not exercise a visibly moving cat.");
        run.Evidence["petV081SleepBounds"] = new { legacySixCellSleepCheck = legacySleep, sleepFrames = diagnostics.Count,
            completeConnectedSilhouettes = true, neighboringCatsExcluded = true, transparentLogicalCanvasPadding = true,
            pagedSourceGeometry = sourceGeometry, anatomyApproval = "Not inferred by connected components; new artwork requires separate per-frame reviewed provenance.",
            sizes = new[] {120,160,200}, captures, allActionFramesWithClearViewportEdges = allActionFrames, movingInteriorHitSamples = testedPixels, movingAndMirroredHeadNativeHits = true, movingMatrices = transforms };
        window.SetEnvironmentVisible(false);
    }

    private static async Task PolicyAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var previousRenderMode = RenderOptions.ProcessRenderMode;
        RenderOptions.ProcessRenderMode = System.Windows.Interop.RenderMode.SoftwareOnly;
        using var window = OpenPet(run);
        try
        {
            MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.Reduced);
            using (MotionPreferences.OverrideSystemMotionForTesting(false))
            {
                Program.Assert(!MotionPreferences.SystemAllowsMotion, "The Windows-off policy fixture did not disable system motion.");
                window.SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode.Gentle);
                window.SetAnimation("idle");
                await AssertVisibleMotionAsync(window.SpriteView, "Gentle with Windows and page animations disabled", run);
                Program.Assert(window.SpriteView.ActiveAmbientClockCount == 1 && window.SpriteView.ActiveFrameTimerCount == 1,
                    "Explicit Gentle mode became static when Windows or page animations were disabled.");
                Program.Assert(window.TryWalk(40), "Gentle walking remained incorrectly tied to Windows animations.");
                window.StopWalk();
                window.SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode.System);
                await Task.Delay(80);
                var stoppedFrame = window.SpriteView.RenderedFrameIndex;
                await Task.Delay(150);
                Program.Assert(!window.SpriteView.HasActiveAnimation && window.SpriteView.ActiveAmbientClockCount == 0 &&
                    window.SpriteView.ActiveFrameTimerCount == 0 && window.SpriteView.ActiveTransitionClockCount == 0 &&
                    window.ActiveMovementTimerCount == 0 && window.SpriteView.RenderedFrameIndex == stoppedFrame && !window.TryWalk(40),
                    "Follow Windows mode ignored the Windows reduced-motion setting.");
            }
            using (MotionPreferences.OverrideSystemMotionForTesting(true))
            {
                window.SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode.System);
                await Task.Delay(100);
                Program.Assert(window.SpriteView.ActiveAmbientClockCount == 1,
                    "Follow Windows mode did not resume when Windows allowed animation, independently of page settings.");
            }
            window.SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode.Gentle);
            await Task.Delay(150);
            var transform = window.SpriteView.AmbientTransform;
            window.SetEnvironmentVisible(false);
            var hiddenFrame = window.SpriteView.RenderedFrameIndex;
            await Task.Delay(250);
            Program.Assert(window.SpriteView.ActiveAmbientClockCount == 0 && window.SpriteView.ActiveFrameTimerCount == 0 && window.SpriteView.ActiveTransitionClockCount == 0 && window.ActiveHoldTimerCount == 0 && window.ActiveMovementTimerCount == 0 && window.SpriteView.RenderedFrameIndex == hiddenFrame && !window.SpriteView.HasActiveAnimation && !window.SpriteView.HasTextureReference, "Hiding the pet retained a texture reference, animation clock or timer.");
            window.SetEnvironmentVisible(true);
            await Task.Delay(100);
            Program.Assert(window.SpriteView.ActiveAmbientClockCount == 1, "Returning to the visible pet did not restart gentle motion.");
            window.SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode.Reduced);
            await Task.Delay(100);
            Program.Assert(!window.SpriteView.HasActiveAnimation && window.SpriteView.ActiveAmbientClockCount == 0 && !window.TryWalk(40), "Explicit Reduced pet mode retained animation or walking.");
            window.Dispose();
            Program.Assert(!window.SpriteView.HasActiveAnimation && window.SpriteView.ActiveAmbientClockCount == 0, "Disposing the pet retained its ambient clock.");
            run.Evidence["petV081MotionPolicy"] = new { forcedSoftwareRenderer = true, windowsAnimationOffSimulatedWithoutChangingOS = true,
                gentleContinuesWhenWindowsAnimationsOff = true, systemModeRespectsWindowsReducedMotion = true, gentleIndependentOfPageReduced = true,
                systemChangesObservedWithPageReduced = true, reducedStopsAllClocks = true, hiddenStopsAllClocks = true,
                returnRestartsGentleMotion = true, disposeStopsAllClocks = true, preHideTransform = transform };
        }
        finally { RenderOptions.ProcessRenderMode = previousRenderMode; MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System); }
    }

    private static async Task ControllerLifecycleAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-default-controller-motion");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var store = new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(directory, "settings"));
        store.Save(new AppSettings { OutputDirectory = Path.Combine(directory, "outputs"), MotionPreference = MotionPreference.Reduced,
            Pet = new PetSettings { Enabled = true, HideForFullscreen = false, Roaming = false, Size = 160 } });
        var engine = new AnimationLifecycleEngine();
        var model = new MainViewModel(engine, PresetCatalog.Load(Path.Combine(run.Root, "shared", "presets.json")), store,
            new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(directory, "logs")));
        using var controller = new PetController(model);
        var window = controller.DesktopWindow;
        var uniqueFrames = new Dictionary<string, int>();
        run.StopActiveWindow = async () => { model.Cancel(); engine.Release.TrySetResult(); await model.ActiveRun; controller.Dispose(); };
        try
        {
            await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle);
            Program.Assert(model.PetSettings.MotionMode == PetMotionMode.Gentle && controller.IsPetVisible &&
                window.SpriteView.HasSpriteAtlas && window.SpriteView.AssetError is null,
                "The unmodified default pet mode or production controller atlas is not available.");
            uniqueFrames["default-real-windows-settings"] = await AssertVisibleMotionAsync(window.SpriteView, "Default controller at actual Windows settings", run);

            var menu = window.InteractionMenu;
            menu.PlacementTarget = window;
            menu.IsOpen = true;
            await Task.Delay(100);
            menu.IsOpen = false;
            uniqueFrames["after-menu-closed"] = await AssertVisibleMotionAsync(window.SpriteView, "Controller after its real context menu closes", run);

            controller.HideTemporarily();
            await Task.Delay(100);
            Program.Assert(!window.IsVisible && !window.SpriteView.HasActiveAnimation && window.SpriteView.ActiveFrameTimerCount == 0 &&
                window.SpriteView.ActiveAmbientClockCount == 0, "Temporary hiding kept the default pet's clocks alive.");
            controller.ToggleVisibility();
            uniqueFrames["after-visible-again"] = await AssertVisibleMotionAsync(window.SpriteView, "Controller after temporary-hide recovery", run);

            var input = Path.Combine(directory, "default-motion-input.mp4");
            File.WriteAllText(input, "Deterministic animation lifecycle fixture.");
            await model.ImportForActionAsync(FileProcessingAction.Video, new[] { input });
            var active = model.RunQueueAsync();
            await engine.Started.Task.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5));
            Program.Assert(model.IsProcessing && window.SpriteView.Animation == "work", "File processing did not select the pet's working animation.");
            uniqueFrames["while-working"] = await AssertVisibleMotionAsync(window.SpriteView, "Controller during an active processing batch", run);
            controller.HideTemporarily();
            await Task.Delay(100);
            Program.Assert(!window.SpriteView.HasActiveAnimation, "Hiding a working pet retained its animation clocks.");
            controller.ToggleVisibility();
            uniqueFrames["working-after-visible-again"] = await AssertVisibleMotionAsync(window.SpriteView, "Working controller after temporary-hide recovery", run);
            engine.Release.TrySetResult();
            await active.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5));
            Program.Assert(!model.IsProcessing && model.Queue.Single().Status == JobStatus.Completed,
                "The animation fixture did not finish its original file-processing batch.");
            uniqueFrames["after-completion"] = await AssertVisibleMotionAsync(window.SpriteView, "Controller after processing completes", run);
            run.Evidence["petV09DefaultControllerMotion"] = new { defaultMode = model.PetSettings.MotionMode.ToString(),
                windowsClientAreaAnimation = SystemParameters.ClientAreaAnimation, MotionPreferences.SystemAllowsMotion,
                systemAnimationOverrideUsed = false, pageReducedPreferencePreserved = true, uniqueRenderedFrames = uniqueFrames,
                menuCloseKeepsMotion = true, hiddenStopsClocks = true, returningRestartsMotion = true,
                workingAndCompletedRemainAnimated = true };
        }
        finally
        {
            model.Cancel(); engine.Release.TrySetResult();
            await model.ActiveRun.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5));
            controller.Dispose();
            Program.Assert(!window.SpriteView.HasActiveAnimation && !window.InteractionMenu.IsOpen,
                "Controller disposal retained the pet's animation or context menu.");
            run.StopActiveWindow = null;
            MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System);
        }
    }

    private static async Task<int> AssertVisibleMotionAsync(PetSpriteView view, string stage, AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var hashes = new HashSet<string>();
        var firstHalfHashes = new HashSet<string>();
        var secondHalfHashes = new HashSet<string>();
        var frameIndices = new HashSet<(string State, int Index)>();
        var ambientTimes = new List<double>();
        var samples = new List<object>();
        if (!run.Evidence.TryGetValue("petContinuousMotionSamples", out var existing) || existing is not List<object> stages)
        {
            stages = new List<object>();
            run.Evidence["petContinuousMotionSamples"] = stages;
        }
        // Sample actual production poses (including paged v3 artwork), or the idle breathing clock.
        var count = view.Animation == "sleep" ? 52 : 36;
        var clockWasAlwaysActive = true;
        var watch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-lifecycle-motion");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var sequence = stages.Count;
        var captures = new List<string>();
        var evidence = new { stage, requestedSampleMilliseconds = count * 100, samples, captures };
        stages.Add(evidence);
        for (var sample = 0; sample < count; sample++)
        {
            await Task.Delay(100);
            await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Render);
            var bitmap = Render(view, 160);
            var hash = Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(Pixels(bitmap)));
            hashes.Add(hash);
            (sample < count / 2 ? firstHalfHashes : secondHalfHashes).Add(hash);
            frameIndices.Add((view.Animation, view.RenderedFrameIndex));
            ambientTimes.Add(view.AmbientTimeSeconds);
            clockWasAlwaysActive &= view.IsVisible && view.HasActiveAnimation;
            samples.Add(new { elapsedMilliseconds = watch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds, view.Animation, view.RenderedFrameIndex,
                view.IsVisible, view.ActiveFrameTimerCount, view.ActiveAmbientClockCount, view.ActiveTransitionClockCount,
                view.FrameIntervalMilliseconds, view.AmbientTimeSeconds, ambientTransform = view.AmbientTransform.ToString(), sha256 = hash });
            if (sample == 0 || sample == count / 2 || sample == count - 1)
            {
                var path = Path.Combine(directory, $"stage-{sequence:D2}-sample-{sample:D2}.png");
                SavePng(bitmap, path); captures.Add(path);
            }
        }
        var frameAdvanced = frameIndices.GroupBy(item => item.State).Any(group => group.Select(item => item.Index).Distinct().Count() > 1);
        var ambientAdvanced = ambientTimes.Max() - ambientTimes.Min() >= .5;
        Program.Assert(clockWasAlwaysActive && view.IsVisible && view.HasActiveAnimation && hashes.Count >= 3 &&
            firstHalfHashes.Count >= 2 && secondHalfHashes.Count >= 2 && (frameAdvanced || ambientAdvanced),
            stage + " did not sustain animation across its complete cycle: " + hashes.Count + " unique real frames, first/last half " +
            firstHalfHashes.Count + "/" + secondHalfHashes.Count + ", frameAdvanced=" + frameAdvanced +
            ", ambientAdvanced=" + ambientAdvanced + ". See petContinuousMotionSamples.");
        return hashes.Count;
    }

    private sealed class AnimationLifecycleEngine : IConversionEngine
    {
        public TaskCompletionSource Started { get; } = new(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
        public TaskCompletionSource Release { get; } = new(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
        public Task<MediaInfo> ProbeAsync(string path, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
            => Task.FromResult(new MediaInfo(true, true, 1, "mp4", "h264", "aac", 1024, 64, 64, 25));
        public async Task<ConversionResult> ConvertAsync(ConversionRequest request, IProgress<ConversionProgress>? progress = null,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            progress?.Report(new(25, TimeSpan.Zero));
            Started.TrySetResult();
            await Release.Task.WaitAsync(cancellationToken);
            Directory.CreateDirectory(request.OutputDirectory);
            var output = Path.Combine(request.OutputDirectory, "completed-motion-fixture.mp4");
            File.WriteAllText(output, "Committed animation lifecycle result.");
            return new(JobStatus.Completed, output, null);
        }
    }

    private static RenderTargetBitmap Render(PetSpriteView view, int size)
    {
        var bitmap = new RenderTargetBitmap(size, size, 96, 96, PixelFormats.Pbgra32);
        bitmap.Render(view);
        return bitmap;
    }
    private static byte[] Pixels(BitmapSource bitmap) { var pixels = new byte[bitmap.PixelWidth * bitmap.PixelHeight * 4]; bitmap.CopyPixels(pixels, bitmap.PixelWidth * 4, 0); return pixels; }
    private static void SavePng(BitmapSource bitmap, string path) { var encoder = new PngBitmapEncoder(); encoder.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(bitmap)); using var file = File.Create(path); encoder.Save(file); }
    private static bool ClearEdges(byte[] pixels, int size) => Enumerable.Range(0, size).All(index => pixels[index * 4 + 3] == 0 && pixels[((size - 1) * size + index) * 4 + 3] == 0 && pixels[(index * size) * 4 + 3] == 0 && pixels[(index * size + size - 1) * 4 + 3] == 0);
    private static bool Interior(byte[] pixels, int size, int x, int y) => new[] {-2,0,2}.All(dx => new[] {-2,0,2}.All(dy => pixels[((y+dy)*size+x+dx)*4+3] > 230));
    private static bool Intersects(Int32Rect left, Int32Rect right) => left.X < right.X + right.Width && right.X < left.X + left.Width && left.Y < right.Y + right.Height && right.Y < left.Y + left.Height;
    private static bool Contains(Int32Rect outer, Int32Rect inner) => outer.X <= inner.X && outer.Y <= inner.Y && outer.X + outer.Width >= inner.X + inner.Width && outer.Y + outer.Height >= inner.Y + inner.Height;
    private static void ValidatePagedSourceFrames(PetSpriteView view, JsonElement manifest, string manifestPath, string animation, List<object> evidence)
    {
        var definition = manifest.GetProperty("animations").GetProperty(animation);
        var suppliedFrames = SegmentFrames(definition.GetProperty("segments")).ToArray();
        var frames = view.GetFrameDiagnostics(animation);
        Program.Assert(frames.Count > 0 && suppliedFrames.Length == frames.Count, $"{animation} dropped a supplied entry, loop, exit or marker-specific frame.");
        Program.Assert(suppliedFrames.All(frame => ExplicitPoint(frame, "head") && ExplicitPoint(frame, "neck")), $"{animation} uses invented fallback accessory anchors instead of explicit frame metadata.");
        if (animation == "walk") Program.Assert(suppliedFrames.All(frame => frame.TryGetProperty("feet", out var feet) && feet.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Array && feet.GetArrayLength() >= 2), "Walking frames lost their explicit foot-contact anchors.");
        var pages = manifest.GetProperty("atlases").EnumerateArray().GroupBy(
            item => Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(manifestPath)!, item.GetProperty("file").GetString()!)), StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            .ToDictionary(group => group.Key, group => group.First(), StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        foreach (var pageFrames in frames.GroupBy(frame => frame.AtlasFile, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            Program.Assert(pageFrames.Key is not null && pages.ContainsKey(pageFrames.Key), $"{animation} has a frame without its source page provenance.");
            var page = pages[pageFrames.Key!];
            var columns = page.GetProperty("columns").GetInt32(); var rows = page.GetProperty("rows").GetInt32();
            var original = new BitmapImage(new Uri(pageFrames.Key!, UriKind.Absolute));
            var ratio = Math.Min(1d, 256d / Math.Max(original.PixelWidth / columns, original.PixelHeight / rows));
            var bitmap = new BitmapImage(); bitmap.BeginInit(); bitmap.CacheOption = BitmapCacheOption.OnLoad;
            bitmap.DecodePixelWidth = Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Round(original.PixelWidth / (double)columns * ratio)) * columns;
            bitmap.DecodePixelHeight = Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Round(original.PixelHeight / (double)rows * ratio)) * rows;
            bitmap.UriSource = new Uri(pageFrames.Key!, UriKind.Absolute); bitmap.EndInit(); bitmap.Freeze();
            var components = Components(bitmap, 0, bitmap.PixelHeight).Where(item => item.Count >= 8).ToArray();
            foreach (var frame in pageFrames)
            {
                var bounds = frame.SourceRect;
                Program.Assert(bounds.X >= 0 && bounds.Y >= 0 && bounds.Width > 0 && bounds.Height > 0 && bounds.X + bounds.Width <= bitmap.PixelWidth && bounds.Y + bounds.Height <= bitmap.PixelHeight,
                    $"{animation} references pixels outside a decoded page.");
                var silhouettes = components.Where(component => Intersects(bounds, component.Bounds)).ToArray();
                Program.Assert(silhouettes.Any(component => component.Count >= 1000), $"{animation} source frame has no complete visible cat silhouette.");
                Program.Assert(silhouettes.All(component => Contains(bounds, component.Bounds)), $"{animation} source rectangle cuts a connected cat, tail or prop across its page cell.");
                Program.Assert(silhouettes.All(component => component.Bounds.X > bounds.X && component.Bounds.Y > bounds.Y && component.Bounds.X + component.Bounds.Width < bounds.X + bounds.Width && component.Bounds.Y + component.Bounds.Height < bounds.Y + bounds.Height),
                    $"{animation} has a significant alpha component touching its source cell boundary.");
            }
            evidence.Add(new { animation, page = pageFrames.Key, checkedFrames = pageFrames.Count(), decodedWidth = bitmap.PixelWidth, decodedHeight = bitmap.PixelHeight,
                completeAlphaComponents = true, clearCellEdges = true, explicitHeadNeckAnchors = true, anatomyNotInferredByThisCheck = true });
        }
    }
    private static bool ExplicitPoint(JsonElement value, string key) => value.TryGetProperty(key, out var point) && point.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Array && point.GetArrayLength() == 2 &&
        point.EnumerateArray().All(number => number.TryGetDouble(out var coordinate) && double.IsFinite(coordinate) && coordinate >= 0 && coordinate <= 1);
    private static IEnumerable<JsonElement> SegmentFrames(JsonElement value)
    {
        if (value.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Object) yield break;
        if (value.TryGetProperty("frames", out var frames))
        {
            foreach (var frame in frames.EnumerateArray()) yield return frame;
            yield break;
        }
        foreach (var property in value.EnumerateObject())
            foreach (var frame in SegmentFrames(property.Value)) yield return frame;
    }
    private static IReadOnlyList<(Int32Rect Bounds, int Count)> Components(BitmapSource source, int fromY, int toY)
    {
        var bitmap = new FormatConvertedBitmap(source, PixelFormats.Bgra32, null, 0);
        var pixels = Pixels(bitmap); var width = bitmap.PixelWidth;
        var visited = new bool[width * bitmap.PixelHeight];
        var result = new List<(Int32Rect, int)>();
        for (var y = fromY; y < toY; y++)
            for (var x = 0; x < width; x++)
            {
                var first = y * width + x;
                if (visited[first] || pixels[first * 4 + 3] <= 16) continue;
                var queue = new Queue<int>(); queue.Enqueue(first); visited[first] = true;
                var minX = x; var maxX = x; var minY = y; var maxY = y; var count = 0;
                while (queue.TryDequeue(out var pixel))
                {
                    var px = pixel % width; var py = pixel / width; count++;
                    minX = Math.Min(minX, px); maxX = Math.Max(maxX, px); minY = Math.Min(minY, py); maxY = Math.Max(maxY, py);
                    for (var dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) for (var dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++)
                    {
                        var nx = px + dx; var ny = py + dy;
                        if (nx < 0 || nx >= width || ny < fromY || ny >= toY) continue;
                        var next = ny * width + nx;
                        if (visited[next] || pixels[next * 4 + 3] <= 16) continue;
                        visited[next] = true; queue.Enqueue(next);
                    }
                }
                result.Add((new Int32Rect(minX,minY,maxX-minX+1,maxY-minY+1),count));
            }
        return result;
    }
    private static async Task<string> EncodeGifAsync(AcceptanceRun run, string directory, string state)
    {
        var gif = Path.Combine(directory, $"{state}.gif");
        var start = new ProcessStartInfo(Path.Combine(run.EngineDirectory, "ffmpeg.exe")) { UseShellExecute = false, CreateNoWindow = true, RedirectStandardError = true, RedirectStandardOutput = true };
        foreach (var value in new[] {"-hide_banner","-loglevel","error","-y","-framerate","30","-i",Path.Combine(directory,state+"-%03d.png"),"-filter_complex","split[a][b];[a]palettegen=reserve_transparent=1[p];[b][p]paletteuse=alpha_threshold=16","-loop","0",gif}) start.ArgumentList.Add(value);
        using var process = Process.Start(start) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Animation preview encoder did not start.");
        var error = process.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync(); var output = process.StandardOutput.ReadToEndAsync();
        await process.WaitForExitAsync().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30));
        Program.Assert(process.ExitCode == 0 && File.Exists(gif), "Animation preview GIF encoding failed: " + await error);
        await output;
        return gif;
    }
    private static IntPtr PackPoint(Point point) => new(((int)Math.Round(point.Y) & 0xFFFF) << 16 | ((int)Math.Round(point.X) & 0xFFFF));
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] private static extern IntPtr SendMessage(IntPtr window, uint message, IntPtr wParam, IntPtr lParam);
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/PetEconomyAcceptance.cs ---
using System.IO;
using System.Text.Json;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

internal static class PetEconomyAcceptance
{
    public static async Task RunAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        await run.CaseAsync("pet-settings-backward-compatible-shared-settings", () => SettingsAsync(run));
        await run.CaseAsync("pet-wallet-rewards-shop-and-atomic-failure", () => EconomyAsync(run));
        await run.CaseAsync("pet-work-cumulative-runtime-decimal-checkpoint-stop-migration-and-restart", () => WorkAsync(run));
        await run.CaseAsync("pet-wallet-serialized-concurrent-settlement-and-real-reopen", () => PersistenceAsync(run));
    }

    private static Task SettingsAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-settings");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var store = new SettingsStore(directory);
        File.WriteAllText(store.FilePath, "{\"OutputDirectory\":\"old-v07-output\",\"PresetIds\":{\"Video\":\"video-mp4\"},\"CompressionMaximumMb\":17,\"MotionPreference\":\"Reduced\"}");
        var settings = store.Load();
        Program.Assert(settings.Pet.Enabled && settings.Pet.Roaming && settings.Pet.Topmost && settings.Pet.Size == 160
            && settings.Pet.CompletionNotifications && settings.Pet.HideForFullscreen, "V0.7 settings did not receive the accepted desktop pet defaults.");
        settings.Pet = new()
        {
            Enabled = false, Roaming = false, Topmost = false, Size = 200, CompletionNotifications = false,
            HideForFullscreen = false, GameExecutables = ["game.exe", "GAME.EXE", "", " second.exe "],
            Left = -1234, Top = 51, MonitorDeviceName = "\\\\.\\DISPLAY2"
        };
        store.Save(settings);
        var reloaded = store.Load();
        Program.Assert(!reloaded.Pet.Enabled && !reloaded.Pet.Roaming && reloaded.Pet.Size == 200
            && !reloaded.Pet.CompletionNotifications && reloaded.Pet.Left == -1234
            && reloaded.Pet.MonitorDeviceName == settings.Pet.MonitorDeviceName, "Desktop pet settings did not survive restart.");
        Program.Assert(reloaded.OutputDirectory == "old-v07-output" && reloaded.CompressionMaximumMb == 17
            && reloaded.MotionPreference == MotionPreference.Reduced && reloaded.PresetIds[ConversionMode.Video] == "video-mp4",
            "Saving the shared pet settings overwrote existing conversion preferences.");
        Program.Assert(reloaded.Pet.GameExecutables.SequenceEqual(["game.exe", "second.exe"]), "Game executable settings were not deduplicated and normalized.");
        Program.Assert(File.Exists(store.FilePath + ".bak") && Directory.GetFiles(directory, "*.tmp").Length == 0,
            "Shared settings did not preserve their previous confirmed revision or left temporary files.");
        var invalid = new PetSettings { Size = 999, Left = double.NaN, Top = double.PositiveInfinity, GameExecutables = null! };
        invalid.Validate();
        Program.Assert(invalid.Size == 160 && invalid.Left is null && invalid.Top is null && invalid.GameExecutables.Count == 0,
            "Invalid pet geometry or a null game list was not repaired.");
        var clone = reloaded.Pet.Clone();
        clone.GameExecutables.Clear();
        Program.Assert(reloaded.Pet.GameExecutables.Count == 2, "Editing a pet settings clone changed the existing settings before save.");
        store.Save(reloaded);
        File.WriteAllText(store.FilePath, "{ damaged settings");
        var recovered = store.Load();
        Program.Assert(recovered.OutputDirectory == "old-v07-output" && recovered.CompressionMaximumMb == 17
            && !recovered.Pet.Enabled && recovered.Pet.Size == 200,
            "A recoverable damaged settings file lost the shared conversion or pet preferences.");
        run.Evidence["petSettings"] = new { oldV07Compatible = true, defaults = "enabled, roaming, topmost, 160 DIP, completion, fullscreen hide", conversionPreferencesRetained = true, settingsPersisted = true, previousRevisionRecovery = true };
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }

    private static Task EconomyAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var disk = new MemoryPersistence();
        var clock = new ManualClock();
        var wallet = new PetStateStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-wallet-model"), clock, disk);
        Program.Assert(wallet.Snapshot.Coins == 30, "A new pet account did not start with 30 coins.");
        var firstBatch = Guid.NewGuid();
        var successfulRequest = Guid.NewGuid();
        Program.Assert(wallet.SettleBatch(firstBatch, [successfulRequest, successfulRequest]).CoinsChanged == 5
            && wallet.Snapshot.Coins == 35, "Multiple passes or repeated success IDs paid more than once for one actual request.");
        var sequence = wallet.Snapshot.Sequence;
        Program.Assert(wallet.SettleBatch(firstBatch, [successfulRequest]).CoinsChanged == 0
            && wallet.Snapshot.Coins == 35 && wallet.Snapshot.Sequence == sequence, "A repeated batch emitted a duplicate reward or write.");
        Program.Assert(wallet.SettleBatch(Guid.NewGuid(), [successfulRequest]).CoinsChanged == 5,
            "A genuine new batch reusing a queue row ID was suppressed by an obsolete request receipt.");
        var noRewardBatch = Guid.NewGuid();
        Program.Assert(wallet.SettleBatch(noRewardBatch, []).CoinsChanged == 0, "A failed, cancelled or password-exhausted batch earned coins.");
        Program.Assert(wallet.SettleBatch(Guid.NewGuid(), [Guid.NewGuid(), Guid.NewGuid()]).CoinsChanged == 10,
            "A partial-success batch did not pay exactly its two successful requests.");
        Program.Assert(wallet.SettleBatch(Guid.NewGuid(), [Guid.NewGuid()]).CoinsChanged == 5,
            "One merged archive request did not receive one reward.");
        var restarted = new PetStateStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-wallet-model"), clock, disk);
        Program.Assert(restarted.Snapshot.Coins == 55 && restarted.SettleBatch(firstBatch, [successfulRequest]).CoinsChanged == 0,
            "Restart lost balance or duplicate settlement protection.");

        Program.Assert(wallet.Buy(PetShopItemId.Fish).Succeeded && wallet.Buy(PetShopItemId.Fish).Succeeded
            && wallet.Snapshot.Quantity(PetShopItemId.Fish) == 2 && wallet.Snapshot.Coins == 35,
            "Purchasing food did not atomically charge and add consumable quantities.");
        Program.Assert(wallet.Feed(PetShopItemId.Fish).Succeeded && wallet.Snapshot.Quantity(PetShopItemId.Fish) == 1,
            "Feeding did not consume exactly one owned food.");
        Program.Assert(wallet.Buy(PetShopItemId.Yarn).Succeeded && wallet.Snapshot.Owns(PetShopItemId.Yarn) && wallet.Snapshot.Coins == 5,
            "The permanent yarn toy was not purchased at its accepted price.");
        Program.Assert(!wallet.Buy(PetShopItemId.Yarn).Succeeded && !wallet.Buy(PetShopItemId.Collar).Succeeded
            && !wallet.Feed(PetShopItemId.Yarn).Succeeded && !wallet.Equip(PetShopItemId.Collar).Succeeded,
            "Duplicate toys, unaffordable purchases, toy feeding or unowned accessories were accepted.");
        Program.Assert(PetShopCatalog.Items.Select(item => item.Price).SequenceEqual([10, 20, 30, 40, 60, 80]),
            "The first shop catalog prices differ from the accepted specification.");
        wallet.SettleBatch(Guid.NewGuid(), Enumerable.Range(0, 40).Select(_ => Guid.NewGuid()));
        Program.Assert(wallet.Buy(PetShopItemId.Collar).Succeeded && wallet.Buy(PetShopItemId.StarClip).Succeeded
            && wallet.Equip(PetShopItemId.Collar).Succeeded && wallet.Equip(PetShopItemId.StarClip).Succeeded,
            "Owned head and neck accessories could not be equipped independently.");
        restarted = new PetStateStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-wallet-model"), clock, disk);
        Program.Assert(restarted.Snapshot.IsEquipped(PetShopItemId.Collar) && restarted.Snapshot.IsEquipped(PetShopItemId.StarClip)
            && restarted.Snapshot.Quantity(PetShopItemId.Fish) == 1 && restarted.Snapshot.Coins == 65,
            "Reopening lost balance, inventory or equipped accessories.");
        Program.Assert(wallet.Unequip(PetAccessorySlot.Neck).Succeeded && !wallet.Snapshot.IsEquipped(PetShopItemId.Collar)
            && wallet.Snapshot.Owns(PetShopItemId.Collar) && wallet.Snapshot.IsEquipped(PetShopItemId.StarClip),
            "Unequipping consumed the accessory or changed the independent head slot.");

        var confirmed = wallet.Snapshot;
        disk.FailWrites = true;
        var failedPayment = wallet.Buy(PetShopItemId.CannedFood);
        Program.Assert(!failedPayment.Succeeded && failedPayment.CoinsChanged == 0
            && wallet.Snapshot.Coins == confirmed.Coins && wallet.Snapshot.Quantity(PetShopItemId.CannedFood) == 0
            && wallet.Snapshot.Sequence == confirmed.Sequence, "A failed atomic purchase changed confirmed balance, inventory, sequence or reported success.");
        Program.Assert(!wallet.Feed(PetShopItemId.Fish).Succeeded && wallet.Snapshot.Quantity(PetShopItemId.Fish) == 1,
            "A failed atomic consumption removed confirmed food.");
        Program.Assert(!wallet.Equip(PetShopItemId.Collar).Succeeded && !wallet.Snapshot.IsEquipped(PetShopItemId.Collar)
            && !wallet.Unequip(PetAccessorySlot.Head).Succeeded && wallet.Snapshot.IsEquipped(PetShopItemId.StarClip),
            "A failed atomic accessory save changed the confirmed equipment.");
        var waitingBatch = Guid.NewGuid();
        var waitingRequest = Guid.NewGuid();
        Program.Assert(!wallet.SettleBatch(waitingBatch, [waitingRequest]).Succeeded && wallet.Snapshot.HasPendingRewards
            && wallet.Snapshot.Coins == confirmed.Coins, "A failed reward save either changed confirmed coins or lost the pending settlement.");
        var failedWrites = disk.WritesAttempted;
        Program.Assert(!wallet.Buy(PetShopItemId.CannedFood).Succeeded && disk.WritesAttempted == failedWrites,
            "Paid transactions continued while a reward was not saved.");
        disk.FailWrites = false;
        Program.Assert(wallet.RetryPendingRewards().CoinsChanged == 5 && wallet.Snapshot.Coins == confirmed.Coins + 5
            && !wallet.Snapshot.HasPendingRewards && wallet.SettleBatch(waitingBatch, [waitingRequest]).CoinsChanged == 0,
            "Retrying failed rewards lost coins or credited the same batch twice.");
        wallet.StateChanged += (_, _) => throw new InvalidOperationException("Controlled failed view observer.");
        Program.Assert(wallet.Buy(PetShopItemId.CannedFood).Succeeded && wallet.Snapshot.Quantity(PetShopItemId.CannedFood) == 1,
            "An observer exception converted a committed purchase into a failure.");
        run.Evidence["petWallet"] = new
        {
            initialCoins = 30, successfulRequestReward = 5, failuresCancelledAndExhaustedReward = 0,
            partialAndMergedRequestsCounted = true, duplicatesWithinBatchAndRestartPrevented = true, genuineLaterOperationsRewarded = true,
            atomicPaymentConsumptionAndEquipment = true, failedRewardsRetainedAndRetried = true,
            paidTransactionsBlockedDuringUnsavedRewards = true, observerExceptionsCannotRollbackSuccess = true,
            method = "Production PetStateStore with controlled atomic persistence failures; no real user settings."
        };
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }

    private static Task WorkAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var clock = new ManualClock();
        var disk = new MemoryPersistence();
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-work-model");
        var wallet = new PetStateStore(directory, clock, disk);
        Program.Assert(wallet.StartWork().Succeeded && wallet.Snapshot.WorkElapsed == TimeSpan.Zero, "Work did not begin at zero.");
        var startWrites = disk.WritesAttempted;
        AdvanceActive(wallet, clock, 10);
        Program.Assert(wallet.Snapshot.WorkEarned == .66m && wallet.Snapshot.Coins == 30 && disk.WritesAttempted == startWrites,
            "Ten seconds did not earn .66 provisional coins, or the wallet wrote every second.");
        Program.Assert(wallet.StartWork().Succeeded && wallet.Snapshot.WorkElapsed == TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10)
            && disk.WritesAttempted == startWrites, "Repeated Start reset or rewrote a running session.");
        AdvanceActive(wallet, clock, 20);
        Program.Assert(wallet.Snapshot.Coins == 32 && disk.WritesAttempted == startWrites + 1,
            "The 30-second checkpoint did not atomically credit exactly 2 coins.");
        wallet.SetWorkAvailability(false, false, true);
        AdvanceActive(wallet, clock, 330);
        Program.Assert(wallet.Snapshot.WorkElapsed == TimeSpan.FromMinutes(6) && wallet.Snapshot.WorkEarned == 24
            && wallet.Snapshot.WorkRunning && wallet.Snapshot.Coins == 54,
            "Hidden, disabled or processing presentation paused runtime work, or work ended at five minutes.");
        wallet.SetSystemPaused(true);
        clock.Advance(TimeSpan.FromHours(2)); wallet.Tick();
        Program.Assert(wallet.Snapshot.WorkElapsed == TimeSpan.FromMinutes(6) && !wallet.Snapshot.WorkRunning,
            "Locked or suspended time earned coins.");
        wallet.SetSystemPaused(false);
        AdvanceActive(wallet, clock, 10);
        var stop = wallet.StopWork();
        Program.Assert(stop.Succeeded && stop.WorkSession?.Duration == TimeSpan.FromSeconds(370)
            && stop.WorkSession.CoinsEarned == 24.66m && wallet.Snapshot.Coins == 54.66m && !wallet.Snapshot.WorkRequested,
            "Stop did not save exact elapsed time and remaining fractional earnings.");
        var stoppedSequence = wallet.Snapshot.Sequence;
        Program.Assert(wallet.StopWork().Succeeded && wallet.Snapshot.Sequence == stoppedSequence && wallet.Snapshot.Coins == 54.66m,
            "Repeated Stop paid or wrote the same session twice.");
        Program.Assert(wallet.StartWork().Succeeded && wallet.Snapshot.WorkElapsed == TimeSpan.Zero && wallet.Snapshot.WorkEarned == 0,
            "The next Start did not reset session counters.");
        AdvanceActive(wallet, clock, 10);
        Program.Assert(wallet.Buy(PetShopItemId.Fish).Succeeded && wallet.Snapshot.Coins == 45.32m,
            "A purchase did not atomically checkpoint the latest .66 coins before paying 10.");
        wallet.StopWork();

        var crashClock = new ManualClock(); var crashDisk = new MemoryPersistence();
        var crashDirectory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-work-crash");
        var crashing = new PetStateStore(crashDirectory, crashClock, crashDisk);
        crashing.StartWork(); AdvanceActive(crashing, crashClock, 37);
        crashClock.Advance(TimeSpan.FromDays(2));
        var recovered = new PetStateStore(crashDirectory, crashClock, crashDisk);
        Program.Assert(!recovered.Snapshot.WorkRequested && recovered.Snapshot.Coins == 32
            && recovered.Snapshot.LastWorkSession?.Duration == TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30),
            "Crash recovery paid offline or uncheckpointed time, or resumed automatically.");
        var recoveredAgain = new PetStateStore(crashDirectory, crashClock, crashDisk);
        Program.Assert(recoveredAgain.Snapshot.Coins == 32 && recoveredAgain.StartWork().Succeeded
            && recoveredAgain.Snapshot.WorkElapsed == TimeSpan.Zero, "Crash settlement repeated or the new session retained old elapsed time.");

        var failureClock = new ManualClock(); var failureDisk = new MemoryPersistence();
        var failure = new PetStateStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-work-failure"), failureClock, failureDisk);
        failure.StartWork(); AdvanceActive(failure, failureClock, 10); failureDisk.FailWrites = true;
        Program.Assert(!failure.StopWork().Succeeded && failure.Snapshot.Coins == 30 && failure.Snapshot.HasPendingRewards
            && !failure.Snapshot.WorkRunning && !failure.StartWork().Succeeded,
            "A failed stop changed confirmed money, lost settlement or allowed a new session.");
        failureClock.Advance(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(2)); failure.Tick();
        failureDisk.FailWrites = false;
        Program.Assert(failure.RetryPendingRewards().Succeeded && failure.Snapshot.Coins == 30.66m
            && !failure.Snapshot.WorkRequested && failure.Snapshot.LastWorkSession?.Duration == TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10),
            "Stop retry paid twice, credited time while blocked, or lost the captured session.");

        var checkpointClock = new ManualClock(); var checkpointDisk = new MemoryPersistence();
        var checkpoint = new PetStateStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-work-checkpoint-failure"), checkpointClock, checkpointDisk);
        checkpoint.StartWork(); AdvanceActive(checkpoint, checkpointClock, 29); checkpointDisk.FailWrites = true;
        AdvanceActive(checkpoint, checkpointClock, 1);
        Program.Assert(checkpoint.Snapshot.HasPendingRewards && !checkpoint.Snapshot.WorkRunning
            && checkpoint.Snapshot.Coins == 30 && checkpoint.Snapshot.WorkEarned == 2,
            "A failed periodic checkpoint confirmed money or continued accruing unsaved work.");
        checkpointClock.Advance(TimeSpan.FromHours(1)); checkpoint.Tick();
        var pendingWrites = checkpointDisk.WritesAttempted;
        Program.Assert(!checkpoint.Buy(PetShopItemId.Fish).Succeeded && checkpointDisk.WritesAttempted == pendingWrites
            && checkpoint.Snapshot.WorkElapsed == TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30),
            "A pending checkpoint allowed a purchase or earned blocked time.");
        checkpointDisk.FailWrites = false; checkpoint.Tick();
        Program.Assert(checkpoint.Snapshot.Coins == 32 && checkpoint.Snapshot.WorkRunning && !checkpoint.Snapshot.HasPendingRewards,
            "A retried checkpoint did not confirm exactly once and resume work.");
        AdvanceActive(checkpoint, checkpointClock, 15); checkpoint.StopWork();
        Program.Assert(checkpoint.Snapshot.Coins == 33 && checkpoint.Snapshot.LastWorkSession?.Duration == TimeSpan.FromSeconds(45),
            "Resuming after checkpoint retry duplicated rewards or retained blocked time.");

        foreach (var remaining in new[] { 290_000d, 0d })
        {
            var migrationDisk = new MemoryPersistence();
            var migrationDirectory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-work-v1-" + remaining);
            var migrationPath = Path.Combine(migrationDirectory, "pet-state.json");
            migrationDisk.WriteAtomically(migrationPath, JsonSerializer.Serialize(new
            {
                Version = 1, Coins = 35, Inventory = new Dictionary<PetShopItemId, int> { [PetShopItemId.Fish] = 2, [PetShopItemId.Yarn] = 1, [PetShopItemId.Collar] = 1 },
                EquippedAccessories = new Dictionary<PetAccessorySlot, PetShopItemId> { [PetAccessorySlot.Neck] = PetShopItemId.Collar }, WorkRequested = true,
                WorkPaused = true, WorkRemainingMilliseconds = remaining, Sequence = 1, LastSettlementSequence = 1,
                SettledBatchIds = new[] { Guid.NewGuid() }
            }));
            var migrated = new PetStateStore(migrationDirectory, clock, migrationDisk);
            var expected = remaining == 0 ? 20m : .66m;
            Program.Assert(migrated.Snapshot.Coins == 35 + expected && !migrated.Snapshot.WorkRequested
                && migrated.Snapshot.LastWorkSession?.CoinsEarned == expected && migrated.Snapshot.Quantity(PetShopItemId.Fish) == 2
                && migrated.Snapshot.Owns(PetShopItemId.Yarn) && migrated.Snapshot.IsEquipped(PetShopItemId.Collar),
                "Legacy paused progress was lost, automatically resumed or paid the wrong amount.");
            Program.Assert(new PetStateStore(migrationDirectory, clock, migrationDisk).Snapshot.Coins == 35 + expected,
                "Legacy migration paid again after reopening.");
        }
        Program.Assert(PetStateStore.EarnedFor(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(999).Ticks) == 0
            && PetStateStore.EarnedFor(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1).Ticks) == .06m
            && PetStateStore.EarnedFor(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(15).Ticks) == 1,
            "Rewards did not floor whole seconds and the cumulative two-decimal total deterministically.");
        run.Evidence["petCompanionWork"] = new { coinsPerMinute = 4, precision = "floor cumulative whole seconds to .01",
            hiddenAndBusyRuntimeCounted = true, lockAndSuspendExcluded = true, checkpointSeconds = 30,
            checkpointAtomicallyCreditsWallet = true, stopAndPurchaseSettleLatest = true, repeatedStartAndStopIdempotent = true,
            crashAndLegacyMigrationFinalizeOnce = true, legacyInventoryAndAccessoriesRetained = true,
            failedStopRetriesExactlyOnce = true, failedCheckpointPausesUntilConfirmedThenResumes = true };
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }

    private static Task PersistenceAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-wallet-real-files");
        var wallet = new PetStateStore(directory);
        var batch = Guid.NewGuid();
        var request = Guid.NewGuid();
        Program.Assert(wallet.SettleBatch(batch, [request]).Succeeded && wallet.Buy(PetShopItemId.Fish).Succeeded,
            "Real atomic wallet settlement or purchase failed.");
        var reopened = new PetStateStore(directory);
        Program.Assert(reopened.Snapshot.Coins == 25 && reopened.Snapshot.Quantity(PetShopItemId.Fish) == 1
            && reopened.SettleBatch(batch, [request]).CoinsChanged == 0, "Real atomic wallet reopen did not preserve confirmed state.");
        Program.Assert(Directory.GetFiles(directory, "*.tmp").Length == 0 && File.Exists(wallet.FilePath + ".bak"),
            "Atomic wallet replacement left temporary files or omitted its recoverable previous revision.");
        var damagedDirectory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-wallet-damaged");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(damagedDirectory);
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(damagedDirectory, "pet-state.json"), "{ broken wallet");
        var damaged = new PetStateStore(damagedDirectory);
        Program.Assert(!damaged.Buy(PetShopItemId.Fish).Succeeded && !damaged.SettleBatch(Guid.NewGuid(), [Guid.NewGuid()]).Succeeded
            && damaged.Snapshot.Coins == 0 && File.ReadAllText(damaged.FilePath) == "{ broken wallet", "A damaged wallet was overwritten by a fresh funded account.");
        var incompleteDirectory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-wallet-incomplete");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(incompleteDirectory);
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(incompleteDirectory, "pet-state.json"), "{}");
        var incomplete = new PetStateStore(incompleteDirectory);
        Program.Assert(incomplete.Snapshot.Coins == 0 && incomplete.Snapshot.LastError is not null
            && !incomplete.Buy(PetShopItemId.Fish).Succeeded && File.ReadAllText(incomplete.FilePath) == "{}",
            "An incomplete wallet silently received funded-account field defaults.");
        File.Delete(wallet.FilePath);
        var backupOnly = new PetStateStore(directory);
        Program.Assert(backupOnly.Snapshot.Coins == 35 && backupOnly.SettleBatch(batch, [request]).CoinsChanged == 0,
            "A missing primary wallet ignored a confirmed previous-revision backup or lost its settlement receipt.");

        var memory = new MemoryPersistence();
        var concurrent = new PetStateStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-wallet-concurrent"), persistence: memory);
        Parallel.For(0, 100, _ =>
        {
            Program.Assert(concurrent.SettleBatch(Guid.NewGuid(), [Guid.NewGuid()]).Succeeded, "A concurrent serialized settlement failed.");
        });
        Program.Assert(concurrent.Snapshot.Coins == 530 && concurrent.Snapshot.LastSettlementSequence == 100
            && memory.MaximumConcurrentWriters == 1, "Concurrent rewards lost coins, skipped settlement sequence or wrote concurrently.");
        var historyDirectory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "pet-wallet-bounded-history");
        var historyPath = Path.Combine(historyDirectory, "pet-state.json");
        var history = Enumerable.Range(0, 2050).Select(_ => Guid.NewGuid()).ToArray();
        var historyDisk = new MemoryPersistence();
        historyDisk.WriteAtomically(historyPath, JsonSerializer.Serialize(new
        {
            Version = 1, Coins = 100, Inventory = new Dictionary<PetShopItemId, int>(),
            EquippedAccessories = new Dictionary<PetAccessorySlot, PetShopItemId>(), WorkRequested = false,
            WorkPaused = false, WorkRemainingMilliseconds = PetStateStore.WorkDuration.TotalMilliseconds,
            Sequence = 2050, LastSettlementSequence = 2050, SettledBatchIds = history
        }));
        var bounded = new PetStateStore(historyDirectory, persistence: historyDisk);
        Program.Assert(bounded.SettleBatch(history[^1], [Guid.NewGuid()]).CoinsChanged == 0
            && bounded.Buy(PetShopItemId.Fish).Succeeded,
            "Loading a large receipt history lost recent duplicate protection or blocked normal transactions.");
        using var savedHistory = JsonDocument.Parse(historyDisk.Read(historyPath)!);
        Program.Assert(savedHistory.RootElement.GetProperty("SettledBatchIds").GetArrayLength() == 2048
            && !savedHistory.RootElement.TryGetProperty("RewardedRequestIds", out _),
            "The wallet retained unbounded batch receipts or an obsolete permanent request ID ledger.");
        run.Evidence["petPersistence"] = new
        {
            realAtomicReplaceAndReopen = true, previousRevisionBackupPresent = true, noTemporaryFiles = true,
            damagedAndIncompleteWalletNeverSilentlyReset = true, missingPrimaryRecoveredFromBackup = true,
            recentBatchReceiptLimit = 2048, boundedHistoryVerified = true, concurrentSuccessfulRequests = 100, finalCoins = 530,
            monotonicSettlementSequence = 100, maximumConcurrentWriters = memory.MaximumConcurrentWriters
        };
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }

    private static void AdvanceActive(PetStateStore wallet, ManualClock clock, int seconds)
    {
        for (var second = 0; second < seconds; second++)
        {
            clock.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
            wallet.Tick();
        }
    }

    private sealed class ManualClock : TimeProvider
    {
        private long timestamp;
        public override long TimestampFrequency => TimeSpan.TicksPerSecond;
        public override long GetTimestamp() => timestamp;
        public void Advance(TimeSpan elapsed) => timestamp += elapsed.Ticks;
    }

    private sealed class MemoryPersistence : IPetStatePersistence
    {
        private readonly Dictionary<string, string> documents = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        private int activeWriters;
        public bool FailWrites { get; set; }
        public int WritesAttempted { get; private set; }
        public int MaximumConcurrentWriters { get; private set; }
        public string? Read(string path) => documents.GetValueOrDefault(path);
        public void WriteAtomically(string path, string contents)
        {
            WritesAttempted++;
            var active = Interlocked.Increment(ref activeWriters);
            MaximumConcurrentWriters = Math.Max(MaximumConcurrentWriters, active);
            try
            {
                if (FailWrites) throw new IOException("Controlled save failure before atomic commitment.");
                using var parsed = JsonDocument.Parse(contents);
                if (documents.TryGetValue(path, out var old)) documents[path + ".bak"] = old;
                documents[path] = contents;
            }
            finally { Interlocked.Decrement(ref activeWriters); }
        }
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/PetIntegrationAcceptance.cs ---
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

internal static class PetIntegrationAcceptance
{
    public static async Task RunAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        await run.CaseAsync("pet-page-tray-lifecycle-pending-files-and-task-priority",()=>LifecycleAsync(run));
        await run.CaseAsync("pet-real-assets-static-poses-and-ui-pages",()=>AssetsAsync(run));
    }
    private static MainViewModel Model(AcceptanceRun run,string name,IConversionEngine engine)
    {
        var settings=new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory,name,"settings"));
        settings.Save(new AppSettings { Pet=new PetSettings { Roaming=false,Topmost=false,HideForFullscreen=false } });
        return new MainViewModel(engine,PresetCatalog.Load(Path.Combine(run.Root,"shared","presets.json")),settings,new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(run.Directory,name,"logs")));
    }
    private static async Task LifecycleAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var engine=new WaitingEngine(); var model=Model(run,"pet-lifecycle",engine);
        var source=Path.Combine(run.Directory,"pet-lifecycle","source.mp4"); File.WriteAllText(source,"unchanged pet lifecycle fixture");run.TrackSource(source);
        var window=new MainWindow(model,()=>true); window.Show();
        using var controller=new PetController(model) { MainWindowProvider=()=>window };
        window.AttachPetController(controller);
        window.HideToBackground=()=> { window.Hide();return true; };
        try
        {
            await model.AddFilesAsync([source]);
            var processing=model.RunQueueAsync(); await engine.Started.Task;
            window.ShowPetPage("settings"); await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle);
            Program.Assert(window.IsPetPageOpen && model.IsProcessing,"The pet page could not open while processing.");
            window.Close();
            Program.Assert(!window.IsVisible && model.IsProcessing && !processing.IsCompleted,"Closing the main window cancelled background processing.");
            controller.ReceiveFiles([source,source,Path.Combine(run.Directory,"missing.mp4")]);
            Program.Assert(controller.PendingPaths.Count==2,"Busy drops were lost or not deduplicated.");
            controller.ChangeSettings(s=>s.Enabled=false);
            Program.Assert(model.IsProcessing && !controller.DesktopWindow.IsVisible && controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView.ActiveFrameTimerCount==0,"Disabling the pet cancelled processing or retained active frames.");
            model.Cancel(); await processing;
            Program.Assert(controller.Account.Snapshot.Coins==30,"A cancelled file job rewarded coins.");
            controller.ClearPendingFiles();
            window.Show(); window.ShowFilePage();
            Program.Assert(!window.IsPetPageOpen && model.CanEdit,"The original file page did not restore after the pet page.");
            run.Evidence["petControllerLifecycle"]=new { closeHidesAndPreservesTask=true,disableDoesNotCancel=true,busyDropsDeduplicated=true,petPageAccessibleWhileBusy=true,cancelNoReward=true };
        }
        finally { if(model.IsProcessing) {model.Cancel();await model.ActiveRun;} window.ApproveExit();window.Close(); }
    }
    private static async Task AssetsAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System);
        var model=Model(run,"pet-pages",new WaitingEngine());
        var window=new MainWindow(model);window.Show();
        using var controller=new PetController(model);
        window.AttachPetController(controller);
        try
        {
            Program.Assert(controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView.HasSpriteAtlas && controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView.AssetError is null,"The production desktop pet atlas failed to load.");
            var oldPage=model.PageTitle;
            foreach(var tab in new[] {"companion","files","shop","inventory","settings","companion"})
            {
                window.ShowPetPage(tab);window.UpdateLayout();await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle);
                Program.CaptureWindow(run,window,"v08-pet-"+tab);
                Program.Assert(window.IsPetPageOpen && model.PageTitle==oldPage,"Pet navigation altered the selected file operation.");
            }
            controller.Account.SettleBatch(Guid.NewGuid(),Enumerable.Range(0,25).Select(_=>Guid.NewGuid()));
            controller.Account.Buy(PetShopItemId.Collar);controller.Account.Buy(PetShopItemId.StarClip);
            controller.Account.Equip(PetShopItemId.Collar);controller.Account.Equip(PetShopItemId.StarClip);
            controller.Account.StartWork();
            controller.HideTemporarily();
            var before=controller.Account.Snapshot.WorkElapsed;
            await Task.Delay(250);
            controller.Account.Tick();
            Program.Assert(controller.Account.Snapshot.WorkRunning && controller.Account.Snapshot.WorkElapsed>before && !controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView.HasActiveAnimation,"Hidden runtime work stopped or hidden animation frames kept running.");
            controller.ToggleVisibility();
            Program.Assert(controller.Account.Snapshot.WorkRunning,"Returning to the pet did not resume companion work.");
            var beforeInteraction = controller.Account.Snapshot.WorkElapsed;
            controller.Interact("pet-head");
            await Task.Delay(200); controller.Account.Tick();
            Program.Assert(controller.CurrentAnimation == "happy" && controller.Account.Snapshot.WorkRunning
                && controller.Account.Snapshot.WorkElapsed > beforeInteraction,
                "Leisure work hid interaction feedback or paused its cumulative clock during the interaction.");
            var stopped = controller.StopWork();
            Program.Assert(stopped.Succeeded && stopped.WorkSession is not null && controller.CurrentAnimation == "success"
                && controller.LatestMessage.Contains(PetController.WorkDurationText(stopped.WorkSession.Duration)),
                "Stopping work did not show its saved duration and success animation.");
            var celebration = controller.LatestMessage;
            controller.StopWork();
            Program.Assert(controller.LatestMessage == celebration, "Repeated Stop replaced or repeated the saved celebration.");
            controller.Account.StartWork();
            await Task.Delay(1100);
            // A saved stop result follows the same presentation route as a manual save retry.
            var retried = controller.Account.StopWork();
            controller.Explain(retried);
            Program.Assert(retried.Succeeded && retried.WorkSession is { CoinsEarned: > 0 }
                && controller.CurrentAnimation == "success" && controller.LatestMessage != celebration
                && controller.LatestMessage.Contains(PetController.WorkDurationText(retried.WorkSession.Duration)),
                "A confirmed retry result did not celebrate the saved work session.");
            celebration = controller.LatestMessage;
            controller.Explain(retried);
            Program.Assert(controller.LatestMessage == celebration, "A repeated saved result celebrated the same session again.");
            run.Evidence["petProductionAssetsAndPages"]=new {atlasLoaded=true,pages=new[] {"companion","files","shop","inventory","settings"},filePageSelectionPreserved=true,hiddenWorkContinues=true,workResumed=true,interactionsAnimateWhileWorkAccrues=true,stopAndConfirmedRetryCelebrateOnce=true};
        }
        finally { window.ApproveExit();window.Close(); }
    }
    private sealed class WaitingEngine:IConversionEngine
    {
        public TaskCompletionSource Started {get;}=new(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
        public Task<MediaInfo> ProbeAsync(string path,CancellationToken token=default)=>Task.FromResult(new MediaInfo(true,true,1,"mp4","h264","aac",SourceBytes:50,Width:64,Height:64));
        public async Task<ConversionResult> ConvertAsync(ConversionRequest request,IProgress<ConversionProgress>? progress=null,CancellationToken cancellationToken=default)
        {
            Started.TrySetResult();progress?.Report(new ConversionProgress(35,TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(350)));
            try {await Task.Delay(Timeout.Infinite,cancellationToken);return new(JobStatus.Completed,null,null);}
            catch(OperationCanceledException) {return new(JobStatus.Cancelled,null,null);}
        }
    }
}

using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Windows.Media;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;
using Microsoft.Win32;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

internal static class PetPerformanceAcceptance
{
    public static async Task RunAsync(AcceptanceRun run,IConversionEngine engine,IReadOnlyList<FormatPreset> presets)
    {
        var settings=new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory,"pet-performance-settings"));
        settings.Save(new AppSettings { Pet=new PetSettings { Enabled=false,Roaming=true,Topmost=false,HideForFullscreen=false,MotionMode=PetMotionMode.Gentle } });
        var model=new MainViewModel(engine,presets,settings,new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(run.Directory,"pet-performance-logs"))) {OutputDirectory=Path.Combine(run.Directory,"pet-performance-output")};
        model.SelectedPreset=presets.First(p=>p.Mode==ConversionMode.Video&&p.ExpectedCodec=="hevc");
        model.MotionPreference=MotionPreference.System;
        var window=new MainWindow(model,()=>true) {ShowActivated=false};window.Show();
        PetController? controller=null;
        run.StopActiveWindow=async()=> {model.Cancel();await model.ActiveRun;};
        try
        {
            await Task.Delay(1500);
            GC.Collect();GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers();GC.Collect();
            using var process=Process.GetCurrentProcess(); process.Refresh();var earlyBaselineMemory=process.PrivateMemorySize64;
            var seconds=run.Quick?10:60;
            var baseline=await CpuAsync(process,seconds,"baseline");
            // Compare memory at the same boundary in both scenes: after their full CPU
            // interval and the same GC/finalizer pass. Keep the early sample so native
            // initialization during the no-pet scene remains visible in the report.
            GC.Collect();GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers();GC.Collect();process.Refresh();var baselineMemory=process.PrivateMemorySize64;
            controller=new PetController(model);
            controller.ChangeSettings(s=>s.Enabled=true);
            Program.Assert(controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView.HasSpriteAtlas,"Performance sampled a fallback rather than the actual pet atlas.");
            await Task.Delay(1500);
            Program.Assert(controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView.ActiveAmbientClockCount==1,"Performance did not sample the actual continuously breathing pet.");
            var visible=await CpuAsync(process,seconds,"visible");
            GC.Collect();GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers();GC.Collect();process.Refresh();var visibleMemory=process.PrivateMemorySize64;
            controller.HideTemporarily();await Task.Delay(300);
            var hidden=await CpuAsync(process,seconds,"hidden");
            Program.Assert(!controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView.HasActiveAnimation && controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView.ActiveAmbientClockCount==0 && controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView.ActiveFrameTimerCount==0 && controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView.ActiveTransitionClockCount==0,"Hidden pet had an active sprite or ambient clock during CPU sampling.");
            controller.ToggleVisibility();
            foreach(var enabled in new[] {false,true}) {controller.ChangeSettings(s=>s.Enabled=enabled);await ConvertAsync(model,run.LongVideo);}
            var enabledDurations=new List<double>();var disabledDurations=new List<double>();
            for(var pair=0;pair<3;pair++)
            {
                foreach(var enabled in pair%2==0?new[] {false,true}:new[] {true,false})
                {
                    controller.ChangeSettings(s=>s.Enabled=enabled);model.Clear();await model.AddFilesAsync([run.LongVideo]);await Task.Delay(250);
                    var watch=Stopwatch.StartNew();await model.RunQueueAsync().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5));watch.Stop();
                    Program.Assert(model.VisibleQueue.Single().Status==JobStatus.Completed,"Performance file conversion did not complete.");
                    (enabled?enabledDurations:disabledDurations).Add(watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds);
                    Console.WriteLine($"PET CONVERSION {enabled} pair {pair+1}: {watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds:F3}s");
                }
            }
            var on=Median(enabledDurations);var off=Median(disabledDurations);
            var conversionIncrease=Math.Max(0,(on-off)/off*100);
            var cpuIncrease=Math.Max(0,visible.Percent-baseline.Percent);
            var memoryIncrease=Math.Max(0,visibleMemory-baselineMemory);
            var cpuPassed=cpuIncrease<=1;var memoryPassed=memoryIncrease<=50*1024*1024;var conversionPassed=conversionIncrease<=5;
            run.Evidence["petPerformance"]=new
            {
                machine=new {os=Environment.OSVersion.VersionString,cpu=CpuName(),logicalProcessors=Environment.ProcessorCount,renderTier=RenderCapability.Tier>>16,dpi=VisualTreeHelper.GetDpi(window).PixelsPerInchX},
