                petMotionMode=PetMotionMode.Gentle.ToString(),windowsClientAreaAnimation=System.Windows.SystemParameters.ClientAreaAnimation,
                sampleSeconds=seconds,processCpuPercentMethod="Process.TotalProcessorTime / Stopwatch elapsed / logical processors * 100",
                baseline,visible,hidden,cpuIncreasePercent=cpuIncrease,earlyBaselinePrivateBytes=earlyBaselineMemory,baselinePrivateBytes=baselineMemory,visiblePrivateBytes=visibleMemory,memoryIncreaseBytes=memoryIncrease,
                baselineSettlingIncreaseBytes=baselineMemory-earlyBaselineMemory,againstEarlyBaselineIncreaseBytes=Math.Max(0,visibleMemory-earlyBaselineMemory),
                memoryMethod="PrivateMemorySize64 after each scene's full CPU interval and an identical full GC/finalizer pass; earlyBaselinePrivateBytes is diagnostic only. The original initial V1.0 run sampled baseline memory before its CPU interval; that failed report is retained separately.",
                enabledConversionSeconds=enabledDurations,disabledConversionSeconds=disabledDurations,enabledMedianSeconds=on,disabledMedianSeconds=off,conversionIncreasePercent=conversionIncrease,
                source=run.LongVideo,preset=model.SelectedPreset.Id,budgets=new {idleCpuIncreasePercent=1,memoryIncreaseMiB=50,conversionMedianIncreasePercent=5},cpuPassed,memoryPassed,conversionPassed,
                systemMotionAllowedForTesting=false,
                limitation="These are measurements on this Windows host; not claims for other hardware, DPI, drivers, or games. The actual default Gentle pet mode was sampled at the host's real Windows animation preference without a system-motion override."
            };
            run.SaveReport();
            Program.Assert(cpuPassed,$"Idle desktop pet CPU increase {cpuIncrease:F3}% exceeded 1%.");
            Program.Assert(memoryPassed,$"Desktop pet private memory increase {memoryIncrease/1048576d:F2} MiB exceeded 50 MiB.");
            Program.Assert(conversionPassed,$"Desktop pet conversion median increase {conversionIncrease:F2}% exceeded 5%.");
        }
        finally {controller?.Dispose();if(model.IsProcessing){model.Cancel();await model.ActiveRun;}window.ApproveExit();window.Close();run.StopActiveWindow=null;}
    }
    private static async Task ConvertAsync(MainViewModel model,string source)
    {model.Clear();await model.AddFilesAsync([source]);await model.RunQueueAsync().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5));Program.Assert(model.VisibleQueue.Single().Status==JobStatus.Completed,"Warmup conversion failed.");}
    private static double Median(List<double> values)=>values.Order().ElementAt(values.Count/2);
    private static string CpuName()
    {
        try {using var key=Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey(@"HARDWARE\DESCRIPTION\System\CentralProcessor\0");return key?.GetValue("ProcessorNameString")?.ToString()?.Trim()??"unavailable";}
        catch(System.Security.SecurityException) {return "unavailable";}
        catch(UnauthorizedAccessException) {return "unavailable";}
    }
    private static async Task<CpuSample> CpuAsync(Process process,int seconds,string name)
    {
        process.Refresh();var cpu=process.TotalProcessorTime;var watch=Stopwatch.StartNew();await Task.Delay(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(seconds));watch.Stop();process.Refresh();
        var percent=(process.TotalProcessorTime-cpu).TotalSeconds/watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds/Environment.ProcessorCount*100;
        Console.WriteLine($"PET CPU {name}: {percent:F4}% over {watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds:F2}s");
        return new(watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds,percent);
    }
    private sealed record CpuSample(double Seconds,double Percent);
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/Program.cs ---
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Security.AccessControl;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Security.Principal;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Input;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Controls;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

/// <summary>Dependency-free acceptance runner. Uses real FFmpeg and a real STA WPF dispatcher.</summary>
internal static class Program
{
    [STAThread]
    private static int Main(string[] args)
    {
        if (DesktopPetPlatformAcceptance.RunProbe(args) is { } petProbeExit) return petProbeExit;
        if (RecoveryFailureAcceptance.RunFakeWorker(args) is { } recoveryWorkerExit) return recoveryWorkerExit;
        if (ArchiveFailureAcceptance.RunFakeWorker(args) is { } fakeWorkerExit) return fakeWorkerExit;
        Trace.Listeners.Add(new ConsoleTraceListener(useErrorStream: true));
        AcceptanceRun run;
        try { run = new AcceptanceRun(args); }
        catch (Exception exception) { Console.Error.WriteLine(exception.Message); return 2; }
        OwnedArtifacts.Configure(run.Directory);
        var application = new Application { ShutdownMode = ShutdownMode.OnExplicitShutdown };
        var dispatcherFailure = false;
        application.DispatcherUnhandledException += (_, eventArgs) =>
        {
            eventArgs.Handled = true;
            if (dispatcherFailure) return;
            dispatcherFailure = true;
            run.RecordFailure("dispatcher-unhandled-exception", eventArgs.Exception);
            application.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(async () =>
            {
                try { if (run.StopActiveWindow is not null) await run.StopActiveWindow(); }
                catch (Exception exception) { run.RecordFailure("dispatcher-failure-cleanup", exception); }
                finally { run.Complete(); run.SaveReport(); application.Shutdown(1); }
            }));
        };
        application.Startup += async (_, _) =>
        {
            try { await ExecuteAsync(run); }
            catch (Exception exception) { run.RecordFailure("runner", exception); }
            finally
            {
                run.Complete();
                run.SaveReport();
                Console.WriteLine($"Report: {run.ReportPath}");
                if (!application.Dispatcher.HasShutdownStarted) application.Shutdown(run.Failures == 0 ? 0 : 1);
            }
        };
        return application.Run();
    }

    private static async Task RunPetAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        await PetEconomyAcceptance.RunAsync(run);
        await FilePetBridgeAcceptance.RunAsync(run);
        await PetAnimationAcceptance.RunSettingsAsync(run);
        await V101WalkingAcceptance.RunGeometryAsync(run);
        await V101PlayerAcceptance.RunMetadataAsync(run);
        await run.CaseAsync("pet-monitor-geometry-game-list-and-long-press", () => { DesktopPetPlatformAcceptance.RunGeometry(run); return Task.CompletedTask; });
        if (!run.NoGui)
        {
            await run.CaseAsync("pet-transparent-window-sprite-clock-and-focus-lifecycle", () => DesktopPetPlatformAcceptance.RunWindowAsync(run));
            await run.CaseAsync("pet-real-fullscreen-and-windowed-game-hide-restore", () => DesktopPetPlatformAcceptance.RunForegroundProbeAsync(run));
            await PetIntegrationAcceptance.RunAsync(run);
            await PetAnimationAcceptance.RunAsync(run);
            await V101WalkingAcceptance.RunWindowAsync(run);
            await V101WalkingAcceptance.RunSegmentWindowAsync(run);
            await V101PlayerAcceptance.RunWindowAsync(run);
            await PetAccessoryAcceptance.RunAsync(run);
            await run.CaseAsync("pet-submenu-slow-pointer-left-right-and-dismissal", () => DesktopPetMenuAcceptance.RunAsync(run));
        }
    }
    private static async Task ExecuteAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        if (run.SelectedCase == "v10") { await V10Acceptance.RunAsync(run); return; }
        if (run.SelectedCase == "v101")
        {
            await V101WalkingAcceptance.RunGeometryAsync(run);
            await V101PlayerAcceptance.RunMetadataAsync(run);
            if (!run.NoGui)
            {
                await V101WalkingAcceptance.RunWindowAsync(run);
                await V101WalkingAcceptance.RunSegmentWindowAsync(run);
                await V101PlayerAcceptance.RunWindowAsync(run);
                await PetAccessoryAcceptance.RunAsync(run);
            }
            else
            {
                run.RecordSkipped("v101-real-walk-turn-landing-and-latest-request", "--no-gui skips the actual desktop window walking check.");
                run.RecordSkipped("v101-real-window-v3-fixture-turn-entry-and-exit-contract", "--no-gui skips the actual v3 fixture window check.");
            }
            return;
        }
        if (run.SelectedCase == "recovery-gui")
        {
            await RecoveryAcceptance.RunGuiOnlyAsync(run);
            return;
        }
        if (run.SelectedCase == "pet-animation")
        {
            await PetAnimationAcceptance.RunSettingsAsync(run);
            await PetAnimationAcceptance.RunAsync(run);
            return;
        }
        if (run.SelectedCase == "home-companion")
        {
            await HomeCompanionAcceptance.RunAsync(run);
            return;
        }
        if (run.SelectedCase == "pet-interaction")
        {
            await HomeCompanionAcceptance.RunPettingAsync(run);
            return;
        }
        if (run.SelectedCase == "pet-menu")
        {
            await run.CaseAsync("pet-submenu-slow-pointer-left-right-and-dismissal", () => DesktopPetMenuAcceptance.RunAsync(run));
            return;
        }
        if (run.SelectedCase == "result-actions")
        {
            await V09Acceptance.RunResultActionsAsync(run);
            return;
        }
        if (run.SelectedCase == "v09-format")
        {
            await V09Acceptance.RunFormatPickerAsync(run);
            return;
        }
        if (run.SelectedCase == "v09")
        {
            await V09Acceptance.RunAsync(run);
            await run.CaseAsync("all-source-hashes-unchanged", () => { run.AssertSourcesUnchanged(); return Task.CompletedTask; });
            return;
        }
        if (run.SelectedCase == "pet")
        {
            await RunPetAsync(run);
            return;
        }
        if (run.ImageObfuscationOnly)
        {
            await ImageObfuscationAcceptance.RunAsync(run);
            await run.CaseAsync("all-source-hashes-unchanged", () => { run.AssertSourcesUnchanged(); return Task.CompletedTask; });
            return;
        }
        if (run.RecoveryOnly)
        {
            await RecoveryAcceptance.RunAsync(run);
            await run.CaseAsync("all-source-hashes-unchanged", () => { run.AssertSourcesUnchanged(); return Task.CompletedTask; });
            return;
        }
        if (run.ArchiveOnly)
        {
            await ArchiveAcceptance.RunAsync(run);
            await run.CaseAsync("all-source-hashes-unchanged", () => { run.AssertSourcesUnchanged(); return Task.CompletedTask; });
            return;
        }
        if (run.GifOnly)
        {
            var gifPresets = PresetCatalog.Load(Path.Combine(run.Root, "shared", "presets.json"));
            var gifEngine = new FfmpegConversionEngine(run.EngineDirectory, new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "logs")));
            await GifAcceptance.RunAsync(run, gifEngine, gifPresets);
            await run.CaseAsync("all-source-hashes-unchanged", () => { run.AssertSourcesUnchanged(); return Task.CompletedTask; });
            return;
        }
        if (run.CompressionOnly)
        {
            var compressionPresets = PresetCatalog.Load(Path.Combine(run.Root, "shared", "presets.json"));
            var compressionEngine = new FfmpegConversionEngine(run.EngineDirectory, new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "logs")));
            await CompressionAcceptance.RunAsync(run, compressionEngine, compressionPresets);
            await run.CaseAsync("all-source-hashes-unchanged", () => { run.AssertSourcesUnchanged(); return Task.CompletedTask; });
            return;
        }
        if (run.SelectedCase == "motion")
        {
            var focusedPresets = PresetCatalog.Load(Path.Combine(run.Root, "shared", "presets.json"));
            await run.CaseAsync("motion-settings-and-completion-cues", () => MotionAcceptance.RunModelAsync(run, focusedPresets));
            await run.CaseAsync("wpf-motion-state-and-clock-lifecycle", () => MotionAcceptance.RunControlAsync(run));
            await run.CaseAsync("wpf-empty-drop-click-routing", () => RunEmptyDropClickRoutingAsync(run, focusedPresets));
            return;
        }
        if (run.SelectedCase == "empty-drop-click")
        {
            var focusedPresets = PresetCatalog.Load(Path.Combine(run.Root, "shared", "presets.json"));
            await run.CaseAsync("wpf-empty-drop-click-routing", () => RunEmptyDropClickRoutingAsync(run, focusedPresets));
            return;
        }
        await run.CaseAsync("fixture-generation", () => CreateFixturesAsync(run));
        if (!run.FixturesReady) return;
        var presets = PresetCatalog.Load(Path.Combine(run.Root, "shared", "presets.json"));
        var engine = new FfmpegConversionEngine(run.EngineDirectory, new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "logs")));
        if (run.SelectedCase == "home-companion-performance")
        {
            await run.CaseAsync("home-companion-shared-desktop-home-resource-and-real-conversion-budgets", () => HomeCompanionPerformanceAcceptance.RunAsync(run, engine, presets));
            return;
        }
        if (run.SelectedCase == "pet-performance")
        {
            await run.CaseAsync("pet-cpu-memory-and-three-paired-real-conversions",()=>PetPerformanceAcceptance.RunAsync(run,engine,presets));
            return;
        }
        if (run.SelectedCase == "motion-performance")
        {
            await run.CaseAsync("wpf-motion-performance-sampling", () => MotionAcceptance.RunPerformanceAsync(run, engine, presets));
            await run.CaseAsync("all-source-hashes-unchanged", () => { run.AssertSourcesUnchanged(); return Task.CompletedTask; });
            return;
        }
        await run.CaseAsync("motion-settings-and-completion-cues", () => MotionAcceptance.RunModelAsync(run, presets));
        await RunPetAsync(run);
        await V10Acceptance.RunAsync(run);
        await V09Acceptance.RunAsync(run);

        await run.CaseAsync("common-import-rejects-directory-missing-and-wrong-media", async () =>
        {
            var importer = new ImportService(engine);
            var folder = await importer.InspectAsync(run.Directory, ConversionMode.Video);
            Assert(!folder.Accepted && folder.Error?.Code == "INPUT_MISSING", "A dropped folder was accepted as a media file.");
            var missing = await importer.InspectAsync(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "missing.mp4"), ConversionMode.Video);
            Assert(!missing.Accepted && missing.Error?.Code == "INPUT_MISSING", "A missing file was accepted.");
            var wrongExtension = await importer.InspectAsync(run.Audio, ConversionMode.Video);
            Assert(!wrongExtension.Accepted && wrongExtension.Error?.Code == "INPUT_TYPE", "Audio extension was accepted in video mode.");
            var disguised = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "素材 中文 空格", "音频伪装 视频.mp4");
            File.Copy(run.Audio, disguised, true);
            run.TrackSource(disguised);
            var wrongMedia = await importer.InspectAsync(disguised, ConversionMode.Video);
            Assert(!wrongMedia.Accepted && wrongMedia.Error?.Code == "VIDEO_MISSING", "Changing an audio extension bypassed media inspection.");
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("format-memory-persists-per-mode", () =>
        {
            var settings = new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "format-memory-settings"));
            var view = new MainViewModel(engine, presets, settings, new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "format-memory-logs")));
            view.OutputDirectory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "outputs", "remembered");
            var audio = FindPreset(presets, ConversionMode.Audio, "m4a");
            var image = FindPreset(presets, ConversionMode.Image, "webp");
            var video = FindPreset(presets, ConversionMode.Video, "avi");
            view.SetMode(ConversionMode.Audio); view.SelectedPreset = audio;
            view.SetMode(ConversionMode.Image); view.SelectedPreset = image;
            view.SetMode(ConversionMode.Video); view.SelectedPreset = video;
            view.SetMode(ConversionMode.Audio); Assert(view.SelectedPreset?.Id == audio.Id, "Audio selection was reset by mode switching.");
            view.SetMode(ConversionMode.Image); Assert(view.SelectedPreset?.Id == image.Id, "Image selection was reset by mode switching.");
            view.SetMode(ConversionMode.Video); Assert(view.SelectedPreset?.Id == video.Id, "Video selection was reset by mode switching.");
            var reopened = new MainViewModel(engine, presets, settings, new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "format-memory-logs")));
            Assert(reopened.SelectedPreset?.Id == video.Id && reopened.OutputDirectory == view.OutputDirectory, "Settings did not survive reopening.");
            reopened.SetMode(ConversionMode.Audio); Assert(reopened.SelectedPreset?.Id == audio.Id, "Remembered audio preset did not survive reopening.");
            reopened.SetMode(ConversionMode.Image); Assert(reopened.SelectedPreset?.Id == image.Id, "Remembered image preset did not survive reopening.");
            return Task.CompletedTask;
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("brand-state-rules", () =>
        {
            Assert(AssistantStateRules.ForBatch([]) == AssistantState.IDLE, "Empty queue should be idle.");
            Assert(AssistantStateRules.ForBatch([JobStatus.Processing, JobStatus.Failed]) == AssistantState.PROCESSING, "Active processing has priority.");
            Assert(AssistantStateRules.ForBatch([JobStatus.Completed, JobStatus.Failed]) == AssistantState.ERROR, "Partial failure must be visible.");
            Assert(AssistantStateRules.ForBatch([JobStatus.Completed, JobStatus.Completed]) == AssistantState.SUCCESS, "Successful batch should succeed.");
            Assert(AssistantStateRules.ForBatch([JobStatus.Cancelled, JobStatus.Pending]) == AssistantState.IDLE, "Cancellation is not a cat error.");
            return Task.CompletedTask;
        });

        foreach (var preset in presets)
        {
            await run.CaseAsync($"conversion-{preset.Id}", async () =>
            {
                var source = preset.Mode switch { ConversionMode.Audio => run.Audio, ConversionMode.Image => run.Image, _ => run.Video };
                var output = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "outputs", preset.Id);
                var result = await engine.ConvertAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), source, preset, output));
                RequireCompleted(result);
                Assert(Path.GetExtension(result.OutputPath!).Equals("." + preset.Extension, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase), "Output extension differs from the selected preset.");
                var info = await engine.ProbeAsync(result.OutputPath!);
                var actualCodec = preset.Mode is ConversionMode.Audio or ConversionMode.Extract ? info.AudioCodec : info.VideoCodec;
                Assert(actualCodec == preset.ExpectedCodec, $"Actual codec {actualCodec} differs from {preset.ExpectedCodec}.");
                Assert(ContainerMatches(info.FormatName, preset.Container), $"Actual container {info.FormatName} differs from {preset.Container}.");
                Assert(Directory.GetFiles(output).Length == 1, "Temporary output was left behind.");
                run.Evidence[preset.Id] = new { result.OutputPath, info.FormatName, info.VideoCodec, info.AudioCodec };
            });
        }

        var avi = FindPreset(presets, ConversionMode.Video, "avi");
        var mkv = FindPreset(presets, ConversionMode.Video, "mkv", "h264");
        var hevc = FindPreset(presets, ConversionMode.Video, "mkv", "hevc");
        var mp3 = FindPreset(presets, ConversionMode.Extract, "mp3");

        await run.CaseAsync("output-collision-source-protection", async () =>
        {
            var output = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "outputs", "collision");
            var first = await engine.ConvertAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), run.Video, avi, output));
            RequireCompleted(first);
            var firstHash = Hash(first.OutputPath!);
            var second = await engine.ConvertAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), run.Video, avi, output));
            RequireCompleted(second);
            Assert(first.OutputPath != second.OutputPath, "Existing output was overwritten.");
            Assert(Hash(first.OutputPath!) == firstHash, "Existing successful output changed.");
            run.AssertSourcesUnchanged();
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("same-folder-same-extension-preserves-original", async () =>
        {
            var sourceHash = Hash(run.Video);
            var result = await engine.ConvertAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), run.Video, FindPreset(presets, ConversionMode.Video, "mp4", "h264"), Path.GetDirectoryName(run.Video)!));
            RequireCompleted(result);
            Assert(!result.OutputPath!.Equals(run.Video, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase), "Same-extension conversion overwrote its source.");
            Assert(Hash(run.Video) == sourceHash, "Source changed when output was in the same folder.");
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("cover-art-is-not-mapped-as-video", async () =>
        {
            var input = await engine.ProbeAsync(run.CoverVideo);
            Assert(input.HasVideo && input.VideoCodec == "h264", "Media detection selected attached artwork as the video stream.");
            var result = await engine.ConvertAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), run.CoverVideo, avi, Path.Combine(run.Directory, "outputs", "cover-art")));
            RequireCompleted(result);
            var output = await engine.ProbeAsync(result.OutputPath!);
            Assert(output.DurationSeconds > .8, "Conversion mapped the still cover art instead of the video.");
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("damaged-input-no-final-output", async () =>
        {
            var output = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "outputs", "damaged");
            var result = await engine.ConvertAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), run.Damaged, avi, output));
            Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Failed && result.Error is not null, "Damaged input did not produce a structured failure.");
            Assert(result.OutputPath is null, "Damaged input returned a final output.");
            AssertNoFiles(output);
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("no-audio-extract-import-and-conversion", async () =>
        {
            var inspection = await new ImportService(engine).InspectAsync(run.SilentVideo, ConversionMode.Extract);
            Assert(!inspection.Accepted && inspection.Error?.Code == "AUDIO_MISSING", "No-audio input should be rejected with a friendly reason.");
            var output = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "outputs", "silent-extraction");
            var result = await engine.ConvertAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), run.SilentVideo, mp3, output));
            Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Failed, "No-audio extraction unexpectedly succeeded.");
            AssertNoFiles(output);
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("output-access-denied-no-partial-file", async () =>
        {
            var output = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "outputs", "access-denied");
            Directory.CreateDirectory(output);
            var directory = new DirectoryInfo(output);
            var original = directory.GetAccessControl(AccessControlSections.Access);
            var originalDacl = original.GetSecurityDescriptorBinaryForm();
            var restricted = new DirectorySecurity();
            restricted.SetSecurityDescriptorBinaryForm(originalDacl, AccessControlSections.Access);
            var user = WindowsIdentity.GetCurrent().User ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Current user SID unavailable.");
            restricted.AddAccessRule(new FileSystemAccessRule(user, FileSystemRights.WriteData | FileSystemRights.AppendData | FileSystemRights.CreateDirectories,
                InheritanceFlags.ContainerInherit | InheritanceFlags.ObjectInherit, PropagationFlags.None, AccessControlType.Deny));
            try
            {
                directory.SetAccessControl(restricted);
                var result = await engine.ConvertAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), run.Video, avi, output));
                Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Failed && result.Error is not null, "Unwritable output did not produce a structured failure.");
                AssertNoFiles(output);
            }
            finally
            {
                // Mark only Access modified on a fresh descriptor so restoration actually
                // persists the original DACL, without requesting SACL/owner privileges.
                var restored = new DirectorySecurity();
                restored.SetSecurityDescriptorBinaryForm(originalDacl, AccessControlSections.Access);
                directory.SetAccessControl(restored);
            }
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("engine-cancellation-cleans-owned-temporary-file", async () =>
        {
            var output = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "outputs", "cancel");
            Directory.CreateDirectory(output);
            var unrelated = Path.Combine(output, ".cat-conversion-other-task.mkv");
            File.WriteAllText(unrelated, "This file belongs to another task and must survive cleanup.");
            var unrelatedHash = Hash(unrelated);
            var before = ActiveFfmpegIds();
            using var cancellation = new CancellationTokenSource();
            var conversion = engine.ConvertAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), run.LongVideo, hevc, output), null, cancellation.Token);
            await WaitForAsync(() => ActiveFfmpegIds().Except(before).Any(), TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10), "FFmpeg process did not start.");
            await Task.Delay(150);
            cancellation.Cancel();
            var result = await conversion.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(15));
            Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Cancelled, "Active conversion was not cancelled.");
            Assert(File.Exists(unrelated) && Hash(unrelated) == unrelatedHash && Directory.GetFiles(output).Length == 1,
                "Cancellation removed another task's file or left its own temporary output.");
            await WaitForAsync(() => !ActiveFfmpegIds().Except(before).Any(), TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5), "FFmpeg child process remained alive.");
            run.AssertSourcesUnchanged();
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("committed-output-survives-presentation-exception", async () =>
        {
            var output = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "outputs", "presentation-exception");
            var throwingProgress = new ThrowingProgress();
            var observerErrors = 0;
            var batch = await new SerialConversionQueue(engine).RunAsync([new(Guid.NewGuid(), run.Video, mkv, output)],
                onCompleted: (_, _) => throw new InvalidOperationException("Simulated view update failure after output commit."),
                progressFactory: _ => throwingProgress, onObserverError: _ => observerErrors++);
            var result = batch.Results.Single().Result;
            RequireCompleted(result);
            Assert(throwingProgress.CompletedReportSeen, "A failing completion observer was not exercised after the output commit.");
            Assert(observerErrors == 1 && batch.CompletedCount == 1, "A view observer failure changed the successful task result.");
            var digest = Hash(result.OutputPath!);
            GC.Collect();
            Assert(File.Exists(result.OutputPath) && Hash(result.OutputPath!) == digest, "Committed output was removed after a presentation failure.");
            run.AssertSourcesUnchanged();
        });

        await run.CaseAsync("serial-queue-continues-after-one-file-fails", async () =>
        {
            var output = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "outputs", "mixed-batch");
            var first = new ConversionRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), run.Video, avi, output);
            var failed = new ConversionRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), run.Damaged, avi, output);
            var last = new ConversionRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), run.Video, mkv, output);
            var started = new List<Guid>();
            var batch = await new SerialConversionQueue(engine).RunAsync([first, failed, last], onStarted: request => started.Add(request.Id));
            Assert(started.SequenceEqual(new[] { first.Id, failed.Id, last.Id }), "Queue skipped or reordered a task after a failure.");
            Assert(batch.CompletedCount == 2 && batch.FailedCount == 1 && batch.AssistantState == AssistantState.ERROR, "Mixed batch summary is incorrect.");
            Assert(batch.Results.Where(value => value.Result.Status == JobStatus.Completed).All(value => File.Exists(value.Result.OutputPath)), "Mixed batch lost a successful result.");
        });

        if (run.NoGui)
        {
            run.RecordSkipped("wpf-window-lifecycle", "--no-gui explicitly skips real WPF acceptance.");
            run.RecordSkipped("wpf-import-close-cancels-probe", "--no-gui explicitly skips real WPF acceptance.");
            run.RecordSkipped("wpf-empty-drop-click-routing", "--no-gui explicitly skips real WPF acceptance.");
            run.RecordSkipped("wpf-motion-state-and-clock-lifecycle", "--no-gui explicitly skips real WPF acceptance.");
        }
        else
        {
            await run.CaseAsync("wpf-window-lifecycle", () => RunWindowLifecycleAsync(run, engine, presets, avi, mkv, hevc));
            await run.CaseAsync("wpf-import-close-cancels-probe", () => RunImportCloseAsync(run, presets));
            await run.CaseAsync("wpf-empty-drop-click-routing", () => RunEmptyDropClickRoutingAsync(run, presets));
            await run.CaseAsync("wpf-motion-state-and-clock-lifecycle", () => MotionAcceptance.RunControlAsync(run));
        }
        await CompressionAcceptance.RunAsync(run, engine, presets);
        await GifAcceptance.RunAsync(run, engine, presets);
        await ArchiveAcceptance.RunAsync(run);
        await RecoveryAcceptance.RunAsync(run);
        await ImageObfuscationAcceptance.RunAsync(run);
        await run.CaseAsync("all-source-hashes-unchanged", () => { run.AssertSourcesUnchanged(); return Task.CompletedTask; });
    }

    private static async Task RunWindowLifecycleAsync(AcceptanceRun run, IConversionEngine engine, IReadOnlyList<FormatPreset> presets,
        FormatPreset avi, FormatPreset mkv, FormatPreset hevc)
    {
        var model = new MainViewModel(engine, presets, new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "ui-settings")), new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "ui-logs")))
        { OutputDirectory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "outputs", "gui") };
        var stopAndExit = false;
        var window = new MainWindow(model, () => stopAndExit) { ShowActivated = false };
        run.StopActiveWindow = async () =>
        {
            model.Cancel(); model.CancelImport();
            await Task.WhenAll(model.ActiveRun, model.ActiveImport).WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(15));
        };
        window.Show();
        try
        {
            await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle);
            MotionAcceptance.AssertVisibleCatsAnimating(window);
            CaptureWindow(run, window, "empty-normal");
            model.SetDragging(true);
            Assert(model.AssistantState == AssistantState.DRAGGING && model.IsDragging, "Drag state was not activated.");
            CaptureWindow(run, window, "empty-dragging");
            model.SetDragging(false);
            Assert(model.AssistantState == AssistantState.IDLE, "Leaving the drag region did not restore idle.");
            var originalWidth = window.Width;
            var originalHeight = window.Height;
            window.Width = 850;
            await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle);
            CaptureWindow(run, window, "empty-narrow");
            foreach (var (width, height, name) in new[] { (620d, 480d, "empty-620x480"), (960d, 540d, "empty-960x540") })
            {
                window.Width = width; window.Height = height;
                await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle);
                AssertControlInside(window, "StartButton");
                AssertControlInside(window, "AddFilesSide");
                CaptureWindow(run, window, name);
            }
            window.Width = originalWidth;
            window.Height = originalHeight;
            model.SetMode(ConversionMode.Video);
            model.SelectedPreset = avi;
            await model.AddFilesAsync([run.Video]);
            await model.RunQueueAsync();
            Assert(window.IsVisible && !model.IsProcessing, "Window closed after successful conversion.");
            MotionAcceptance.AssertVisibleCatsAnimating(window);
            Assert(model.Queue.Single().Status == JobStatus.Completed && model.AssistantState == AssistantState.SUCCESS, "Success state was not displayed.");
            var firstCue = model.CompletionCue;
            Assert(firstCue == 1, "The first real successful batch did not emit exactly one completion cue.");
            model.SetDragging(true); model.SetDragging(false);
            Assert(model.AssistantState == AssistantState.SUCCESS, "Dragging restored an incorrect prior state.");
            Assert(model.CompletionCue == firstCue, "Restoring success after drag emitted a duplicate completion cue.");
            CaptureWindow(run, window, "success-compact");
            var firstOutput = model.Queue.Single().OutputPath ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("First GUI output absent.");
            Assert((await engine.ProbeAsync(firstOutput)).FormatName.Contains("avi", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase), "GUI selected format was not applied.");

            var stayOpen = Stopwatch.StartNew();
            var required = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(run.Quick ? 2 : 60);
            while (stayOpen.Elapsed < required)
            {
                await Task.Delay(500);
                Assert(window.IsVisible && !model.IsProcessing, "Window closed while idle after completion.");
            }
            run.Evidence["windowStayedVisibleSeconds"] = stayOpen.Elapsed.TotalSeconds;
            Console.WriteLine($"Window remained visible for {stayOpen.Elapsed.TotalSeconds:F1}s after conversion.");

            var secondSource = run.CloneVideo("第二次 转换.mp4");
            model.SelectedPreset = mkv;
            await model.AddFilesAsync([secondSource]);
            await model.RunQueueAsync();
            Assert(window.IsVisible && model.Queue.Count(value => value.Status == JobStatus.Completed) == 2, "Second conversion did not succeed in the same window.");
            Assert(model.CompletionCue == firstCue + 1, "The second successful batch did not emit a new unique cue.");
            var secondOutput = model.Queue.Last().OutputPath ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Second GUI output absent.");
            Assert((await engine.ProbeAsync(secondOutput)).FormatName.Contains("matroska", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase), "Second GUI conversion is not a real MKV.");

            var damagedAfterImport = run.CloneVideo("导入后 损坏.mp4", trackHash: false);
            await model.AddFilesAsync([damagedAfterImport]);
            File.WriteAllText(damagedAfterImport, "not a video any more");
            await model.RunQueueAsync();
            Assert(window.IsVisible && model.Queue.Last().Status == JobStatus.Failed && model.Queue.Last().Error is not null,
                "Failed conversion did not keep the window open with a friendly error.");
            Assert(model.AssistantState == AssistantState.ERROR, "Failure state was not displayed.");
            Assert(model.CompletionCue == firstCue + 1, "Failed real conversion emitted a success cue.");
            CaptureWindow(run, window, "error-compact");

            model.SelectedPreset = hevc;
            var cancelSource = run.CloneVideo("取消中 视频.mp4", longVideo: true);
            var waitingSource = run.CloneVideo("下一个 待处理.mp4", longVideo: true);
            await model.AddFilesAsync([cancelSource, waitingSource]);
            var activeRun = model.RunQueueAsync();
            await WaitForAsync(() => model.IsProcessing, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5), "GUI queue did not start.");
            await Task.Delay(200);
            CaptureWindow(run, window, "processing-compact");
            window.Close(); // Default/continue choice: the active task must keep running.
            Assert(window.IsVisible && model.IsProcessing, "Continue choice did not preserve the active window.");
            model.Cancel();
            await activeRun.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(15));
            Assert(window.IsVisible && !model.IsProcessing, "Cancellation closed the window or left it busy.");
            Assert(model.Queue.Single(value => value.SourcePath == cancelSource).Status == JobStatus.Cancelled, "Active row was not cancelled.");
            Assert(model.Queue.Single(value => value.SourcePath == waitingSource).Status == JobStatus.Pending, "Waiting task was consumed after stopping.");
            Assert(model.AssistantState == AssistantState.IDLE, "User cancellation should return the cat to idle.");
            Assert(model.CompletionCue == firstCue + 1, "Cancelled real conversion emitted a success cue.");

            var exitRun = model.RunQueueAsync();
            await WaitForAsync(() => model.IsProcessing, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5), "Remaining pending task did not restart.");
            await Task.Delay(200);
            stopAndExit = true;
            window.Close();
            await exitRun.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(15));
            await WaitForAsync(() => !window.IsVisible, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5), "Stop and exit did not close after child-process cleanup.");
            Assert(File.Exists(firstOutput) && File.Exists(secondOutput), "Closing removed previously successful output files.");
            run.AssertSourcesUnchanged();
            run.Evidence["sameWindowSecondConversion"] = true;
            run.Evidence["failureCancellationCloseLifecycle"] = true;
        }
        finally
        {
            if (model.IsProcessing) model.Cancel();
            model.CancelImport();
            try { await Task.WhenAll(model.ActiveRun, model.ActiveImport).WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(15)); }
            catch (Exception exception) { run.RecordFailure("wpf-lifecycle-cleanup", exception); }
            if (window.IsVisible) { stopAndExit = true; window.Close(); }
            run.StopActiveWindow = null;
        }
    }

    private static async Task RunImportCloseAsync(AcceptanceRun run, IReadOnlyList<FormatPreset> presets)
    {
        var engine = new SlowProbeEngine();
        var model = new MainViewModel(engine, presets, new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "import-close-settings")), new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "import-close-logs")));
        var window = new MainWindow(model) { ShowActivated = false };
        run.StopActiveWindow = async () => { model.CancelImport(); await model.ActiveImport.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5)); };
        window.Show();
        try
        {
            var import = model.AddFilesAsync([run.Video]);
            await WaitForAsync(() => model.IsImporting && engine.ProbeStarted, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5), "Import probe did not start.");
            window.Close();
            await import.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5));
            await WaitForAsync(() => !window.IsVisible, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5), "Importing window did not close after probe cancellation.");
            Assert(engine.ProbeCancellationObserved && !model.IsImporting && model.Queue.Count == 0, "Closing during import left the probe active or added an incomplete task.");
        }
        finally
        {
            model.CancelImport();
            try { await model.ActiveImport.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5)); }
            catch (Exception exception) { run.RecordFailure("wpf-import-cleanup", exception); }
            if (window.IsVisible) window.Close();
            run.StopActiveWindow = null;
        }
    }

    private static async Task RunEmptyDropClickRoutingAsync(AcceptanceRun run, IReadOnlyList<FormatPreset> presets)
    {
        // No mouse injection, click event, file picker, or media fixture is used here.
        var model = new MainViewModel(new SlowProbeEngine(), presets,
            new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "empty-click-settings")), new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "empty-click-logs")));
        var window = new MainWindow(model);
        window.Show();
        try
        {
            window.Activate();
            await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ApplicationIdle);
            var drop = window.FindName("DropArea") as Button ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Empty drop button not found.");
            var scroller = window.FindName("EmptyScrollViewer") as ScrollViewer ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Empty scroll viewer not found.");
            var text = window.FindName("EmptyClickInstruction") as UIElement ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Empty click instruction not found.");
            var cat = window.FindName("EmptyCat") as DependencyObject ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Empty cat not found.");
            var catImage = FindImageDescendant(cat) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Cat bitmap descendant not found.");
            MotionAcceptance.AssertVisibleCatsAnimating(window);
            Assert(!scroller.Focusable && !scroller.IsTabStop, "The inner empty-state scroller can focus and intercept the outer button's mouse-down.");

            var originalFocusable = scroller.Focusable;
            var originalTabStop = scroller.IsTabStop;
            var emptyContent = scroller.Content as UIElement ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Empty drop content not found.");
            var targets = new[] { ("instruction-text", text), ("cat-image-descendant", (UIElement)catImage), ("empty-content-container", emptyContent) };
            var observations = new List<object>();
            try
            {
                foreach (var (name, target) in targets)
                {
                    Assert(drop.Focus() && drop.IsKeyboardFocused, "The active test window could not focus the outer drop button.");
                    Assert(!scroller.Focus() && drop.IsKeyboardFocused, "The fixed scroller stole keyboard focus from the drop button.");
                    var fixedHandled = ObserveMouseDownAtScroller(target, scroller);
                    Assert(!fixedHandled, name + " mouse-down was marked handled by the fixed inner scroller.");
                    Assert(drop.IsKeyboardFocused, "Mouse-down did not retain focus on the outer drop button.");

                    // Reproduce the old configuration in this test instance only. Class handlers
                    // run before this handledEventsToo observer and before the parent button.
                    scroller.Focusable = true;
                    scroller.IsTabStop = true;
                    Assert(drop.Focus() && drop.IsKeyboardFocused, "Negative control could not reset focus.");
                    var oldHandled = ObserveMouseDownAtScroller(target, scroller);
                    Assert(oldHandled && scroller.IsKeyboardFocused, "Focusable=true did not reproduce the old mouse-down interception.");
                    observations.Add(new { target = name, sourceType = target.GetType().FullName, fixedHandledAtScroller = fixedHandled, oldHandledAtScroller = oldHandled });
                    scroller.Focusable = originalFocusable;
                    scroller.IsTabStop = originalTabStop;
                }
            }
            finally
            {
                scroller.Focusable = originalFocusable;
                scroller.IsTabStop = originalTabStop;
                Mouse.Capture(null);
            }
            run.Evidence["emptyDropClickRouting"] = new
            {
                windowWasActive = window.IsActive, observations,
                method = "Synthetic Mouse.MouseDownEvent raised through the actual WPF visual route; observed after ScrollViewer class handling, before the parent Button.",
                negativeControl = "Focusable=true and IsTabStop=true reproduce the old interception in the same window, then are restored.",
                physicalMouseInput = false, filePickerOpened = false
            };
        }
        finally { Mouse.Capture(null); window.Close(); }
    }

    private static bool ObserveMouseDownAtScroller(UIElement source, ScrollViewer scroller)
    {
        bool? handledAtScroller = null;
        MouseButtonEventHandler observer = (_, args) =>
        {
            if (args.ChangedButton == MouseButton.Left) handledAtScroller = args.Handled;
        };
        scroller.AddHandler(Mouse.MouseDownEvent, observer, handledEventsToo: true);
        try
        {
            source.RaiseEvent(new MouseButtonEventArgs(Mouse.PrimaryDevice, Environment.TickCount, MouseButton.Left)
            { RoutedEvent = Mouse.MouseDownEvent });
        }
        finally { scroller.RemoveHandler(Mouse.MouseDownEvent, observer); Mouse.Capture(null); }
        Assert(handledAtScroller.HasValue, "The descendant mouse-down did not reach the actual inner scroller.");
        return handledAtScroller!.Value;
    }

    private static Image? FindImageDescendant(DependencyObject parent)
    {
        for (var index = 0; index < VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(parent); index++)
        {
            var child = VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(parent, index);
            if (child is Image { Source: not null } image) return image;
            var nested = FindImageDescendant(child);
            if (nested is not null) return nested;
        }
        return null;
    }

    private static async Task CreateFixturesAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-f", "lavfi", "-i", "testsrc2=size=96x64:rate=12", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "sine=frequency=440:sample_rate=44100",
            "-t", "1", "-c:v", "libopenh264", "-b:v", "200k", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", "-c:a", "aac", run.Video]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-f", "lavfi", "-i", "color=c=blue:size=96x64:rate=12", "-t", "1", "-c:v", "libopenh264", "-b:v", "100k", run.SilentVideo]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-f", "lavfi", "-i", "sine=frequency=440:sample_rate=44100", "-t", "1", "-c:a", "pcm_s16le", run.Audio]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-f", "lavfi", "-i", "color=c=orange:size=96x64", "-frames:v", "1", "-threads", "1", run.Image]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-i", run.Video, "-i", run.Image, "-map", "1:v:0", "-map", "0:v:0", "-map", "0:a:0",
            "-c", "copy", "-disposition:v:0", "attached_pic", run.CoverVideo]);
        await RunFfmpegAsync(run, ["-f", "lavfi", "-i", "testsrc2=size=640x360:rate=30", "-t", "20", "-c:v", "libopenh264", "-b:v", "1M", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", run.LongVideo]);
        File.WriteAllText(run.Damaged, "invalid media fixture");
        foreach (var path in new[] { run.Video, run.SilentVideo, run.Audio, run.Image, run.CoverVideo, run.LongVideo, run.Damaged }) run.TrackSource(path);
        run.FixturesReady = true;
    }

    private static async Task RunFfmpegAsync(AcceptanceRun run, string[] arguments)
    {
        var start = new ProcessStartInfo(Path.Combine(run.EngineDirectory, "ffmpeg.exe"))
        { UseShellExecute = false, CreateNoWindow = true, RedirectStandardError = true, RedirectStandardOutput = true };
        foreach (var value in new[] { "-hide_banner", "-nostdin", "-y", "-loglevel", "error" }.Concat(arguments)) start.ArgumentList.Add(value);
        using var process = Process.Start(start) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Fixture FFmpeg failed to start.");
        var stderr = process.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync();
        var stdout = process.StandardOutput.ReadToEndAsync();
        try { await process.WaitForExitAsync().WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(90)); }
        catch { if (!process.HasExited) process.Kill(true); await process.WaitForExitAsync(); throw; }
        await stdout;
        Assert(process.ExitCode == 0, "Fixture generation failed: " + await stderr);
    }

    internal static void CaptureWindow(AcceptanceRun run, MainWindow window, string name)
    {
        window.UpdateLayout();
        var content = (FrameworkElement)window.Content;
        var dpi = VisualTreeHelper.GetDpi(content);
        var bitmap = new RenderTargetBitmap((int)Math.Ceiling(content.ActualWidth * dpi.DpiScaleX),
            (int)Math.Ceiling(content.ActualHeight * dpi.DpiScaleY), dpi.PixelsPerInchX, dpi.PixelsPerInchY, PixelFormats.Pbgra32);
        var background = new DrawingVisual();
        using (var drawing = background.RenderOpen()) drawing.DrawRectangle(window.Background ?? new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(245, 246, 250)), null,
            new Rect(0, 0, content.ActualWidth, content.ActualHeight));
        bitmap.Render(background);
        bitmap.Render(content);
        var encoder = new PngBitmapEncoder();
        encoder.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(bitmap));
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "screenshots");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var path = Path.Combine(directory, name + ".png");
        using (var output = File.Create(path)) encoder.Save(output);
        run.Evidence[name] = new { path, window.ActualWidth, window.ActualHeight, dpi.PixelsPerInchX, dpi.PixelsPerInchY,
            note = "WPF visual rendering at observed host DPI; not a physical scaling or screenshot of other applications." };
    }

    internal static void AssertControlInside(MainWindow window, string name)
    {
        window.UpdateLayout();
        var content = (FrameworkElement)window.Content;
        var element = window.FindName(name) as FrameworkElement ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing control " + name);
        Assert(element.IsVisible && element.ActualWidth > 0 && element.ActualHeight > 0, name + " is not visible.");
        var bounds = element.TransformToAncestor(content).TransformBounds(new Rect(0, 0, element.ActualWidth, element.ActualHeight));
        Assert(bounds.Left >= -1 && bounds.Top >= -1 && bounds.Right <= content.ActualWidth + 1 && bounds.Bottom <= content.ActualHeight + 1,
            $"{name} extends beyond the visible content at {window.ActualWidth}x{window.ActualHeight}: {bounds}.");
    }

    private static FormatPreset FindPreset(IReadOnlyList<FormatPreset> presets, ConversionMode mode, string extension, string? codec = null)
        => presets.First(value => value.Mode == mode && value.Extension == extension && (codec is null || value.ExpectedCodec == codec));
    private static bool ContainerMatches(string actual, string expected)
        => actual.Split(',').Contains(expected, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            || expected is "mp4" or "m4a" && actual.Contains("mov", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            || expected == "webp" && actual.Contains("webp", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            || expected == "png" && actual.Contains("png", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            || expected is "image" or "image2" && (actual.Contains("pipe", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) || actual == "image2");
    private static void RequireCompleted(ConversionResult result)
        => Assert(result.Status == JobStatus.Completed && result.OutputPath is not null && File.Exists(result.OutputPath),
            $"Conversion failed: {result.Status}, {result.Error?.Code}, {result.Error?.Message}, {result.Error?.TechnicalDetails}");
    private static void AssertNoFiles(string directory)
        => Assert(!Directory.Exists(directory) || Directory.GetFiles(directory, "*", SearchOption.AllDirectories).Length == 0, "Failure/cancellation left an output or temporary file.");
    private static HashSet<int> ActiveFfmpegIds()
    {
        var ids = new HashSet<int>();
        foreach (var process in Process.GetProcessesByName("ffmpeg")) { using (process) ids.Add(process.Id); }
        return ids;
    }
    private static async Task WaitForAsync(Func<bool> condition, TimeSpan timeout, string message)
    {
        var watch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        while (!condition()) { if (watch.Elapsed > timeout) throw new InvalidOperationException(message); await Task.Delay(50); }
    }
    internal static string Hash(string path) => Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(File.ReadAllBytes(path)));
    internal static void Assert(bool condition, string message) { if (!condition) throw new InvalidOperationException(message); }

    private sealed class ThrowingProgress : IProgress<ConversionProgress>
    {
        public bool CompletedReportSeen { get; private set; }
        public void Report(ConversionProgress value)
        {
            if (value.Percent == 100) CompletedReportSeen = true;
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Simulated presentation observer exception.");
        }
    }

    private sealed class SlowProbeEngine : IConversionEngine
    {
        public bool ProbeStarted { get; private set; }
        public bool ProbeCancellationObserved { get; private set; }
        public async Task<MediaInfo> ProbeAsync(string path, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            ProbeStarted = true;
            try { await Task.Delay(Timeout.Infinite, cancellationToken); }
            catch (OperationCanceledException) { ProbeCancellationObserved = true; throw; }
            return new(true, false, 1, "mp4", "h264", null);
        }
        public Task<ConversionResult> ConvertAsync(ConversionRequest request, IProgress<ConversionProgress>? progress = null, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
            => throw new InvalidOperationException("Import-only test must not start a conversion.");
    }
}

internal sealed class AcceptanceRun
{
    private readonly Dictionary<string, string> sourceHashes = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    private readonly List<CaseResult> cases = [];
    private readonly DateTimeOffset started = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;
    public string Root { get; }
    public string Directory { get; }
    public string EngineDirectory { get; }
    public string ReportPath => Path.Combine(Directory, "report.json");
    public bool Quick { get; }
    public bool NoGui { get; }
    public bool CompressionOnly { get; }
    public bool CompressionAcceptancePassed { get; set; }
    public bool GifOnly { get; }
    public bool GifAcceptancePassed { get; set; }
    public bool ArchiveOnly { get; }
    public bool ArchiveAcceptancePassed { get; set; }
    public bool RecoveryOnly { get; }
    public bool RecoveryAcceptancePassed { get; set; }
    public bool ImageObfuscationOnly { get; }
    public bool ImageObfuscationAcceptancePassed { get; set; }
    public bool V09AcceptancePassed { get; set; }
    public string? SelectedCase { get; }
    public bool FixturesReady { get; set; }
    public int Failures => cases.Count(value => value.Status == "failed");
    private bool finished;
    public Func<Task>? StopActiveWindow { get; set; }
    public Dictionary<string, object> Evidence { get; } = new();
    private string Inputs => Path.Combine(Directory, "素材 中文 空格");
    public string Video => Path.Combine(Inputs, "猫咪 视频.mp4");
    public string CoverVideo => Path.Combine(Inputs, "带封面 视频.mp4");
    public string SilentVideo => Path.Combine(Inputs, "无声 视频.mp4");
    public string LongVideo => Path.Combine(Inputs, "长视频 取消.mp4");
    public string Audio => Path.Combine(Inputs, "猫咪 音频.wav");
    public string Image => Path.Combine(Inputs, "猫咪 图片.png");
    public string Damaged => Path.Combine(Inputs, "损坏 文件.mp4");

    public AcceptanceRun(string[] args)
    {
        Quick = args.Contains("--quick");
        NoGui = args.Contains("--no-gui");
        CompressionOnly = args.Contains("--compression-only");
        GifOnly = args.Contains("--gif-only");
        ArchiveOnly = args.Contains("--archive-only");
        RecoveryOnly = args.Contains("--recovery-only");
        ImageObfuscationOnly = args.Contains("--image-obfuscation-only");
        if (ImageObfuscationOnly && (RecoveryOnly || ArchiveOnly || CompressionOnly || GifOnly || Option(args, "--case") is not null)) throw new ArgumentException("--image-obfuscation-only cannot be combined with other focused modes.");
        if (RecoveryOnly && (ArchiveOnly || CompressionOnly || GifOnly || Option(args, "--case") is not null)) throw new ArgumentException("--recovery-only cannot be combined with other focused modes.");
        if (ArchiveOnly && (CompressionOnly || GifOnly || Option(args, "--case") is not null)) throw new ArgumentException("--archive-only cannot be combined with other focused modes.");
        SelectedCase = Option(args, "--case");
        if ((CompressionOnly || GifOnly) && SelectedCase is not null) throw new ArgumentException("--compression-only / --gif-only cannot be combined with --case.");
        if (CompressionOnly && GifOnly) throw new ArgumentException("--compression-only cannot be combined with --gif-only.");
        if (SelectedCase is not null && SelectedCase is not ("empty-drop-click" or "motion" or "motion-performance" or "pet" or "pet-performance" or "v09" or "v10" or "v101" or "v09-format" or "recovery-gui" or "result-actions" or "pet-menu" or "pet-animation" or "pet-interaction" or "home-companion" or "home-companion-performance")) throw new ArgumentException("Unknown --case. Supported: empty-drop-click, motion, motion-performance, pet, pet-performance, v09, v10, v101, v09-format, recovery-gui, result-actions, pet-menu, pet-animation, pet-interaction, home-companion, home-companion-performance.");
        if (SelectedCase is not null && SelectedCase is not ("v10" or "v101") && NoGui) throw new ArgumentException("Focused WPF cases require a desktop session; remove --no-gui.");
        Root = Path.GetFullPath(Option(args, "--root") ?? FindRoot());
        EngineDirectory = Path.GetFullPath(Option(args, "--engine-directory") ?? Path.Combine(Root, "tools", "ffmpeg", "bin"));
        if (!ArchiveOnly && !RecoveryOnly && (SelectedCase is null or "motion-performance" or "pet-performance" or "home-companion-performance") && !File.Exists(Path.Combine(EngineDirectory, "ffmpeg.exe"))) throw new FileNotFoundException("Bundled FFmpeg missing. Use --root <workspace>.");
        Directory = Path.GetFullPath(Option(args, "--artifacts") ?? Path.Combine(Root, "artifacts", "validation", DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.ToString("yyyyMMdd-HHmmss") + "-" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N")[..6]));
        System.IO.Directory.CreateDirectory(SelectedCase is null or "motion-performance" or "pet-performance" or "home-companion-performance" ? Inputs : Directory);
    }
    public async Task CaseAsync(string name, Func<Task> body)
    {
        var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        Console.WriteLine("RUN " + name);
        try { await body(); cases.Add(new(name, "passed", clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, null)); Console.WriteLine("PASS " + name); }
        catch (Exception exception) { cases.Add(new(name, "failed", clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, exception.ToString())); Console.Error.WriteLine("FAIL " + name + ": " + exception.Message); }
        SaveReport();
    }
    public void RecordFailure(string name, Exception exception) => cases.Add(new(name, "failed", 0, exception.ToString()));
    public void Complete() => finished = true;
    public void RecordSkipped(string name, string reason) => cases.Add(new(name, "skipped", 0, reason));
    public void TrackSource(string path) => sourceHashes[path] = Program.Hash(path);
    public void AssertSourcesUnchanged()
    {
        foreach (var (path, hash) in sourceHashes) Program.Assert(File.Exists(path) && Program.Hash(path) == hash, "Source file changed: " + path);
    }
    public string CloneVideo(string name, bool longVideo = false, bool trackHash = true)
    {
        var target = Path.Combine(Inputs, name);
        File.Copy(longVideo ? LongVideo : Video, target, true);
        if (trackHash) TrackSource(target);
        return target;
    }
    public void SaveReport() => File.WriteAllText(ReportPath, JsonSerializer.Serialize(new
    {
        startedUtc = started, updatedUtc = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow, operatingSystem = Environment.OSVersion.VersionString,
        architecture = System.Runtime.InteropServices.RuntimeInformation.OSArchitecture.ToString(),
        framework = System.Runtime.InteropServices.RuntimeInformation.FrameworkDescription,
        runtimeDirectory = System.Runtime.InteropServices.RuntimeEnvironment.GetRuntimeDirectory(),
        engineDirectory = EngineDirectory, Quick, guiIncluded = !NoGui, selectedCase = SelectedCase, CompressionOnly, GifOnly, ArchiveOnly, RecoveryOnly, ImageObfuscationOnly,
        fullLifecycleRequirementSeconds = 60, finished, success = finished && Failures == 0,
        compressionAcceptancePassed = CompressionAcceptancePassed,
        gifAcceptancePassed = GifAcceptancePassed,
        archiveAcceptancePassed = ArchiveAcceptancePassed,
        recoveryAcceptancePassed = RecoveryAcceptancePassed,
        imageObfuscationAcceptancePassed = ImageObfuscationAcceptancePassed,
        v09AcceptancePassed = V09AcceptancePassed,
        fullAutomatedAcceptancePassed = finished && Failures == 0 && SelectedCase is null && !CompressionOnly && !GifOnly && !ArchiveOnly && !RecoveryOnly && !ImageObfuscationOnly && !Quick && !NoGui && CompressionAcceptancePassed && GifAcceptancePassed && ArchiveAcceptancePassed && RecoveryAcceptancePassed && ImageObfuscationAcceptancePassed && V09AcceptancePassed && cases.Any(value => value.Name == "wpf-window-lifecycle" && value.Status == "passed"),
        results = cases, evidence = Evidence,
        sourceSha256 = sourceHashes, limitations = new[] { "Results cover the operating system recorded above; other Windows versions require their own run.", "Manual visual, drag/drop, keyboard and display scaling review is separate from this runner.", "--quick uses a two-second GUI dwell and does not satisfy the 60-second release gate.", "CompositionTarget.Rendering measurements are dispatcher/render scheduling intervals, not presented GPU frame times or a guarantee of 60 FPS." }
    }, new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true }));
    private static string? Option(string[] args, string key)
    {
        var index = Array.IndexOf(args, key);
        return index < 0 ? null : index + 1 < args.Length ? args[index + 1] : throw new ArgumentException("Missing value for " + key);
    }
    private static string FindRoot()
    {
        foreach (var start in new[] { Environment.CurrentDirectory, AppContext.BaseDirectory })
            for (var candidate = new DirectoryInfo(start); candidate is not null; candidate = candidate.Parent)
                if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(candidate.FullName, "shared", "presets.json")) && System.IO.Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(candidate.FullName, "tools", "ffmpeg"))) return candidate.FullName;
        throw new DirectoryNotFoundException("Cannot locate workspace. Use --root <workspace>.");
    }
    private sealed record CaseResult(string Name, string Status, double DurationSeconds, string? Detail);
}



--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/RecoveryAcceptance.cs ---
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.IO.Compression;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

/// <summary>Numeric-space, original real archives, process supervision, and STA UI acceptance.</summary>
internal static class RecoveryAcceptance
{
    public static async Task RunAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        int before = run.Failures;
        string root = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "恢复素材 中文 空格");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(root);
        var log = new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "recovery-logs"));
        string dll = Path.Combine(run.Root, "tools", "7zip", "bin", "7z.dll");
        string worker = WorkerPath(run.Root);
        var engine = new PasswordRecoveryEngine(dll, worker, log);
        var archive = new ArchiveEngine(dll, worker);
        var fixtures = new List<Fixture>();
        await run.CaseAsync("recovery-engine-and-original-fixture-provenance", () =>
        {
            Program.Assert(engine.IsReady, "Password recovery worker/DLL is incomplete.");
            string source = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "fixtures", "recovery");
            if (!File.Exists(Path.Combine(source, "provenance.json"))) source = Path.Combine(run.Root, "tests", "fixtures", "recovery");
            using var provenance = JsonDocument.Parse(File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(source, "provenance.json")));
            Program.Assert(provenance.RootElement.GetProperty("publicPasswordsOnly").GetBoolean()
                && !provenance.RootElement.GetProperty("rarToolRedistributed").GetBoolean(), "Recovery fixture provenance is incomplete.");
            foreach (var record in provenance.RootElement.GetProperty("archives").EnumerateArray())
            {
                var files = record.GetProperty("files").EnumerateArray().Select(f => f.GetProperty("name").GetString()!).ToArray();
                foreach (var file in record.GetProperty("files").EnumerateArray())
                {
                    string name = file.GetProperty("name").GetString()!;
                    string input = Path.Combine(source, name), target = Path.Combine(root, name);
                    Program.Assert(Program.Hash(input).Equals(file.GetProperty("sha256").GetString(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase), "Original recovery fixture hash differs: " + name);
                    File.Copy(input, target); run.TrackSource(target);
                }
                fixtures.Add(new(record.GetProperty("name").GetString()!, Path.Combine(root, files[0]),
                    record.GetProperty("publicTestPassword").GetString()!, files.Select(f => Path.Combine(root, f)).ToArray()));
            }
            Program.Assert(fixtures.Count == 25, "Original recovery matrix is incomplete.");
            run.Evidence["recovery-fixtures"] = new { count = fixtures.Count, originalSource = true,
                provenanceSha256 = Program.Hash(Path.Combine(source, "provenance.json")), noExternalPasswordCorpus = true };
            return Task.CompletedTask;
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("recovery-numeric-spaces-and-leading-zero", () =>
        {
            foreach (int digits in new[] { 4, 6 })
            {
                long count = 0; string? last = null;
                foreach (string value in RecoveryCandidates.EnumerateDigits(digits))
                {
                    Program.Assert(value.Length == digits && value.All(char.IsAsciiDigit), "Numeric enumerator emitted an invalid candidate.");
                    Program.Assert(int.Parse(value, System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) == count, "Numeric enumerator skipped/repeated a combination.");
                    if (count == 0) Program.Assert(value == new string('0', digits), "Leading zeros were lost.");
                    last = value; count++;
                }
                Program.Assert(count == RecoveryCandidates.SpaceSize(digits) && last == new string('9', digits), "Numeric space missed its last combination.");
            }
            Program.Assert(new RecoveryProgress(3, 10).Remaining == 7 && new RecoveryProgress(3, 10).Percent == 30, "Progress arithmetic is wrong.");
            ExpectArgument(() => RecoveryCandidates.SpaceSize(5));
            ExpectArgument(() => RecoveryCandidates.Total(new((RecoveryMode)99)));
            ExpectArgument(() => RecoveryCandidates.CpuPercent((RecoveryIntensity)99));
            run.Evidence["recovery-numeric-space"] = new { fourDigits = 10000, sixDigits = 1000000,
                checkedEveryCombination = true, method = "Enumerated both complete candidate spaces; slow KDF archives only exercise early hits/cancellation, not a million native decodes." };
            return Task.CompletedTask;
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("recovery-common-stage-order-variants-dedup-and-limits", () =>
        {
            using var provenance = JsonDocument.Parse(File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(run.Root, "shared", "recovery-patterns-provenance.json")));
            Program.Assert(provenance.RootElement.GetProperty("ruleVersion").GetString() == "numeric-patterns-v1"
                && provenance.RootElement.GetProperty("externalDatasets").GetArrayLength() == 0, "Numeric patterns do not have their original rule provenance.");
            var stages = RecoveryCandidates.CommonStages(["7304", "73", "001234", "7304", "0000"], 4);
            Program.Assert(stages[0].SequenceEqual(new[] { "7304", "0000" }), "Exact values do not precede transformations.");
            Program.Assert(stages[1].SequenceEqual(new[] { "0073", "7300", "7373", "0012", "1234" }), "Transformation rules/order changed or combined separate inputs.");
            var all = stages.SelectMany(s => s).ToArray();
            Program.Assert(all.Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Count() == all.Length && all.All(s => s.Length == 4 && s.All(char.IsAsciiDigit)), "Cross-stage dedup/length constraint failed.");
            Program.Assert(RecoveryCandidates.Total(new(), ["7304", "73", "001234", "7304", "0000"]) == all.Length, "Common candidate preview differs from actual staged list.");
            foreach (int digits in new[] { 4, 6 })
            {
                var builtIn = RecoveryCandidates.BuiltIn(digits);
                Program.Assert(builtIn.Count is > 0 and <= 40 && builtIn.Distinct().Count() == builtIn.Count, "Original dictionary is oversized/duplicated.");
                Program.Assert(RecoveryCandidates.CommonStages(null, digits)[2].SequenceEqual(builtIn), "Built-in candidates were not confined to stage three.");
                var record = provenance.RootElement.GetProperty("lengths").GetProperty(digits.ToString());
                string checksum = Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(string.Join('\n', builtIn) + "\n")));
                Program.Assert(record.GetProperty("count").GetInt32() == builtIn.Count
                    && record.GetProperty("patterns").EnumerateArray().Select(p => p.GetString()).SequenceEqual(builtIn)
                    && record.GetProperty("sha256Utf8Lines").GetString() == checksum,
                    "Published original-pattern provenance differs from the running recovery generator.");
            }
            Program.Assert(RecoveryCandidates.NormalizeNumbers([" 0012 ", "0012"]).SequenceEqual(new[] { "0012" }), "Common numbers lost zeros/dedup.");
            Program.Assert(RecoveryCandidates.NormalizeNumbers(Enumerable.Range(0, 100).Select(i => i.ToString())).Count == 100, "Exactly 100 common entries were rejected.");
            foreach (var invalid in new[] { "", "１２３４", "12a", "-1", "1\n2", new string('1', 21) })
                ExpectArgument(() => RecoveryCandidates.NormalizeNumbers([invalid]));
            ExpectArgument(() => RecoveryCandidates.NormalizeNumbers(Enumerable.Range(0, 101).Select(i => i.ToString())));
            return Task.CompletedTask;
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("recovery-serial-queue-failure-observers-and-cancellation", () => RunQueueAsync());

        foreach (var fixture in fixtures.Where(f => f.Password is "0000" or "000000"))
        foreach (var mode in Enum.GetValues<RecoveryMode>())
        {
            string name = "recovery-real-" + mode + "-" + fixture.Name;
            await run.CaseAsync(name, async () =>
            {
                var progress = new ProgressRecorder();
                var options = new RecoveryOptions(mode, fixture.Password.Length, RecoveryIntensity.High);
                string[]? numbers = mode == RecoveryMode.CommonPasswords ? [fixture.Password] : null;
                long total = RecoveryCandidates.Total(options, numbers);
                var result = await engine.RecoverAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), fixture.Source, options, numbers), progress).WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(90));
                Program.Assert(result.Status == RecoveryStatus.Found && result.Password == fixture.Password, "Real recovery failed: " + result.Status + " / " + result.Error?.Code);
                Program.Assert(result.CompletedCandidates > 0 && result.CompletedCandidates <= total && result.TotalCandidates == total, "Found result has invalid counters.");
                Program.Assert(result.CompletedCandidates < total, "An early hit was falsely displayed as a fully tested candidate space.");
                progress.AssertConsistent(total);
                Program.Assert(progress.Values.Any(p => p.Stage == "verifying"), "Recovery never exposed the complete verification phase.");
                run.Evidence[name] = new { result.Status, result.CompletedCandidates, result.TotalCandidates,
                    fullyVerified = true, sourceHashesUnchanged = true, outputsWritten = 0 };
                run.AssertSourcesUnchanged();
            });
        }
        if (fixtures.Count > 0)
        {
            await RecoveryFailureAcceptance.RunRar4EncryptedHeaderRegressionAsync(run, dll, worker,
                Path.Combine(root, "rar4-solid.rar"));
            await RunNegativeAsync(run, engine, archive, root);
            await RecoveryFailureAcceptance.RunAsync(run, Path.Combine(root, "zip-ZipCrypto-6.zip"));
            await run.CaseAsync("recovery-vm-batch-snapshot-transfer-secret-lifetime", () => RunViewModelAsync(run, root));
            if (!run.NoGui)
            {
                await run.CaseAsync("recovery-wpf-page-and-compact-layout", () => RunGuiAsync(run, root));
                await run.CaseAsync("recovery-wpf-close-cancels-and-clears-session-secrets", () => RunCloseAsync(run, root));
            }
        }
        await run.CaseAsync("recovery-source-readonly-and-log-privacy", () =>
        {
            run.AssertSourcesUnchanged();
            Program.Assert(!Directory.GetDirectories(root).Any(), "Recovery unexpectedly created extraction directories beside the source archives.");
            string diagnostics = DiagnosticBody(File.Exists(log.FilePath) ? File.ReadAllText(log.FilePath) : "");
            Program.Assert(!diagnostics.Contains("7304", StringComparison.Ordinal) && !diagnostics.Contains("8405", StringComparison.Ordinal), "Recovery results/candidates entered diagnostic logs.");
            string sample = "[2026-10-12T00:12:34.8405914+00:00] [84050000000000000000000000000000] body 7304\n8405 detail\n[8405] ordinary text";
            Program.Assert(DiagnosticBody(sample) == "body 7304\n8405 detail\n[8405] ordinary text", "Privacy matching removed diagnostic body/detail digits.");
            return Task.CompletedTask;
        });
        run.RecoveryAcceptancePassed = run.Failures == before;
    }

    private static async Task RunNegativeAsync(AcceptanceRun run, PasswordRecoveryEngine engine, ArchiveEngine archive, string root)
    {
        foreach (var mode in new[] { RecoveryMode.CommonPasswords, RecoveryMode.Cpu })
        await run.CaseAsync("recovery-exhaustion-no-automatic-fallback-" + mode, async () =>
        {
            var progress = new ProgressRecorder(); var options = new RecoveryOptions(mode, 4, RecoveryIntensity.High);
            var result = await engine.RecoverAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), Path.Combine(root, "zip-exhaustion.zip"), options), progress).WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(90));
            long total = RecoveryCandidates.Total(options);
            Program.Assert(result.Status == RecoveryStatus.Exhausted && result.Password is null && result.CompletedCandidates == total && result.TotalCandidates == total, "Exhaustion did not stop exactly at its selected candidate set.");
            progress.AssertConsistent(total);
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("recovery-user-exact-priority", async () =>
        {
            var result = await engine.RecoverAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), Path.Combine(root, "zip-priority.zip"), new(), ["7304", "73"]));
            Program.Assert(result.Status == RecoveryStatus.Found && result.Password == "7304" && result.CompletedCandidates == 1, "Exact user password did not run before variants/dictionary.");
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("recovery-mixed-passwords-never-confirmed", async () =>
        {
            var result = await engine.RecoverAsync(new(Guid.NewGuid(), Path.Combine(root, "zip-mixed.zip"), new(), ["7304", "8405"]));
            Program.Assert(result.Status == RecoveryStatus.Exhausted && result.Password is null && result.PossiblePassword,
                "An entry password was accepted as a complete archive password or the possible-password evidence was lost.");
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("recovery-missing-input-format-and-options", async () =>
        {
            await ExpectFailure(engine, Path.Combine(root, "absent.zip"), new(), "recovery.input-missing");
            await ExpectFailure(engine, Path.Combine(root, "wrong.tar"), new(), "recovery.format");
            await ExpectFailure(engine, Path.Combine(root, "zip-priority.zip"), new(Digits: 5), "recovery.options");
            await ExpectFailure(engine, Path.Combine(root, "zip-priority.zip"), new(Limits: new(MaximumBytes: 0)), "recovery.limits");
            var missing = new PasswordRecoveryEngine(Path.Combine(root, "absent.dll"), WorkerPath(run.Root));
            await ExpectFailure(missing, Path.Combine(root, "zip-priority.zip"), new(), "recovery.engine-missing");
        });
        await run.CaseAsync("recovery-unencrypted-and-encrypted-empty-data", async () =>
        {
            string unencrypted = Path.Combine(root, "plain.zip");
            using (var zip = ZipFile.Open(unencrypted, ZipArchiveMode.Create))
            using (var writer = new StreamWriter(zip.CreateEntry("plain.txt").Open())) writer.Write("Original plaintext recovery negative fixture.");
            run.TrackSource(unencrypted); await ExpectFailure(engine, unencrypted, new(), "recovery.not-encrypted");
