                && model.CompletionCue == 1 && controller.Account.Snapshot.Coins == 35,
                "Background completion lost the original result or emitted the wrong completion/reward count.");
            window.Show(); window.ShowHomePage(); window.ShowTaskCenterPage(); window.ShowFilePage(); model.SetMode(ConversionMode.Video);
            await FlushAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(model.CompletionCue == 1 && controller.Account.Snapshot.Coins == 35 && model.TaskCenterItems.Single().OutputPath == row.OutputPath,
                "Returning from another page replayed completion or changed the task result link.");
            Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, "v09-background-completed-source");
            run.Evidence["v09BackgroundCompletion"] = new { closeHidesWithoutCancelling = true, committedResultRetained = true,
                completionCueExactlyOnce = true, rewardExactlyOnce = true, taskOutputLinkPreserved = true };
        }
        finally { model.Cancel(); await model.ActiveRun.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10)); window.ApproveExit(); window.Close(); }
    }

    public static Task RunResultActionsAsync(AcceptanceRun run) => run.CaseAsync(
        "v09-completed-result-actions-natural-centering-wrap-and-shell-paths", () => ResultActionsAsync(run));

    private static async Task ResultActionsAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var preference = ThemeManager.Preference;
        var observations = new List<object>();
        static IEnumerable<T> Descendants<T>(DependencyObject parent) where T : DependencyObject
        {
            for (var index = 0; index < VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(parent); index++)
            {
                var child = VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(parent, index);
                if (child is T match) yield return match;
                foreach (var nested in Descendants<T>(child)) yield return nested;
            }
        }
        try
        {
            foreach (var action in new[] { FileProcessingAction.Video, FileProcessingAction.CompressMedia,
                FileProcessingAction.Gif, FileProcessingAction.ObfuscateImage, FileProcessingAction.CreateArchive, FileProcessingAction.ExtractArchive })
            {
                var engine = new ControlledEngine { ArchiveRequiresPassword = false }; var model = Model(run, engine, "result-actions-" + action);
                var extension = action is FileProcessingAction.CompressMedia or FileProcessingAction.ObfuscateImage ? ".png"
                    : action == FileProcessingAction.ExtractArchive ? ".zip" : ".mp4";
                await model.ImportForActionAsync(action, new[] { Touch(run, "result-actions-" + action, "结果 中文, 空格" + extension) });
                Program.Assert(model.CanStart, "Result fixture cannot start " + action + ": " + model.StartHint + "; edit=" + model.CanEdit + "; pending=" + model.PendingCount);
                await model.RunQueueAsync();
                Program.Assert(model.CompletedCount == 1, "Result fixture did not complete " + action + ": " + model.StatusMessage + "; error=" + model.LastError?.Message);
                var window = new MainWindow(model) { ShowActivated = false }; window.Show();
                try
                {
                    window.ShowFilePage();
                    foreach (var theme in new[] { "Light", "Dark" })
                    foreach (var (width, height) in new[] { (1200d, 820d), (960d, 540d), (620d, 480d) })
                    {
                        model.ThemePreference = theme; window.Width = width; window.Height = height; await FlushAsync(window);
                        var gridName = model.IsArchivePage ? "ArchiveQueueGrid" : model.IsImageObfuscationPage ? "ImageObfuscationQueueGrid" : "QueueGrid";
                        var grid = (DataGrid)window.FindName(gridName);
                        grid.ScrollIntoView(grid.Items[0]); await FlushAsync(window);
                        var row = (DataGridRow)grid.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromIndex(0);
                        var buttons = Descendants<Button>(row).Where(button => button.IsVisible && button.Content is string label &&
                            label is "打开结果" or "打开所在位置").ToArray();
                        Program.Assert(buttons.Length == 2 && double.IsNaN(grid.RowHeight), "Completed row lacks two result actions or uses fixed row height: " + action + "; buttons=" + buttons.Length + "; height=" + grid.RowHeight + "; status=" + model.StatusMessage + "; error=" + model.LastError?.Message + "; labels=" + string.Join(",", Descendants<Button>(row).Select(b => b.Content + ":" + b.IsVisible)));
                        Program.Assert(ReferenceEquals(VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(buttons[0]), VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(buttons[1])), "Result actions do not share their wrapping layout.");
                        foreach (var button in buttons)
                        {
                            button.ApplyTemplate(); var surface = (Border)button.Template.FindName("Surface", button);
                            var text = Descendants<TextBlock>(button).Single(value => value.Text == (string)button.Content);
                            var textBounds = text.TransformToAncestor(button).TransformBounds(new Rect(text.RenderSize));
                            var rowBounds = button.TransformToAncestor(row).TransformBounds(new Rect(button.RenderSize));
                            Program.Assert(double.IsNaN(text.LineHeight) && text.LineStackingStrategy == LineStackingStrategy.MaxHeight &&
                                Math.Abs(textBounds.Top + textBounds.Height / 2 - button.ActualHeight / 2) <= .75,
                                "Result action text is not naturally centered: " + action + "/" + button.Content);
                            Program.Assert(button.ActualHeight >= 36 && Math.Abs(surface.ActualHeight - button.ActualHeight) < .75 &&
                                rowBounds.Top >= 3 && rowBounds.Bottom <= row.ActualHeight - 3 && rowBounds.Left >= 3 && rowBounds.Right <= row.ActualWidth - 3,
                                "A result action or its focus outline is clipped by its row: " + action);
                        }
                        buttons[0].Focus(); await FlushAsync(window);
                        Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, $"result-actions-{theme.ToLowerInvariant()}-{action.ToString().ToLowerInvariant()}-{width:0}x{height:0}");
                        var first = buttons[0].TranslatePoint(new Point(), row); var second = buttons[1].TranslatePoint(new Point(), row);
                        observations.Add(new { action, theme, width, height, rowHeight = row.ActualHeight, wraps = second.Y > first.Y + 1 });
                    }
                }
                finally { window.ApproveExit(); window.Close(); }
            }
            var shellDirectory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "定位目录 中文, 空格"); Directory.CreateDirectory(shellDirectory);
            var shellFile = Path.Combine(shellDirectory, "结果 中文, 空格.png"); File.WriteAllText(shellFile, "Native shell parsing fixture.");
            foreach (var path in new[] { shellFile, shellDirectory })
            {
                var item = ResultFileActions.ParseItem(path);
                try { Program.Assert(item != IntPtr.Zero, "Native shell could not parse a Unicode/comma file or directory result."); }
                finally { System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal.FreeCoTaskMem(item); }
            }
            run.Evidence["resultActions"] = new { observations, nativeShellParsesFileAndDirectory = true,
                note = "Production completed-row rendering, focused button bounds and native shell item parsing; default application launching and Explorer selection are manual integration checks." };
        }
        finally { ThemeManager.SetPreference(preference); }
    }

    private static IReadOnlyList<FormatPreset> Presets(AcceptanceRun run) => PresetCatalog.Load(Path.Combine(run.Root, "shared", "presets.json"));
    private static SettingsStore Store(AcceptanceRun run, string name) => new(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "v09-" + name, "settings"));
    private static MainViewModel Model(AcceptanceRun run, ControlledEngine engine, string name)
    {
        var store = Store(run, name); store.Save(new AppSettings { OutputDirectory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "v09-" + name, "outputs"),
            MotionPreference = MotionPreference.Reduced, Pet = new PetSettings { Enabled = false, Roaming = false, Topmost = false } });
        return new MainViewModel(engine, Presets(run), store, new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "v09-" + name, "logs")), engine, engine, engine);
    }
    private static string Touch(AcceptanceRun run, string name, string file)
    {
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "v09-" + name); Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var path = Path.Combine(directory, file); File.WriteAllText(path, "Deterministic V0.9 navigation fixture."); run.TrackSource(path); return path;
    }
    private static async Task FlushAsync(Window window)
    { await window.Dispatcher.InvokeAsync(window.UpdateLayout, DispatcherPriority.ContextIdle); await Task.Delay(20); }
    private static void AssertEmptyContentInsideDropViewport(MainWindow window)
    {
        var drop = (Button)window.FindName("DropArea");
        var scroller = (ScrollViewer)window.FindName("EmptyScrollViewer");
        static ScrollContentPresenter? FindViewport(DependencyObject parent)
        {
            for (var index = 0; index < VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(parent); index++)
            {
                var child = VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(parent, index);
                if (child is ScrollContentPresenter viewport) return viewport;
                if (FindViewport(child) is { } nested) return nested;
            }
            return null;
        }
        var presenter = FindViewport(scroller) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing real empty-state scroll viewport.");
        var viewport = Rect.Intersect(new Rect(0, 0, drop.ActualWidth, drop.ActualHeight),
            presenter.TransformToAncestor(drop).TransformBounds(new Rect(0, 0, presenter.ActualWidth, presenter.ActualHeight)));
        Program.Assert(drop.IsVisible && !viewport.IsEmpty && viewport.Width > 0 && viewport.Height > 0,
            "The empty-state drop viewport is not visible.");
        foreach (var name in new[] { "EmptyAssistantText", "EmptyCat" })
        {
            var element = (FrameworkElement)window.FindName(name);
            var bounds = element.TransformToAncestor(drop).TransformBounds(new Rect(0, 0, element.ActualWidth, element.ActualHeight));
            Program.Assert(element.IsVisible && element.ActualWidth > 0 && element.ActualHeight > 0 &&
                bounds.Left >= viewport.Left - 2 && bounds.Top >= viewport.Top - 2 &&
                bounds.Right <= viewport.Right + 2 && bounds.Bottom <= viewport.Bottom + 2,
                $"{name} is clipped by the real drop viewport on {window.Model.CurrentFileAction} at {window.ActualWidth}x{window.ActualHeight}: content={bounds}, viewport={viewport}.");
        }
    }
    private static double Contrast(Color first, Color second)
    {
        static double L(Color color) { static double C(byte v) { var n = v / 255d; return n <= .04045 ? n / 12.92 : Math.Pow((n + .055) / 1.055, 2.4); } return .2126 * C(color.R) + .7152 * C(color.G) + .0722 * C(color.B); }
        var a = L(first); var b = L(second); return (Math.Max(a, b) + .05) / (Math.Min(a, b) + .05);
    }

    private sealed class ControlledEngine : IConversionEngine, IArchiveEngine, IPasswordRecoveryEngine, IImageObfuscationEngine
    {
        public bool Hold { get; set; }
        public bool FailConversion { get; set; }
        public bool ArchiveRequiresPassword { get; set; } = true;
        public string? RecoveryPassword { get; set; }
        private Action<double?>? reportProgress;
        public void ReportProgress(double? percent) => (reportProgress ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("No active engine progress observer."))(percent);
        public List<object> Requests { get; } = new();
        public TaskCompletionSource Started { get; } = new(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
        public TaskCompletionSource Release { get; } = new(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
        public bool CancellationObserved { get; private set; }
        public Task<MediaInfo> ProbeAsync(string path, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            var mode = ImportService.Classify(path);
            return Task.FromResult(new MediaInfo(mode != ConversionMode.Audio, mode != ConversionMode.Image, 1,
                mode == ConversionMode.Image ? "png" : mode == ConversionMode.Audio ? "wav" : "mp4", "h264", "aac", 1024, 64, 64, 25));
        }
        private async Task WaitAsync(object request, CancellationToken token)
        {
            Requests.Add(request); Started.TrySetResult();
            if (!Hold) return;
            try { await Release.Task.WaitAsync(token); }
            catch (OperationCanceledException) { CancellationObserved = true; throw; }
        }
        private static string Commit(string directory, Guid id)
        { Directory.CreateDirectory(directory); var path = Path.Combine(directory, id.ToString("N") + ".fixture"); File.WriteAllText(path, "Committed deterministic result."); return path; }
        public async Task<ConversionResult> ConvertAsync(ConversionRequest request, IProgress<ConversionProgress>? progress = null, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        { reportProgress = percent => progress?.Report(new(percent, TimeSpan.Zero)); ReportProgress(null);
            await WaitAsync(request, cancellationToken); return FailConversion
            ? new(JobStatus.Failed, null, new("fixture.failed", "Deterministic failure.", "Retry from the source page."))
            : new(JobStatus.Completed, Commit(request.OutputDirectory, request.Id), null); }
        Task<ArchiveInspection> IArchiveEngine.InspectAsync(string path, string? password, CancellationToken token)
            => Task.FromResult(new ArchiveInspection(path, "zip", 1024, 1, ArchiveRequiresPassword));
        public async Task<ArchiveResult> ExecuteAsync(ArchiveRequest request, IProgress<ArchiveProgress>? progress = null, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        { reportProgress = percent => progress?.Report(new(percent, 0, null, 0, null, Stage: percent.HasValue ? "extracting" : "checking")); ReportProgress(null);
            await WaitAsync(request, cancellationToken);
            if (request is ExtractArchiveRequest)
            {
                var directory = Path.Combine(request.OutputDirectory, request.Id.ToString("N"));
                Directory.CreateDirectory(directory); File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(directory, "result.txt"), "Committed deterministic extraction.");
                return new(JobStatus.Completed, directory, ArchiveOutputKind.Directory);
            }
            return new(JobStatus.Completed, Commit(request.OutputDirectory, request.Id), ArchiveOutputKind.File); }
        public async Task<RecoveryResult> RecoverAsync(RecoveryRequest request, IProgress<RecoveryProgress>? progress = null, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        { reportProgress = percent => progress?.Report(percent is { } value ? new((long)value, 100, "testing") : new(0, 0, "checking")); ReportProgress(null);
            await WaitAsync(request, cancellationToken); return new(
            RecoveryPassword is null ? RecoveryStatus.Exhausted : RecoveryStatus.Found, RecoveryPassword,
            CompletedCandidates: 10, TotalCandidates: 10); }
        Task<ImageObfuscationInfo> IImageObfuscationEngine.InspectAsync(string path, CancellationToken token)
            => Task.FromResult(new ImageObfuscationInfo(64, 64, 8, true, false));
        public async Task<ImageObfuscationResult> ProcessAsync(ImageObfuscationRequest request, IProgress<ImageObfuscationProgress>? progress = null, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        { reportProgress = percent => progress?.Report(new(percent, percent.HasValue ? "processing" : "checking")); ReportProgress(null);
            await WaitAsync(request, cancellationToken); return new(JobStatus.Completed, Commit(request.OutputDirectory, request.Id), null, new(1024, 100, 64, 64, request.Options.Mode, request.Options.Iterations, true)); }
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/V09WindowFrameAcceptance.cs ---
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Automation;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Controls.Primitives;
using System.Windows.Interop;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Shell;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Themes;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

internal static class V09WindowFrameAcceptance
{
    public static async Task RunAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var preference = ThemeManager.Preference;
        var window = new MainWindow { ShowActivated = false };
        window.Show();
        try
        {
            window.ShowHomePage();
            var handle = new WindowInteropHelper(window).Handle;
            var border = window.Content as Border ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Themed frame missing.");
            var layout = (DockPanel)border.Child; var caption = (DockPanel)layout.Children[0];
            var buttons = (StackPanel)caption.Children[0];
            var maximize = buttons.Children.OfType<Button>().Single(b => AutomationProperties.GetName(b) == "最大化");
            var chrome = WindowChrome.GetWindowChrome(window);
            Program.Assert(chrome is not null && chrome.CaptionHeight == 32 && chrome.ResizeBorderThickness.Left >= 6,
                "Caption drag region or native resize border is missing.");
            foreach (var theme in new[] { "Light", "Dark" })
            {
                ThemeManager.SetPreference(theme); await SettleAsync(window);
                Program.Assert(caption.Background is SolidColorBrush captionBrush &&
                    captionBrush.Color == ((SolidColorBrush)window.FindResource("Surface")).Color &&
                    border.BorderBrush is SolidColorBrush edgeBrush && edgeBrush.Color == ((SolidColorBrush)window.FindResource("Border")).Color,
                    "Caption or outer border did not follow the live theme.");
                var home = (RadioButton)window.FindName("NavHome"); home.ApplyTemplate();
                Program.Assert(home.Template.FindName("Surface", home) is Border homeSurface && homeSurface.Background is SolidColorBrush homeBrush &&
                    homeBrush.Color == ((SolidColorBrush)window.FindResource("AccentTint")).Color,
                    "Selected home navigation retained a color from the previous theme.");
                Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, "v09-window-frame-" + theme.ToLowerInvariant());
            }
            Program.Assert(Hit(handle, caption.PointToScreen(new Point(190, 16))) == 2, "Caption cannot initiate native window dragging.");
            Program.Assert(Hit(handle, border.PointToScreen(new Point(2, 100))) == 10, "Native left resize edge is missing.");
            Program.Assert(Hit(handle, maximize.PointToScreen(new Point(20, 16))) == 9, "Native maximize target for snap layouts is missing.");
            SendMessage(handle, 0x0112, new IntPtr(0xF030), IntPtr.Zero);
            await SettleAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(window.WindowState == WindowState.Maximized && AutomationProperties.GetName(maximize) == "向下还原",
                "Native maximize command or accessible restore name failed.");
            Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, "v09-window-frame-maximized");
            maximize.RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(ButtonBase.ClickEvent)); await SettleAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(window.WindowState == WindowState.Normal, "Caption restore button failed.");
            SendMessage(handle, 0x0112, new IntPtr(0xF020), IntPtr.Zero); await SettleAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(window.WindowState == WindowState.Minimized, "Native minimize command failed.");
            SystemCommands.RestoreWindow(window); await SettleAsync(window);
            Program.Assert(window.WindowState == WindowState.Normal, "Native restore after minimize failed.");
            run.Evidence["v09WindowFrame"] = new { liveLightDarkCaptionAndBorder = true, nativeCaptionHit = 2,
                nativeResizeHit = 10, nativeMaximizeHit = 9, nativeMaximizeMinimizeRestore = true,
                accessibleCaptionButtons = true, note = "Native Win32 commands and hit tests on this Windows host. Physical dragging, cross-monitor DPI and Windows 11 snap flyout need corresponding environments." };
        }
        finally { window.ApproveExit(); window.Close(); ThemeManager.SetPreference(preference); }
    }
    private static async Task SettleAsync(Window window)
    { await Task.Delay(160); window.UpdateLayout(); }
    private static long Hit(IntPtr hwnd, Point point)
        => SendMessage(hwnd, 0x0084, IntPtr.Zero, new IntPtr(((int)point.Y << 16) | ((int)point.X & 0xFFFF))).ToInt64();
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] private static extern IntPtr SendMessage(IntPtr hwnd, int message, IntPtr wParam, IntPtr lParam);
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/V101PlayerAcceptance.cs ---
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

/// <summary>Player/metadata fixtures deliberately reuse old poses; this does not claim new artwork quality.</summary>
internal static class V101PlayerAcceptance
{
    public static Task RunMetadataAsync(AcceptanceRun run) => run.CaseAsync("v101-lazy-page-metadata-contact-anchors-and-exit-variants", () =>
    {
        var fixture = CreateFixture(run);
        var before = PetSpriteView.CachedAtlasBytes;
        using var first = new PetSpriteView(); using var second = new PetSpriteView();
        first.LoadManifest(fixture);
        Program.Assert(first.AssetError is null && first.HasSpriteAtlas && first.HasAnimation("walk"), "A v3 segmented fixture failed to parse.");
        Program.Assert(PetSpriteView.CachedAtlasBytes == before && !first.HasTextureReference, "Reading clip definitions decoded an atlas before a view was visible.");
        Program.Assert(second.TryShareLoadedSpritesFrom(first) && PetSpriteView.CachedAtlasBytes == before && !second.HasTextureReference, "Sharing immutable definitions pinned an image page.");
        var walk = first.GetFrameDiagnostics("walk");
        Program.Assert(walk.Count == 40 && walk.All(x => x.Feet?.Count == 2 && x.AtlasFile is not null), "The manifest dropped foot/contact geometry or marker-specific retreat frames.");
        Program.Assert(walk.Any(x => x.HeadAngle != 0 && x.NeckAngle != 0 && x.AccessoryScale != 1), "The manifest dropped accessory rotation/scale metadata.");
        Program.Assert(first.WalkContactPhases.SequenceEqual(new[] { 0d, .5d }) && first.WalkStrideLength == 48, "Gait distance/contact metadata was lost.");
        Program.Assert(first.CurrentAccessoryOcclusion is { IsFrozen: true } foreground && foreground.FillContains(new Point(80, 112)), "Normalized foreground-paw metadata did not reach the native accessory coordinate plane.");
        // The second marker starts from a different real source cell, rather than reusing marker zero's exit.
        Program.Assert(walk[34].SourceRect != walk[37].SourceRect, "Distinct planted-foot exits collapsed to the same starting pose.");
        run.Evidence["v101PlayerMetadata"] = new { textureBytesBefore = before, textureBytesAfter = PetSpriteView.CachedAtlasBytes,
            metadataOnlyLoad = true, distinctExitMarkers = new[] { 0, 15 }, feetRetained = true,
            fixtureArt = "Legacy cells reused only to test playback; not evidence of animation inbetweens." };
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    });

    public static Task RunWindowAsync(AcceptanceRun run) => run.CaseAsync("v101-segment-deadlines-latest-target-distance-binding-and-hidden-release", async () =>
    {
        MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System);
        using var view = new PetSpriteView(); view.LoadManifest(CreateFixture(run)); view.SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode.Gentle);
        var host = new Window { Width = 230, Height = 230, Content = view, ShowInTaskbar = false, WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterScreen };
        try
        {
            host.Show(); host.UpdateLayout();
            view.SetAnimation("work");
            // Occupy the real dispatcher across the entry/loop boundary, then allow one overdue tick.
            Thread.Sleep(420);
            await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.ContextIdle);
            Program.Assert(view.ActiveActionName == "work" && view.ActiveSegmentName == "loop" && view.RenderedFrameIndex >= 9 && view.RenderedFrameIndex <= 24,
                "An overdue UI tick slowed the entry/loop sequence instead of catching up across its segment boundary.");
            view.SetAnimation("feed"); view.SetAnimation("play"); view.SetAnimation("happy");
            Program.Assert(view.Animation == "happy", "The newest requested destination was not retained immediately.");
            await WaitAsync(() => view.ActiveActionName == "happy" && view.ActiveSegmentName == "loop", "A stale transition prevented the latest destination from taking effect.");
            Program.Assert(view.ActiveTransitionClockCount == 0 && VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount((DependencyObject)VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(view, 0)) == 2,
                "A whole-body overlay or opacity transition is still present.");

            view.SetAnimation("walk");
            await WaitAsync(() => view.IsWalkLoopReady, "The walking entry did not finish.");
            view.UpdateWalkDistance(12);
            var frozenPhase = view.RenderedFrameIndex;
            Program.Assert(frozenPhase == 7 && view.ActiveFrameTimerCount == 0, "Walking phase was not tied to its actual quarter-stride displacement.");
            await Task.Delay(220);
            Program.Assert(view.RenderedFrameIndex == frozenPhase, "Stationary world position advanced the walking feet.");
            view.UpdateWalkDistance(24);
            Program.Assert(view.RenderedFrameIndex == 15, "A half stride did not land at the second foot contact.");
            var secondExitStart = view.GetFrameDiagnostics("walk")[37].SourceRect;
            var canceledCallbacks = 0;
            view.CompleteWalk(() => canceledCallbacks++);
            Program.Assert(view.ActiveSegmentName == "exit" && view.CurrentFrameDiagnostics?.SourceRect == secondExitStart,
                "Stopping on the second planted foot selected a mismatched retreat pose.");
            view.SetAnimation("feed");
            await WaitAsync(() => view.ActiveActionName == "feed", "The new interaction did not replace a pending walking completion.");
            Program.Assert(canceledCallbacks == 0 && view.Animation == "feed", "A stale walking callback overwrote a newer request.");

            view.SetAnimation("walk"); await WaitAsync(() => view.IsWalkLoopReady, "A second walking entry did not finish.");
            view.UpdateWalkDistance(0);
            var completedCallbacks = 0;
            view.CompleteWalk(() => { completedCallbacks++; view.SetAnimation("happy"); });
            await WaitAsync(() => view.ActiveActionName == "happy" && view.ActiveSegmentName == "loop", "An exit completion overwrote its callback's new action.");
            Program.Assert(completedCallbacks == 1, "A walking completion was lost or delivered more than once.");

            view.SetAnimation("walk"); await WaitAsync(() => view.IsWalkLoopReady, "The walk did not restart for the latest-target round trip.");
            view.UpdateWalkDistance(0); view.CompleteWalk(() => canceledCallbacks++);
            view.SetAnimation("feed"); view.SetAnimation("walk");
            await WaitAsync(() => view.IsWalkLoopReady, "An old walk exit marked a newly requested walk as already complete.");
            Program.Assert(view.Animation == "walk" && canceledCallbacks == 0, "A superseded walk completion survived a latest-target round trip.");

            foreach (var action in new[] { "page0", "page1", "page2", "page3", "page0", "page2" })
            {
                view.SetAnimation(action); await WaitAsync(() => view.ActiveActionName == action, "Page churn did not reach its newest action.");
                Program.Assert(view.AssetError is null && PetSpriteView.CachedAtlasBytes <= 16 * 1024 * 1024, "Paged animation exceeded its hard decoded-cache budget.");
            }
            var hiddenFrame = view.RenderedFrameIndex;
            view.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed; await Task.Delay(180);
            Program.Assert(!view.HasTextureReference && !view.HasActiveAnimation && view.RenderedFrameIndex == hiddenFrame,
                "A hidden view retained a decoded page or playback clock.");
            view.Visibility = Visibility.Visible; await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Render);
            Program.Assert(view.HasTextureReference, "A restored view did not reacquire its current page.");
            view.SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode.Reduced); view.SetAnimation("work"); view.SetAnimation("happy");
            Program.Assert(view.ActiveActionName == "happy" && !view.HasActiveAnimation, "Reduced motion retained a transition or failed to adopt the latest state.");
            run.Evidence["v101PlayerRuntime"] = new { actualWpfDispatcher = true, busyDispatcherMilliseconds = 420,
                latestDestination = "happy", noBodyOverlay = true, displacementBoundWalk = true,
                markerSpecificExit = 15, staleCompletionCanceled = true, hiddenReleasesPageLease = true,
                decodedCacheBytes = PetSpriteView.CachedAtlasBytes, decodedCacheLimitBytes = 16 * 1024 * 1024 };
        }
        finally { host.Close(); }
    });

    private static string CreateFixture(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "v101-player-fixture"); Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var files = new[] { "pet-files.png", "pet-active.png", "pet-calm.png", "pet-extras.png" };
        var assetRoot = Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(typeof(PetSpriteView).Assembly.Location)!, "Assets", "Pet");
        string LegacyPage(string name)
        {
            var original = Path.Combine(assetRoot, name);
            if (File.Exists(original)) return original;
            var preserved = Directory.GetFiles(assetRoot, "*-" + name, SearchOption.AllDirectories);
            return preserved.Length == 1 ? preserved[0] : throw new FileNotFoundException("The preserved legacy fixture atlas is missing or ambiguous: " + name);
        }
        var pages = files.Select(file => new { file = LegacyPage(file), columns = 6, rows = 4 }).ToArray();
        object Pose(int cell, int atlas = 0, double ms = 1000d / 30, string contact = "none") => new
        {
            atlas, cell, ms, placement = new[] { 8, 8, 240, 240 }, head = new[] { .73, .21 }, neck = new[] { .54, .73 },
            feet = new[] { new[] { .4, .9 }, new[] { .6, .9 } }, contact, headAngle = cell * 2d, neckAngle = cell * .5, accessoryScale = 1 + cell * .01,
            accessoryOcclusion = new[] { new[] { new[] { .45, .67 }, new[] { .60, .67 }, new[] { .60, .80 }, new[] { .45, .80 } } }
        };
        object Segment(IEnumerable<object> frames, bool loop) => new { frames = frames.ToArray(), loop };
        object Action(int atlas, bool exits = false) => exits ? new
        {
            canvasSize = new[] { 256, 256 }, baseline = .94, exitAt = new[] { 0, 30 },
            segments = new Dictionary<string, object>
            {
                ["enter"] = Segment(new[] { Pose(0, atlas, 40), Pose(1, atlas, 40) }, false),
                ["loop"] = Segment(Enumerable.Range(0, 60).Select(i => Pose(i % 6, atlas)), true),
                ["exit"] = Segment(new[] { Pose(0, atlas, 40), Pose(1, atlas, 40), Pose(0, atlas, 40) }, false)
            }
        } : (object)new { canvasSize = new[] { 256, 256 }, baseline = .94,
            segments = new { loop = Segment(new[] { Pose(0, atlas, 60), Pose(1, atlas, 60) }, true) } };
        var actions = new Dictionary<string, object> { ["idle"] = Action(0), ["work"] = Action(0, true), ["feed"] = Action(1, true), ["play"] = Action(3, true), ["happy"] = Action(1) };
        actions["walk"] = new
        {
            canvasSize = new[] { 256, 256 }, baseline = .94, strideLength = 48, exitAt = new[] { 0, 15 }, contactPhases = new[] { 0d, .5d },
            segments = new Dictionary<string, object>
            {
                ["enter"] = Segment(new[] { Pose(0, 1, 40), Pose(1, 1, 40), Pose(2, 1, 40), Pose(0, 1, 40) }, false),
                ["loop"] = Segment(Enumerable.Range(0, 30).Select(i => Pose(i == 15 ? 3 : i % 6, 1, contact: i == 0 ? "left" : i == 15 ? "right" : "none")), true),
                ["exitByMarker"] = new Dictionary<string, object>
                {
                    ["0"] = Segment(new[] { Pose(0, 1, 40), Pose(1, 1, 40), Pose(0, 1, 40) }, false),
                    ["15"] = Segment(new[] { Pose(3, 1, 40), Pose(2, 1, 40), Pose(0, 1, 40) }, false)
                }
            }
        };
        for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) actions["page" + i] = Action(i);
        var path = Path.Combine(directory, "animations.json");
        File.WriteAllText(path, JsonSerializer.Serialize(new { version = 3, atlases = pages, animations = actions })); return path;
    }
    private static async Task WaitAsync(Func<bool> condition, string message)
    {
        var watch = Stopwatch.StartNew(); while (!condition() && watch.Elapsed < TimeSpan.FromSeconds(4)) await Task.Delay(15);
        Program.Assert(condition(), message);
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/V101WalkingAcceptance.cs ---
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.Json.Nodes;
using System.Windows;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

internal static class V101WalkingAcceptance
{
    public static Task RunGeometryAsync(AcceptanceRun run) => run.CaseAsync("v101-walk-contact-distance-and-continuous-deceleration", () =>
    {
        double[] contacts = [0, .5];
        foreach (var available in new[] { 7d, 23.9, 24, 40, 48, 73, 100, 160 })
        {
            var aligned = PetWalkTrajectory.ContactAlignedDistance(available, 48, contacts);
            Program.Assert(aligned >= 0 && aligned <= available && Math.Abs(aligned / 24 - Math.Round(aligned / 24)) < .00001,
                "A planned walk exceeded its available distance or stopped between foot contacts.");
        }
        Program.Assert(PetWalkTrajectory.ContactAlignedDistance(double.NaN, 48, contacts) == 0 &&
            PetWalkTrajectory.ContactAlignedDistance(30, 0, contacts) == 0, "Invalid movement geometry was not rejected.");
        foreach (var distance in new[] { 0d, .01, 13, 23.99, 24, 35, 47.99, 48, 63, 95.99 })
        {
            var next = PetWalkTrajectory.NextContactDistance(distance, 96, 48, contacts);
            Program.Assert(next >= distance && next <= 96 && next - distance <= 24.00001 &&
                Math.Abs(next / 24 - Math.Round(next / 24)) < .00001,
                "A stop request skipped the next planted foot or moved backwards.");
        }
        const double start = 13, end = 24, initialVelocity = 36;
        const double duration = 2 * (end - start) / initialVelocity;
        var first = PetWalkTrajectory.Sample(start, end, initialVelocity, duration, 0);
        var last = PetWalkTrajectory.Sample(start, end, initialVelocity, duration, duration);
        Program.Assert(first == (start, initialVelocity) && last == (end, 0), "A stopping trajectory lost velocity continuity or failed to land at rest.");
        var previous = start;
        for (var sample = 1; sample <= 120; sample++)
        {
            var result = PetWalkTrajectory.Sample(start, end, initialVelocity, duration, duration * sample / 120);
            Program.Assert(result.Distance >= previous && result.Distance <= end && result.Velocity >= 0,
                "Stopping introduced backwards motion or overshot the planted foot.");
            previous = result.Distance;
        }
        run.Evidence["v101WalkGeometry"] = new { referenceCanvasDips = 160, strideDips = 48, contactPhases = contacts,
            distanceBoundPhase = true, endpointsWithinAvailableSpace = true, monotoneStopping = true, velocityContinuousStop = true };
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    });

    public static Task RunWindowAsync(AcceptanceRun run) => RunWindowAsync(run, segmentFixture: false);
    public static Task RunSegmentWindowAsync(AcceptanceRun run) => RunWindowAsync(run, segmentFixture: true);

    private static Task RunWindowAsync(AcceptanceRun run, bool segmentFixture) => run.CaseAsync(segmentFixture
        ? "v101-real-window-v3-fixture-turn-entry-and-exit-contract"
        : "v101-real-walk-turn-landing-and-latest-request", async () =>
    {
        MotionPreferences.SetPreference(MotionPreference.System);
        using var window = new DesktopPetWindow();
        window.Configure(false);
        var manifest = Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(typeof(PetSpriteView).Assembly.Location)!, "Assets", "Pet", "animations.json");
        if (segmentFixture) manifest = WriteSegmentFixture(manifest, run.Directory);
        using var manifestDocument = JsonDocument.Parse(File.ReadAllText(manifest));
        var walkDefinition = manifestDocument.RootElement.GetProperty("animations").GetProperty("walk");
        var hadEnter = walkDefinition.TryGetProperty("segments", out var segments) && segments.TryGetProperty("enter", out _);
        window.LoadSprites(manifest);
        Program.Assert(window.SpriteView.HasSpriteAtlas && window.SpriteView.AssetError is null, "The native walking manifest failed to load.");
        window.SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode.Gentle);
        var monitor = DesktopEnvironment.GetMonitors().First(item => item.Primary);
        window.SetPosition(monitor.WorkArea.Left + 220 * monitor.DpiScaleX, monitor.WorkArea.Top + 120 * monitor.DpiScaleY);
        window.SetEnvironmentVisible(true);
        window.UpdateLayout();
        await Task.Delay(60);
        var beforeTurn = window.Position;
        Program.Assert(window.TryWalk(-80), "A visible idle pet did not accept walking.");
        var hadTurn = window.SpriteView.HasAnimation("turn");
        if (hadTurn)
        {
            Program.Assert(window.SpriteView.Animation == "turn" && window.SpriteView.RenderTransform.Value.M11 >= 0,
                "Turning instantly mirrored the cat before its drawn turn segment.");
            await Task.Delay(80);
            Program.Assert(Math.Abs(window.Position.X - beforeTurn.X) < 1, "World movement began before the turn finished.");
        }
        var entryWait = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        while (!window.SpriteView.IsWalkLoopReady && entryWait.Elapsed < TimeSpan.FromSeconds(6))
        {
            Program.Assert(Math.Abs(window.Position.X - beforeTurn.X) < 1, "World movement began before turn/entry reached the walk loop.");
            await Task.Delay(10);
        }
        Program.Assert(window.SpriteView.IsWalkLoopReady, "The turn/entry did not reach a walk loop.");
        await WaitUntilAsync(() => window.WalkDistanceDips > 4, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(7), "The turn/entry never reached actual distance-bound walking.");
        var moved = window.WalkDistanceDips;
        window.SetAnimation("work");
        window.SetAnimation("happy");
        window.SetAnimation("feed");
        await WaitUntilAsync(() => !window.IsWalking, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(7), "A normal stop did not complete its foot contact and exit.");
        Program.Assert(window.SpriteView.Animation == "feed" && window.WalkDistanceDips >= moved && window.ActiveMovementTimerCount == 0,
            "An old walk completion overwrote the latest animation request or retained movement work.");
        Program.Assert(window.SpriteView.RenderTransform.Value.M11 < 0, "Landing abruptly reset the facing direction.");
        var stopped = window.Position;
        await Task.Delay(150);
        Program.Assert(Math.Abs(window.Position.X - stopped.X) < 1 && Math.Abs(window.Position.Y - stopped.Y) < 1,
            "A stale walk timer moved the pet after landing.");
        window.SetAnimation("idle");
        Program.Assert(window.TryWalk(70), "The pet could not start a second walk in the opposite direction.");
        window.SetEnvironmentVisible(false);
        var hiddenPosition = window.Position;
        await Task.Delay(250);
        Program.Assert(!window.IsWalking && window.ActiveMovementTimerCount == 0 && Math.Abs(window.Position.X - hiddenPosition.X) < 1,
            "Hiding retained a pending turn callback or native movement.");
        window.SetEnvironmentVisible(true);
        var beforeInterruptedEntry = window.Position;
        Program.Assert(window.TryWalk(70), "The pet could not restart after hiding during a turn.");
        window.SetAnimation("work");
        window.SetAnimation("happy");
        window.SetAnimation("feed");
        await WaitUntilAsync(() => !window.IsWalking, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(8), "Stopping during turn/entry did not reach its planted exit.");
        Program.Assert(window.SpriteView.Animation == "feed" && window.ActiveMovementTimerCount == 0 &&
            Math.Abs(window.Position.X - beforeInterruptedEntry.X) < 1 && Math.Abs(window.Position.Y - beforeInterruptedEntry.Y) < 1,
            "An interaction during turn/entry moved the pet or lost the latest requested action.");
        window.SetAnimation("idle");
        window.SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode.Reduced);
        Program.Assert(!window.TryWalk(40) && window.ActiveMovementTimerCount == 0, "Reduced motion allowed a walking/turning clock.");
        run.Evidence[segmentFixture ? "v101NativeWalkSegmentFixture" : "v101NativeWalk"] = new { realWindow = true,
            assetKind = segmentFixture ? "isolated v3 logic fixture using legacy poses; not new animation art" : "current production manifest",
            turnSegmentPresent = hadTurn, enterSegmentPresent = hadEnter,
            drawnTurnBeforeMirror = hadTurn ? (bool?)true : null, entryBeforeTranslation = hadEnter ? (bool?)true : null,
            latestRequestAfterLanding = "feed", retainedFacing = true, hiddenCancelsPendingCallbacks = true,
            interruptedEntryReachesPlantedExitWithoutTranslation = true };
    });

    private static string WriteSegmentFixture(string productionManifest, string directory)
    {
        var fixture = JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(productionManifest))!.AsObject();
        fixture["version"] = 3;
        // This fixture tests state sequencing only. Existing poses deliberately
        // stand in for segments and cannot be counted as V1.0.1 animation art.
        foreach (var atlas in fixture["atlases"]!.AsArray())
            atlas!["file"] = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(productionManifest)!, atlas["file"]!.GetValue<string>()));
        var actions = fixture["animations"]!.AsObject();
        actions["turn"] = JsonSerializer.SerializeToNode(new { atlas = 1, row = 0, frames = 3, frameMs = 90, loop = false });
        JsonNode Segment(int[] cells, bool loop) => JsonSerializer.SerializeToNode(new { frameMs = 60, loop,
            frames = cells.Select(cell => new { atlas = 1, cell }).ToArray() })!;
        actions["walk"] = new JsonObject
        {
            ["canvasSize"] = JsonSerializer.SerializeToNode(new[] { 256, 256 }),
            ["strideLength"] = 48,
            ["contactPhases"] = JsonSerializer.SerializeToNode(new[] { 0d, .5 }),
            ["exitAt"] = JsonSerializer.SerializeToNode(new[] { 0, 3 }),
            ["segments"] = new JsonObject { ["enter"] = Segment([0, 1], false), ["loop"] = Segment([0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5], true), ["exit"] = Segment([3, 0], false) }
        };
        var fixtureDirectory = Path.Combine(directory, "v101-walking-segment-fixture");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(fixtureDirectory);
        var path = Path.Combine(fixtureDirectory, "animations.json");
        File.WriteAllText(path, fixture.ToJsonString(new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true }));
        return path;
    }

    private static async Task WaitUntilAsync(Func<bool> condition, TimeSpan timeout, string message)
    {
        var elapsed = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        while (!condition() && elapsed.Elapsed < timeout) await Task.Delay(20);
        Program.Assert(condition(), message);
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/V10Acceptance.cs ---
using System.IO;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Data;
using System.Windows.Documents;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Localization;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Themes;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Acceptance;

internal static class V10Acceptance
{
    public static async Task RunAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var language = Locale.Language;
        try
        {
            await run.CaseAsync("v10-language-catalog-placeholders-settings-migration", () => SettingsAsync(run));
            await run.CaseAsync("v10-owned-temporary-files-identity-and-user-file-protection", () => ArtifactsAsync(run));
            await ArchiveJournalAcceptance.RunAsync(run);
            if (!run.NoGui)
            {
                await run.CaseAsync("v10-immediate-language-switch-preserves-controls-bindings-and-user-input", () => SwitchAsync(run));
                await run.CaseAsync("v101-settings-choice-alignment-two-languages-two-themes-three-widths", () => SettingsLayoutAsync(run));
                await run.CaseAsync("v10-language-switch-during-real-queue-state-preserves-batch-and-result", () => BusyAsync(run));
                await run.CaseAsync("v10-english-all-pages-two-themes-three-widths-and-about-links", () => PagesAsync(run));
            }
        }
        finally { Locale.Language = language; OwnedArtifacts.Configure(run.Directory); }
    }

    private static Task SettingsAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        Program.Assert(Locale.Catalog.Count >= 1000, "Full English catalog is missing.");
        Program.Assert(Locale.ValidateCatalog().Count == 0, string.Join("\n", Locale.ValidateCatalog()));
        var store = new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "v10-language-settings"));
        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(store.FilePath)!);
        foreach (var value in new object?[] { null, "future", 42, new { future = true } })
        {
            File.WriteAllText(store.FilePath, JsonSerializer.Serialize(new { Language = value, OutputDirectory = "D:\\用户结果", RecoveryDigits = 6, CompressionMaximumMb = 17 }));
            var settings = store.Load();
            Program.Assert(settings.Language == "zh-CN" && settings.OutputDirectory == "D:\\用户结果" && settings.RecoveryDigits == 6 && settings.CompressionMaximumMb == 17, "Language migration discarded existing settings.");
        }
        var data = store.Load(); data.Language = "en-US"; store.Save(data);
        Program.Assert(store.Load().Language == "en-US", "English preference failed to persist.");
        File.Copy(store.FilePath, store.FilePath + ".bak", true);
        File.WriteAllText(store.FilePath, "null");
        Program.Assert(store.Load().Language == "en-US" && store.Load().OutputDirectory == data.OutputDirectory, "A null settings document did not recover the prior profile.");
        Locale.Language = "en-US";
        Program.Assert(Locale.T("设置") == "Settings", "Basic English label is missing.");
        var captured = "用户 自定义 {文件}.mp4";
        var template = Locale.Catalog.Keys.First(key => key.Contains("{0}", StringComparison.Ordinal) && !key.Contains("{1}", StringComparison.Ordinal) && key.Contains("个文件", StringComparison.Ordinal));
        var source = string.Format(template, captured);
        var translated = Locale.TranslateText(source);
        Program.Assert(translated.Contains(captured, StringComparison.Ordinal), "Translation changed a captured user filename.");
        Locale.Language = "zh-CN";
        Program.Assert(Locale.TranslateText(translated) == source, "Rendered template did not switch back to Chinese.");
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }

    private static async Task ArtifactsAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var dataRoot = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "v10-owned-artifacts"); Directory.CreateDirectory(dataRoot);
        OwnedArtifacts.Configure(dataRoot);
        var output = Path.Combine(dataRoot, "user-output"); Directory.CreateDirectory(output);
        var source = Path.Combine(output, "原文件.txt"); var result = Path.Combine(output, "已转换.png");
        File.WriteAllText(source, "user source"); File.WriteAllText(result, "committed result");
        var own = Path.Combine(output, ".cat-test-" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N") + ".tmp");
        var unknown = Path.Combine(output, ".cat-test-user-file.tmp");
        File.WriteAllText(own, "owned transient"); File.WriteAllText(unknown, "unregistered user file");
        OwnedArtifacts.Register(own);
        Program.Assert(OwnedArtifacts.Cleanup(dataRoot).Count == 0 && !File.Exists(own), "Registered file was not cleaned.");
        Program.Assert(File.ReadAllText(source) == "user source" && File.ReadAllText(result) == "committed result" && File.Exists(unknown), "Cleanup deleted user files or searched by prefix.");
        var replaced = Path.Combine(output, ".cat-test-replaced.tmp"); File.WriteAllText(replaced, "old owned temporary"); OwnedArtifacts.Register(replaced);
        File.Move(replaced, replaced + ".moved"); await Task.Delay(20); File.WriteAllText(replaced, "user replacement");
        Program.Assert(OwnedArtifacts.Cleanup(dataRoot).Contains(replaced) && File.ReadAllText(replaced) == "user replacement", "Cleanup ignored file identity.");
        // A preserved replacement remains unknown even though its pathname once belonged to the app.
        File.Delete(replaced); OwnedArtifacts.Cleanup(dataRoot);
        Program.Assert(File.ReadAllText(replaced + ".moved") == "old owned temporary", "Cleanup chased a renamed file.");
        var ledger = Path.Combine(dataRoot, "owned-temporary-files.json");
        File.WriteAllText(ledger, "[null]"); var refused = false;
        try { OwnedArtifacts.Cleanup(dataRoot); } catch (InvalidDataException) { refused = true; }
        Program.Assert(refused && File.ReadAllText(source) == "user source", "A corrupt receipt did not fail safely.");
        File.Delete(ledger);
        OwnedArtifacts.Configure(run.Directory);
    }

    private static MainViewModel Model(AcceptanceRun run, string name, HoldEngine? engine = null)
    {
        var directory = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "v10-" + name);
        var store = new SettingsStore(Path.Combine(directory, "settings"));
        store.Save(new AppSettings { Language = "zh-CN", OutputDirectory = Path.Combine(directory, "用户的输出"), MotionPreference = MotionPreference.Reduced,
            Pet = new PetSettings { Enabled = false, Roaming = false, Topmost = false, MotionMode = PetMotionMode.Reduced } });
        return new MainViewModel(engine ?? new HoldEngine(), PresetCatalog.Load(Path.Combine(run.Root, "shared", "presets.json")), store, new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(directory, "logs")));
    }
    private static async Task Flush(MainWindow window)
    { await window.Dispatcher.InvokeAsync(window.UpdateLayout, DispatcherPriority.ContextIdle); await Task.Delay(50); }
    private static IEnumerable<T> Children<T>(DependencyObject parent) where T : DependencyObject
    {
        for (var i = 0; i < VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(parent); i++)
        {
            var child = VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(parent, i);
            if (child is T item) yield return item;
            foreach (var nested in Children<T>(child)) yield return nested;
        }
    }
    private static IEnumerable<string> Texts(MainWindow window) => Children<TextBlock>(window).Where(item => item.IsVisible).Select(item => item.Text);

    private static async Task SwitchAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var model = Model(run, "live-switch"); var window = new MainWindow(model) { ShowActivated = false }; window.Show();
        try
        {
            window.ShowSettingsPage(); await Flush(window);
            foreach (var expander in Children<Expander>(window)) expander.IsExpanded = true;
            await Flush(window);
            var input = Children<TextBox>(window).First(box => box.Text == model.EngineDirectory);
            input.Text = "D:\\我的引擎\\User Input";
            var tooltipSource = new ContentControl();
            input.SetBinding(ToolTipService.ToolTipProperty, new Binding(nameof(ContentControl.Content)) { Source = tooltipSource });
            LocalizedUi.AttachTree(window);
            var tooltipBinding = BindingOperations.GetBindingExpression(input, ToolTipService.ToolTipProperty);
            var title = (TextBlock)window.FindName("PageTitleText");
            var binding = BindingOperations.GetBindingExpression(title, TextBlock.TextProperty);
            model.Language = "en-US"; await Flush(window);
            Program.Assert(Texts(window).Contains("Settings"), "Settings did not switch immediately.");
            Program.Assert(Children<TextBox>(window).Contains(input) && input.Text == "D:\\我的引擎\\User Input", "Language switch recreated a control or modified input.");
            Program.Assert(ReferenceEquals(binding, BindingOperations.GetBindingExpression(title, TextBlock.TextProperty)), "Translation replaced a live binding.");
            tooltipSource.Content = "设置"; await Flush(window);
            Program.Assert((string?)input.ToolTip == "Settings" && ReferenceEquals(tooltipBinding, BindingOperations.GetBindingExpression(input, ToolTipService.ToolTipProperty)), "A tooltip binding that initially had no text stopped updating or was replaced.");
            model.Language = "zh-CN"; await Flush(window);
            Program.Assert(Texts(window).Contains("设置") && input.Text == "D:\\我的引擎\\User Input" && (string?)input.ToolTip == "设置", "Switching back lost Chinese, input or a dynamic tooltip.");
        }
        finally { window.ApproveExit(); window.Close(); Locale.Language = "zh-CN"; }
    }

    private static async Task BusyAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var engine = new HoldEngine(); var model = Model(run, "busy-language", engine);
        var source = Path.Combine(run.Directory, "我的视频.mp4"); File.WriteAllText(source, "controlled fixture"); run.TrackSource(source);
        var window = new MainWindow(model) { ShowActivated = false }; window.Show();
        try
        {
            await model.AddFilesAsync(new[] { source });
            var row = model.Queue.Single(); var running = model.RunQueueAsync();
            await engine.Started.Task.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10));
            var request = engine.Request!; var output = model.OutputDirectory;
            window.ShowSettingsPage(); model.Language = "en-US"; await Flush(window);
            Program.Assert(model.IsProcessing && ReferenceEquals(row, model.Queue.Single()) && ReferenceEquals(request, engine.Request) && !engine.Cancelled && model.OutputDirectory == output, "Language switch changed the running batch.");
            engine.Release.TrySetResult(); await running;
            Program.Assert(row.Status == JobStatus.Completed && row.OutputPath is not null && File.Exists(row.OutputPath), "Language switch prevented the result from committing.");
            model.Language = "zh-CN"; await Flush(window);
            Program.Assert(row.Status == JobStatus.Completed && model.TaskCenterItems.Count == 1, "Language switch duplicated or cleared tasks.");
        }
        finally { engine.Release.TrySetResult(); if (model.IsProcessing) { model.Cancel(); await model.ActiveRun; } window.ApproveExit(); window.Close(); Locale.Language = "zh-CN"; }
    }

    private static async Task SettingsLayoutAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var model = Model(run, "settings-alignment");
        var window = new MainWindow(model) { ShowActivated = false };
        var preference = ThemeManager.Preference;
        var observations = new List<object>();
        window.Show();
        try
        {
            foreach (var theme in new[] { "Light", "Dark" })
            foreach (var width in new[] { 620d, 900d, 1200d })
            {
                ThemeManager.SetPreference(theme); window.Width = width; window.Height = 820;
                window.ShowSettingsPage(); await Flush(window);
                var choices = Children<ComboBox>(window).ToArray();
                var language = choices.Single(box => box.Items.OfType<ComboBoxItem>().Any(item => item.Tag is "zh-CN"));
                var appearance = choices.Single(box => box.Items.OfType<ComboBoxItem>().Any(item => item.Tag is "Light"));
                var motion = choices.Single(box => box.Items.OfType<ComboBoxItem>().Any(item => item.Tag is MotionPreference.Reduced));
                foreach (var locale in new[] { "zh-CN", "en-US" })
                {
                    model.Language = locale; await Flush(window);
                    var controls = new[] { language, appearance, motion };
                    var left = controls.Select(control => control.TransformToAncestor(window).Transform(new Point()).X).ToArray();
                    var widths = controls.Select(control => control.ActualWidth).ToArray();
                    Program.Assert(left.Max() - left.Min() <= .5, "Language, appearance and animation preferences have different left edges.");
                    Program.Assert(widths.All(value => value >= 239.5 && value <= 320.5), "A settings choice fell outside its 240–320 DIP width range.");
                    Program.Assert(controls.All(control => control.IsArrangeValid && control.IsMeasureValid), "Settings alignment was checked before layout completed.");
                    observations.Add(new { language = locale, theme, windowWidth = width, left, widths });
                    Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, "v101-settings-" + locale + "-" + theme + "-" + width);
                }
            }
            run.Evidence["v101SettingsChoiceAlignment"] = observations;
        }
        finally { window.ApproveExit(); window.Close(); Locale.Language = "zh-CN"; ThemeManager.SetPreference(preference); }
    }

    private static async Task PagesAsync(AcceptanceRun run)
    {
        var model = Model(run, "english-layout"); var window = new MainWindow(model) { ShowActivated = false };
        using var pet = new PetController(model); window.AttachPetController(pet); window.Show();
        var preference = ThemeManager.Preference;
        var observations = new List<object>();
        try
        {
            model.Language = "en-US"; Locale.ClearMissingKeys();
            var pages = new (string Name, Action Show)[] {
                ("home", window.ShowHomePage), ("tasks", window.ShowTaskCenterPage), ("settings", window.ShowSettingsPage), ("about", window.ShowAboutPage),
                ("video", () => { model.SetMode(ConversionMode.Video); window.ShowFilePage(); }), ("audio", () => { model.SetMode(ConversionMode.Audio); window.ShowFilePage(); }),
                ("image", () => { model.SetMode(ConversionMode.Image); window.ShowFilePage(); }), ("extract", () => { model.SetMode(ConversionMode.Extract); window.ShowFilePage(); }),
                ("gif", () => { model.SetGifPage(); window.ShowFilePage(); }), ("compression", () => { model.SetCompressionPage(); window.ShowFilePage(); }),
                ("archive", () => { model.SetArchiveCreatePage(); window.ShowFilePage(); }), ("unpack", () => { model.SetArchiveExtractPage(); window.ShowFilePage(); }),
                ("recovery", () => { model.SetPasswordRecoveryPage(); window.ShowFilePage(); }), ("obfuscate", () => { model.SetImageObfuscatePage(); window.ShowFilePage(); }),
                ("restore-image", () => { model.SetImageDeobfuscatePage(); window.ShowFilePage(); }), ("pet", () => window.ShowPetPage()),
                ("shop", () => window.ShowPetPage("shop")), ("pet-settings", () => window.ShowPetPage("settings")) };
            foreach (var theme in new[] { "Light", "Dark" })
            foreach (var width in new[] { 620d, 900d, 1200d })
            {
                ThemeManager.SetPreference(theme); window.Width = width; window.Height = 820;
                foreach (var page in pages)
                {
                    page.Show(); await Flush(window);
                    var untranslated = Texts(window).Where(text => Regex.IsMatch(text, "[\\u4e00-\\u9fff]") && !text.Contains("大肥猫瑞瑞", StringComparison.Ordinal) && text != "简体中文" && !Path.IsPathFullyQualified(text)).Distinct().ToArray();
                    observations.Add(new { theme, width, page = page.Name, untranslated });
                    if (width == 1200 || page.Name is "settings" or "about") Program.CaptureWindow(run, window, "v10-en-" + theme + "-" + width + "-" + page.Name);
                    if (page.Name == "about")
                    {
                        var block = Children<TextBlock>(window).First(item => item.Inlines.OfType<Hyperlink>().Any());
                        var addresses = Children<TextBlock>(window).SelectMany(item => item.Inlines.OfType<Hyperlink>()).Select(link => link.NavigateUri.AbsoluteUri).ToArray();
                        Program.Assert(addresses.Contains("https://b23.tv/LIbsjus") && addresses.Contains("mailto:bigcatruirui@outlook.com"), "About contact links changed.");
                        Program.Assert(AppVersion.Label == "V1.0.1", "Displayed version did not come from 1.0.1.");
                    }
                }
            }
            run.Evidence["v10EnglishPages"] = observations;
            var missing = Locale.MissingKeys.ToArray(); run.Evidence["v10MissingLanguageKeys"] = missing;
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(run.Directory, "v10-english-page-audit.json"), JsonSerializer.Serialize(observations, new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true }));
            Program.Assert(observations.All(item => ((string[])item.GetType().GetProperty("untranslated")!.GetValue(item)!).Length == 0), "English page audit found untranslated labels; see v10-english-page-audit.json.");
        }
        finally { window.ApproveExit(); window.Close(); Locale.Language = "zh-CN"; ThemeManager.SetPreference(preference); }
    }

    private sealed class HoldEngine : IConversionEngine
    {
        public TaskCompletionSource Started { get; } = new(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
        public TaskCompletionSource Release { get; } = new(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
        public ConversionRequest? Request { get; private set; }
        public bool Cancelled { get; private set; }
        public Task<MediaInfo> ProbeAsync(string path, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default) => Task.FromResult(new MediaInfo(true, true, 1, "mp4", "h264", "aac", 20, 64, 64, 25));
        public async Task<ConversionResult> ConvertAsync(ConversionRequest request, IProgress<ConversionProgress>? progress = null, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            Request = request; Started.TrySetResult(); progress?.Report(new(25, TimeSpan.Zero));
            try { await Release.Task.WaitAsync(cancellationToken); }
            catch (OperationCanceledException) { Cancelled = true; throw; }
            Directory.CreateDirectory(request.OutputDirectory); var path = Path.Combine(request.OutputDirectory, request.Id + ".mp4"); File.WriteAllText(path, "committed fixture");
            return new(JobStatus.Completed, path, null);
        }
    }
}

# Archive acceptance fixtures

RAR fixtures are from SharpCompress 0.50.4, under its accompanying MIT license.
They are used only by the acceptance runner, never executed, and are not included
in the production application. No WinRAR executable is redistributed.

Source: https://github.com/adamhathcock/sharpcompress/tree/0.50.4/tests/TestArchives/Archives

- Rar.rar (RAR4): SHA256 3319DE3E8A91A58D08D8A83F48EBAE7E6B022C542B4A58775BAB64F4063988A9
- Rar5.rar (RAR5): SHA256 60DB161DE57DC59AA12E0C45B1B70D78904DA3D104E74748972AC38643F12802

Both decode to the same three files and three directories. Expected content
hashes were independently checked with the official 7-Zip 26.04 CLI:

- тест.txt: 4D581D93D369F6E1C9B295FF38D82DABD577F927DFAF0C35818C015C85E322D9
- exe/test.exe: 8557928804F57ECC340B3BB38B095A3607474EC8DEB0076F316FCFE02B562106
- jpg/test.jpg: B251C7501FB0F55DD4A92FEABE0A6F5733BC40A02679498155FAE9B30138FC53


--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/fixtures/image-obfuscation/generate-vectors.py ---
"""Independent interoperability vectors. Original SHA256/Fisher-Yates reference;
Gilbert traversal comes from the pinned BSD-2-Clause author source, not a website.
Run from the workspace with Python 3; only writes the adjacent vectors.json.
"""
import hashlib, importlib.util, json, pathlib, struct
root = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
source = root / 'src/CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure/ImageObfuscationSources/gilbert2d.py'
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('gilbert_reference', source)
gilbert = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(gilbert)

def pixels(w, h, bits):
    data = bytearray()
    for i in range(w*h):
        if bits == 8:
            data.extend([(i*17+c*43)&255 for c in range(3)] + [[0,1,127,255][i%4]])
        else:
            for c in range(4):
                value = (i*997+c*12289)&65535 if c < 3 else [0,1,32767,65535][i%4]
                data.extend(struct.pack('>H', value))
    return data

def permutation(n, domain, w, h):
    key = f'CatFileAssistant.ImageObfuscation.v1|{domain}|{w}|{h}'.encode()
    def words():
        counter = 0
        while True:
            yield from struct.unpack('<8I', hashlib.sha256(key+struct.pack('<Q',counter)).digest())
            counter += 1
    stream = words()
    indices = list(range(n))
    for index in reversed(range(1,n)):
        bound = index+1
        threshold = ((1<<32)-bound)%bound
        value = next(stream)
        while value < threshold: value = next(stream)
        other = value%bound
        indices[index],indices[other] = indices[other],indices[index]
    return indices

def transform(data, w, h, bits, mode):
    stride = bits//8*4
    result = bytearray(len(data))
    if mode == 'Gilbert':
        path = list(gilbert.gilbert2d(w,h))
        offset = int(w*h*0.6180339887498949)
        order = [(y*w+x) for x,y in path]
        pairs = zip(order,order[offset:]+order[:offset])
    elif mode == 'Pixel':
        pairs = enumerate(permutation(w*h,'pixel',w,h))
    else:
        groups = {}
        for y in range(0,h,32):
            for x in range(0,w,32):
                size = min(32,w-x),min(32,h-y)
                groups.setdefault(size,[]).append((x,y))
        for (tw,th),tiles in groups.items():
            order = permutation(len(tiles),f'block:{tw}x{th}',w,h)
            for dest,src in enumerate(order):
                dx,dy = tiles[dest]; sx,sy = tiles[src]
                for row in range(th):
                    result[((dy+row)*w+dx)*stride:((dy+row)*w+dx+tw)*stride] = data[((sy+row)*w+sx)*stride:((sy+row)*w+sx+tw)*stride]
        return result
    for dest,src in pairs:
        result[dest*stride:(dest+1)*stride] = data[src*stride:(src+1)*stride]
    return result

vectors = []
for w,h in [(2,3),(65,47),(17,13),(1,19),(19,1)]:
    for bits in [8,16]:
        for mode in ['Gilbert','Block','Pixel']:
            count = 3 if (w,h)==(65,47) else 1
            data = pixels(w,h,bits)
            for _ in range(count): data = transform(data,w,h,bits,mode)
            vectors.append(dict(width=w,height=h,bitDepth=bits,mode=mode,iterations=count,sha256=hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest().upper()))
document = dict(protocol='CatFileAssistant.ImageObfuscation.v1', reference='Independent Python reference plus pinned Jakub Cerveny Gilbert traversal',
                gilbertSourceSha256=hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest(), generatorSha256=hashlib.sha256(pathlib.Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest(), vectors=vectors)
pathlib.Path(__file__).with_name('vectors.json').write_text(json.dumps(document,indent=2)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')

--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/fixtures/recovery/generate.ps1 ---
param(
  [string]$SevenZip = (Join-Path (Split-Path (Split-Path (Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent) -Parent) -Parent) 'tools\7zip\bin\7z.exe'),
  [string]$Rar = 'C:\Program Files\WinRAR\Rar.exe'
)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$fixtureRoot = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($PSScriptRoot).TrimEnd('\')
$sourceRoot = Join-Path $fixtureRoot 'source'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $sourceRoot | Out-Null
[IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $sourceRoot 'plain.txt'), "CatFileAssistant original recovery fixture v1.`nOnly synthetic public test data.`n", [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
[IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $sourceRoot 'second.txt'), "Independent entry validates whole-archive password verification.`n", [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
$random = [Random]::new(7304)
$payload = [byte[]]::new(16384)
$random.NextBytes($payload)
[IO.File]::WriteAllBytes((Join-Path $sourceRoot 'binary.bin'), $payload)
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $SevenZip) -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Rar)) { throw 'Fixture generators are missing. This development script never downloads or redistributes them.' }
$records = [Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
function New-Fixture([string]$name, [string]$tool, [string[]]$arguments, [string]$password, [string[]]$entries) {
  $archivePath = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Join-Path $fixtureRoot $name))
  if (-not $archivePath.StartsWith($fixtureRoot + '\', [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { throw 'Fixture path escaped its own directory.' }
  # Only outputs explicitly named by this fixture are replaced. Existing sources and unrelated files are retained.
  if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $archivePath) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $archivePath -Force }
  & $tool @arguments
  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "Fixture generation failed: $name" }
  $baseName = [IO.Path]::GetFileNameWithoutExtension($name)
  $parts = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $fixtureRoot -File | Where-Object { $_.Name -eq $name -or $_.Name.StartsWith($name + '.', [StringComparison]::Ordinal) -or $_.Name.StartsWith($baseName + '.part', [StringComparison]::Ordinal) })
  if ($parts.Count -eq 0) { throw "Fixture output missing: $name" }
  $records.Add([ordered]@{
    name=$name; generator=[IO.Path]::GetFileName($tool); generatorVersion=(Get-Item -LiteralPath $tool).VersionInfo.ProductVersion
    arguments=$arguments; publicTestPassword=$password; entries=$entries
    files=@($parts | Sort-Object Name | ForEach-Object { [ordered]@{name=$_.Name; bytes=$_.Length; sha256=(Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant()} })
  })
}
Push-Location $sourceRoot
try {
  foreach ($digits in @(4,6)) {
    $password = '0' * $digits
    foreach ($encryption in @('AES256','ZipCrypto')) {
      $name = "zip-$encryption-$digits.zip"
      New-Fixture $name $SevenZip @('a','-tzip','-y','-bso0','-bsp0','-bse1',('-mem=' + $encryption),('-p' + $password),(Join-Path $fixtureRoot $name),'plain.txt') $password @('plain.txt')
    }
    foreach ($header in @('on','off')) {
      $name = "7z-header-$header-$digits.7z"
      New-Fixture $name $SevenZip @('a','-t7z','-y','-bso0','-bsp0','-bse1','-m0=lzma2',('-mhe=' + $header),('-p' + $password),(Join-Path $fixtureRoot $name),'plain.txt') $password @('plain.txt')
    }
    foreach ($version in @(4,5)) {
      foreach ($header in @($true,$false)) {
        $headerLabel = if ($header) { 'on' } else { 'off' }
        $name = "rar$version-header-$headerLabel-$digits.rar"
        $passwordArgument = if ($header) { '-hp' + $password } else { '-p' + $password }
        New-Fixture $name $Rar @('a','-y','-idq','-ep1','-m0',('-ma' + $version),$passwordArgument,(Join-Path $fixtureRoot $name),'plain.txt') $password @('plain.txt')
      }
    }
  }
  $name = 'zip-priority.zip'
  New-Fixture $name $SevenZip @('a','-tzip','-y','-bso0','-bsp0','-bse1','-mem=ZipCrypto','-p7304',(Join-Path $fixtureRoot $name),'plain.txt') '7304' @('plain.txt')
  $name = 'zip-exhaustion.zip'
  New-Fixture $name $SevenZip @('a','-tzip','-y','-bso0','-bsp0','-bse1','-mem=ZipCrypto','-p12cat8',(Join-Path $fixtureRoot $name),'plain.txt') '12cat8' @('plain.txt')
  $name = 'zip-mixed.zip'
  New-Fixture $name $SevenZip @('a','-tzip','-y','-bso0','-bsp0','-bse1','-mem=ZipCrypto','-p7304',(Join-Path $fixtureRoot $name),'plain.txt') '7304 / 8405 per entry' @('plain.txt','second.txt')
  & $SevenZip a '-tzip' '-y' '-bso0' '-bsp0' '-bse1' '-mem=ZipCrypto' '-p8405' (Join-Path $fixtureRoot $name) 'second.txt'
  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Mixed-password update failed.' }
  $records[$records.Count - 1].files = @([ordered]@{name=$name; bytes=(Get-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $fixtureRoot $name)).Length; sha256=(Get-FileHash -LiteralPath (Join-Path $fixtureRoot $name) -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant()})
  $records[$records.Count - 1].updateArguments = @('a','-tzip','-mem=ZipCrypto','-p8405',$name,'second.txt')
  $name = '7z-solid.7z'
  New-Fixture $name $SevenZip @('a','-t7z','-y','-bso0','-bsp0','-bse1','-m0=lzma2','-ms=on','-mhe=on','-p0000',(Join-Path $fixtureRoot $name),'plain.txt','second.txt') '0000' @('plain.txt','second.txt')
  $name = '7z-split.7z'
  New-Fixture $name $SevenZip @('a','-t7z','-y','-bso0','-bsp0','-bse1','-m0=Copy','-mhe=on','-v5k','-p000000',(Join-Path $fixtureRoot $name),'binary.bin') '000000' @('binary.bin')
  foreach ($version in @(4,5)) {
    $name = "rar$version-solid.rar"
    New-Fixture $name $Rar @('a','-y','-idq','-ep1','-m1','-s',('-ma' + $version),'-hp0000',(Join-Path $fixtureRoot $name),'plain.txt','second.txt') '0000' @('plain.txt','second.txt')
    $name = "rar$version-split.rar"
    New-Fixture $name $Rar @('a','-y','-idq','-ep1','-m0','-v5k',('-ma' + $version),'-hp000000',(Join-Path $fixtureRoot $name),'binary.bin') '000000' @('binary.bin')
  }
} finally { Pop-Location }
$provenance = [ordered]@{
  version=1; generatedAt=(Get-Date).ToString('o'); ownership='Original CatFileAssistant synthetic text and deterministic pseudorandom bytes; no external password corpus or user files.'
  publicPasswordsOnly=$true; rarToolRedistributed=$false; rarLicenseReference='https://www.rarlab.com/license.htm'
  source=@(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $sourceRoot -File | Sort-Object Name | ForEach-Object {[ordered]@{name=$_.Name; bytes=$_.Length; sha256=(Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant()}})
  archives=$records
}
[IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $fixtureRoot 'provenance.json'), ($provenance | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10), [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
Write-Output "Generated $($records.Count) original recovery archive cases."

--- ORIGINAL FILE: tests/fixtures/recovery/README.md ---
# Original password recovery acceptance fixtures

These archives contain only text written for this project and deterministic
pseudorandom bytes generated with seed 7304. All passwords are public test data.
They contain no executables, user data, leaked password lists, or external assets.

`generate.ps1` records each generator, version, arguments, source SHA-256, archive
SHA-256, and public test password in `provenance.json`. ZIP and 7z are generated
with the project's official 7-Zip. RAR4/5 samples were generated with the developer
machine's installed RAR 5.61 CLI; RAR/WinRAR binaries and registration information
are never copied into this fixture set or the application.

Regenerating RAR samples requires legally usable RAR software on the development
machine. The [official RARLAB EULA](https://www.rarlab.com/license.htm) distinguishes
using licensed software to produce/distribute archives from redistributing the
archiver itself. Installation or a registration file alone does not establish a
license's validity. No zero-risk legal claim is made.

Coverage includes 4/6 digit passwords with leading zeros, ZIP AES-256/ZipCrypto,
7z and RAR4/5 with and without encrypted headers, solid archives, complete volume
sets, an outside-scope password for exhaustive-negative testing, and a ZIP whose
two entries have different passwords. Samples are included only in the acceptance
runner, never the production application. Independent 7-Zip test and streamed
SHA-256 checks validate them without executing or committing extracted files.
